### [1] [Topology of a Smile: Persistent Homology in Dental Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21422)
*Leon Dahlmeier,Sara Kališnik,Albert Mehl,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于持久同调（persistent homology）和支持向量机（SVM）的方法，用于在CBCT牙科扫描中自动完成牙齿分类和病理诊断，分别达到97.67%和96.77%的准确率，优于在相同数据上训练的CNN（70.27%和86.67%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT扫描能为牙科诊断和治疗规划提供三维图像，但人工分析和记录这些扫描影像劳动密集且耗时。为减轻临床负担，需要自动化牙齿分类和病理识别等关键步骤。

Method: 利用拓扑数据分析中的持久同调框架（识别连接分量、孔洞和空洞等多尺度拓扑特征）提取CBCT三维影像的形状特征，结合支持向量机（SVM）分类器完成牙齿标注和病理诊断任务。

Result: 牙齿标注任务平均准确率97.67%，诊断任务平均准确率96.77%；在相同数据上训练的CNN仅分别达到70.27%和86.67%，本文方法显著优于CNN基线。

Conclusion: 持久同调结合SVM在CBCT牙齿分类和诊断任务上展现了优于深度学习（CNN）的性能，为牙科影像自动化分析提供了一种有效的替代方案。

Abstract: CBCT (Cone Beam Computed Tomography) scans provide detailed three-dimensional images, widely used in dentistry for diagnostic and treatment planning tasks. While invaluable, analyzing and documenting these scans is labor-intensive, prompting efforts to automate key steps like the classification and segmentation of anatomical structures to identify tooth types and associated pathologies. In this article, we propose an approach to automation that leverages persistent homology, a framework from topological data analysis that studies the shape of data by identifying features like connected components, holes, and voids across multiple scales. Persistent homology, together with a support vector machine, allows us to classify teeth in a CBCT scan and to perform diagnostics. Our method advances the state of the art, reaching average accuracy scores of 97.67% for tooth-labeling and 96.77% for diagnostic tasks, outperforming a CNN trained on the same data with accuracy of 70.27% and 86.67%, respectively.

</details>


### [2] [EditStream: A Unified Autoregressive Framework for Interactive Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21424)
*Yuqian Zhou,Zhenghong Zhou,Zongze Wu,Cameron Smith,Richard Zhang,Jiebo Luo,Eli Shechtman,Zhe Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditStream是一个统一的交互式视频生成与编辑框架，基于DiT模型将文本生成视频、图像生成视频、视频到视频、编辑传播、参考引导编辑和相机位姿变换等多种任务整合到单一模型中，并通过VMM与自回归展开的两阶段蒸馏实现快速少步自回归生成，兼顾生成质量与时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频生成与编辑在创意设计中日益重要，但现有的扩散视频模型通常较慢且难以支持多种编辑任务的无缝统一。作者希望构建一个既能高质量生成、又能高效流式交互的统一框架，解决少步自回归生成中的过度饱和、运动退化、时间不稳定和训练复杂等挑战。

Method: EditStream采用单一DiT模型，通过灵活的任务特定条件统一多种视频生成与编辑任务。核心创新是两阶段蒸馏：Velocity Moment Matching (VMM)在学生到达的中间状态匹配条件速度矩以保持生成质量与运动，自回归展开(unrolling)让学生暴露于自身预测以提升时间稳定性。两者结合使模型成为快速少步自回归生成器，适合流式交互。

Result: EditStream在统一框架内支持Text-to-Video、Image-to-Video、Video-to-Video、Editing Propagation、Reference-guided Video Editing和Camera Pose Change六类任务，解决了少步自回归视频生成中的过度饱和、运动退化、时间不稳定和训练复杂度问题，为高质量扩散视频模型与交互式创意工作流之间架起桥梁。

Conclusion: EditStream提供了一个实用且可扩展的统一方案，使基于扩散的高质量视频模型能够用于交互式创作，展示了任务统一与快速自回归蒸馏相结合的可行性。

Abstract: Interactive video generation and editing are becoming increasingly important for creative design. In this report, we introduce EditStream: a unified framework for interactive video generation and editing. EditStream unifies multiple video creation and manipulation tasks within a single DiT-based model through flexible task-specific conditioning, and further transforms it into a fast, few-step autoregressive model for efficient streaming. It supports Text-to-Video, Image-to-Video, Video-to-Video, Editing Propagation, Reference-guided Video Editing, and Camera Pose Change, enabling flexible control over video generation, transformation, and editing within one system. To make the unified model practical for interactive use, we develop a two-stage distillation approach that combines Velocity Moment Matching (VMM) with autoregressive unrolling. VMM matches conditional velocity moments at student-reached intermediate states to preserve generation quality and motion, while unrolling exposes the student to its own autoregressive predictions to improve temporal stability. Together, they alleviate common challenges in few-step autoregressive video generation, including over-saturation, degraded motion, temporal instability, and complex training. EditStream provides a practical and scalable solution that bridges high-quality diffusion-based video models with interactive creative workflows.

</details>


### [3] [Aligning Human Sense: Calibrated Distributional Reward Learning for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21425)
*Nai-Xin Zhai,Weihua Cheng,Dexu Yu,Yikai Gu,Hanwen Du,Junchen Fu,Chenxi Huang,Yingwei Song,Liyuan Lillian Ma,Yang Ran,Youhua Li,Yongxin Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视频生成的统一偏好感知学习框架，通过精英引导过滤校准偏好数据、将视频质量建模为多维奖励分布并用Wasserstein距离对齐、以及将基于Wasserstein的分布对齐引入GRPO策略优化，解决奖励信号可靠性、多维度偏好坍缩和全局结构匹配三个挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成质量对齐人类偏好是生成质量评估的关键，但现有方法面临三大挑战：1）人类偏好数据受主观噪声和偏差影响导致奖励信号不可靠；2）标量奖励模型将多维度偏好坍缩为单一值，丢失动态权衡信息；3）策略优化中广泛使用的KL散度仅施加局部约束，无法捕获人类偏好的全局结构。

Method: 1) 精英引导过滤（elite-guided filtering）校准偏好数据，构建可靠监督信号；2) 将视频质量建模为多维奖励分布以捕获人类偏好的不确定性，使用Wasserstein距离将学习到的奖励分布与经验人类偏好分布对齐；3) 将基于Wasserstein的分布对齐引入GRPO，引导策略优化更好地匹配视频上人类偏好的全局结构。

Result: 在奖励建模和视频生成上的实验表明，所提方法提高了奖励信号的可靠性和生成视频的感知一致性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过多维奖励分布建模和Wasserstein距离对齐，统一解决了偏好数据噪音、多维偏好坍缩和局部约束三个问题，提升了视频生成的对齐质量和感知一致性。

Abstract: Video generation is central to AI-powered content creation. Aligning generated videos with human preferences is a key criterion for evaluating generation quality. Despite significant progress in visual quality, three key challenges remain. First, the reliability of reward signals is constrained by the quality of human preference data, which is often affected by subjective noise and bias. Second, standard scalar reward models collapse multi-aspect human preferences into a single value, leading to the loss of dynamic trade-offs across multiple preference dimensions. Third, in policy optimization, the widely adopted KL divergence imposes primarily local constraints and may fail to capture the global structure of human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose a unified preference-aware learning framework for video generation. First, we introduce elite-guided filtering to calibrate preference data and construct reliable supervision for reward model training. We then model video quality as a multidimensional reward distribution to capture the uncertainty inherent in human preferences, and use the Wasserstein distance to align the learned reward distribution with the empirical human preference distribution. Finally, we introduce Wasserstein-based distributional alignment into GRPO, guiding policy optimization to better match the global structure of human preferences over videos. Experiments on reward modeling and video generation demonstrate that our approach improves the reliability of reward signals and the perceptual consistency of generated videos. Our code is available at https://github.com/alignhs26/ahs.

</details>


### [4] [AI Visual Inspection for Garment Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21426)
*Ray Wai Man Kong,Ding Ning,Theodore Ho Tin Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一个基于CNN的AI视觉检测系统用于服装缝纫线质量检测，在黑色、红色和深绿色面料上成功检测跳线缺陷，但在浅蓝、银色和荧光黄等视觉差异大的面料及断线缺陷上性能受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服装制造业面临提高质量、降低成本及向工业4.0数字化转型的压力。缝纫线检测是最具挑战性的质量控制环节之一，断针、跳针等缺陷难以通过人工检测一致性地识别，人工检测易受疲劳、主观判断和不一致表现影响，导致缺陷漏检、返工和效率下降。

Method: 构建基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的AI视觉检测系统，使用黑色织物和黑色缝纫线样本进行初始训练，并在黑、红、深绿、浅蓝、银和荧光黄六种颜色的织物上进行实验测试。

Result: 系统成功检测到黑色、红色和深绿色材料上的跳线缺陷，但对断线缺陷以及浅蓝、银色和荧光黄等视觉特征差异显著的面料检测性能受限。

Conclusion: 模型精度强烈依赖于训练数据的多样性及其跨不同织物和缝纫线颜色的泛化能力。

Abstract: The garment manufacturing industry is under increasing pressure to improve product quality, reduce costs, and accelerate digital transformation toward Industry 4.0. One of the most challenging quality-control activities is sewing-line inspection, where defects such as broken stitches and skipped stitches are difficult to detect consistently through manual inspection. Human-based inspection is often affected by fatigue, subjective judgement, and inconsistent performance, resulting in defect leakage, rework, and reduced production efficiency.
  This study presents the development and validation of an Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based visual inspection system for garment sewing-line quality control. The system utilizes Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to detect sewing defects and was initially trained using black fabric and black sewing thread samples. Experimental testing was conducted on black, red, dark green, light blue, silver, and fluorescent yellow fabrics. The results demonstrated successful detection of jump sewing-line defects on black, red, and dark green materials, while performance limitations were observed for broken sewing-line defects and fabrics with significantly different visual characteristics, including light blue, silver, and fluorescent yellow colours. These findings indicate that model accuracy is strongly influenced by the diversity of training data and the ability to generalize across different fabric and thread colours.

</details>


### [5] [Few-Shot Cross-Dataset Adaptation for Tuberculosis Detection Using DenseNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21427)
*Bidhan Biswas,Shahadat Hossain Sohag,Nabil Ashab,Soumit Kumar Kundu,Saif Mahmud Parvez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对结核病胸片检测中的跨数据集域偏移问题，以TBX11K为源域、Mendeley TB为目标域，系统研究了少样本条件下三种训练策略（冻结骨干微调、全量微调、从零训练）对模型性能的影响。实验表明全量微调DenseNet121在每类仅75个标注样本时即可达到98.36%的准确率，证明全量微调预训练模型是缓解低资源临床场景域偏移的高效实用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结核病是全球高致死率的细菌性疾病，尽管现有深度学习模型在单一数据集上检测结核病表现良好，但跨数据集的严重域偏移（不同成像协议、患者人群、设备）导致模型泛化困难，在实际部署中性能显著下降。作者旨在通过可控的少样本跨域实验，探索在低资源场景下如何有效缓解域偏移问题。

Method: 采用受控的跨数据集评估框架，以TBX11K作为源域，Mendeley TB数据集作为目标域。系统改变目标域标注样本数量，在三种训练范式下对比性能：冻结骨干的适配（仅训练分类头）、源域预训练DenseNet121的全量微调、以及从零开始训练。通过绘制适配曲线分析不同策略对域偏移的缓解效果。

Result: 实验结果表明全量微调策略在极少量目标域样本下即可取得优异表现，每类仅使用75个标注样本即达到98.36%的准确率。适配曲线显示微调能够有效缓解域偏移带来的性能下降，即使在数据有限的情况下模型依然保持良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在低资源临床部署场景中，对源域预训练模型进行全量微调是缓解域偏移的最有效且实用的策略，能够以极少的标注样本获得接近完美的检测性能，为医疗影像领域跨域泛化提供了可行的实践指导。

Abstract: Tuberculosis (TB) is one of the most common and dangerous bacterial ailments. Every year, it causes a large number of deaths worldwide. Although many deep learning models can detect tuberculosis from chest X-rays quite accurately, severe domain shift across datasets makes the task challenging. Different imaging protocols, patient demographics, and equipment across domains make the task of generalization difficult. In real-world settings, a model may perform well on one dataset but show a noticeable drop in performance when tested on another. In this work, we address this domain adaptation challenge through a few-shot scaling study. A controlled cross-dataset evaluation is presented in this paper using TBX11K as the source domain and the Mendeley TB dataset as the target domain. It is investigated how varying the number of target samples affects model performance under three training regimes: frozen backbone adaptation, full fine-tuning of a source-pretrained DenseNet121 model, and training from scratch. The results indicate that the model can perform well even with limited data and can achieve 98.36\% accuracy with just 75 labeled samples per class. The adaptation curves demonstrate how fine-tuning effectively mitigates domain shift. These findings establish full fine-tuning of pretrained models as a highly effective and practical strategy for mitigating domain shift in low-resource clinical deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [6] [Measuring Gender Representation in Animated Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21429)
*David Bamman,Allison Cooper,Ruby Alvarez Rubio,Reina Kushihashi,Madison Mar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个计算流水线，用于在224部热门动画电影中识别动画角色，并检验关于性别表征的多个假设。研究发现：虽然女性角色的整体表现在动画和真人电影中大致相当（1980-2025），但人类角色（女性明显更多）与非人类角色（以男性为主）之间存在显著差异；与以往对迪士尼的研究不同，动画电影中女性角色并未在反派角色中减少；女性角色比真人电影中的女性角色更可能共同出现在同一场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动画电影是文化教化的重要载体，但此前对动画电影中性别表征的大规模实证研究主要关注演员的性别构成统计，缺乏更精细的工具（如评估角色在总屏幕时间中的可见性）。作者旨在开发更细粒度的分析工具来检验性别表征假设。

Method: 开发了一个用于识别动画电影中角色的计算流水线（动画角色识别系统），并将其应用于224部热门动画电影语料库，对性别表征的多个假设进行检验。

Result: 研究发现：1）动画电影中女性角色的整体表现与真人电影大致吻合（1980-2025）；2）人类角色和非人类角色之间存在显著差异——人类角色中女性表征更多，非人类角色主要以男性为主；3）与以往迪士尼研究相反，动画电影中女性角色在反派角色中并未减少；4）动画电影中女性角色比真人电影中更可能共同出现在同一场景中。

Conclusion: 该研究为动画电影中的性别表征提供了更精细的计算分析，揭示了人类与非人类角色在性别呈现代际上的显著差异，并纠正了以往关于迪士尼动画反派性别趋势的认知。

Abstract: Animated films--often developed with an audience of children in mind--are an important vector for enculturation, and empirical work that has examined the representation of gender at scale in these films has largely focused on counting the gender composition of the cast rather than deploying a more fine-grained instrument (such as assessing the visibility of those characters in overall screentime). In this work, we develop a computational pipeline for recognizing animated characters in these films, and use it to test several hypotheses about gender representation in a corpus of 224 popular animated movies. We find that while the overall representation of female characters in animated films largely tracks with those of live-action films (over the period 1980-2025), we see stark differences between the representation of human characters (much greater representation among women and girls) and non-humans (largely male). Contrary to past work on Disney, we do not see female characters declining in antagonist roles in animated films, and characters who are women and girls are much more likely to share scenes together than their live action contemporaneous counterparts.

</details>


### [7] [Boosting Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering with Structured Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21431)
*Qiyou Liu,Yong Zhang,Jianjie Luo,Zhenguo Yang,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCoRe框架，通过结构化上下文推理（三阶段：上下文获取、选择、压缩）将异质视觉描述和检索知识转化为显式实体-关系三元组，解决长非结构化提示导致的推理退化问题，在OK-VQA和A-OKVQA上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KVQA方法直接拼接异质视觉描述和检索知识构成长的非结构化提示，导致推理性能下降，源于过多无关上下文和缺乏显式关系结构。

Method: SCoRe三阶段框架：(1) Context Acquisition——生成多样视觉笔记并通过高效两阶段多模态检索获取显式知识；(2) Context Selection——用LLM引导筛选相关的视觉、显式和隐式知识；(3) Context Compression——Relational Logic Distillation (RLD)将原始文本转化为显式实体-关系三元组，作为简洁结构化提示用于最终答案预测。

Result: 在OK-VQA和A-OKVQA基准上一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 结构化的关系三元组提示比长非结构化文本提示更能提升LLM的KVQA推理性能，显式关系结构对多模态推理至关重要。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering aims to answer questions about an image by integrating external knowledge with visual and textual information. Recent approaches often rely on in-context learning to prompt Large Language Models (LLMs) with multimodal context in a zero-shot or few-shot manner. However, we observe that directly concatenating heterogeneous visual descriptions and retrieved knowledge into long, unstructured prompts often degrades reasoning performance, due to both excessive irrelevant context and the lack of explicit relational structure. In this paper, we propose an LLM-based Structured Context Reasoning (SCoRe) framework that infers both explicit and implicit relationships for prediction. SCoRe consists of three stages: Context Acquisition, which generates diverse visual notes and retrieves explicit knowledge via an efficient two-stage multimodal retrieval strategy; Context Selection, which filters relevant visual, explicit, and implicit knowledge using LLM-guided selection; and Context Compression, which performs Relational Logic Distillation (RLD) to transform raw text into explicit entity-relation triplets. These relational triplets serve as a concise and structured prompt for final answer prediction. Extensive experiments on the OK-VQA and A-OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate that SCoRe consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [8] [DesignAgent3D: Interactive 3D Scene Editing via Designer-like Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21438)
*Xiujin Liu,Tianyu Yang,Yilun Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DesignAgent3D 是一个交互式多模态智能体框架，将3D场景编辑重新定义为"规划-感知-行动"的类设计师范式，通过与用户交互澄清模糊意图、在3D场景中精确定位编辑目标、并保持多视角一致性，同时支持NeRF和3D高斯泼溅主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本引导的3D场景编辑方法通常将任务视为单次条件生成，无法解决用户的模糊意图，且在杂乱3D场景中容易出现对象定位漂移、遮挡下的跟踪失败以及多视角"贴纸效应"。

Method: 提出Plan-Perceive-Act三元范式：Plan阶段通过交互式多轮对话澄清语义不明确的编辑目标；Perceive阶段将意图编辑具体定位到3D场景中的特定对象或区域；Act阶段施加受控视觉修改并保持场景一致性，将编辑集成到底层3D表示（NeRF或3DGS）中，支持持久化和多视角一致的新视角渲染。

Result: 在NeRF和3D Gaussian Splatting两种骨干上均显著优于SOTA基线，在语义意图对齐、空间定位精度和多视角一致性方面表现卓越。

Conclusion: 通过将交互式规划和精确空间感知引入3D编辑，设计者式智能体范式有效解决了语义模糊性和定位漂移问题，为3D场景编辑提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Text guided 3D scene editing provides an intuitive interface for modifying reconstructed environments, but remains difficult because natural language design requests are often semantically underspecified and must be grounded in cluttered 3D scenes. Existing methods typically formulate the task as one-shot conditional generation from a single prompt, failing to resolve ambiguous user intents or achieve precise spatial grounding. Consequently, they suffer from severe object localization drift, tracking failure under occlusions, and the notorious multi-view "sticker effect." To overcome these limitations, we present DesignAgent3D, an interactive multimodal agentic framework that reformulates 3D scene editing as a designer-like Plan-Perceive-Act paradigm. The agent first plans by interacting with the user to clarify underspecified design goals, then perceives by grounding the intended edit to specific objects or regions in the 3D scene, and finally acts by applying controlled visual modifications while preserving scene consistency. The edits are further integrated into the underlying 3D representation, supporting persistent and multi-view consistent novel-view rendering. Extensive experiments across both NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting backbones demonstrate that DesignAgent3D significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, delivering superior semantic intent alignment, impeccable spatial localization accuracy, and high-fidelity multi-view consistency.

</details>


### [9] [WorldMind: Decoupled Game World Model for State-Aware NPC Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21439)
*Zhiyang Deng,Boran Zhang,Danze Chen,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldMind 是首个将游戏世界模型中的 NPC 行为解耦为四层（理解、决策、控制、生成）的框架，通过紧凑状态表示实现状态感知的 NPC 行为规划。同时引入 BOSS-140K 数据集（含丰富内部游戏状态配对的动作视频）及自动化采集 agent。实验表明其紧凑状态重建可靠、规划符合机制，约 70% 成对比较中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型中 NPC 行为要么隐式纠缠于视频生成，要么依赖外部显式控制信号，缺乏一个显式的状态接地决策接口，导致模型难以产出响应式、状态感知的 NPC 行为。

Method: 将交互式世界建模解耦为四层：(1) 理解层从生成帧构建紧凑状态；(2) 决策层基于紧凑状态推理 NPC 下一步动作；(3) 控制层将动作翻译为时间对齐的条件；(4) 生成层合成视觉结果。四层通过闭环交互连接以使 NPC 行为接地于演化中的游戏状态。并构建 BOSS-140K 数据集和自动化采集 agent。

Result: 在 BOSS-140K 上验证了紧凑状态重建的可靠性和基于机制规划的有效性；约 70% 的成对比较中 WorldMind 因更战术合理且连贯的 NPC 行为而被偏好。

Conclusion: WorldMind 通过解耦的四层架构和状态接地闭环，实现了更响应式和状态感知的游戏 NPC 行为，为游戏世界模型提供了一种可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Game world models have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in generating visually coherent and action-controllable gameplay videos. However, non-player character (NPC) behavior in existing models is either implicitly entangled with video generation or explicitly prescribed through external control signals. Consequently, a game world model has to jointly understand the state, plan the NPC's response and render its visual outcome, limiting its ability to produce responsive and state-aware NPC behavior. The challenge lies in the lack of an explicit interface for state-grounded decision-making. To this end, we introduce WorldMind, to our knowledge the first decoupled framework for state-aware NPC behavior in game world models. WorldMind separates interactive world modeling into four layers: an Understanding Layer that constructs a compact state from generated frames; a Decision Layer that reasons over the compact state to plan the NPC's next action; a Control Layer that translates the actions into temporally aligned conditions; and a Generation Layer that synthesizes their visual outcomes. By reconnecting layers in a closed interaction loop, WorldMind grounds NPC behavior in the evolving game state. We further introduce BOSS-140K, a dataset of gameplay videos paired with rich internal game states, together with an agent that automates the collection at scale. Experiments on BOSS-140K demonstrate reliable compact state reconstruction and mechanics-grounded planning, with WorldMind preferred over the baselines in approximately 70% of pairwise comparisons for its more tactically appropriate and coherent NPC behavior. Project page: https://teawhite.cn/worldmind_projectpage/

</details>


### [10] [Text-Guided Visual Dependency Graph Learning with Cross-Modal Attention Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21443)
*Fei Wang,Yutong Zhang,Yang Ye,Jinxian Chen,Wang Wenshuai,Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CM-GLasso框架，将视觉-语言表示学习与稀疏高斯图模型结合，通过文本可视化、跨模态注意力蒸馏和联合ADMM优化，估计可解释的跨模态条件依赖结构，支持无参数的分类和轻量级分割，在8个基准上取得SOTA级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多模态视觉-语言特征中估计可解释的条件依赖结构尚未被充分探索，现有方法通常先估计再分解类别级图，缺乏统一框架。

Method: （i）将类别属性描述渲染为图像，通过同一SigLIP-2视觉编码器处理，产生共享坐标系下的patch级注意力足迹；（ii）跨注意力蒸馏将高维patch压缩为少量语义图节点，其注意力足迹相似度提供跨模态结构先验用于非均匀L1惩罚；（iii）联合ADMM公式在单一凸目标中估计共享与类别特定精度矩阵。

Result: 在8个基准上达到竞争性或更优性能；受控协议下平均分类准确率91.97%为最高，VOC分割mIoU 74.75%、ADE20K mIoU 64.01%均为受控基线中最高，同时输出共享-特定分解的稀疏条件依赖图。

Conclusion: CM-GLasso成功将图模型稀疏结构学习与多模态表示学习统一，在分类和分割任务上表现优越，并能产出可解释的跨模态条件依赖图，为多模态理解提供了结构化的新范式。

Abstract: Estimating interpretable conditional-dependence structures from multimodal visual-linguistic features remains largely unexplored. We propose CM-GLasso (Cross-Modal Graphical Lasso), a framework that bridges vision-language representation learning and sparse Gaussian Graphical Models. CM-GLasso introduces three key components: (i) a text visualization strategy that renders class-attribute descriptions as images and processes them through the same SigLIP-2 vision encoder as natural images, yielding prototype-indexed patch-level attention footprints in a shared feature coordinate system; (ii) a cross-attention distillation mechanism that condenses high-dimensional patches into a small set of semantic graph nodes, whose attention-footprint similarities yield cross-modal structural priors for non-uniform L1 penalization; (iii) a joint ADMM formulation that estimates shared and class-specific precision components within a single convex objective, avoiding the need to first estimate and then decompose separate class-wise graphs. The learned sparse graph topologies directly support a parameter-free, precision-based classification rule and a lightweight topology-aware segmentation head. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CM-GLasso achieves competitive or superior performance compared with strong feature-based and task-specific baselines. Under the matched controlled protocol, it attains the highest average classification accuracy (91.97%) and the highest segmentation mIoU among the controlled baselines on VOC (74.75%) and ADE20K (64.01%), while also yielding explicit sparse conditional-dependence graphs with common-specific decomposition.

</details>


### [11] [ViTexSZ: Heterogeneous Vision-Text Knowledge Distillation for EEG Seizure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21445)
*Chenxi Liu,Mingzhao Li,Yicong Liu,Hao Miao,Hongyuan Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Gaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTexSZ是一个异构视觉-文本知识蒸馏框架，用于EEG癫痫检测。它将EEG转换为波形图像，通过查询式多通道对齐模块将源相关视觉特征映射到统一token空间，并利用多模态大语言模型作为异构教师将EEG表示与临床语义对齐，最后通过知识蒸馏将教师表示迁移到轻量级学生模型。在四个EEG癫痫数据集上取得最优准确率，相对提升最高达12.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG癫痫检测的时间序列方法通常针对固定电极通道配置设计，难以适用于通道布局不规则的异构EEG记录。虽然视觉和语言建模提供了有前景的替代方案，但将异构EEG表示与临床语义对齐仍具挑战性。

Method: 1) 将EEG记录转换为结构化波形图像；2) 引入查询式多通道对齐模块，将源相关的视觉特征映射到统一token空间；3) 通过多模态大语言模型构建异构教师，融合对齐后的EEG表示与临床提示词，将高层临床语义与癫痫证据关联；4) 使用视觉-文本知识蒸馏将教师表示迁移到轻量级学生模型用于检测。

Result: 在四个EEG癫痫数据集上验证了ViTexSZ在亚临床和一般癫痫检测场景中的泛化能力，所有数据集上均达到最高准确率，较第二好基线相对提升最高达12.9%。

Conclusion: ViTexSZ成功解决了异构EEG通道配置下的癫痫检测问题，通过视觉-文本蒸馏框架有效整合临床语义，在多个数据集上实现了领先的检测性能。

Abstract: Automated seizure detection from electroencephalography (EEG) is essential for continuous neurological monitoring, particularly for subclinical epileptic seizures that may exhibit only subtle electrographic changes. Existing time-series methods are often designed for fixed EEG channel configurations, thereby limiting their applicability to heterogeneous EEG recordings with irregular channel layouts. Although visual and language modeling offer promising alternatives, aligning heterogeneous EEG representations with clinical semantics remains challenging. We introduce ViTexSZ, a heterogeneous Vision-Text knowledge distillation framework for EEG seizure detection. ViTexSZ converts EEG recordings into structured waveform images and introduces a query-based multi-channel alignment module that maps source-dependent visual features into a unified token space. A heterogeneous teacher further integrates the aligned EEG representations with clinical prompts through a multimodal large language model, associating high-level clinical semantics with seizure-related evidence. Vision-text knowledge distillation then transfers the teacher representations to a lightweight student during detection. Experiments on four EEG seizure datasets demonstrate the generalizability of ViTexSZ across both subclinical and general seizure detection scenarios, achieving the highest accuracy on all datasets and relative improvements of up to 12.9% over the second-best baselines, showing its effectiveness.

</details>


### [12] [BIMScript: Material-Aware Structured Scene Programs for BIM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21447)
*Prakash Kondibhau Naikade,Thomas B. Moeslund,Andreas Møgelmose*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BIMScript 扩展了结构化语言模型（如 SceneScript），将场景重建为一个参数化命令程序，并在单一语法内同时解决三个关键问题：场景由什么构成（材质/条件属性）、生成速度（CUDA-graph 解码器 3.4x 加速 + 草稿-验证方案）、以及每个元素的精确位置（几何吸附 + 混合离散-连续解码头）。系统通过 100k 合成场景的 VLM 材质护照语料库监督，并端到端验证了直接导入 Revit/BIM 工具的流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化语言模型（如 SceneScript）能将场景重建为可编辑的语义显式程序，但其最吸引人的应用——将现有建筑自动导入 BIM 工具——仍受三个问题阻碍：场景构成元素（what）、生成速度（how fast）、元素精确位置（where）。本文旨在统一解决这三个挑战，使结构化语言重建真正落地于 BIM 工作流。

Method: 1) 扩展布局语言，为每个元素添加材质和条件属性，通过构建的 100k 合成场景（1.9M 伪标注元素）VLM 材质护照语料库监督训练，并使用 lifted-feature 点编码器将图像外观路由到材质 token。2) 识别解码瓶颈为 kernel-launch 和 host-synchronization 开销而非计算，设计输出精确的 CUDA-graph 解码器（1.9 vs 6.4 ms/step, 3.4x 加速）及利用确定性实体 schema 的 grammar-parallel 草稿-验证方案。3) 针对 5cm token 网格粒度，提出训练无关的几何吸附和混合离散-连续解码头回归子 bin 偏移来恢复残差。每个命令一对一映射到原生 Revit 对象，通过工作插件和 IFC4 导出验证端到端 BIM 导入。

Result: CUDA-graph 解码器实现 3.4x 推理加速（1.9 vs 6.4 ms/step）；材质/条件属性扩展通过 VLM 语料库监督有效路由图像外观到材质 token；几何吸附和混合解码头分别测量了各自对残差误差的恢复量；端到端验证了程序可一对一映射到 Revit 原生对象并通过工作插件实现 IFC4 导出。

Conclusion: BIMScript 展示了结构化语言模型在 BIM 自动导入场景中的完整可行性，同时解决了构成、速度和精度三大瓶颈，证明了单一语法可以统一承载材质语义、高效推理和亚网格精度，并支持 LLM 驱动的可持续性推理。但研究基于合成扫描场景，真实世界泛化尚未验证。

Abstract: Structured-language models such as SceneScript reconstruct a scene as a short program of parametric commands, an inherently editable and semantically explicit representation. We ask three questions that stand between such models and their most compelling application, automated ingestion of existing buildings into BIM tools, studied here on synthetic scans: \emph{what} is the scene made of, \emph{how fast} can it be produced, and \emph{exactly where} is each element. BIMScript answers all three within one grammar. First, we extend the layout language with per-element \emph{material} and \emph{condition} attributes, supervised by a vision-language-model material-passport corpus we build over 100k synthetic scenes (1.9M pseudo-labeled elements), and route image appearance to the material tokens through a lifted-feature point encoder. Second, we show that autoregressive decoding of these programs is dominated not by compute but by kernel-launch and host-synchronization overhead, and remove it with an output-exact CUDA-graph decoder (1.9 vs 6.4\,ms/step, $3.4\times$) plus a grammar-parallel, tolerance-verified draft-and-verify scheme that exploits the deterministic entity schema. Third, we address the model's 5cm token-grid granularity with training-free geometric snapping and a hybrid discrete--continuous decoder head that regresses a sub-bin offset, and measure how much of the residual error each recovers. Because each command maps one-to-one onto a native Revit object, we validate direct ingestion into a BIM authoring tool end to end with a working add-in and its IFC4 export, and the same program's language form is designed to support LLM-driven, sustainability-aware reasoning over the built asset.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Visual Similarity: Entity-Aligned Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21450)
*Hangrui Xu,Zhengxian Wu,Yunyao Yu,Zhuohong Chen,Rui Cong,Xiangwen Deng,Zhifang Liu,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了KBMR，首个面向知识型视觉问答的MLLM嵌入检索器，通过连续语义蒸馏和MLLM语义判别器解决CLIP式双编码器在实体级语义对齐上的不足，在检索Recall@1和端到端VQA准确率上分别提升14.7%和9.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA检索管道主要使用CLIP风格的双编码器，其优先考虑表面视觉相似性而非实体级语义对齐，导致语义相同但视觉差异大的概念或视觉相似但实体不同的情况检索失败。

Method: 1) 提出KBMR，基于MLLM的嵌入检索器，利用其自回归能力将图像映射到保留概念身份的语义空间；2) 引入MLLM语义判别器生成连续的实体一致性权重；3) 提出连续语义蒸馏目标，实现硬负样本采样和超越二元标签的软监督。

Result: KBMR显著优于CLIP基线，检索Recall@1提升最高14.7%，端到端VQA准确率提升9.4%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MLLM-based嵌入检索是KB-VQA中优于CLIP双编码器范式的有效方向，连续语义蒸馏解决了Wikipedia规模检索中的噪声监督问题。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) relies on retrieving external information to answer queries involving long-tail entities. However, existing retrieval pipelines predominantly employ CLIP-style dual encoders, which prioritize surface-level visual similarity over entity-level semantic alignment. This paradigm often fails when semantically identical concepts exhibit large visual variations or when distinct entities appear visually similar. To address this, we propose KBMR, the first MLLM-based embedding retriever tailored for KB-VQA. Leveraging the robust autoregressive capabilities of MLLMs, KBMR maps images into a semantic space that better preserves concept identity. To tackle the challenge of noisy supervision in Wikipedia-scale retrieval, we introduce an MLLM-based semantic discriminator that generates continuous entity-consistency weights. These weights guide a novel continuous semantic distillation objective, enabling effective hard negative sampling and soft supervision beyond rigid binary labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that KBMR significantly outperforms CLIP baselines, yielding up to a 14.7% improvement in retrieval Recall@1 and a 9.4% gain in end-to-end VQA accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/realHarryX/KBMR.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-Scale Fruit Capsules: Dilated Convolutions and Dynamic Routing for In-the-Wild Explainable Fruit Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21454)
*Subhankar Chattoraj,Sawon Pratiher,Samiran Das,Hubert Konik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FruitCapsNet，一种胶囊网络用于野外水果分类。其用膨胀卷积替换标准卷积前端，使感受野以恒定参数成本指数增长，多尺度上下文在动态路由前被编码，超参数由贝叶斯优化选择。在四个数据集（含新构建的19类PD-19）上超越十个微调迁移学习骨干网络，仅用其三分之一的深度，在最难数据集上以+2.7%最大优势胜出。Grad-CAM分析证明改进源于整体水果区域归因而非边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外水果分类中同一水果以多种呈现形式出现（成串、未采摘、去皮、袋装、切片等），类内差异大而类间差异小。标准卷积网络通过池化丢弃姿态和位置信息，难以泛化到这些多样呈现形式，需要一种能保留空间关系并编码多尺度上下文的架构。

Method: 提出FruitCapsNet，用膨胀卷积替换胶囊网络的标准卷积前端，使感受野以恒定参数成本指数增长，每个胶囊在动态路由解决部分-整体空间一致性之前编码多尺度上下文。超参数（含膨胀因子）通过贝叶斯优化而非网格搜索选择。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（SMP、FruitsGB、Fruits-360）和新的19类10,639图PD-19数据集上，FruitCapsNet以三分之一的深度超过十个微调迁移骨干网络，在最难数据集上以+2.7%最大差距胜出。Grad-CAM从DigitCaps层的显著图显示改进源于整体水果区域归因而非边缘。

Conclusion: 胶囊网络结合膨胀卷积在野外水果分类中优于传统迁移学习骨干，且通过可解释性分析证据表明性能提升不是数据集伪影。该方法为处理类内变异大的分类任务提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: The same fruit appears in a bunch, unpicked, peeled, bagged in plastic, or sliced on a dish, so automated fruit classification in the wild (AFCW) must absorb wide intra- class and narrow inter-class variability in shape, size, colour and texture. Convolutional networks route information through pooling, which discards the pose and location of the region of interest and therefore generalises poorly across these presentations. We propose FruitCapsNet, a capsule network whose Fruit Capsules replace the standard convolutional front end with dilated convolutions: the receptive field grows exponentially at constant parameter cost, so each capsule encodes multi-scale context before dynamic routing resolves part whole spatial agreement. Hyper-parameters, including the dilation factor, are selected by Bayesian optimisation rather than grid search. On three public datasets (SMP, FruitsGB, Fruits-360) and a new 19-class, 10,639-image in-the-wild dataset (PD-19), FruitCapsNet exceeds ten fine-tuned transfer-learning backbones at one-third the depth, with the largest margin (+2.7% over the nearest competitor) on the hardest set. Grad-CAM saliency propagated from the DigitCaps layer shows that the improvement comes from attributing decisions to whole-fruit regions rather than to object edges, giving post-hoc evidence that the gain is not a dataset artefact.

</details>


### [15] [Tomatoes, Potatoes, and Onions: Questioning the Need for Faces in Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21455)
*Guray Ozgur,Fadi Boutros,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TPO——一个由西红柿、土豆、洋葱等非人脸物体构成的免人脸演示攻击检测（PAD）数据集。基于基础模型的PAD架构，仅用TPO训练即可在四个跨数据集人脸PAD基准上达到92.70%的平均AUC，胜过合成人脸训练，并与真实人脸数据集训练的模型相当。将TPO并入常规人脸PAD训练可稳定提升跨数据集性能。表示和频域分析表明，可迁移的PAD表示编码的是呈现过程中的共性线索而非物体语义或单一频谱伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸PAD被建模为面像特定问题，但实际上打印、重放、重拍引入的视觉伪影并不依赖面部外观。作者希望验证是否存在与面部内容无关的可迁移PAD表示，从而为隐私保护和身份无关的PAD开发开辟新路径。

Method: 1) 构建TPO数据集：以接近随机选取的番茄、土豆、洋葱为拍摄对象，采集bona fide、打印和重放三种类型，协议紧密镜像常规人脸PAD数据集。2) 采用基于基础模型的PAD架构，分别用TPO、合成人脸、真实人脸数据集训练并做跨数据集评测。3) 进行双向迁移实验（人脸PAD数据集→TPO）及将TPO并入人脸PAD训练的组合实验。4) 进行表示分析和频域分析以探究可迁移表示的本质。

Result: TPO训练的检测器在四个标准跨数据集人脸PAD基准上平均AUC达92.70%，优于合成人脸训练，与真实人脸训练相当。人脸PAD模型迁移到TPO显著高于随机水平。将TPO并入人脸PAD训练在固定优化预算下持续提升跨数据集性能。频率分析表明可迁移表示并非单一频谱伪影所能解释。

Conclusion: 可迁移的演示攻击检测表示可以独立于面部内容学习，证明当前PAD方法捕捉的主要是呈现过程本身的视觉特征而非目标物体语义，为隐私保护型PAD开发提供了新方向。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) is traditionally formulated as a face-specific problem, although many of the visual artifacts introduced by print, replay, and recapture processes are not inherently tied to facial appearance. In this work, we investigate whether transferable PAD representations can be learned without using faces during downstream PAD training. To this end, we introduce TPO, a controlled face-free presentation attack dataset consisting of bona fide, print, and replay recordings of, almost randomly chosen, tomatoes, potatoes, and onions acquired under protocols that closely mirror conventional face PAD datasets. Using a foundation-model-based PAD architecture, we demonstrate that a detector trained on TPO achieves an average AUC of 92.70% across four standard cross-dataset face PAD benchmarks, outperforming training on synthetic faces and remaining competitive with models trained on real face datasets. Conversely, models trained on face PAD datasets transfer consistently above chance to TPO, suggesting that the learned representations capture characteristics of the presentation process rather than object semantics. Furthermore, incorporating TPO into conventional face PAD training consistently improves cross-dataset performance under fixed optimization budgets, indicating that face-free data provides complementary information rather than simply additional training samples. Finally, representation and frequency analyses provide further evidence that transferable PAD representations cannot be explained by a single spectral artifact but instead encode richer presentation cues shared across object categories. Together, these results provide empirical evidence that transferable presentation attack representations can be learned independently of facial content, opening new opportunities for privacy-preserving and identity-independent PAD development.

</details>


### [16] [CLSC DETR: Reliable Candidate Ranking via Cross Layer Geometric Support for UAV Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21457)
*Junyan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLSC DETR 提出跨层局部支持与分类-定位一致性校准模块，提升DETR在无人机小目标检测中的候选排序可靠性，在VisDrone和UAVDT上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机小目标因尺度小、分布密集、经常遮挡，导致单查询缺乏足够的几何证据，现有DETR方法通过单查询估计定位质量不够可靠，候选排序不稳定。

Method: 1) Cross Layer Local Support模块：建立最终层查询与中间层候选的对应关系，聚合互补几何证据以更可靠地估计定位质量；2) Classification and Localization Consistency Calibration模块：根据定位质量和分类可靠性自适应调整分类分数以改进候选排序。

Result: 在VisDrone上相比基线AP提升1.5%、AP75提升2.0%，在UAVDT上取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过跨层几何证据聚合和分类-定位一致性校准，有效提升小目标检测的候选排序稳定性和检测精度。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) object detection is critical for applications such as target search, where accurate detection of small objects in complex aerial scenes remains challenging. The limited spatial extent, dense distribution, and frequent occlusion of small objects make reliable candidate ranking particularly difficult. Existing Detection Transformer (DETR) based methods improve ranking by estimating localization quality from individual queries and incorporating it into classification scores. However, a single query often lacks sufficient geometric evidence for small objects with weak boundary cues, resulting in unreliable quality estimation and unstable ranking. To address this limitation, we propose Cross Layer Local Support and Consistency Calibration for DETR, termed CLSC DETR. Specifically, the Cross Layer Local Support module establishes correspondences between final layer queries and intermediate layer candidates to aggregate complementary geometric evidence for more reliable localization quality estimation, while the Classification and Localization Consistency Calibration module adaptively adjusts classification scores according to localization quality and classification reliability to improve candidate ranking. Experiments show that CLSC DETR improves AP and AP$_{75}$ over the baseline by 1.5\% and 2.0\% on VisDrone, respectively, while achieving consistent improvements on UAVDT.

</details>


### [17] [FigmaTrace: Capturing Creative Nuances in Human Figma Design Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21460)
*Darshan Deshpande,Yoshinari Fujinuma,Martyna Markiewicz,Devanshu Bansal,Shivani Jain,Nicholas Saban,Chirag Maheshwari,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FigmaTrace数据集：基于专家定义的126个开放式长时程设计任务，利用新颖的“设计阶段”方法将200+小时的人类视频数据转化为3469条设计轨迹，训练VLM模型后性能媲美Claude-Opus-5和GPT-5.6-Sol，并在4个分布外GUI环境中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在客观可验证领域（如目标检测）表现提升，但在主观创意设计任务中仍欠佳，主要原因是缺乏捕捉人类专家多样化偏好与决策过程的高质量工作流数据。

Method: 1) 定义专家策展的设计技能分类体系；2) 扩展为126个开放式长时程主观任务；3) 采集200+小时人类视频数据；4) 提出基于设计阶段（design phase）的视频到轨迹转换方法（优于传统基于长度分割的方法）；5) 在FigmaTrace上训练4个VLM模型。

Result: 训练模型在4个分布外agentic GUI环境中达到与Claude-Opus-5和GPT-5.6-Sol相当的性能；消融实验证明基于设计阶段的转换方法优于长度基方法；对Qwen3.8-27B最佳模型做了定性分析以关联性能提升与数据集特征。

Conclusion: FigmaTrace数据集及基于设计阶段的轨迹转换方法可有效提升VLM在主观创意设计任务中的表现，数据集与最佳模型已开源。

Abstract: Vision Language Models have recently shown improvements in several objective and verifiable domains such as object detection but continue to underperform on subjective and creative design tasks. A major contributor to this performance gap is the lack of high quality human workflow data that captures a diverse set of preferences and decisions that make human experts good at design tasks. In this work, we first define a unique, expert curated taxonomy of design skills and best practices which we further expand into a set of 126 open ended, subjective, long horizon tasks. Built on top of this and expert solutions, our dataset FigmaTrace contains over 200 hours of human captured video data converted into 3469 design trajectories using a novel design phase-based method. We use our dataset to train four models and show that training on FigmaTrace leads to a performance improvement comparable to frontier closed models such as \textsc{Claude-Opus-5} and \textsc{GPT-5.6-Sol} on four out of distribution agentic GUI environments. We further perform a useful ablation to attribute these performance improvements to a design phase-based video to trajectory conversion which outperforms prior length-based conversion approaches. Finally, we perform a qualitative analysis on the best performing \textsc{Qwen3.8-27B} outputs to better correlate performance improvements to FigmaTrace's trends. We open source our dataset and the best model for the community.

</details>


### [18] [Complexity Induction: Compositional Generalization via Structured Label Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21464)
*Aleksandr Abramov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文证明通过结构化扭曲训练数据（称为“复杂度诱导”），无需修改CNN架构即可诱导组合泛化能力。使用合成几何形状图像，类标签为扁平字符串（如“red-circle”），训练时排除某些颜色-形状组合。基于Jaccard字符串相似度应用两种扭曲方法：混合标签（软目标分布）和扩展数据集（结构化的错误标注样本），两者均能诱导对未见组合的预测能力，且在表征层与嵌入因式分解层不同机制生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索组合泛化（compositional generalization）能否在不修改网络架构的前提下，通过训练数据的结构设计来实现。作者从认知发展角度出发，类比自然语言在认知发展中的作用，试图揭示训练信号的结构性复杂度如何影响网络内部表征组织与组合解释。

Method: 使用合成彩色几何形状图像数据集，类别定义为扁平字符串标签（无显式属性分解），训练时排除部分颜色-形状组合。基于类名间Jaccard字符串相似度设计两种数据扭曲：(1) 混合标签——将类间重叠编码为软目标分布；(2) 扩展数据集——添加结构上相关但标签错误的训练样本。设随机错误标签对照组验证结构的作用。

Result: 两种方法均能诱导对未见颜色-形状组合的预测能力。混合标签通过利用CNN对未组合的嵌入结构激活分类器；扩展数据集则改善嵌入因式分解本身。随机（非结构化）错误标签对照实验确认效果取决于扭曲的结构而非噪声本身。

Conclusion: 结构化训练信号的复杂性可双向影响学习表征的内部组织及其组合解释能力。该原则可能与自然语言在认知发展中的角色存在深层联系，为理解语言与概念组成的关系提供计算层面的证据。

Abstract: We demonstrate that structured distortion of training data - which we term complexity induction - can induce compositional generalization in a standard CNN classifier without architectural modification. Using synthetic images of colored geometric shapes, we encode classes as flat string labels (e.g., "red-circle") with no explicit attribute decomposition, and exclude certain color-shape combinations from training entirely. We apply two distortion methods derived from Jaccard string similarity between class names: mixed labels (soft target distributions encoding inter-class overlap) and expanded dataset (false training samples with structurally motivated incorrect labels). Both methods induce the ability to predict unseen class combinations, and act at different levels: mixed labels activate the classifier for unseen combinations by exploiting the CNN's natural embedding structure, while expanded training improves the embedding factorization itself. A control with random (unstructured) false labels confirms that the effect depends on the structure of the distortion, not on noise per se. These results suggest that structured complication of training signals can influence both the internal organization of learned representations and their compositional interpretation - a principle that may underlie the role of natural language in cognitive development.

</details>


### [19] [3D Point Cloud from Close-Range Photogrammetry for Defect Characterisation of Rubberised Concrete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21468)
*Jiacheng Liu,Mohammed Alnahhal,Ailar Hajimohammadi,Sara Gonizzi Barsanti,Jinling Wang,Mohsen Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将近景摄影测量（SfM+MVS）工作流用于橡胶混凝土（RuC）试件，使用Canon DSLR和iPhone 16生成高分辨率3D模型，开发了RGB引导的裂缝提取方法，并进行前后变形分析，证明了该方法可作为LiDAR的灵活高分辨率替代方案用于实验室表面检测与变形监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LiDAR系统（如TLS）受激光光斑尺寸限制，无法检测宽度小于光斑的微裂缝，不适用于细尺度缺陷分析；而近景摄影测量在高度曲折材料上的细尺度应用尚未充分探索，因此需要验证其在可持续复合材料上的可行性。

Method: 采用SfM和MVS算法对橡胶混凝土试件进行近景摄影测量重建；使用Canon DSLR和iPhone 16采集高分辨率图像集生成致密3D模型；开发RGB引导的裂缝提取方法以增强表面缺陷识别并隔离裂缝区域；进行加载前后（Pre and Post-Test）的变形位移量化分析。

Result: DSLR重建实现了亚毫米级分辨率，优于iPhone 16；RGB引导的裂缝提取方法能够清晰分离裂缝区域，提供几何结构良好的缺陷形态表征；前后变形分析成功量化了测试阶段的表面位移，验证了该工作流作为LiDAR替代方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 近景摄影测量工作流是实验室环境下表面检测和变形监测的高分辨率、灵活替代方案，为未来自动化3D特征表征和材料性能评估建立了稳健的几何基线。

Abstract: While three-dimensional (3D) point clouds are widely used in civil engineering, mainstream LiDAR systems such as Terrestrial Laser Scanning (TLS) are physically constrained to laboratory environments. Since their laser spot size typically exceeds the width of microcracks, the beam physically bridges over voids, rendering TLS unsuitable for fine-scale defect analysis. Alternatively, close-range photogrammetry utilising Structure-from-Motion (SfM) and Multi-View Stereo (MVS) algorithms offers a solution for testing highly tortuous materials, and its utility at fine-scale remains underexplored. This study adapts photogrammetric workflows specifically for rubberised concrete (RuC), a sustainable composite exhibiting high ductility and complex fracture morphologies. High-resolution image sets were captured using a Canon DSLR and an iPhone 16 to generate dense 3D models. Comparisons revealed that the DSLR-based reconstruction achieved sub-millimetre resolution, demonstrating superior performance for fine-scale surface monitoring. An RGB-guided crack extraction method was developed to enhance the identification of surface defects and isolate potential crack areas from the background. The extracted crack regions were visually distinguishable and provided a well-structured geometrical representation of defect morphology. Furthermore, a Pre and Post-Test deformation analysis was conducted to quantify surface displacement across testing stages. The results confirm that this close-range photogrammetry workflow is a flexible, high-resolution alternative to LiDAR for surface inspection and deformation monitoring of specimens in laboratory settings. Ultimately, this approach establishes a robust geometric baseline for future automated 3D feature characterisation and material performance evaluation.

</details>


### [20] [EXPL-FR: Explaining Face Recognition Models via Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21486)
*Guray Ozgur,Mustafa Efe Tamyapar,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EXPL-FR是一个面向深度人脸识别模型的可解释性框架，通过轻量适配器将视觉-语言模型(VLM)与冻结的人脸识别嵌入空间对齐，无需文本训练即可将属性提示词转化为FR空间锚点，提供身份级、逐图像和差分解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度人脸识别模型准确率接近饱和但完全不透明，从业者无法询问相似度分数依赖哪些语义属性。现有可解释方法通常需要架构访问或标签监督，缺乏无需标签即可审计模型语义依据的方案。

Method: 利用VLM图像编码器与人脸识别空间的共享嵌入特性，训练轻量适配器将图像映射到FR空间；同一适配器复用于文本编码器，将978个属性提示词(22类)转化为FR空间锚点。通过人脸验证协议和仅改变适配器的消融验证迁移有效性，并提出无标签可检测性度量筛选最具区分性的语义概念。

Result: 覆盖4种FR骨干和2种VLM编码器，无需架构访问。在三种监督设置(人工标签、VLM伪标签、纯提示词驱动)下对比真实验证行为；纯提示词审计可在无标签情况下按实测种族RFW误差排序4个FR模型，并按真实验证代价排序受控属性变化。

Conclusion: 并非所有概念都能在FR空间中存活，因为FR模型通过丢弃跨身份变化中不必要的因素换取了不变性。100个可检测性最高的概念构成模型的可读语义签名，其身份区分能力优于完整词汇表。

Abstract: Deep face recognition (FR) models reach near-saturated accuracy but remain opaque: a practitioner cannot ask which semantic attributes a similarity score relied upon. EXPL-FR answers this inside the FR model's own embedding space. A lightweight adapter aligns a vision-language model's (VLM) image encoder with the frozen FR space, trained on face images alone and never on text. Because the VLM's encoders share one space, the same adapter applies to the text encoder, turning 978 attribute prompts in 22 categories, also extendable, into FR-space anchors at no extra cost. We do not assume this transfer works: a face-verification protocol measures it, and an ablation changing only the adapter isolates its contribution. Not every concept survives, because an FR model earns its invariances by discarding the factors it must verify identities across. A label-free detectability measure compares each concept's separability in FR space against the VLM space, and the 100 most detectable form the model's readable semantic signature, which separates identities better than the full vocabulary. We cover four FR backbones and two VLM encoders, EXPL-FR needs no architecture access, and supports identity-level, per-image, and differential explanations. We benchmark attribute-level auditing under three supervision settings, human labels (current practice), VLM pseudo-labels, and our fully prompt-driven audit, against real verification behavior. With no labels, the prompt-driven audit ranks four FR models by their measured per-ethnicity RFW errors and ranks controlled attribute changes by their true verification cost.

</details>


### [21] [TASSO: TAsk-Specific Subspace Optimization for Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21487)
*Chang Sun,Francesco Barbato,Matteo Caligiuri,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASSO提出了一种新的持续学习范式，通过子空间学习和几何感知知识蒸馏两个互补技术，在CLIP视觉语言模型的持续适应中同时缓解灾难性遗忘和零样本退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLM)在持续学习场景下适应新任务时，同时面临灾难性遗忘和零样本能力退化两大问题，严重制约了其在多任务场景下的实用性。

Method: TASSO包含两个互补技术：(1) 子空间学习——学习一系列任务特定的低秩投影器，将潜在表示投影后再优化交叉熵损失；(2) 几何感知知识蒸馏——使用基于测地线距离的损失函数，从先前任务模型蒸馏知识，同时有效保持潜在空间几何结构。

Result: 在CLIP模型的多领域任务增量学习和类增量学习基准上，TASSO在缓解遗忘和保持零样本能力方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦任务特定流形和几何感知蒸馏，TASSO避免了全嵌入维度的不必要参数更新，既提高了学习效率，又提供了强正则化，有效解决持续学习中的遗忘和零样本退化问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot capabilities, making them an attractive solution for continual learning across diverse tasks. However, during continual adaptation, both catastrophic forgetting and zero-shot degradation occur, severely degrading performance. In this paper, we introduce TASSO, a new paradigm that efficiently preserves the latent space geometry while ensuring network plasticity. We achieve this with two complementary techniques: subspace learning and geometry-aware knowledge distillation. Specifically, we first learn a sequence of task-specific low-rank projectors, which we use to project the latent representations before optimizing cross-entropy. Secondly, we employ a geodesic-distance-based loss that distills knowledge from the previous-task model while effectively preserving the latent space geometry. These design choices not only avoid unnecessary parameter updates along the full embedding dimensions but also improve learning by focusing on task-specific manifolds. Moreover, the geometry-aware distillation provides strong regularization and significantly reduces both catastrophic forgetting and zero-shot degradation throughout the continual learning sequence. Experimental results with the CLIP vision language model in the multi-domain task incremental and class incremental learning benchmarks demonstrate clear improvements over state-of-the-art methods in mitigating forgetting and preserving zero-shot capabilities.

</details>


### [22] [DamageScope: Vision-Language Retrieval at Scale for Disaster Damage Assessment from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21529)
*Ravi K. Rajendran,Biplob Debnath,Murugan Sankaradas,Srimat T. Chakradhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DamageScope是一个基于检索增强生成框架的卫星图像灾害损失评估系统，结合VLM与LLM实现自然语言交互查询的自动化财产损坏分析，通过多向量嵌入聚类算法（索引速度提升14倍）和双存储数据架构（延迟与成本降低约3倍）实现大规模部署的可行性与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害后及时评估财产损失至关重要，传统人工现场检查耗时、昂贵且有安全风险。卫星图像与VLM虽有潜力实现规模化远程评估，但将VLM集成到大尺度地球观测管道中面临计算效率、数据组织和信息检索三大挑战。

Method: 提出DamageScope框架：基于RAG架构，从卫星图像提取结构化视觉表示支持自然语言查询；提出多向量嵌入聚类算法替代传统单向量方法，索引时间降低14倍；采用双存储数据架构减少LLM API调用，降低运营成本并将响应延迟降低约3倍。

Result: 多向量嵌入聚类算法在索引速度上比传统单向量方法快14倍；双存储架构将LLM API调用最小化，运营成本和响应延迟均降低约3倍。系统在规模化卫星图像灾害评估中展现了良好的可扩展性与实用性。

Conclusion: DamageScope通过平衡可扩展性与操作效率，为真实世界灾害评估任务提供了稳健且实际的解决方案，是实现卫星图像自动化远程损失评估的有效途径。

Abstract: Timely and accurate assessment of property damage is critical following natural disasters. Traditional on-site inspections are labor-intensive, costly, and often pose safety risks. Advances in satellite imagery and vision-language models (VLMs) enable scalable remote damage assessment; however, integrating VLMs into large-scale Earth observation pipelines presents challenges in computational efficiency, data organization, and information retrieval. To address these challenges, we present DamageScope, a retrieval-augmented framework that combines satellite imagery with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate property damage analysis. Built on a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework, DamageScope extracts structured visual representations from satellite imagery to support interactive natural language queries for damage assessment. To address scalability, we introduce a novel multi-vector embedding-based clustering algorithm that outperforms traditional single-vector embedding approaches while reducing indexing time by up to 14x. Furthermore, a dual-store data architecture minimizes LLM API calls, reducing both operational cost and response latency by up to approximately 3x. By effectively balancing scalability and operational efficiency, DamageScope provides a robust and practical solution for real-world damage assessment tasks.

</details>


### [23] [presto: Efficient, Training-free, and Open-world Object Placement via Imaginary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21543)
*Weixuan Ding,Shang Liu,Hanyu Pei,Zeyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出presto，一个零样本、免训练的开放世界物体放置框架，将物体放置重构为基于MLLM推理的启发式搜索任务，在想象动作空间中迭代优化物体位置和尺度，通过粗到细的搜索策略实现快速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体放置方法依赖手工规则或在有限数据集上监督学习，泛化能力和可解释性受限，尤其在涉及新物体和新场景的开放世界场景中表现不佳。

Method: 将开放世界物体放置建模为启发式搜索任务，由多模态大语言模型(MLLM)进行推理指导。在想象动作空间中零样本、免训练地迭代精调物体位置和尺度，采用粗到细的搜索策略加速收敛，并评估两种决策变体：度量引导选择(Metric-guided Selection)和MLLM作为评判者(MLLM-as-a-judge)。

Result: 在多个基准上达到state-of-the-art性能，尤其在未见过的开放世界场景中表现突出。人类研究表明MLLM-as-a-judge变体比度量驱动方法产生更符合感知的放置结果，揭示标准评估指标与人类视觉判断之间存在差距。

Conclusion: presto验证了MLLM推理驱动搜索在开放世界物体放置任务上的有效性，并指出了现有自动评估指标与人类感知之间的一致性差距，为该领域提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: Object placement is critical in image composition, requiring spatially and semantically coherent positioning of objects within diverse scenes. Existing approaches typically rely on hand-crafted rules or supervised learning on limited datasets, which restricts their generalization and interpretability, especially in open-world scenarios involving novel objects and scenes. In this work, we reformulate open-world object placement as a heuristic search task guided by reasoning from a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM). We introduce \textsf{presto}, a zero-shot, training-free framework that operates within an imaginary action space to iteratively refine object position and scale. Our coarse-to-fine search strategy ensures fast convergence, and we evaluate two decision-making variants: Metric-guided Selection and MLLM-as-a-judge. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that \textsf{presto}~achieves state-of-the-art performance, particularly in previously unseen, open-world settings. Human studies further reveal that the MLLM-as-a-judge variant produces more perceptually coherent placements than metric-driven approaches, highlighting a gap between standard evaluation metrics and human visual judgment.

</details>


### [24] [Extending the Horizon of Early Diagnosis: Lung Cancer Prediction with Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21571)
*Olivera Kotevska,Ian Goethert,Michael McGee,Maria Mahbub,Sean R. Wilkinson,Rowena Yip,Myvizhi Esai Selvan,Zeynep H. Gumus,Claudia Henschke,Robert J. Klein,Providencia Morales,Samuel M Aguayo,Ioana Danciu,Mayanka Chandrashekar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了Vision Transformers (ViTs)用于在临床诊断前1-2年预测肺癌的能力。使用波士顿VA医院25.9万张胸片和约9.1万项影像研究，通过混合欠采样/过采样和类别加权损失应对约1:150的极端类别不平衡。比较了三种ViT配置（从零训练、ImageNet预训练、Corona预训练微调），发现迁移学习使AUC提升6-10个百分点，均衡准确率提升10-12%。中等重采样比例在性能与计算效率间取得良好平衡，最高可减少70%运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因，早期诊断对提高生存率至关重要，但早期恶性肿瘤在胸片上往往很细微，给放射科医生带来挑战。作者旨在利用Vision Transformers从常规胸片中预测未来1-2年内的肺癌风险，探索其作为一种无创、可扩展的早期筛查工具的潜力。

Method: 使用来自Jamaica Plains VA医院的259,361张胸片（91,020项影像研究）构建数据集。通过混合欠采样和过采样（包括1:1欠采样和1.5:2过采样等比率）结合类别加权损失优化来处理约1:150的极端类别不平衡。评估了三种ViT配置：从零训练、ImageNet预训练、Corona预训练后微调，比较其AUC、均衡准确率、敏感度等指标。

Result: 预训练模型超过从零训练基线6-10个百分点的AUC和约10-12%的均衡准确率。ImageNet预训练模型整体表现最稳定，Corona预训练模型在某些设置下敏感度更高但变异性更大。中等重采样比率在敏感度、精确度和计算效率间取得有利权衡，运行时间最多减少70%而性能损失不大。

Conclusion: ViT在早期肺癌风险预测方面展现出潜力，但性能仍低于临床部署阈值。研究支持进一步开发基于ViT的分诊系统，用于标记高风险患者以便更早评估。中等重采样策略为实际应用提供了计算效率与性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Lung cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, and early diagnosis is critical for improving survival. However, early-stage malignancies can be subtle on chest X-rays, creating challenges for radiologists. This study evaluates Vision Transformers (ViTs) for predicting lung cancer one to two years before clinical diagnosis. We analyzed 259,361 chest X-rays from 91,020 imaging studies at the Jamaica Plains VA Hospital in Boston, MA. The dataset showed extreme class imbalance, approximately 1:150 cancer to non-cancer, which was addressed using hybrid under- and over-sampling and class-weighted loss optimization. Three ViT configurations were evaluated: a model trained from scratch, an ImageNet-pretrained model, and a Corona-pretrained model fine-tuned on the lung cancer dataset. Transfer learning improved performance, with pretrained models exceeding the scratch baseline by 6-10 percentage points in AUC and about 10-12 percent in balanced accuracy. ImageNet-pretrained models showed the most stable overall performance, while Corona-pretrained models achieved higher sensitivity in some settings but greater variability. Moderate resampling ratios, including 1:1 undersampling and 1.5:2 oversampling, provided favorable trade-offs between sensitivity, precision, and computational efficiency, reducing runtime by up to 70 percent without major performance loss. These findings demonstrate the potential of ViTs for early lung cancer risk prediction from routine chest X-rays. Although performance remains below clinical deployment thresholds, the results support further development of ViT-based triage systems to flag high-risk patients for earlier evaluation.

</details>


### [25] [Perturb the Thought, Not the Pixels: Latent-Space Rollout Diversification for Reinforcement Learning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21595)
*Michael Jerge,Joseph Pelczar,Justin Downes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出噪声对比GRPO（NC-GRPO），在提示编码的最后一层隐藏状态注入尺度校准的高斯噪声，为一半的rollout组创建分支起始状态，通过策略梯度将分支点敏感性转化为训练信号，在不改变目标函数、奖励和推理协议的情况下提升VLM的数学推理、域内准确率和幻觉鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能提升VLM的推理能力，而组内rollout多样化能进一步放大收益。现有方法依赖解码温度或像素空间图像扰动，作者质疑扰动是否应放在模型的潜在空间（latent space）而非像素空间。

Method: 在提示编码pass的最后一层隐藏层注入尺度校准的高斯噪声，使一半rollout组从偏移的出发状态分支。到达答案的分支被强化，被偏移干扰的则被抑制，将分支点处的敏感性转化为策略梯度信号。目标函数、奖励和推理协议保持不变，仅需对推理引擎进行约50行改动，模态无关。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B + Geometry3K训练下，NC-GRPO在五个held-out基准上显著提升OOD数学推理（pooled McNemar p≤0.001），同时提升域内准确率和幻觉鲁棒性。机制消融表明独立随机多样性（而非噪声预算或方向）是有效成分；噪声尺度研究显示推理特化与通用能力之间存在可调节的平衡点。

Conclusion: 潜在空间扰动是比像素空间扰动更优的rollout多样化手段，NC-GRPO以极低工程成本（约50行改动）集成进标准RLVR管线，且在幻觉鲁棒性上优于像素空间噪声（后者在感知密集型基准上取得更大OOD均值但存在不足）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning ability of vision-language models (VLMs), and diversifying the rollouts within each optimization group amplifies its gains. Existing approaches diversify through decoding temperature or pixel-space image distortion; we ask whether the perturbation belongs in the model's latent space instead. We introduce Noise-Contrastive GRPO (NC-GRPO), which injects scale-calibrated Gaussian noise into the last hidden layer of the prompt-encoding pass for half of each rollout group, branching those rollouts from a displaced departure state. Branches that reach the answer despite the displacement are reinforced over those derailed by it, converting sensitivity at the branch point into policy-gradient signal; the objective, reward, and inference protocol are untouched. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B trained on Geometry3K, NC-GRPO significantly improves out-of-domain mathematical reasoning over vanilla GRPO across five held-out benchmarks (pooled McNemar $p \le 0.001$) while also improving in-domain accuracy and hallucination robustness -- the latter an axis on which image-space noise regresses even while posting a larger OOD average on perception-heavy benchmarks. Mechanism ablations indicate that independent stochastic diversity, not noise budget or direction, is the active ingredient, and a noise-scale study exposes a dial between reasoning specialization and general capability. NC-GRPO is designed to be modality-agnostic and integrates into a standard RLVR pipeline as a ~50-line change to the inference engine.

</details>


### [26] [Semantic Slots for Video Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21636)
*Khalil Sabri,Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau,Nicolas Saunier,Wassim Bouachir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SemanticSlots，一种基于Transformer解码器的视频目标中心学习方法。核心观点是视频OCL的瓶颈在解码器而非编码器——传统解码器迫使slots空间锚定，阻碍运动适应。SemanticSlots让slots作为语义查询，具备目标位置不变性，可从单帧分解后续视频帧，无需时序预测器或辅助时序损失。在YouTube-VIS上mBO比VideoSAUR提升31点，优于SOTA方法21点，ARI达86.6%，mBO达62.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频目标中心学习（OCL）方法一直聚焦于改进编码器架构以保证时序一致性，但作者认为真正瓶颈在解码器。传统解码器迫使slots在空间上锚定，使得slots难以适应目标的运动变化，限制了视频分解的泛化能力。

Method: 提出SemanticSlots，采用基于Transformer的解码器，利用图像上下文信息，将slots从编码边界精度和空间位置的重担中解放出来。slots充当语义查询，天然具备目标位置不变性，通过检索匹配特征而非记忆坐标来实现目标分解。这一性质允许仅用单帧计算的slots即可分解后续视频帧，消除了对复杂时序预测器或辅助时序损失的需求。

Result: 在YouTube-VIS数据集上，SemanticSlots相比VideoSAUR在mBO上提升31个百分点，比当前SOTA方法高出21个百分点，达到86.6% ARI和62.8% mBO。

Conclusion: 证明了视频OCL中解码器设计才是关键瓶颈，语义化slots设计可以显著提升视频分解性能，并简化模型架构（无需复杂时序建模模块）。

Abstract: Video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) has traditionally focused on refining the encoder architecture to ensure temporal consistency. In this paper, we argue that the primary bottleneck lies in the decoder. We show that traditional decoders force slots to be spatially anchored, hindering their ability to adapt to motion. We propose SemanticSlots, which uses a Transformer-based decoder that leverages image context, relieving slots from encoding boundary precision and spatial location. This allows slots to function as semantic queries that are inherently object position invariant, retrieving matching features rather than memorizing coordinates. More importantly, this property allows slots computed from a single frame to decompose subsequent video frames, eliminating the need for complex temporal predictors or auxiliary temporal losses. Results on YouTube-VIS show that SemanticSlots improves upon VideoSAUR by 31 points in mBO and outperforms current state-of-the-art methods by 21 points, achieving 86.6% ARI and 62.8% mBO.

</details>


### [27] [SketchFlow: Zero-Shot Vector Sketch Generation via GMM Prior Flow in CLIP Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21659)
*Jin Zhou,Hongliang Yang,Pengfei Xu,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchFlow是一个基于最优传输(OT)理论和流匹配(flow matching)的矢量草图生成框架。它利用预训练CLIP模型在潜在空间中将文本概念与草图特征对齐，通过向离散类别嵌入注入噪声构建连续GMM先验，用OT-CFM学习从先验到目标草图特征的确定性向量场，最终由融合1D U-Net和Transformer的混合扩散解码器生成高质量笔画轨迹。在视觉质量和人类绘画风格遵循度上大幅超越现有基线，并支持零样本生成和概念间连续语义插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量草图是简洁直观的抽象人类表达媒介，但高质量文本到草图配对数据严重稀缺，现有文本条件生成方法要么依赖不稳定耗时的优化，要么难以零样本泛化到未见类别。核心挑战在于弥合离散文本概念与连续草图特征之间的模态差距。

Method: 1) 利用预训练CLIP模型绕过图像级文本标注，将跨模态对齐建模为CLIP潜在空间中的连续映射问题；2) 向离散类别嵌入注入噪声，构建连续高斯混合模型(GMM)先验以桥接模态差距；3) 采用最优传输条件流匹配(OT-CFM)模型学习从GMM先验到目标草图特征分布的确定性向量场；4) 设计融合1D U-Net与Transformer架构的混合扩散解码器，将特征解码为快速高保真笔画轨迹。

Result: 实验表明SketchFlow在视觉质量和人类自然绘画风格遵循度上显著优于现有基线。几何保持框架支持对超出QuickDraw训练词汇表提示的局部零样本合成（包括未见概念标签和语义修饰符），并支持不同概念间的平滑连续语义插值。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SketchFlow通过OT-CFM与混合扩散解码器的结合，有效解决了草图生成中文本-草图配对数据稀缺和模态差距问题，实现了高质量、风格化、可零样本泛化并支持语义插值的矢量草图生成，为该领域提供了一种稳定且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Vector sketches remain one of the most concise and immediate mediums for abstract human expression. However, generating high-quality vector strokes that exhibit human-like drawing styles remains an open challenge due to the severe scarcity of fine-grained, high-quality text-to-sketch paired data. Existing text-conditioned generation methods often rely on unstable, time-consuming optimization or struggle to generalize to unseen categories in a zero-shot manner. To address these limitations, we present SketchFlow, a novel generative framework rooted in Optimal Transport (OT) theory and flow matching. By leveraging pre-trained CLIP models to bypass labor-intensive image-level text annotations, we formulate cross-modal alignment as a continuous mapping problem directly within the CLIP latent space. To bridge the inevitable modality gap between discrete text concepts and continuous sketch features, we first inject noise into discrete category embeddings to construct a continuous Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) prior. We then utilize an Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching (OT-CFM) model to learn a deterministic vector field mapping from this continuous GMM prior to the target sketch feature distribution. Finally, a Hybrid Diffusion Decoder, fusing 1D U-Net and Transformer architectures, is designed to decode these features into fast and high-fidelity stroke trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SketchFlow substantially outperforms existing baselines in visual quality and adherence to natural human drawing styles. Furthermore, our geometry-preserving framework demonstrates promising local zero-shot synthesis for prompts beyond the QuickDraw training vocabulary, including unseen concept labels and semantic modifiers, while enabling smooth, continuous semantic interpolation between distinct concepts. Source code is available at: https://github.com/QiuHong-1202/SketchFlow.

</details>


### [28] [Emotion Intensity Matters: Generating Realistic Expressions in Virtual Humans with CVAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21697)
*Vitor Miguel Xavier Peres,Lara Volpato,Gabriel Ferri Scnheider,Soraia Raupp Musse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）的方法，在仅有7,680个真实人类面部表情样本的小数据集上训练模型，合成可控情感强度（低/高两级、六种基本情绪）的面部表情数据，用于虚拟角色动画制作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在情感计算和角色动画领域，为虚拟人生成具有表现力的面部表情仍具挑战性。传统方法需要演员表演或人工艺术干预，成本高且难以扩展。作者希望利用生成模型从少量真实数据中学习，实现无需人工干预的可控情感表情合成。

Method: 使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）架构，以六种基本情绪及两个强度级别（低/高）为条件标签，在7,680个面部表情样本上进行训练，学习潜在表征并生成保持语义一致性的合成表情数据。

Result: 即使训练数据量有限（仅7,680个样本），CVAE仍能学习有意义的潜在表征，生成连贯的情绪变化，并保持不同强度级别下的关键表达特征，支持跨强度泛化。

Conclusion: CVAE方法能够从小规模数据集生成情感丰富的虚拟角色面部表情，无需演员表演或手动艺术干预，可控制情绪强度，为虚拟角色动画提供了低成本、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating expressive facial behavior in virtual humans (VHs) remains a central challenge in affective computing and character animation. This paper presents a novel approach based on Conditional Variational Autoencoders (CVAEs), trained on real human facial expression data, to synthesize controllable emotional expressions at varying intensities. Using a dataset comprising six basic emotions represented at two intensity levels (low and high), we train a CVAE model to generate synthetic facial expression data while preserving semantic consistency with real human expressions. Despite the limited amount of training data (only 7,680 facial expression samples), the proposed approach learns meaningful latent representations and generates coherent emotional variations. Our method enables control over emotional intensity, making it suitable for animating virtual characters without requiring actor performances or manual artistic intervention. Our research aimed to evaluate whether the method (CVAE) preserves the characteristics associated with the different intensity levels present in the dataset. Results show that the proposed model preserves key expressive characteristics across intensity levels while supporting generalization across emotional intensity levels, contributing to the creation of emotionally expressive virtual characters from relatively small datasets.

</details>


### [29] [StereoDiffuer: Diffusion-based Progressive Geometry Modeling with Saliency Attention Perception for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21710)
*Bohan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StereoDiffuer，一种基于迭代扩散过程的立体匹配框架，结合显著性注意力感知模块提取几何线索（物体边界、细薄结构、尖锐边缘），通过置信度引导的条件扩散去噪过程逐步精化视差图，在Scene Flow和KITTI基准上取得竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立体匹配方法在挑战性区域难以保持细粒度几何细节，导致模糊边缘和过平滑预测，需要显式建模几何细节的解决方案。

Method: 提出扩散式立体匹配框架StereoDiffuer：(1) Saliency Attention Perception (SAP)模块提取显著性几何线索；(2) 置信度引导的SAP特征与初始视差估计结合，作为迭代去噪扩散过程的条件；(3) 扩散过程修正残差视差误差并恢复成本体积正则化和上采样过程中被抑制的几何细节。

Result: 在Scene Flow和KITTI基准上验证了框架的有效性，性能与对比的立体匹配方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 扩散生成模型结合显著性几何感知能够有效恢复立体匹配中丢失的细粒度几何细节，提升边界和薄结构区域的视差质量。

Abstract: With the advance of deep neural networks, the quality of disparity maps obtained through stereo matching has steadily improved. However, existing stereo matching methods still struggle to preserve fine-grained geometric details, resulting in blurred edges and over-smoothed predictions in challenging regions. To address these limitations, we propose StereoDiffuer, an iterative diffusion-based stereo matching framework that explicitly models geometric details and progressively refines disparity estimates. The framework incorporates a Saliency Attention Perception (SAP) module to extract salient geometric cues, including object boundaries, thin structures, and sharp edges. Confidence-guided SAP features are combined with the initial disparity estimate to condition an iterative denoising diffusion process, which corrects residual disparity errors and restores geometric details suppressed during cost-volume regularization and upsampling. Experimental results on the Scene Flow and KITTI benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and its competitive performance relative to the compared stereo matching methods.

</details>


### [30] [The Plan, Not the Decoder: Diagnosing and Repairing Compositional Failure in Reasoning-Augmented Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21713)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究推理增强文本到图像模型（如GoT-R1）中"规划器-解码器"的可分离性：通过编辑模型输出的文本规划（物体名、属性、包围盒）来判定错误来源。实验证实解码器是忠实的执行器（94%布局正确），而规划器才是瓶颈——存在"左/右"不对称偏差（98% vs 54%）和光栅序偏差。通过符号验证、重采样、就地修复、重写几何或替换规划可在不重训练的情况下提升5.0-13.3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强的文本到图像模型会先输出可读的文本规划再生成图像，但失败时无法判断是规划本身错误还是解码器不忠实执行。由于规划可编辑，两者可分离，这为诊断和修复提供了新的切入点。

Method: 1) 通过交换规划中两个包围盒验证"规划-执行"可分离性，用检测器和VQA指标对比并辅以人工标注；2) 采用几何评分（而非VQA）评估空间关系；3) 通过提及顺序控制实验隔离光栅序偏差；4) 提出多种无须重训练的规划修复策略：符号验证+重采样、就地修复、重写盒几何、整体替换规划。

Result: 解码器忠实执行94%的规划关系；规划器是瓶颈，存在"left"准确率98%对"right"54%的方向不对称和光栅序偏差。规划修复无需重训练即可提升：+5.0（符号验证重采样）、+6.0（就地修复）、+10.7（重写几何）、+13.3（整体替换）。增益对规划文本风格和似然不敏感，但对几何敏感。盒-文矛盾会引发物体重复和身份融合。

Conclusion: 模块化"规划器-解码器"设计可行，前提是规划内部一致。发布了规划保真度评估协议、全部规划和12k生成图像，为该方向提供了系统性评估基准。

Abstract: Reasoning-augmented text-to-image models such as GoT-R1 emit an explicit textual plan - object names, attributes, and bounding boxes - before generating image tokens. When such a model fails a compositional prompt, is the plan wrong, or is the plan right and the decoder unfaithful? Because the plan is machine-readable it can be edited before decoding, which makes the two separable. We first validate the ruler. Swapping the two bounding boxes inside the model's own chain demonstrably flips the generated layout: detector-based accuracy falls 0.75 -> 0.48 (p<1e-3), while a widely used VQA-based spatial metric rises. A five-rater human study agrees with the detector on 81% of items and with the VQA judge on 57%. All spatial results therefore use geometric scoring. Under sound measurement the decoder is a faithful executor: 94% of generated layouts realize the planned relation, and object-box binding survives reordering of the plan's object segments. The planner is the bottleneck. It writes wrong relations for phrasing-dependent reasons - 98% accuracy on "left" against 54% on "right" for semantically identical layouts, a raster-order bias we isolate with a mention-order control - and cluttered geometry that the decoder faithfully reproduces. Editing the plan therefore fixes the image without retraining: symbolic verification with resampling gives +5.0 points (p<1e-3), minimal in-place repair +6.0 (p=.02), rewriting only box geometry +10.7 (p<1e-4), and replacing the plan outright +13.3 (p=1e-4). Gains are indifferent to the plan's prose style and to its likelihood under the planner, but not to its geometry. Modular planner-decoder designs are therefore viable, provided the plan is internally consistent: box-text contradictions induce object duplication and identity fusion. We release the plan-fidelity evaluation protocol, all plans, and 12k generated images.

</details>


### [31] [Calibrate What You SHIP: Post-Selection Risk Control for Verifier-Guided Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21748)
*Xuanhua Yin,Shunqi Mao,Wei Guo,Chuanzhi Xu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIP（Selection-aware Held-out calibration of Inference Policies），解决文本到图像生成系统中测试时搜索带来的候选级风险校准与发布输出级风险控制之间的失配问题。SHIP在留出提示上完整运行或重放部署策略，用独立目标评判器评估实际发布的图像，并在规定预算下选择最宽松的满足风险上界的阈值。在GenEval2+FLUX上，将发布风险从0.310降至0.162，并通过同时置信控制提供有限样本有效性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像系统在测试时使用搜索/选择/拒绝策略，但发布阈值通常在单张图像上校准。搜索改变了哪些提示获得输出以及哪个候选被发布，导致候选级风险控制不必然意味着发布输出风险受控。这种"候选到策略的校准失配"需要形式化并解决。

Method: SHIP通过两个组件解决此问题：(1) 形式化估计量偏移——通过提示重加权和提示内选择来描述搜索策略对输出分布的改变；(2) 策略级校准——在留出提示上完整运行或重放部署策略，用独立目标评判器评估实际发布的图像，选择最宽松的满足风险上界预算的阈值。对于可重放策略和预设阈值网格，利用同时置信控制实现有限样本有效性保证。

Result: 在GenEval2上使用FLUX（N=16）时，池化候选阈值产生发布风险0.310，而SHIP降至0.162。在200个缓存流划分上，固定网格证书无目标越界。固定、顺序和自适应T2I推理流程的实验表明，策略级校准恢复了更低风险的工作点，同时暴露了风险、覆盖率和计算之间的策略相关权衡。

Conclusion: 可靠的推理时扩展需要对完整部署策略诱导的输出分布进行校准，而非仅对单个候选进行风险控制。策略级校准是必要的，能够实现性能与风险之间的更优权衡。

Abstract: Verifier-guided text-to-image systems increasingly use test-time search to select, refine, or stop among multiple candidates, yet release thresholds are often calibrated on individual images. This creates a candidate-to-policy calibration mismatch: search changes both which prompts receive an output and which candidate is released, so candidate-level risk control need not imply control of released-output risk. We formalize this estimand shift through prompt reweighting and within-prompt selection, and introduce SHIP, Selection-aware Held-out calibration of Inference Policies. SHIP runs or replays the complete deployed policy on held-out prompts, evaluates the image it actually releases using an independent target judge, and selects the most permissive threshold whose risk upper bound satisfies a prescribed budget. For replayable policies with a prespecified threshold grid, simultaneous confidence control provides finite-sample validity. Experiments across fixed, sequential, and adaptive T2I inference procedures show that policy-level calibration recovers lower-risk operating points while exposing policy-dependent tradeoffs among risk, coverage, and compute. On GenEval2 with FLUX at N=16, a pooled-candidate threshold yields released risk 0.310, whereas SHIP reduces it to 0.162. Across 200 cached-stream splits, the fixed-grid certificate has no target crossing. Reliable inference-time scaling therefore requires calibrating the output distribution induced by the complete deployed policy.

</details>


### [32] [Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency (FDC): Data Pruning for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21754)
*Dongyao Zhu,Ranga Raju Vatsavai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次将数据剪枝(DP)方法系统性地应用于遥感变化检测任务，基准测试六种代表性DP方法发现现有方法相比随机选择无可靠优势。通过540个随机子集的回归分析，发现"变化分布保真度"是决定子集质量的最关键因素，并据此提出FDC两阶段剪枝方法，在变化检测基准和不同骨干网络上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据剪枝在分类和分割任务中已被证明能减少训练数据规模并提升模型性能，但其在遥感变化检测领域的潜力尚未被探索。作者希望系统性地评估现有DP方法在该领域的适用性，并揭示决定变化检测数据子集质量的关键因素。

Method: 1) 基准测试六种代表性DP方法，覆盖建筑和森林变化数据集、CNN和Transformer模型、三种剪枝预算；2) 对540个随机抽样子集进行系统性回归研究，用四个描述符（标签统计、图像多样性、特征空间几何）刻画子集，并用随机森林模型识别影响子集质量的主导因素；3) 基于发现提出FDC（Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency）两阶段剪枝方法。

Result: 现有DP基线在变化检测中无可靠优势，最强的Feature Diversity被约33%的随机子集匹配或超越；变化分布保真度是最重要的子集质量决定因素，像素级图像多样性和标签-特征一致性为次要因素；提出的FDC方法在变化检测基准和骨干网络上一致优于现有基线，尤其在低剪枝比例下。

Conclusion: 现有数据剪枝方法忽视了变化检测任务特有的"变化分布保真度"属性，导致其失效。FDC方法通过优先保证变化分布的保真度并辅以多样性和一致性考量，实现了更优的数据子集选择，为变化检测领域的数据高效训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: Despite the success of data pruning (DP) in reducing training data sizes and improving downstream model performance in classification and segmentation tasks, its potential in remote sensing change detection remains unexplored. For the first time, we benchmark six representative DP methods across building- and forest-change datasets, CNN- and transformer-based models, and three pruning budgets, and show that existing baselines yield no reliable advantage over random selection. Notably, even the strongest evaluated baseline, Feature Diversity, is matched or exceeded by $\sim$33\% of randomly sampled subsets. To understand the underlying mechanism, we conduct a systematic regression study over 540 randomly sampled data subsets, characterizing each with four descriptors covering label statistics, image diversity, and feature-space geometry. Random Forest models show that \emph{change distribution fidelity} is the most prominent factor in determining the quality of change detection data subsets, a property absent from the existing pruning literature. Our analyses further show that pixel-wise image diversity and label-feature consistency are secondary factors. We translate these findings into Fidelity-Diversity-Consistency (FDC), a simple two-stage pruning method that shows consistent improvements over existing baselines across change detection benchmarks and backbones, especially at lower pruning ratios. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/ddydyd32/fidelity-diversity-consistency}{https://github.com/ddydyd32/fidelity-diversity-consistency}.

</details>


### [33] [Learning to Look Again: Loss-Gap Supervision for Free-form Crop Routing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21762)
*Jinchang Zhu,Rong Fu,Yi Ding,Chenghao Wu,Ying Liu,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GapSight 提出一种视觉"再阅读"框架：VLM先全局浏览，再选择性回到自由形式区域寻找局部证据。通过目标模型自身的失败信号（全局视图与裁剪增强视图的损失/选项边际对比）生成训练标签，并用轻量级自由形式裁剪路由器将标签蒸馏为一次性推理策略，预测是否需要复查、期望收益和连续裁剪框。在LLaVA-1.5-7B、InternVL2.5-8B和Qwen2-VL-2B上提升六个基准的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在细节密集问题上失败的根本原因是答案在图像中可见，但在图像被压缩为低分辨率全局视图后丢失。简单地给每个查询分配更多视觉token会无差别消耗计算资源，且可能干扰依赖全局上下文的任务。需要一种选择性、自适应的视觉再阅读机制。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：比较全局-only视图与候选裁剪增强视图下的答案损失或多选选项边际，将能改善目标答案的裁剪框作为模型特定的回顾标签。2) 用轻量级自由形式裁剪路由器从全局状态蒸馏这些标签，得到一次性推理策略，预测是否回顾、期望效用及连续裁剪框。3) 推理阶段：VLM先全局浏览，路由器决定是否以及在哪里放大查看。

Result: 在三个VLM（LLaVA-1.5-7B、InternVL2.5-8B、Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct）上，GapSight在OCR、文档、图表、信息图、VStarBench和MME-RealWorld-Lite六个基准上均超过Base无缩放基线。在InternVL2.5-8B上，六基准平均从52.25提升至64.29，超过CropVLM(57.16)、ViCrop(55.84)和ZoomRefine(54.43)。机制分析表明路由器能挽救具体错误答案、按任务自适应调整动作率，并形成有利的token-性能权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 损失差监督（loss-gap supervision）是教会VLM"何时何地再去看"的实用途径。GapSight证明了利用模型自身失败信号进行选择性视觉回顾的有效性，为VLM的细粒度理解提供了一种计算高效的范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) fail many detail-centric questions for a concrete reason: the answer is visible in the image, yet lost after the image is compressed into a low-resolution global view. Allocating more visual tokens to every query improves some OCR and document cases, but it spends computation indiscriminately and can disturb tasks that rely on global context. We propose GapSight, a framework for learning visual re-reading: a VLM first takes a global glance, then selectively returns to a free-form region when the question calls for local evidence. The supervision comes from the target model's own failure signal. Offline, we compare answer loss or multiple-choice option margin under a global-only view and candidate crop-augmented views; crops that improve the target answer become model-specific review labels. A lightweight free-form crop router distills these labels into a one-shot inference policy that predicts whether to review, expected utility, and a continuous crop box from the global state. Across LLaVA-1.5-7B, InternVL2.5-8B, and Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct, GapSight improves the Base no-zoom baseline on six benchmarks spanning OCR, documents, charts, infographics, VStarBench, and MME-RealWorld-Lite. On InternVL2.5-8B, GapSight raises the six-benchmark average from 52.25 to 64.29, above CropVLM (57.16), ViCrop (55.84), and ZoomRefine (54.43). Mechanism analyses show that the router rescues concrete wrong answers, adapts its action rate by task, and forms a favorable token-performance profile. These results position loss-gap supervision as a practical route to teaching VLMs when and where to look again.

</details>
