### [34] [LiteEvent-AE: Lightweight Autoencoder for Event-Based Vision on Low-Latency Energy-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21764)
*Riadul Islam,Joey Mule,Dhandeep Challagundla,Shahmir Rizvi,Sean Carson,Rachit Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个紧凑可配置的事件驱动自编码器，用于神经形态数据的有效压缩，同时保留时空结构用于下游推理。在SEFD和EBCD数据集上达到接近或优于YOLOv9的精度，参数减少最多35.6倍，并在Jetson Nano上实现44.8 FPS实时处理，在树莓派4B上能耗降低约726.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视觉为低功耗AI提供稀疏低延迟信号，但异步和噪声特性使传统深度学习模型在低功耗嵌入式平台上过于计算密集，缺乏适合事件流的高效压缩与推理框架。

Method: 设计轻量卷积编码的紧凑事件驱动自编码器，集成自适应事件阈值化机制和最小分类头，在保持事件时空结构的同时显著降低计算成本，并部署到树莓派4B和NVIDIA Jetson Nano等资源受限硬件上进行能耗验证。

Result: 在SEFD和EBCD数据集上达到与YOLOv9竞争或更优的精度，参数减少至35.6倍；Jetson Nano上44.8 FPS实时吞吐，树莓派4B CPU上50%自编码器推理功耗16.19 J，能耗约为YOLOv9的1/726.3。

Conclusion: 紧凑事件驱动模型可显著降低计算和能耗开销，为自主、移动和嵌入式环境中的高速感知提供环境友好的低功耗AI方案。

Abstract: Event-based vision has emerged as a promising paradigm for energy-aware artificial intelligence (AI), offering sparse, low-latency visual signals that reduce redundant data processing and support sustainable edge computing. However, the asynchronous and noise-prone nature of event streams creates challenges for conventional deep learning models, which are often too computationally intensive for low-power embedded platforms. This work presents a compact and configurable event-driven autoencoder that efficiently compresses neuromorphic data while preserving essential spatiotemporal structure for downstream inference. The architecture integrates lightweight convolutional encoding with robust performance under adaptive event thresholding and a minimal classifier head, enabling substantial reductions in computational cost without degrading recognition fidelity. Extensive evaluations on the Smart Event Face Dataset (SEFD) and Event-Based Crossing Dataset (EBCD) show that the proposed framework achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to YOLOv9 while requiring up to 35.6$\times$ fewer parameters. To assess real-world sustainability, the model is deployed on resource-constrained hardware: a Raspberry Pi 4B and a NVIDIA Jetson Nano. On NVIDIA Jetson Nano, it delivers real-time throughput of 44.8 FPS. On a Raspberry Pi 4B CPU, the 50\% autoencoder classifier consumes 16.19 J for the evaluated inference workload, corresponding to approximately 726.3$\times$ lower energy consumption than YOLOv9 under the same evaluation protocol. These results demonstrate the potential of compact event-driven models to advance environmentally conscious, low-power AI systems for high-speed perception in autonomous, mobile, and embedded computing environments.

</details>


### [35] [SpatialDiff: 3D-Aware Object Movement via Implicit Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21776)
*Zheng Liu,Zijian He,Huiguo He,Weizhi Zhong,Yejun Tang,Huan Yang,Kun Gai,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialDiff是一种图像编辑方法，通过隐式3D空间建模和全局空间监督，让模型在复杂场景（多层深度、部分遮挡）中实现精确且一致的物体空间移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法在处理复杂场景中的空间移动（物体跨越不同深度层或被部分遮挡）时效果不佳，因为它们主要依赖2D数据集的前置信息，即使引入显式位置信息也无法捕获真实的3D空间关系。

Method: 两核心创新：(1) 隐式3D空间建模——引入3D先验知识，让模型内部构建三维空间结构的综合理解；(2) 全局空间监督——约束潜在空间特征，使模型感知编辑操作引起的物体空间位置变化。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在复杂场景中显著提升了空间移动的准确性和保真度。

Conclusion: 通过引入隐式3D空间建模和全局空间监督，SpatialDiff能够有效捕获复杂场景的3D空间结构，实现更精确的物体空间移动编辑。

Abstract: Recent advances in image editing allow impressive manipulation of objects, existing methods still struggle to handle spatial movement in complex scenes, such as objects span different depth layers or are partially occluded. Most image editing methods focus solely on prior information from 2D datasets, emphasizing planar features while lacking support for spatial structures. Even approaches that incorporate explicit positional information fail to capture true 3D spatial relationships, thus limiting accurate object movement in complex scenes. In this paper, we present SpatialDiff, a method that effectively captures 3D spatial structures, enabling precise and consistent object movements in complex scenes. Our core innovations are twofold: (1) Implicit 3D Spatial Modeling, which introduces 3D prior knowledge and enables the model to internally build a comprehensive understanding of the three-dimensional spatial structure; and (2) Global Spatial Supervision, which constrains the latent spatial features to enable the model to perceive changes in object spatial positions caused by editing operations. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves the accuracy and fidelity of spatial movement in complex scenes.

</details>


### [36] [DefaultShift: Auditing Semantic Default Shift in Accelerated Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21784)
*Xuanhua Yin,Chuanzhi Xu,Shunqi Mao,Wei Guo,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DefaultShift框架，用于审计加速文本到图像模型在替换原始模型时是否保持了"语义默认值"（semantic defaults）分布。通过配对审计、封闭语义词汇标注、概率质量移动测量，在14组参考-替换模型对上检测到可测的分布偏移，并提出DefaultShift-Select离线校准方法，在不显著损失质量的情况下降低10.3%-35.1%的语义偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速文本到图像生成模型正在取代较慢的生成器，但加速过程可能静默地改变未指定属性的分布，即使单个输出依然合理且对齐。现有质量、偏好和多样性评估都无法检验替换模型是否保留了参考模型的语义默认值分布，需要一个可量化和可操作的审计方法。

Method: DefaultShift采用配对审计方法：用封闭语义词汇表对重复采样输出进行标注，测量概率质量移动，并将可解释的排序与确认性交叉拟合推断分离。DefaultShift-Select是离线校准方法，在保持质量的同时选择数据以最小化人类测量的语义偏移。

Result: 在14组参考-替换模型对上，调整后颜色差异范围0.054-0.303且具有配方特定方向。1000张图像的人类审计复现了排序。DefaultShift-Select将Turbo、DMD2和FLUX的人类测量偏移降低10.3%-35.1%而不显著损失质量。在均衡评估下，选定数据比未校准替代数据恢复4.3准确率点和7.5最差组点。

Conclusion: DefaultShift使加速模型下的语义保留变得可测量和可操作，提供了一种新的评估维度来保证快速生成模型在替换缓慢模型时不会静默改变语义分布。

Abstract: Few-step text-to-image models increasingly replace slower generators, yet acceleration can silently change distributions over unspecified attributes even when individual outputs remain plausible and aligned. We call these distributions semantic defaults and their change under replacement semantic default shift. Existing quality, preference, and diversity evaluations do not test whether a replacement preserves its reference model's semantic defaults. We introduce DefaultShift, a paired audit that labels repeated samples with closed semantic vocabularies, measures probability-mass movement, and separates interpretable ranking from confirmatory cross-fit inference. Across 14 reference and replacement pairs, adjusted color discrepancies range from 0.054 to 0.303 with recipe-specific directions. A 1,000-image human audit reproduces the ordering. We further introduce DefaultShift-Select, an offline calibration method that reduces human-measured shift by 10.3 percent to 35.1 percent across Turbo, DMD2, and FLUX without material quality loss. Under balanced evaluation, selected data recover 4.3 accuracy points and 7.5 worst-group points over uncalibrated replacement data. DefaultShift makes semantic preservation under acceleration measurable and actionable.

</details>


### [37] [HP-UniIF: Hierarchical Prompt Learning for Unified Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21786)
*Xingxin Xu,Siqi Zhao,Xin Li,Xinjie Yao,Yiming Sun,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HP-UniIF是一个统一的视觉框架，利用扩散先验将异构图像融合、退化复原和下游感知任务整合到一个系统中，通过深度层次条件调制策略解耦不同层级的优化目标，在多个融合任务和退化场景中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的图像融合应用通常需要同时支持异构融合、退化复原和任务导向感知，但现有统一框架难以同时处理这些正交目标，导致表征纠缠和子任务性能下降。

Method: 提出HP-UniIF框架，引入深度层次条件调制策略：在瓶颈层使用任务提示调制（Task Prompt Modulation）适应不同融合范式；在浅层使用退化提示路由器（Degradation Prompt Router）注入退化感知约束用于局部复原；在解码阶段使用应用提示库（Application Prompt Bank）将生成结果与下游任务对齐。

Result: 在多种融合任务、多样化退化和各种下游应用中均展现出优越性能，验证了统一框架同时处理异构融合、复原和感知的有效性。

Conclusion: HP-UniIF通过层次化解耦的扩散先验条件调制，实现了异构融合、视觉复原和下游感知的统一，在产生视觉忠实结果的同时保留任务相关语义。

Abstract: General image fusion seeks to integrate complementary information from multiple source images, yet real-world applications often require a single system to support heterogeneous fusion, degradation restoration, and task-oriented perception simultaneously. Existing unified frameworks struggle with these orthogonal objectives, resulting in entangled representations and degraded performance across subtasks. We propose HP-UniIF, a unified vision framework that leverages diffusion priors to bridge heterogeneous fusion, visual restoration, and downstream perception. To address the limited adaptability of diffusion models to domain-, degradation-, and task-level objectives within one pipeline, HP-UniIF introduces a depth-wise hierarchical conditional modulation strategy that decouples these objectives across network stages. Task prompt modulation at bottleneck layers adapts the backbone to different fusion paradigms, the degradation prompt router at shallow layers injects degradation-aware constraints for local restoration, and the application prompt bank at decoding stages aligns generation with downstream tasks. This hierarchical design enables HP-UniIF to produce visually faithful results while preserving task-relevant semantics. Extensive experiments across multiple fusion tasks, diverse degradations, and various downstream applications demonstrate the superior performance of HP-UniIF.

</details>


### [38] [SAFE-G: Structure-aware Faithful Evidence-guided Generation for Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21796)
*Long Shu,Shuochen Liu,Wei Chen,Junda Lin,Zhi Zheng,Huijun Hou,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAFE-G框架，通过结构感知细粒度图检索和强化学习证据对齐策略，解决KB-VQA中证据定位不精确和推理不忠实的问题，在Encyclopedic-VQA和InfoSeek上分别提升8.9%和3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA方法难以捕捉复杂上下文中的结构关联以有效过滤噪声，且无法保证推理过程严格忠实于检索到的证据。

Method: 1) 粗粒度混合搜索（融合视觉和文本模态）召回候选文档；2) 结构感知细粒度图检索捕捉结构依赖以过滤噪声并精确定位证据；3) 引入带证据接地奖励的强化学习策略，仅当所选证据正确时才将奖励归于正确答案，强制模型锚定检索上下文进行忠实推理。

Result: 在Encyclopedic-VQA基准上超过先前方法8.9%，在InfoSeek基准上超过3.5%，显著提升整体推理准确率。

Conclusion: 结构感知检索与证据接地强化学习的结合能有效提升KB-VQA的证据定位精度和推理忠实度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) aims to answer queries that necessitate reasoning over external knowledge sources beyond the visual content. Typically, current methods fuse multimodal features to retrieve external information, subsequently leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to derive answers from the retrieved evidence. However, these methods often struggle to capture structural associations within complex contexts to effectively filter noise. Furthermore, they frequently fail to ensure that the reasoning process remains strictly faithful to the retrieved evidence. To address these challenges, we propose SAFE-G, a Structure-Aware Faithful Evidence-guided Generation framework, which enables precise evidence localization and trustworthy reasoning. Specifically, we first employ a coarse-grained hybrid search fusing visual and textual modalities to recall candidate documents, and subsequently implement a structure-aware fine-grained graph retrieval that captures structural dependencies to filter noise and pinpoint precise evidence. Moreover, we introduce a reinforcement learning (RL) strategy with an evidence-grounded reward that assigns credit to correct answers only when the selected evidence is correct. This strict alignment constraint compels the model to anchor its response in the retrieved context, effectively enhancing its capability to locate evidence via multimodal features and perform faithful reasoning. Extensive experiments on the Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek benchmarks demonstrate that SAFE-G outperforms prior methods by a margin of 8.9% and 3.5%, substantially enhancing the overall reasoning accuracy. Our source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MINE-USTC/SAFE-G.

</details>


### [39] [FlashReg: GPU-Accelerated 3-Clique Point Cloud Registration for Real-Time Correspondence-to-Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21804)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashReg提出一种面向GPU的GPU点云配准对应到位姿估计器，通过快速一阶和二阶图构建及数据流优化的三点团搜索，避免了稠密打分二值图的构建，在嵌入式GPU上相比TurboReg将对应到位姿延迟降低2-3倍，峰值显存占用约为其50%，配准召回率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的点云配准通过识别几何一致对应集实现高鲁棒性，但构建二阶兼容图及枚举候选团在计算和内存上开销巨大，尤其在片上或嵌入式GPU环境下难以满足高吞吐感知管线的需求。

Method: FlashReg包含两个核心组件：(1) Fast First- and Second-Order Graph (FFSOG)构建，直接从二值一阶图构建容量受限的稀疏二阶图，避免物化稠密打分二阶图；(2) 数据流优化的三点团(3-clique)搜索，从紧凑的逐行候选池中选择枢轴节点，并通过排序稀疏邻域交集枚举三元组。

Result: 在室内和室外基准上，FlashReg相比TurboReg将对应到位姿延迟降低2-3倍，配准召回率相当，峰值分配的张量内存约为TurboReg的50%（嵌入式GPU上）。

Conclusion: FlashReg可作为一种高吞吐配准后端，适用于自主感知管线（特别是嵌入式/板载GPU场景），在保持鲁棒性的同时显著提升计算效率和内存效率。

Abstract: Graph-based point cloud registration achieves high robustness by identifying geometrically consistent correspondence sets, but constructing second-order compatibility graphs and enumerating candidate cliques remain compute- and memory-intensive. This work presents FlashReg, a GPU-oriented correspondence-to-pose estimator that avoids materializing the dense scored second-order graph. Its Fast First- and Second-Order Graph (FFSOG) construction builds a capacity-bounded sparse second-order graph directly from the binary first-order graph. A dataflow-optimized three-node clique (3-clique) search then selects pivots from compact per-row candidate pools and enumerates triples through sorted sparse-neighborhood intersections. Across indoor and outdoor benchmarks, FlashReg reduces correspondence-to-pose latency by 2--3x relative to TurboReg at comparable registration recall, while using about 50% of its peak allocated tensor memory on an embedded GPU. These results make FlashReg suitable as a high-throughput registration backend within onboard perception pipelines.

</details>


### [40] [Through the Schrödinger Bridge: Benchmarking Antemortem Image Restoration from Postmortem Autolysis to Enhance Forensic Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21813)
*Shuang Hao,Jiacheng Yue,Yaxuan Zhao,Fan Wang,Jianhua Ma,Erwen Huang,Chunfeng Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出法医组织病理学自溶恢复（autolysis restoration）任务：在无配对监督下，将死后严重自溶的组织图像转化为具有诊断意义的"生前"表示。作者构建了AutoPath数据集（69例、近万张10x切片补丁），并将问题形式化为Schrödinger Bridge来建模随机的严重形态退化，同时指出现有图像级生成指标（如FID）与诊断效用的错配，提出基于切片的诊断分布一致性评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法医组织病理学受死后自溶（不可逆、随机的形态退化）严重干扰，导致诊断主观性增加。恢复自溶图像到诊断上合理的"生前"状态，对提高法医实践的客观性具有重要价值。由于自溶带来的大尺度非确定性形态变化以及像素级配对数据不可得，现有监督学习和cycle/structure一致的非配对翻译方法均不适用。

Method: 1) 新任务定义：无配对监督下的法医组织病理自溶恢复；2) 构建AutoPath数据集：同一标本分相邻组织块，一块立即处理、一块诱发自溶，获得69例不同肝脏状况的近万张10x补丁对（但非像素对齐，为同源无配对）；3) 将问题建模为自溶分布与非自溶分布之间的Schrödinger Bridge，以原理化方式建模随机、严重的形态退化；4) 提出法医学导向的slide-level诊断分布一致性评估，替代FID等泛化图像指标。

Result: 建立了可复现的基准（包含任务定义、真实数据集和评估方法论）。证明了通用图像级生成指标（如FID）与诊断实用性不匹配，并提出面向法医学的诊断分布一致性评估方案。具体恢复性能指标在摘要中未给出数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为法医病理学的自溶恢复提供了首个系统性的任务定义、数据集和评估框架，推动该领域向严格且有实际意义的方向发展。Schrödinger Bridge框架为建模随机严重形态退化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Forensic histopathology, essential for determining cause of death and disease diagnosis, is severely impeded by postmortem autolysis, i.e., an irreversible, stochastic degradation process that distorts tissue morphology and introduces diagnostic subjectivity, thereby underscoring the value of restoring autolyzed images to a diagnostically plausible, pre-autolysis state for improving objectivity in forensic practice. This restoration task is fundamentally challenging due to the large, non-deterministic morphological changes caused by autolysis and the infeasibility of pixel-wise paired data, which invalidates assumptions underlying supervised and cycle/structure-consistent unpaired translation methods. To address this, we formalize forensic histopathology autolysis restoration as a new task: under unpaired supervision, transform postmortem images with severe autolysis into diagnostically meaningful ``antemortem'' representations. We contribute AutoPath, the first homologous yet unpaired dataset for this problem, constructed by splitting specimens into adjacent tissue blocks---one processed immediately, the other exposed to induce autolysis---yielding nearly ten thousand $10\times$ patches from 69 cases with varying liver conditions. We further frame the problem as a Schrödinger Bridge between the autolyzed and non-autolyzed distributions, offering a principled approach to modeling stochastic, severe morphological degradation. Critically, we demonstrate the misalignment of generic image-level generative metrics (e.g., FID) with diagnostic utility and propose a forensically grounded, slide-level diagnostic distribution consistency evaluation. Overall, this work establishes a reproducible benchmark (encompassing task definition, a real-world dataset, and an evaluation methodology) toward rigorous and practically meaningful progress in autolysis restoration for forensic pathology.

</details>


### [41] [PatchGate: Narrowing the Verbalization Gap with Intrinsic Object Inventories in Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21819)
*Jihyung Ko,Eunji Jung,Hyeongsub Kim,Ziseok Lee,Jae Won Cho,Sanghyun Jo,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PatchGate是一个针对视觉语言模型(VLM)的训练-free框架，通过在第一阶段提取冻结VLM内部的patch级视觉证据构建图像条件对象集，在第二阶段用包含-排除解码(VIED)校准生成logits，同时提升可见对象覆盖率和降低对象幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free方法只抑制模型已倾向于提及的幻觉对象，而无法恢复模型遗漏的可见对象。论文旨在同时解决对象级可靠性的两个维度：避免幻觉对象提及，同时覆盖所有可见对象。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) Visual Evidence eXtraction (VEX) 从冻结VLM的LM解码器后半层的patch级词汇证据中，无任务提示地构建图像条件对象集；(2) Visual-Evidence Inclusion-Exclusion Decoding (VIED) 利用该对象证据校准解码logits，提升有证据支持但未被提及的对象，抑制证据弱但过度提及的对象。

Result: 在AMBER数据集上，可见对象覆盖率从49.4提升至56.0(+13.4%)，对象幻觉通过CHAIR从7.5降至6.6(-12.0%)。无需外部检测器或微调，仅增加一次额外前向传播。

Conclusion: PatchGate证明冻结VLM内部的patch级词汇证据可以同时提升图像描述的对象精度和完整性，通过训练-free方式缩小内在对象集与最终输出提及对象之间的差距。

Abstract: Reliable image captioning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires captions to be both precise and complete, avoiding unsupported object mentions while covering visible objects. Existing training-free methods primarily address the former requirement, suppressing unsupported object words by intervening on model-predicted mentions during generation. Because they operate only on objects the model is already likely to mention, visible objects omitted from the output remain difficult to recover. We propose PatchGate, a training-free framework that extracts prompt-free object evidence intrinsic to a frozen VLM before generation and uses it to narrow the gap between an intrinsic object set and final object mentions. In the first stage, Visual Evidence eXtraction (VEX) reads patch-level lexical evidence from the latter half of LM decoder layers and constructs an image-conditioned object set without any task prompt. In the second stage, Visual-Evidence Inclusion-Exclusion Decoding (VIED) uses this object evidence to calibrate decoding logits, promoting evidence-supported but under-verbalized objects and suppressing weakly supported but over-verbalized objects. On AMBER, PatchGate improves both sides of object-level reliability, increasing visible-object coverage from 49.4 to 56.0 (+13.4%) and reducing object hallucination by lowering CHAIR from 7.5 to 6.6 (-12.0%), without external detectors or fine-tuning and with one extra forward pass.

</details>


### [42] [Towards Alias-Free 4D Gaussian Representations with Motion-Aware Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21828)
*Ankit Dhiman,Kunal A Kathare,Pranav Vignesh,Lokesh R Boregowda,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向4D动态场景表示（如动态3DGS/NeRF）的运动感知三维平滑滤波器，通过非参数方法估计时间与焦距-深度比率的联合分布，在推理时采样以自适应调整平滑强度，有效缓解不同视点下（尤其缩放操作）的混叠伪影，且可与多种4D表示集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景的4D表示（时间作为第四维）在从发散视点生成新视角时（尤其是推近/拉远操作）仍存在严重的混叠伪影。现有的3D平滑滤波器（如Mip-Splatting）未能考虑局部运动，因此依然存在混叠问题。

Method: 提出运动感知的3D平滑滤波器：通过非参数估计方法估计时间t与焦距-深度比率focal-to-depth ratio的联合密度函数；推理时从该联合分布中采样，确定合适的平滑滤波器强度，使滤波器强度基于局部运动信息自适应调整。

Result: 在标准数据集上的评估表明，该方法相比最先进的方法取得了更优的性能，且可灵活集成到多种4D表示（如动态3DGS、动态NeRF）中。

Conclusion: 运动感知平滑滤波器能有效缓解4D动态场景表示的混叠伪影，且不牺牲渲染质量，是一种通用的可集成策略。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis of dynamic scenes, crucial for AR/VR applications, remains a challenging problem. Recent methods adapt representations like 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) for dynamic scenes by incorporating time as the fourth dimension (4D representations). These 4D representations still suffer from aliasing artifacts, especially when generating novel views from divergent viewpoints (zoom-in/zoom-out operations). While using 3D smoothing filters like those proposed in Mip-Splatting might seem like a possible solution, they fail to account for local motion and also exhibit aliasing. To address this, we propose a motion-aware 3D smoothing filter specifically designed for 4D representations. Our approach adapts the filter strength based on local motion information, effectively mitigating aliasing without compromising rendering quality. This is achieved by estimating the joint density function of time and focal-to-depth ratio using a non-parametric estimation method. During inference, we sample from this joint distribution to determine the appropriate smoothing filter. This flexible strategy can be integrated with various 4D representations. Our evaluations on standard datasets demonstrate superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [43] [Towards Bitstream-corrupted Harsh Visual Understanding: Through Bitstream Language Modeling as Robust Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21837)
*Chaoran Huang,Fangcheng Li,Tianyi Liu,Wenyang Liu,Kejun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BLMSP框架，通过比特流语言建模从损坏的比特流中学习原生语义先验，注入视频恢复、字幕生成和人体姿态估计等BcHVU任务，构建了607k损坏比特流片段的数据集CHP，实验显示PSNR提升2.51dB、CIDEr提升0.20、PCK@0.2提升0.18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界多媒体通信中，严重损坏的比特流经解码后会产生视频劣化，导致像素不可逆失真和语义丢失，现有视觉模型难以应对这种病态问题。

Method: 提出BLMSP框架：1) 构建Video Bitstream Byte Model (VBBM)，集成字节级建模与跨编解码器语义蒸馏，从多种损坏比特流格式的字节序列中解读鲁棒语义；2) 将学习到的比特流语义作为先验注入BcHVU模型骨干网络，用于视频恢复、字幕生成和人姿态估计；3) 构建大规模CHP数据集（607k损坏比特流片段+287k配对劣化视频）。

Result: 在视频恢复、字幕生成、人体姿态估计三个任务上分别平均提升PSNR 2.51dB、CIDEr 0.20、PCK@0.2 0.18，验证损坏比特流可作为鲁棒语义先验解决像素失真和语义丢失问题。

Conclusion: 实验证明损坏比特流中包含的语义信息可作为鲁棒先验，有效缓解BcHVU中的像素失真和语义损失问题。

Abstract: Bitstream-corrupted Harsh Visual Understanding (BcHVU) aims to understand harshly degraded videos originally decoded from a severely corrupted bitstream in real-world multimedia communication. The ill-posed nature of BcHVU poses a major challenge for existing vision models, as even subtle bitstream corruption can lead to irreversible pixel distortion and significant semantic loss. To address these challenges in BcHVU, we propose Bitstream Language Modeling as Robust Semantic Priors (BLMSP), a framework for learning and injecting bitstream-native semantic cues. Our proposed BLMSP framework learns to extract bitstream-native semantic cues by bitstream language modeling, and leverages them as priors by injecting into off-the-shelf vision models of BcHVU tasks. Specifically, we present a Video Bitstream Byte Model (VBBM) that integrates byte-level modeling and cross-codec semantic distillation, enabling it to interpret robust semantics from byte sequences in multiple corrupted bitstream formats. The learned bitstream semantics are leveraged as robust priors and fused into BcHVU model backbones for improving the quality of video restoration, captioning, and human pose estimation. To train BLMSP, we construct a large-scale multi-source Corrupted-bitstream Harsh-video Paired (CHP) dataset containing 607k corrupted bitstream segments and 287k paired harsh video clips. Extensive experimental results show that the learned bitstream priors improve video restoration, captioning, and human pose estimation by 2.51 dB in PSNR, 0.20 in CIDEr, and 0.18 in PCK@0.2 on average, respectively. These results demonstrate that corrupted bitstream can serve as robust semantic priors in solving pixel distortion and semantic loss in BcHVU.

</details>


### [44] [FIRM-Video: Check Before You Score for Reliable Text-to-Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21839)
*Peiyuan Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Hongbo Liu,Xiaoxing Hu,Mingxin Liu,Shuran Ma,Yunhang Shen,Jian Hu,Haihan Gao,Haoyu Cao,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FIRM-Video，一个基于check-before-score原则的统一清单驱动数据构建框架，用于文本到视频评估与对齐。该框架为指令遵循、世界连贯性和感知质量三个维度构建检查清单，验证每个标准并聚合已验证的决策，生成自然语言分析用于端到端奖励建模。构建了FIRM-Video-90K数据集（88,044个实例来自29,348个视频）和FIRM-Video-Bench基准（750个人工标注），基于Qwen3-VL的8B模型在MAE和VBench分数上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频的奖励模型在评估精度与推理效率之间存在权衡，现有方法依赖固定评价标准或开放推理的整体评判者，导致检查不完整、理由不可信、归因纠缠不清的问题。

Method: FIRM-Video采用check-before-score原则：(1) 对指令遵循维度，将提示分解为带权重的原子需求；(2) 对世界连贯性维度，构建基于可见实体和动作的、提示校准的、目标特定的检查；(3) 对感知质量维度，应用通用视觉缺陷分类。每个检查任务逐条验证时域视觉证据，仅聚合已验证的决策，并将验证结果转换为自然语言分析用于端到端奖励建模。

Result: 构建FIRM-Video-90K（88,044个维度特定实例，来自29,348个视频）和FIRM-Video-Bench（250个视频上的750个点级人工标注）。基于Qwen3-VL的FIRM-Video-8B在FIRM-Video-Bench上取得最优整体MAE，在三个视频生成器的Best-of-8采样中始终获得最高VBench Total、Quality和Semantic分数。

Conclusion: FIRM-Video通过清单驱动的检查-评分范式有效提升了文本到视频奖励模型的评估准确性和对齐质量，显示了细粒度、可验证的判断优于整体式评判的优势。

Abstract: Reliable reward models are essential for text-to-video evaluation and alignment. However, the trade-off between evaluation accuracy and inference efficiency places high demands on the quality of training supervision. Existing approaches often rely on holistic judges with fixed rubrics or open-ended reasoning, leading to incomplete inspection, unfaithful justification, and entangled attribution. We introduce FIRM-Video, a unified checklist-driven data construction framework based on a check-before-score principle: construct dimension-specific checklists, verify each criterion against temporal visual evidence, and aggregate only verified decisions. For Instruction Following, FIRM-Video decomposes prompts into weighted atomic requirements; for World Coherence, it constructs prompt-calibrated, target-specific checks grounded in visible entities and actions; and for Perceptual Quality, it applies a generic taxonomy of visual defects. The verified criteria and scores are further transformed into natural-language analyses for end-to-end reward modeling. Subsequently, we construct FIRM-Video-90K with 88,044 dimension-specific instances from 29,348 videos, and introduce FIRM-Video-Bench with 750 point-wise human annotations across 250 videos. The Qwen3-VL-based FIRM-Video-8B achieves the best overall MAE on FIRM-Video-Bench while consistently delivering the highest VBench Total, Quality, and Semantic Scores in Best-of-8 sampling across three video generators.

</details>


### [45] [BC-IHV: Conditioning the Color Space for Stable Rectified-Flow Low-Light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21847)
*Yi Ai,Zheng Chen,Yuanhao Cai,Yulun Zhang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SA-RF（结构锚定整流流）用于低光图像增强，通过分离色度/亮度通道、尺度匹配条件金字塔和HybridAda机制保持图像结构对应；同时引入BC-IHV可学习的Box-Cox极坐标颜色空间，用解析可逆的强度映射控制梯度动态范围。在三个LOL基准和跨数据集测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光图像增强需校正模糊曝光而不破坏输入已支持的结构信息。生成式传输虽能建模曝光歧义，但可能改变几何与色度内容；固定可逆颜色坐标仅作为表示，但其逆映射会重塑RGB域梯度。现有方法未充分解决这两方面问题。

Method: SA-RF包含三个关键设计：(1)分离的色度/亮度骨干网络维持对应关系；(2)尺度匹配条件金字塔；(3)HybridAda机制——空间交叉注意力负责位置特定检索，池化AdaLN负责全局曝光调制。另提出BC-IHV可学习Box-Cox极坐标颜色空间，通过单一指数参数以解析可逆的强度映射动态控制逆梯度范围，平衡暗区扩展与梯度条件化。

Result: 在三个LOL基准、盲图像质量评估和跨数据集测试中，SA-RF在重建质量和感知质量上均一致优于当前最先进方法。消融实验验证了所提框架和颜色表示各自的有效性。

Conclusion: SA-RF通过结构锚定的生成式整流流框架与可学习的Box-Cox颜色空间，有效解决了低光增强中曝光歧义与结构保持的矛盾，可学习的颜色表示相比固定线性/对数法则更具优势。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) must correct ambiguous exposure without overwriting structure already supported by the input. Generative transport can model exposure ambiguity; however, its flexibility may also alter observable geometry and chromatic content. Moreover, fixed invertible color coordinates are usually treated only as representations, although their inverse mappings reshape the RGB-domain gradients received by the enhancement network. To address these issues, we propose Structure-Anchored Rectified Flow (SA-RF), which maintains correspondence through separate chromaticity/intensity stems, a scale-matched condition pyramid, and HybridAda. HybridAda assigns location-specific retrieval to spatial cross-attention and global exposure modulation to pooled AdaLN. We further introduce BC-IHV, a learnable Box--Cox polar color space whose analytically invertible intensity mapping controls the inverse-gradient dynamic range through a single exponent. This allows the representation to balance dark-range expansion and gradient conditioning instead of adopting a fixed linear or logarithmic law. Experiments on three LOL benchmarks, blind image-quality evaluation, and cross-dataset tests demonstrate consistent reconstruction and perceptual advantages over the sota. Controlled studies further support the effectiveness of both the proposed framework and color representation.

</details>


### [46] [GaussVid: Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting with 3D-Aware Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21849)
*Xinhui Liu,Can Wang,Wei Jiang,Wei Wang,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个3D感知的视频修复框架,专门用于提升稀疏视角3DGS重建的质量。通过构建大规模3DGS视频数据集进行专用微调,并引入相机条件几何先验(以首尾帧为锚点编码相机关系),将空间结构显式注入视频生成流程,实现跨视角一致的几何约束修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在稀疏视角下重建存在明显伪影。现有视频扩散模型虽有强大的时空先验,但直接微调用于修复时缺乏多相机几何感知,导致多视角不一致。需要弥合2D视频生成与3D多视图约束之间的差距。

Method: 1) 构建大规模3DGS视频数据集用于专用微调;2) 引入相机条件几何先验,利用首帧和末帧作为边界锚点,编码对应的相机关系;3) 将空间结构显式注入视频生成管线,引导网络进行几何grounded的、跨视角一致的修复。

Result: 在视频先验修复方法中,该方法在像素级和结构级保真度(PSNR/SSIM)上达到最佳,改善多视角一致性,同时在感知质量(LPIPS)上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 边界锚定的相机感知先验能有效引导稀疏3DGS重建的几何一致性修复,优于直接微调视频扩散模型的方法。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has achieved remarkable success in novel view synthesis; however, reconstructions under sparse views often exhibit noticeable artifacts. While recent video diffusion models provide strong spatio-temporal priors for 3DGS restoration, directly fine-tuning them for restoration is suboptimal, as they lack awareness of the underlying multi-camera geometry, resulting in multi-view inconsistencies. In this work, we propose a novel 3D-aware video restoration framework designed to enhance the quality of sparse 3DGS reconstruction. Specifically, we construct a large-scale 3DGS video dataset to enable specialized fine-tuning. To bridge the gap between 2D video generation and 3D multi-view constraints, we introduce a camera-conditioned geometric prior. By using the first and last frames as boundary anchors and encoding the corresponding camera relationships, we explicitly inject spatial structure into the video generation pipeline. This boundary-anchored, camera-aware prior guides the network toward geometrically grounded restoration that remains coherent across viewpoints. Extensive experiments show that, among video-prior restoration methods, our approach attains the best pixel- and structure-level fidelity (PSNR/SSIM) and improves multi-view consistency, while remaining competitive in perceptual quality (LPIPS).

</details>


### [47] [Frame-Level Evaluation in Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection Mostly Measures Video-Level Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21854)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了弱监督视频异常检测的一个关键缺陷：常用的Micro-AUROC/AP评估指标因在视频间比较帧而掩盖了检测器缺乏帧级时间排序能力的问题。作者通过精确分解Within-AUROC和Cross-AUROC，证明仅0.071-0.388%的异常-正常帧比较发生在同一视频内，并提出了"temporal dilution"基准属性。实验表明视频级恒定输出（每帧同一分数）即可达到81.40-97.18的Micro-AUROC，且替换为视频均值可保留98.6%的AUROC裕度，说明检测器可以仅进行视频级判别而不做任何帧内时序定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频异常检测模型在视频级标签下训练，却被当作时间定位器用池化帧的Micro-AUROC评估。这种评估方式可能允许模型仅通过区分视频获得高分，而不真正实现帧级时序定位。作者旨在严格证明这一评估失效问题，并提出一个基准测试属性（temporal dilution）来解释为什么会发生。

Method: 将视频级语义分解为Within-AUROC（视频内时序排序）和Cross-AUROC（视频间比较）。推导在V个视频中两个类别均分布时，同视频内异常-正常帧比较比例随O(1/V)衰减的理论界限。设计实验：在视频级监督下训练二元分类器，将每视频分数重复到所有帧（视频恒定输出），并在72个受控实验中比较其与原始帧级输出的Micro-AUROC差距。验证了ShanghaiTech、XD-Violence、UCF-Crime三个基准及作者发布的官方输出。

Result: 三个基准中仅0.071-0.388%的异常-正常帧比较发生在同一视频内。视频恒定输出达到81.40-97.18 Micro-AUROC。72个受控实验中，将帧分数替换为视频均值保留中位数98.6%的Micro-AUROC裕度。同样的模式在作者发布的输出和XD-Violence官方AP评估下成立。

Conclusion: 现有Micro-AUROC/AP评估指标在弱监督视频异常检测中被视频级差异主导，无法有效衡量帧级时间定位能力。检测器只要能把视频分开就能获得高分数，无需进行帧内时刻排序。需要重新设计评估协议来正确衡量弱监督异常检测器的帧级定位能力。

Abstract: Weakly supervised video anomaly detectors are trained with video-level labels but are commonly evaluated as temporal localizers using Micro-AUROC or AP over pooled test frames. Because these metrics compare frames from different videos, a detector can score well by separating videos without accurately ordering moments within them. We exactly decompose Micro-AUROC by video identity into Within-AUROC for temporal ordering within videos and Cross-AUROC for comparisons across videos. Across ShanghaiTech, XD-Violence, and UCF-Crime, only 0.071-0.388% of comparisons between anomalous and normal frames occur within the same video. When both classes remain distributed across V videos, this share decreases as O(1/V), a benchmark property we call temporal dilution. We train anomaly video binary classifiers under the same video-level supervision and repeat each video score across all frames. These video-constant outputs reach 81.40-97.18 Micro-AUROC despite having no within-video variation. Across 72 controlled runs, replacing every frame score with its video mean preserves a median 98.6% of the Micro-AUROC margin above chance. The same empirical pattern holds for author-released outputs and for XD-Violence under its official AP evaluation. A detector can therefore achieve a high pooled score even when it assigns the same score to every moment within each video.

</details>


### [48] [GuardPaint:SpeculativeSafetyDecodingforText-to-ImageGeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21869)
*Shreyash Dhoot,Paras Dhiman,Arsh Abbas Naqvi,Aranbi Dutta,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GuardPaint 提出了一个投机解码框架，通过在扩散轨迹内部进行安全干预来保护文本到图像生成，而不修改基础模型。它使用轻量级auditor监测中间图像、定位不安全区域，并通过策略对齐的inpainter和受保护的锦标赛机制进行外科手术式修复，在五个越狱家族和多种模型（SD1.5/SDXL/SD3.5/FLUX.1-dev）上显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I安全防护主要在生成前（提示词过滤）或生成后（图像分类）起作用，扩散过程本身缺乏防护，且通常只拒绝生成而非进行安全视觉修复。对抗性提示可以操纵去噪轨迹向违规内容方向生成，需要一种在扩散内部进行干预的安全机制。

Method: GuardPaint作为投机解码框架：1) 轻量级auditor监测扩散轨迹中每一步的中间图像；2) 定位不安全区域；3) 仅在需要时触发外科手术式inpainting修复；4) 候选修复由策略对齐的inpainter生成；5) 通过受保护的锦标赛（guarded tournament）筛选编辑，仅在提高策略合规性同时保留提示保真度和感知质量时才接受。

Result: 在五个越狱家族（SneakPrompt、MMA、PGJ、DACA、RABell）以及UNet和flow-matching架构（SD1.5、SDXL、SD3.5、FLUX.1-dev）上，GuardPaint显著降低了攻击成功率和有害内容生成，同时最小化了对图像质量、提示保真度和良性行为的影响。

Conclusion: GuardPaint证明在扩散轨迹内部进行投机解码式的安全干预是可行且有效的，为T2I安全性提供了一种既不改基础模型又能进行视觉级安全修复的新范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models offer powerful visual generation, but their controllability creates a critical safety challenge: adversarial prompts can steer the denoising trajectory toward policy-violating content such as explicit nudity or graphic violence. Existing safeguards mostly act before generation through prompt filtering or after generation through image classification, leaving the diffusion process itself unguarded and often yielding only refusal rather than safe visual repair.
  We introduce GuardPaint, a speculative decoding framework for safe T2I generation that intervenes inside the diffusion trajectory without modifying the base model. A lightweight auditor monitors intermediate images, localizes unsafe regions, and triggers surgical inpainting repair only where needed. Candidate repairs are generated by a policy-aligned inpainter and selected through a guarded tournament that accepts edits only when they improve policy compliance while preserving prompt fidelity and perceptual quality.
  Across five jailbreak families SneakPrompt, MMA, PGJ, DACA, and RABell and UNet/flow-matching models including SD~1.5, SDXL, SD~3.5, and FLUX.1-dev. GuardPaint reduces attack success and harmful generations with minimal degradation to image quality, prompt fidelity, and benign behavior.
  Content warning: This paper contains examples involving nudity and violence that some readers may find disturbing, distressing, or offensive.

</details>


### [49] [ViSMoE: Visual-Aware Sparse Mixture-of-Experts for Embodied Referring Expression Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21878)
*Shuo Feng,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViSMoE框架，通过视觉感知的路由策略在稀疏混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）中分别处理视图和物体两种不同类型的视觉信息，从而获得更具判别性的视觉表征，提升具身指代表达定位（Embodied Referring Expression Grounding）性能，在REVERIE和SOON数据集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法使用普通视觉编码器统一处理视图和物体，无法区分这两种不同类型的视觉信息，导致视图和物体的表征模糊，影响具身智能体在导航和物体定位任务中的性能。

Method: ViSMoE框架包含稀疏混合专家模块和视觉感知路由策略。路由策略根据输入是视图还是物体信息，将不同视觉输入分配给对应的专家，实现类型特定的视觉信息处理，生成判别性更强的视图与物体表征。

Result: 在REVERIE和SOON两个标准数据集上，ViSMoE均超越了此前的最先进方法（SOTA），验证了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过视觉感知的稀疏MoE路由机制，分别处理视图和物体信息，能够有效提升具身指代表达定位的性能，为具身导航中多类型视觉信息的处理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Embodied Referring Expression Grounding is the task of enabling an agent to navigate in real environments and to localize a remote object based on natural language instructions. In this scenario, the agent needs to select one view for navigation at each step and identify a specific object among all candidate objects at the destination. However, most of the previous approaches fail to distinguish between views and objects, instead processing them using the vanilla vision encoder, which results in ambiguous representations of both views and objects. To address the above issues, we propose ViSMoE, which equips sparse Mixture-of-Experts with a visual-aware routing policy for the embodied agent. This framework processes different types of visual information specifically, resulting in discriminative visual representations for both views and objects. Experimental results on REVERIE and SOON datasets demonstrate that ViSMoE outperforms the previous state-of-the-art methods, showing the superiority of our proposed method.

</details>


### [50] [Region-Weighted Losses and Model Fusion for Cross-Modal PET Attenuation Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21881)
*Khoa Tuan Nguyen,Joris Vankerschaver,Wesley De Neve*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了作者在Big Cross-Modal Attenuation Correction (BIC-MAC)挑战赛中的方法：从NAC-PET、DIXON MRI和topogram合成Hounsfield单位的伪CT，并评估由此重建的AC-PET。核心贡献是三个关键观点：损失函数比网络架构更重要（在Carney衰减系数μ空间计算L1误差并按解剖区域加权）；只有当这个损失函数就位后，未配准的DIXON MRI才能作为额外输入通道生效；两个独立训练模型的固定凸组合在四个指标中的三个上优于其任一成员，并在公共验证排行榜上综合排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET成像需要衰减校正（AC），但传统基于CT的校正方法需要额外的CT扫描，增加了辐射剂量和扫描成本。该挑战旨在从非衰减校正PET（NAC-PET）、DIXON MRI和topogram中合成伪CT，从而无需单独CT扫描即可实现衰减校正，降低患者辐射暴露并简化临床流程。

Method: 方法包含三个关键设计：(1) 采用增强的L1损失函数，在Carney衰减系数（μ）空间而非线性衰减系数或CT值空间计算误差，并与CT评估指标本身一致，同时按解剖区域进行加权；(2) 在这一损失函数的基础上，将未配准的DIXON MRI作为额外的输入通道与NAC-PET和topogram共同输入；(3) 对两个独立训练的模型输出进行固定的凸组合融合，以提升整体性能。

Result: 该方法的三个核心观点各有贡献：μ空间加权L1损失显著优于组织者的3D U-Net基线；在正确损失函数就位后，DIXON MRI输入带来了额外增益；模型融合使组合输出在四项指标中的三项上优于两个独立成员模型。该方法在公开验证排行榜上综合排名第一。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在跨模态医学图像合成任务中，损失函数的设计（尤其是在度量本身的分布空间中进行优化）比网络架构的变更更为关键，并且多模型集成提供了一种简单有效的性能提升手段。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the Big Cross-Modal Attenuation Correction (BIC-MAC) challenge, which asks for a pseudo-CT in Hounsfield Units to be synthesized from Non-Attenuation-Corrected PET (NAC-PET), DIXON MRI and a topogram, and scores both the pseudo-CT and the Attenuation-Corrected PET (AC-PET) reconstructed from it. Three ideas carried our improvements over the organizers' 3D U-Net baseline. The loss matters more than the architecture: we compute the $L_1$ error in the Carney attenuation-coefficient ($μ$) space that the CT metric itself uses, weighted by anatomical region. Only once that loss was in place did the unregistered DIXON MRI work as extra input channels. A fixed convex combination of two independently trained models then beat both of its members on three of the four metrics and ranks first overall on the public validation leaderboard.

</details>


### [51] [VIG: Visual Information Gain as a Reward Signal for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21883)
*Wen Luo,Xiaohan Yi,Xiaotao Huang,Liqun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIG（视觉信息增益），一种信息论GRPO奖励，通过比较有无图像时策略的预测不确定性来评估每个推理token是否真正锚定于图像，从而在多模态大推理模型中实现精度-效率的优化，无需外部标注或辅助奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大型推理模型常依赖长CoT，其中大量token（如重复视觉描述、自我反思等）并未真正利用图像信息，却显著增加推理成本。现有CoT压缩方法只优化输出长度，无法判断推理token是否真正与图像内容相关，缺乏对视觉信息密度的度量。

Method: 提出VIG奖励：对策略进行两次前向传播（一次带图像、一次不带图像），计算每个推理token在有无图像条件下的预测不确定性差异，以此衡量该token的视觉信息增益；将该信息论奖励集成到GRPO框架中，在线计算，无需参考链、外部标注或辅助奖励模型。

Result: 在六个主要多模态推理基准和Qwen3-VL-Thinking三个规模（2B/4B/8B）上评估，并在8B上额外验证R1-Onevision-Bench，VIG一致性地改善了精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理源于提升视觉信息密度——每个推理token都应通过锚定到图像来证明其存在价值，而非单纯设置长度预算。核心假设得到实验支持：让每个token通过锚定图像"挣得"其位置，比强加长度约束更有效。

Abstract: Multimodal large reasoning models often rely on long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces in which a substantial fraction of tokens, such as repeated visual descriptions, self-reflection, and other visually-disengaged filler, inflate inference cost without contributing to the answer. Existing CoT compression methods optimize output length but never measure whether a reasoning token is actually grounded in the image. We propose \textbf{VIG} (Visual Information Gain), an information-theoretic GRPO reward that scores each reasoning token by how much the image reduces its predictive uncertainty. VIG is computed online from two forward passes of the same policy, one with and one without the image, so no reference chains, external annotations, or auxiliary reward models are needed. Across six main multimodal reasoning benchmarks and three Qwen3-VL-Thinking model sizes (2B/4B/8B), plus an additional R1-Onevision-Bench evaluation on 8B, VIG consistently improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off, supporting our central claim: \emph{efficient multimodal reasoning emerges from raising visual information density, where every reasoning token earns its place by anchoring to the image, rather than from imposing a length budget.} Our source code is available at https://github.com/chaser682/vig.

</details>


### [52] [Pixel-Space Diffusion via Observation Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21885)
*Shaojie Guo,Lichen Ma,Haoyang Tong,Yu He,Zipeng Guo,Xiaoan Liu,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang,Junshi Huang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Observation Operator Diffusion框架，用时间索引的观测轨迹（从粗结构到全图）替代固定的clean-image目标，结合Gaussian-Lanczos算子族实现路径一致训练目标，并引入GL-CoDA解码器进行粗到细特征细化，在ImageNet-256上达到FID 1.52。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素空间扩散模型在整个去噪过程中强制模型预测完整图像，但在高噪声下图像结构尚不可预测，导致低信噪比梯度和优化困难。作者通过实证发现粗到细的结构恢复顺序与固定目标之间存在尺度-时间不匹配。

Method: 用time-indexed观测轨迹替换标准流路径上的固定全图监督，轨迹由Gaussian-Lanczos算子族在不同观测尺度下实例化，构建路径一致训练目标；同时提出GL-CoDA解码器，在解码各阶段注入尺度特异的Gaussian-Lanczos观测，实现粗到细的特征细化。

Result: 比现有方法收敛显著更快，同时生成质量持续提升，在ImageNet-256上FID达到1.52。

Conclusion: 通过将监督轨迹和特征细化与图像结构的内在恢复顺序对齐，可以有效解决像素空间扩散模型的尺度-时间不匹配问题，显著改善训练效率和生成质量。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models directly model image distributions but remain difficult to optimize. Recent methods alleviate this challenge through target reparameterization, while still relying on a fixed clean-image target throughout denoising. Through empirical analysis, we identify a scale-time mismatch: image structures become predictable from coarse to fine as noise decreases, whereas existing models are forced to predict the full image even under high noise, resulting in low-SNR gradients that hinder optimization. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Observation Operator Diffusion, a unified framework that aligns both the supervision trajectory and feature refinement with the intrinsic recovery order of image structures. Specifically, we replace fixed full-image supervision along the standard flow path with a time-indexed observation trajectory that evolves from coarse structures to the full image during denoising. This trajectory is instantiated with a family of Gaussian-Lanczos operators at varying observation scales, yielding a path-consistent training objective. We further introduce GL-CoDA, a decoder that injects scale-specific Gaussian-Lanczos observations across decoding stages for coarse-to-fine feature refinement. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach converges substantially faster while consistently improving generation quality, achieving an FID of 1.52 on ImageNet-256.

</details>


### [53] [A Scalable Vector Graphics Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21893)
*Leonardo Zini,Elia Frigieri,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了SLS（SVG潜空间），一种基于Transformer的自编码器，为SVG路径（矢量图形的基本组成元素）学习紧凑、连续、稠密且可逆的潜在表征空间。通过统一BPE词表建模SVG命令、坐标和视觉属性，SLS学习固定大小的潜在向量，可解码回高保真、风格一致的SVG路径。嵌入位于单位超球面上，支持高效的相似性搜索、组合和下游条件生成，相比token方法FLOPs降低150倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在光栅图像领域已拥有VAE等连续、稠密、可逆的潜空间基础构建模块，但矢量图形（SVG）领域缺少类似的连续潜空间表示，阻碍了矢量图形上的生成模型、检索和下游任务的发展。

Method: 提出Transformer-based自编码器SLS：将SVG路径的指令、坐标和视觉属性统一编码为BPE词表token，通过编码器压缩为固定尺寸的向量表征，解码器将其还原为有效的SVG路径。嵌入被约束在单位超球面上以支持向量空间操作。

Result: SLS提供了稳健、可逆、结构化的SVG潜空间，支持高效相似性搜索和组合操作；在多种下游任务中相比基于token的方法FLOPs降低超过150倍，展示良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: SLS为矢量图形研究建立了通用潜空间基础，填补了SVG领域缺乏连续可逆潜在表示的空白，可加速矢量图形相关的生成、检索和条件任务。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics are a fundamental medium for resolution-independent visual content, yet the deep learning community lacks a continuous, dense, and invertible latent space for vector representations, the kind of foundational building block that Variational Autoencoders and their descendants have long provided for raster images. We introduce SLS (SVG Latent Space), a Transformer-based autoencoder that learns compact dense representations of individual SVG paths, the atomic visual elements from which any SVG image can be composed. By modeling SVG commands, coordinate data, and visual properties within a unified BPE-based token vocabulary, SLS learns fixed-size latent representations that jointly capture structure and appearance, and can be decoded back into valid, style-consistent SVG paths with high fidelity. The resulting embedding space is robust, invertible, and structured: embeddings lie on a unit hypersphere, enabling efficient similarity search, composition, and downstream conditioning through simple vector-space operations. Finally, we demonstrate that SLS generalizes across diverse tasks reducing their FLOPs by over 150 times compared to token-based approaches, and establishing a general-purpose latent foundation for vector graphics research.

</details>


### [54] [Entity-Constrained CBCT Retrieval for Low-Resource Dental Record Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21913)
*Nhi Ngoc-Yen Nguyen,Thai Nguyen,Kiet Huynh Cao Tuan,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ECCR框架，用于CBCT牙齿记录补全任务，通过参数无关的检索守卫机制，仅允许不扩展先验实体集合的图像条件证据被采纳，在极低标注资源下达到SOTA效果并获第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极高标注稀缺场景下（仅50例CBCT标注），多模态检索虽能提供视觉证据，但盲目采用完整检索记录会引入先验中不存在的实体（FDI位置或ICD编码），造成"视觉上合理但实体不成立"的有害结果。需要一种机制分离证据可用性与证据权威性。

Method: ECCR为参数无关框架：1）由语料库先验直接提供完整记录；2）用冻结的3D编码器检索图像条件化诊断证据；3）仅当检索到的证据不扩展先验的FDI/ICD实体集合时才被采纳，保证断言实体集由构造不变。

Result: 公开验证集加权得分0.3134，优于全记录多模态检索(0.2237)和静态文本先验(0.2915)；守卫阻止63.3%检索候选（否则将注入先验外的实体）；最终测试获得11.37/97.4，位列第二。

Conclusion: 在极端低资源场景下，控制多模态证据被允许修改的范围比迁移完整检索记录更可靠。证据权威与证据可用性分离是有效的设计原则。

Abstract: Completing dental records from cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) is difficult when annotation is scarce and individual clinical fields are supported by different types of evidence. MMDental Task 3 requires seven-field record completion from only 50 labeled CBCT cases and scores the correctness of structured FDI positions and ICD codes; consequently, a visually plausible retrieved record can still be harmful when it introduces an unsupported entity. We propose Entity-Constrained CBCT-Guided Retrieval (ECCR), a parameter-free framework that separates evidence availability from evidence authority. A corpus-derived prior first supplies the complete record. A frozen 3D encoder retrieves image-conditioned Diagnosis evidence, which is appended only if it does not expand the prior FDI or ICD entity set, so the asserted entity set is invariant by construction. On public validation, ECCR reaches a weighted score of 0.3134, improving on both full-record multimodal retrieval (0.2237) and a static text-only prior (0.2915); the guard blocks 63.3% of retrieved candidates, each of which would otherwise have injected an FDI position or ICD code absent from the prior. On the final test evaluation, ECCR obtains 11.37 of a 97.4-point attainable maximum, securing second place overall. The result indicates that, in an extreme low-resource setting, controlling what multimodal evidence is allowed to modify can be more reliable than transferring an entire retrieved record.

</details>


### [55] [AirAlign: Geometry-Aware Relative Pose Alignment for UAV Last-Meter Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21926)
*Jinyi Zhou,Shuo Feng,Yufei Wu,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AirAlign是一个面向无人机RGB图像对的相对位姿对齐框架，用于低空环境最后进近阶段的"最后一米"导航。它使用预训练视觉几何重建模型作为骨干提取几何感知特征，并通过场景分离的交叉验证和多模型集成提升性能，在ACMMM 2026 PairUAV挑战赛中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无人机导航在最终进近阶段需要高精度的位姿对齐来实现目标信息获取或操作，但视角和外观的巨大差异使得这一"最后一米"导航任务极具挑战性。

Method: 使用预训练的视觉几何重建模型作为骨干网络，从源-目标图像对中提取几何感知特征；将训练集按场景分离划分多重折用于不可见场景交叉验证和模型选择；推理时对多个选中模型预测取平均形成集成输出。

Result: 在ACMMM 2026 Workshop on UAVs in Multimedia的PairUAV挑战赛中验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，消融研究确认了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: AirAlign通过结合几何感知预训练特征、场景分离交叉验证和模型集成，有效解决了无人机位姿对齐中的视角和外观变化挑战，为RGB-only相对位姿对齐提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation in modern low-altitude environments requires more accurate pose alignment in the final approach stage for target information acquisition or manipulation, making "last-meter" navigation increasingly important. However, severe viewpoint and appearance variations make this task challenging. To tackle this problem, we propose AirAlign, a framework for RGB-only image-pair relative pose alignment for UAVs. AirAlign uses a pretrained visual geometry reconstruction model as the backbone to extract geometry-aware features from source-target image pairs. In addition, to better utilize the limited training data, we split the training set into multiple scene-disjoint folds for unseen cross-validation and model selection. During inference, the predictions of the selected models are averaged to form the ensemble output of the overall framework. Experiments on the PairUAV challenge at the ACMMM 2026 Workshop on UAVs in Multimedia demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method, while comprehensive ablation studies validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [56] [C$^2$Path: Class-Conditional Pathway Decoupling for Vision-Language Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21937)
*Lecheng Xu,Feifei Shao,Ouyangzi Ye,Zhen Wang,Lin Li,Kexin Li,Zhao Wang,Changqin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C$^2$Path提出了一种基于类条件计算路径解耦的视觉语言增量目标检测框架，通过类别专家库和类条件解耦模块生成类感知路由信号，动态组合ClassLoRA适配器，实现不同类别间的隔离参数更新和分离特征注入，从而缓解类别知识耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量目标检测方法存在两类类别知识耦合问题：共享参数更新导致的类别边界侵蚀，以及混合特征编码导致的类别表征纠缠。作者认为有效的增量学习需要类别特定的计算路径来实现隔离的参数更新和分离的类别注入。

Method: 提出C$^2$Path框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 类别专家库：由可学习的低秩计算节点组成，捕获类别特定知识；(2) 类条件解耦模块：利用token级类别线索生成类感知路由信号，动态组合ClassLoRA适配器，形成类别特定的计算路径，实现不同类别间的隔离更新和分离注入。

Result: 在COCO 2017数据集上的多种增量学习设置下，C$^2$Path持续优于现有最先进方法，提供了有效的可扩展持续类别扩展方案。

Conclusion: 类条件计算路径解耦是解决增量目标检测中类别知识耦合问题的有效策略，通过专家库与动态路由机制可以实现类别特定知识的隔离学习与注入，为视觉语言检测器的持续类别扩展提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Incremental Object Detection (IOD) aims to enable detectors to continuously learn novel categories while preserving previously acquired knowledge. However, existing methods suffer from two forms of \textbf{class knowledge coupling}: class boundary erosion induced by shared parameter updates and class representation entanglement arising from mixed feature encoding. We argue that effective incremental learning requires class-specific computational pathways that enable isolated parameter updates and separated class-wise injection. To this end, we propose \textbf{C$^2$Path}, a class-conditional pathway decoupling framework for vision-language incremental object detection that leverages token-level class cues to establish dedicated and updatable computational pathways for different categories. Specifically, C$^2$Path introduces a category expert library and a class-conditional decoupling module. The expert library consists of learnable low-rank computational nodes that capture category-specific knowledge, while the decoupling module generates class-aware routing signals to dynamically compose \textit{ClassLoRA} adapters from these experts, thereby forming class-specific computational pathways for isolated updates and separated injection across categories. Extensive experiments on COCO 2017 under multiple incremental learning settings demonstrate that C$^2$Path consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing an effective and scalable solution for continual category expansion in vision-language detectors.

</details>


### [57] [Sparse Multi-Stage Expert-Agent Routing for Complex Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21948)
*Sike Xiang,Shuang Chen,Qian sun,Jia Cheng,Yusi Wei,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种稀疏多阶段专家-代理路由框架，将临床诊断建模为阶段式路由过程，随证据动态激活稀疏的医学专家代理并维护演化中的病例状态。同时引入ClinFEScore事实感知语义评估协议。实验显示将激活专家数从17降至3，临床验证诊断准确率达91.5%，且评估分数与临床医生判断高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM临床推理系统要么单次预测，要么依赖固定多代理工作流，专家参与静态或过度穷举，效率低且不贴合真实临床推理的动态性（证据逐步涌现、需协调多专科且资源有限）。

Method: Sparse Multi-Stage Expert-Agent Routing框架：基于多模态逐步获得的临床证据，维护演进的病例状态，适应性地激活稀疏医学专家代理集合，并配有跨阶段的专家专属记忆。同时提出ClinFEScore，一种事实感知语义评估协议，用于超出表面相似性的自由文本诊断结论评估。

Result: 在重构的MAC和AgentClinic-NEJM多阶段病例上，将平均激活专家数从17.0降至3.0且保持强的诊断质量；在200例真实医院MDT病例上，ClinFEScore与临床医生判断高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.81, Pearson r=0.87），方法达到91.5%临床医生验证的诊断准确率，每例约5次专家代理/LLM调用。

Conclusion: 稀疏阶段式协调是LLM临床推理中高效且临床相关的方法，验证了按需激活专家而非穷举参与的可行性。

Abstract: Complex clinical reasoning requires models to update diagnostic hypotheses as new evidence emerges and to coordinate different medical specialities under limited consultation resources. Existing LLM-based clinical reasoning systems typically perform single-pass prediction or rely on fixed multi-agent workflows, making expert participation either static or unnecessarily exhaustive. We propose Sparse Multi-Stage Expert-Agent Routing, a language-based clinical reasoning framework that models diagnosis as a stage-wise routing process. Given progressively available clinical evidence derived from multiple modalities, the framework maintains an evolving case state and adaptively activates a sparse set of medical expert agents, supported by expert-specific memory across stages. To evaluate free-text diagnostic conclusions beyond surface similarity, we further introduce ClinFEScore, a fact-aware semantic evaluation protocol for clinical reasoning outputs. On reconstructed multi-stage cases from MAC and AgentClinic-NEJM, our framework reduces the average number of activated experts from 17.0 to 3.0 whilst maintaining strong fact-level diagnostic quality. On 200 real-world hospital MDT cases, ClinFEScore correlates strongly with clinician judgements (Spearman's $ρ=0.81$; Pearson's $r=0.87$), whilst our method achieves 91.5\% clinician-verified diagnostic accuracy with approximately five expert-agent/LLM calls per case. These results support sparse stage-wise coordination as an efficient and clinically relevant approach to LLM-based clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [58] [Trustworthy Visual Quality Inspection under Data Scarcity in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21967)
*Panagiotis Sapoutzoglou,Jessy Ribaira,Martin Kanounnikoff,Bas Tijsma,Christian Geiß,Maria Pateraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向制造业自动化视觉检测的分阶段流水线：利用扩散模型生成合成缺陷样本以缓解数据稀缺，并结合贝叶斯分类器提供置信度估计，将模糊样本交由人工复核而非强制分类。初步结果表明该方法能降低数据稀缺下训练可信检测模型的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产线上缺陷样本稀缺，且硬标签决策存在不对称成本（误收增加漏检风险，误拒浪费良品），需要兼顾数据增强与置信度感知的检测方案。

Method: 采用扩散模型生成合成缺陷样本进行数据增强；使用贝叶斯分类器输出置信度并对模糊样本延迟至人工审查；嵌入多级互补检查的分阶段流水线中。

Result: 初步结果显示扩散生成的缺陷结合不确定性感知分类，可在数据稀缺条件下降低达到可信部署模型的成本。评估覆盖分类、定位以及决策/合成数据/流水线结构三个信任点。

Conclusion: 工作仍处于进行中，初步结果表明合成缺陷增强与不确定性感知分类的组合有望在数据稀缺的产线环境中提升检测模型的可信度与经济性。

Abstract: Automated visual inspection in manufacturing aims to replace slow and inconsistent manual checks, but its economic value depends on whether its decisions can be trusted enough to automate routine inspection while reserving human expertise for ambiguous cases. In production-line settings, defective samples are scarce, since the process is optimized to produce good parts, which limits any learning-based inspector trained on real data alone. Compounding this, defect decisions emitted as hard labels with no confidence estimate carry an asymmetric cost: a false reject wastes a good product, while a false accept may increase the risk of undetected defects progressing through the production process. We address both problems by mitigating data scarcity through the generation of synthetic defective samples with a diffusion model, and meeting the need for confidence-aware decisions with a Bayesian classifier that defers ambiguous units to human review rather than misclassifying them. These components are embedded in a staged pipeline of successive, complementary checks. We evaluate how synthetic augmentation affects classification and localization on a test set of real defects, and examine the system's trustworthiness at three points: the decision, the synthetic data, and the pipeline structure. This work-in-progress reports preliminary results suggesting that diffusion-generated defects, combined with uncertainty-aware classification, can lower the cost of reaching a trustworthy, deployable inspection model under data scarcity.

</details>


### [59] [Retrieval-Augmented Visual Prompting: Guiding Foundation Models in Two-Photon Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21970)
*Salvatore Calcagno,Marco Finocchiaro,Giovanni Bellitto,Daniela Giordano,Concetto Spampinato,Federica Proietto Salanitri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出检索增强视觉提示（RAVP）框架，在无需微调权重的情况下，通过将检索到的带标注示例图像直接注入SAM 3的输入，实现对双光子钙成像数据的零样本神经元检测与实例分割。实验表明单示例注入比多示例更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双光子钙成像数据对基础模型构成挑战：图像外观跨记录与实验条件差异大、标注稀缺、且常需快速适配。现有范式依赖微调适配模型权重，作者反问：能否通过推理时输入设计直接引导基础模型，避免参数适配。

Method: RAVP框架基于SAM 3，每个目标图块被附加一个检索到的带标注示例，该示例的边界框作为概念提示（concept prompt）。研究了多种示例选择策略：荧光引导的启发式方法，以及一个轻量级召回预测器用于估计哪个示例对目标图块最有效。

Result: 在Allen Brain Observatory上的实验表明，示例增强推理持续提升零样本神经元检测与实例分割性能。消融实验显示，单个精心挑选的示例比多个检索示例的提示更有效。

Conclusion: 推理时视觉记忆注入可作为参数适配的有效替代方案，为专业生物医学成像场景提供简单且有效的适应基础模型的方式。

Abstract: Two-photon calcium imaging presents a challenging setting for foundation models: image appearance varies substantially across recordings and experimental conditions, annotations are scarce, and rapid adaptation is often needed. Rather than adapting model weights through fine-tuning, we ask whether a foundation model can be guided at inference time by injecting external visual memory directly into its input. We implement this idea with SAM 3 and introduce Retrieval-Augmented Visual Prompting (RAVP), a framework in which each target tile is augmented with a retrieved annotated exemplar whose bounding box is used as a concept prompt.
  RAVP turns retrieval into a form of visual prompting and enables adaptation through input design alone. We study multiple exemplar selection strategies, including fluorescence-guided heuristics and a lightweight recall predictor trained to estimate which exemplar is most informative for a target tile. Experiments on the Allen Brain Observatory show that exemplar-augmented inference consistently strengthens zero-shot neuron detection and instance segmentation. Ablation studies further show that a single carefully selected exemplar is more effective than prompting with multiple retrieved examples. These results position inference-time visual memory injection as a simple and effective alternative to parameter adaptation for foundation models in specialized biomedical imaging.

</details>


### [60] [Improved denoising diffusion probabilistic models with efficient non-diagonal covariance modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21972)
*Rui Xia,Ayan Das,Artem Artemev,Andi Zhang,Guillaume Hennequin,Alberto Bernacchia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Kronecker-DCT (K-DCT)协方差模型，用于加速DDPM采样。该模型利用Kronecker分解表示颜色通道间协方差，并用DCT在频域建模空间协方差，将计算复杂度从二次降至对数线性，在保证可负担开销的同时显著提升少步采样下的FID和似然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDPM采样可通过近似去噪后验协方差来利用二阶信息以加速，但此前方法（如对角近似）未能捕捉自然图像中像素间和颜色通道间的强非对角相关性以及缓慢衰减的幂律频谱特性，导致加速效果受限。

Method: 提出K-DCT协方差模型：(1) K个不同颜色通道间的协方差用Kronecker分解建模；(2) 空间协方差在DCT频域中建模，利用自然图像的幂律频谱特性；(3) 利用预训练的score model在CIFAR-10、Celeb-A、ImageNet和LSUN等数据集上学习K-DCT结构的去噪后验协方差摊销。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Celeb-A、ImageNet和LSUN数据集上，K-DCT方法在FID和似然度两方面均优于此前最优的去噪采样器，尤其在少去噪步数（few-step）场景下优势明显；计算和内存开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: K-DCT协方差模型通过更贴合自然图像统计特性的协方差近似，实现了更高效且更高质量的DDPM加速采样，为扩散模型采样加速提供了一种计算高效的二阶信息利用方案。

Abstract: The sampling process of Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPMs) can be accelerated by leveraging second-order information in the form of approximations to the denoising posterior covariance -- allowing samples of acceptable quality to be produced in fewer but larger sampling steps. Previous attempts at using such information have used drastic (e.g.\ diagonal) simplifications of the covariance. These do not do justice to the peculiar statistical structure of natural images, which exhibit strong non-diagonal correlations between pixels and color channels, and a slow-decaying power-law frequency spectrum. Here, we develop a novel covariance model that captures these features. Our Kronecker-DCT (K-DCT) model uses a Kronecker-factored decomposition of inter-color covariances and spatial covariances modeled in the frequency domain using the Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT). The use of the DCT reduces the computational complexity from quadratic to log-linear, resulting in negligible computational and memory overhead in each denoising step. By learning K-DCT-structured amortizations of the denoising posterior covariance using pre-trained score models on CIFAR-10, Celeb-A, ImageNet and LSUN datasets, we show improved performance compared to previous SOTA denoising samplers, both in terms of FID and likelihoods, especially in the regime of few denoising steps.

</details>


### [61] [Close Shortcut Wins Long: Seeking Diverse and Stable Generators for Data-Free Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22003)
*Kailin Lyu,Zherui Zhang,Junhao Dong,Kexue Fu,Weiguang Pang,Rongtao Xu,Qizheng Wang,Di Wu,Chee-Keong Kwoh,Longxiang Gao,Shibiao Xu,Changwei Wang,Ce Hao,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSWL框架，从频域视角解决数据无关知识蒸馏（DFKD）中生成器存在的"生成捷径学习"问题（过度依赖特定频率分量和位置），通过特征级频域增强抑制捷径行为，并设计跨阶段频率重建（CSFR）辅助任务隐式构建EMA机制提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成器的DFKD方法在频域表现"生成捷径学习"，过度依赖特定频率成分和位置，导致合成图像质量不一致和类别多样性差，且训练不稳定。

Method: 1）特征级频域增强：鼓励生成器关注完整频谱，抑制捷径学习；2）Cross-Stage Frequency Reconstruction (CSFR)辅助任务：隐式构造EMA机制，促进长期优化与训练稳定。

Result: 在多个图像识别数据集和多分辨率上，包括下游任务，验证了CSWL在提升生成多样性和训练稳定性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 从频域视角引入的CSWL框架能有效提升DFKD生成器的多样性和训练稳定性，缓解生成捷径学习现象。

Abstract: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD) preserves privacy by transferring knowledge without real data access. However, existing generator-based DFKD methods suffer from over-reliance on teacher preferences and pattern collapse, exhibiting "generative shortcut learning" in the frequency domain: dependent on specific frequency components and frequency positions, resulting in inconsistent synthetic image quality and class diversity. In this paper, we propose a CSWL framework aimed at introducing insights from the frequency domain perspective to improve generator diversity and training stability to Close the phenomenon of Shortcut learning to Win in the Longer term. To address the issue of generative shortcut learning, we introduce frequency-domain augmentation at the feature level, encouraging the generator to attend to the full frequency spectrum and thereby suppress shortcut learning behavior. To tackle training instability, we propose a Cross-Stage Frequency Reconstruction (CSFR) auxiliary task, which implicitly constructs an Exponential Moving Average (EMA) mechanism to promote long-term optimization and stability. Extensive experiments, including downstream tasks and various image recognition datasets at multiple resolutions, validate the effectiveness of CSWL in improving both diversity and stability from the frequency view.

</details>


### [62] [ORBIT++: Benchmarking SfM in the Wild with 360° Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22039)
*Sara Sabour,Linyi Jin,Richard Tucker,Amir Hertz,Marcus Brubaker,Saurabh Saxena,Junhwa Hur,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Deqing Sun,David J. Fleet,Richard Szeliski,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORBIT是一个利用在线全景360°视频构建的相机位姿估计基准，通过从全景视频中裁剪并重投影生成具有挑战性的透视视角片段，同时保持可靠的ground-truth轨迹，用于评估SfM方法在复杂真实场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SfM方法在复杂视频（挑战性相机运动、动态场景）中常失败，且缺乏可靠的ground-truth基准来衡量真实世界进展和定位改进空间。

Method: 利用在线全景360°视频作为数据来源，全景特性提供更丰富的视觉上下文以跟踪相机运动（即使部分视图受模糊、运动或动态物体影响），跟踪完整360°视频中的相机运动后，裁剪并重投影选定区域以生成透视视角片段作为基准（ORBIT）。

Result: COLMAP以及近期的基于优化的和feed-forward的SfM方法在ORBIT基准上均难以准确估计相机位姿，证明该基准具有挑战性。

Conclusion: ORBIT为研究者在真正具有挑战性的真实世界SfM问题上衡量进展提供了有价值的测试平台。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is a cornerstone of 3D perception, yet current methods often fail when applied to complex videos involving challenging camera motions or dynamic scenes. Compounding the problem, the field lacks reliable ground-truth benchmarks for such difficult scenarios, making it hard to gauge real-world progress or to pinpoint where improvements are most needed. To address this gap, we introduce a new benchmark for evaluating camera pose estimation. Our key insight is to leverage online panoramic 360° video as a source of data from which to construct challenging clips, while still enabling robust ground-truth trajectory recovery. The panoramic nature of these videos provides richer visual context for tracking camera motion, even when parts of the view are affected by blur, motion, or dynamic objects. After tracking camera motion across full 360° videos, we crop and reproject selected portions to generate perspective-view clips that serve as our benchmark, called ORBIT. Experiments show that COLMAP, as well as recent optimization-based and feed-forward SfM methods struggle to accurately estimate camera poses on our benchmark. Hence, ORBIT provides a valuable testbed where researchers can meaningfully measure progress on truly challenging, real-world SfM problems.

</details>


### [63] [Robust Global Structure-from-Motion via View Graph Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22054)
*Jiamin Xu,Lixing Yao,Weichen Dai,Renshu Gu,Zunjie Zhu,Weiwei Xu,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于子图引导的视图图剪枝框架，用于提升全局结构从运动（SfM）在视觉模糊匹配条件下的鲁棒性。通过先在局部一致子图内进行全局SfM获得可靠相机位姿，再使用RANSAC跨子图剪枝移除不一致边，最后在精化后的视图图上执行全局SfM，显著改善了相机位姿估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局SfM相比增量SfM具有更好的可扩展性，但其性能高度依赖于视图图的质量，视觉上模糊的匹配会产生错误的边，导致相机注册错误和重建伪影。现有方法缺乏有效的鲁棒性保障机制。

Method: 1) 将视图图划分为局部一致的子图；2) 在每个子图内执行全局SfM获得可靠相机位姿；3) 利用RANSAC跨子图进行边剪枝，移除不一致的边；4) 在精化后的视图图上执行最终全局SfM。

Result: 在模糊、序列化和无序图像数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法提升了全局SfM在挑战性条件下的鲁棒性；与神经渲染的联合评估显示改进的相机估计带来更高质量的新视角合成结果。

Conclusion: 子图内部一致性信息可用于有效识别并剪除视图图中的不可靠边连接，显著增强全局SfM在模糊匹配下的鲁棒性，并进一步惠及下游新视角合成任务。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion (SfM) aims to estimate camera poses and reconstruct 3D structures from a collection of unordered images. Compared with incremental SfM, global SfM achieves better scalability by jointly estimating camera poses based on a view graph constructed from pairwise correspondences. However, its performance is highly sensitive to erroneous edges caused by visually ambiguous matches, which may lead to incorrect camera registration and reconstruction artifacts. In this work, we propose a subgraph-guided view graph pruning framework for robust global SfM. Our key idea is to exploit the internal consistency of reliable subgraphs to identify and remove unreliable connections. Specifically, we first partition the view graph into locally consistent subgraphs and perform global SfM within each subgraph to obtain reliable camera poses. We then apply RANSAC-based edge pruning across subgraphs to remove inconsistent edges, and finally perform global SfM on the refined view graph. Extensive experiments on ambiguous, sequential, and unordered image datasets demonstrate that our method improves the robustness of global SfM under challenging conditions. Further evaluation with neural rendering shows that the improved camera estimation leads to higher-quality novel view synthesis results.

</details>


### [64] [Competitive Memory Readout for Robust Video Object Segmentation: 2nd Place Technical Report for the MOSEv2 Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22064)
*Mingqi Gao,Sijie Li,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出针对ECCV 2026 LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道的方法，基于SAM 3架构，通过引入竞争性记忆读取（Competitive Memory Readout）显式纳入同类别干扰物证据，并配合轻量自适应恢复规则防止对弱目标或重现目标的过度抑制，最终以66.20主指标获得赛道第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAM 3的仅目标记忆检索在面对同类别干扰物（如其他相似物体）时会将干扰物隐式表示为背景，导致目标身份混淆。在长期遮挡、消失重现、大外观变化及强干扰等复杂时序动态下，目标身份保持能力不足。

Method: 在SAM 3的记忆读取阶段引入竞争性记忆读取（Competitive Memory Readout），显式引入同类别竞争者的证据参与目标信息的记忆检索；随后应用轻量自适应恢复规则（adaptive restoration）来防止竞争机制过度抑制弱目标或重新出现的目标。

Result: 在MOSEv2赛道取得主指标66.20，排名第2。

Conclusion: 保留SAM 3原始跟踪管线的前提下，通过改进记忆读取机制显著提升了挑战性视频中的目标身份保持能力，验证了显式建模同类别干扰物对于鲁棒视频目标分割的有效性。

Abstract: We present our solution for the MOSEv2 track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge at ECCV 2026. The challenge evaluates robust video object segmentation under complex temporal dynamics, including long-term occlusion, disappearance and reappearance, large appearance changes, and strong interference from visually similar objects. Our method builds on SAM~3 and focuses on its memory readout. Standard target-only memory retrieval can confuse the annotated target with same-class non-target objects because such distractors are represented only implicitly as background. Our method introduces Competitive Memory Readout, which explicitly incorporates same-class competitor evidence when retrieving target information from memory. To prevent excessive suppression of weak or reappearing targets, we further apply a lightweight adaptive restoration rule after competition. The resulting system retains the original SAM~3 tracking pipeline while improving target identity preservation in challenging videos. Our submission achieves 66.20 on the primary challenge score and ranks 2nd in the MOSEv2 track.

</details>


### [65] [ADMIL: Attention-Distilled Multiple Instance Learning for Selective Foundation Model Inference in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22066)
*Duncan Stothers,Ren-Chin Wu,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADMIL提出了一种选择性计算框架，通过将ABMIL教师的注意力分布蒸馏到一个轻量级EfficientNet tile选择模型（PriorNet）中，在推理时仅对top-K个tile调用昂贵的病理基础模型嵌入，即可在BRACS、PANDA和CAMELYON16数据集上匹配完整教师模型的表现，同时避免超过98%的基础模型（Virchow2）tile嵌入和推理FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于注意力机制的多示例学习（ABMIL）使用病理基础模型嵌入在滑片级任务上表现优异，但穷举推理需要对每个前景tile都应用大型图像编码器，计算开销巨大。然而注意力分布往往集中在少数信息丰富的区域，存在大量冗余计算可以消除。

Method: ADMIL包含两个阶段：训练时将ABMIL教师的注意力分布通过KL散度蒸馏到轻量级PriorNet（基于EfficientNet架构），使其学习从原始tile像素预测注意力分数；推理时PriorNet对前景tile池打分并选择top-K个tile，仅对这些子集调用昂贵的病理基础模型生成嵌入，再由selected-bag ABMIL学生模型进行滑片级预测。

Result: 在BRACS、PANDA和CAMELYON16上，ADMIL分别在K=4、8、128个tile时匹配完整教师模型的头部性能，避免了超过98%的Virchow2基础模型tile嵌入和推理FLOPs。随机选择和教师注意力oracle对照实验表明，性能提升依赖于任务相关的tile选择性而非简单的数量减少。量化与定性分析显示PriorNet以高保真度恢复了教师的tile排序。

Conclusion: ADMIL证明了几乎所有的昂贵tile编码都可以被移除而不牺牲滑片级性能，为临床场景中需要低延迟和计算成本优化的部署提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) using pathology foundation model embeddings is effective for slide-level tasks, but exhaustive inference requires applying a large image encoder to every foreground tile despite the subsequent attention distribution often concentrating over a small subset of informative regions. We introduce ADMIL (Attention-Distilled Multiple Instance Learning), a selective-compute framework that distills an ABMIL teacher's attention into a lightweight tile-selection model, PriorNet. Using an EfficientNet architecture, PriorNet learns the teacher attention distribution from raw tile pixels with KL divergence; at inference, it scores the foreground pool, selects the top-K tiles, and invokes the expensive foundation model only on that subset before a selected-bag ABMIL student predicts the slide label. Across BRACS, PANDA, and CAMELYON16, ADMIL matches full-teacher headline performance at K=4, 8, and 128 tiles, respectively, avoiding >98% of foundation model (Virchow2) tile embeddings and model inference FLOPs. Random and teacher-attention oracle controls show that this result depends on task-relevant selection rather than tile-count reduction alone. Quantitative and qualitative analyses suggest that PriorNet recovers the teacher's tile ordering with high fidelity while focusing on task-relevant morphological regions. ADMIL shows that nearly all expensive tile encodings can be removed without sacrificing slide-level performance, providing a potential path for more efficient deployment in clinical settings where latency and compute costs are key considerations.

</details>


### [66] [Spiking Neural Networks for Energy-Efficient Object Detection in Forward-Looking Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22072)
*Gwenevere Frank,Gert Cauwenberghs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikeYOLO 是首个将全脉冲神经网络（SNN）应用于前视声呐（FLS）目标检测的工作，在三个 FLS 数据集上以显著更低的计算能耗达到与 CNN 基线（YOLOv8m）竞争的精度，并对乘性散斑噪声具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV 是功率受限平台，在长航时任务中推进与计算/传感争夺有限电量。AUV 常在黑暗或浑浊水域依赖声呐作为主要感知模态，而现有 CNN 目标检测方案（如 YOLOv8m）虽为 SOTA，但能耗高昂（322 mJ/推理）。FLS 图像具有稀疏高对比度的结构，与脉冲编码天然匹配，但此前尚无工作系统评估 SNN 在 FLS 目标检测中的适用性。因此作者提出 SpikeYOLO 以探索能效与精度的更优权衡。

Method: 提出 SpikeYOLO，一个全脉冲网络，使用替代梯度（surrogate gradients）训练。网络结构基于 YOLO 架构重新设计为脉冲形式，利用二进制脉冲激活实现稀疏的仅累加（accumulate-only）运算，从而在理论上大幅降低计算能耗。在三个 FLS 目标检测数据集（UATD、Marine-Debris-FLS 等）上与 YOLOv8m、YOLO-SONAR、Fast R-CNN 等 CNN 基线进行基准对比，并评估对乘性散斑噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: SpikeYOLO T=2 在 UATD 上实现 3.3 倍更低的理论计算能耗（97 vs 322 mJ），精度接近（0.529 vs YOLOv8m 的 0.575 mAP@0.5:0.95）；在稀疏的 Marine-Debris-FLS 数据集上 mAP@0.5 与 YOLOv8m 相当，并超过 YOLO-SONAR 和 Fast R-CNN 基线，同时能耗低 4.4 倍；对散斑噪声鲁棒性显著更强（σ=0.4 时仅退化 3.0%，而 YOLOv8m 退化 8.9%），在 σ=0.6 时整体精度超越 YOLOv8m。

Conclusion: SpikeYOLO 证明了 FLS 图像的结构特性与脉冲编码天然匹配，全脉冲网络能够在显著降低能耗的同时保持与 CNN 相当的检测精度，并在噪声鲁棒性上更优，为 AUV 在真实水域环境中的低功耗感知提供了有前景的路线。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are increasingly important tools in industries ranging from research, to energy, to defense. AUVs are power-constrained platforms operating in remote environments with fixed battery capacities, where propulsion competes with compute and sensors for power over lengthy mission durations. AUVs frequently operate in dark or turbid waters where optical sensing is of limited value, and rely on sonar as their primary sensing modality. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are the state-of-the-art solution for object detection in forward-looking sonar imagery, but are energy expensive (e.g. YOLOv8m: 322 mJ/inference). Spiking neural networks (SNNs) rely on binary spike activations and thus sparse accumulate-only operations, allowing them to be remarkably energy efficient, particularly when paired with dedicated neuromorphic hardware. The sparse, high-contrast structure of forward-looking sonar (FLS) returns is structurally matched to spike coding in a way that optical imagery is not. No prior work has assessed the suitability of SNNs for object detection in FLS imagery. SpikeYOLO, a fully spiking network trained with surrogate gradients, was benchmarked against state-of-the-art CNN baselines on three FLS object detection datasets. Key results: SpikeYOLO T=2 achieves 3.3$\times$ lower theoretical compute energy on UATD (97 vs 322 mJ) at competitive accuracy (0.529 mAP@0.5:0.95 vs. YOLOv8m's 0.575); SpikeYOLO matches YOLOv8m on mAP@0.5 and outperforms YOLO-SONAR and Fast R-CNN baselines on the sparse Marine-Debris-FLS dataset at 4.4$\times$ lower energy; SpikeYOLO demonstrates superior robustness to multiplicative speckle noise (3.0% degradation at $σ{=}0.4$ vs. 8.9% for YOLOv8m), outperforming YOLOv8m outright at $σ{=}0.6$, directly relevant to real-world FLS deployment.

</details>


### [67] [When More References Hurt: Contamination-Aware DINOv2 Memory Banks for Few-Shot Steel Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22082)
*Hannaneh Kalantari,Javad Khoramdel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究patch-memory异常检测器在参考库含噪声（未经验证工业图像）时的鲁棒性问题。基于DINOv2的AnomalyDINO框架，作者提出用少量可信正常图像作为种子，通过与干净种子的距离打分候选patch，剔除最可疑的20%后合并，再通过贪心coreset选择固定预算。在Severstal数据集上，该方法将异常patch污染从9.46%降至2.59%，AUPRC从0.0950提升至0.1084。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测常假设参考patch库全部正常，但实际获取额外工业图像时无法保证纯度。未验证图像中的异常patch会污染内存库，严重降低检测性能。作者希望用极少量可信正常图像作为"种子"，安全地从大规模未验证参考中恢复有用正常patch，无需缺陷掩码。

Method: 基于AnomalyDINO的DINOv2 patch-memory框架：1) 用少量可信正常图像构成干净种子库；2) 对候选patch按到种子库的距离打分；3) 丢弃最可疑的20% patch；4) 将保留patch与种子合并；5) 用贪心coreset选择强制执行固定内存预算。评估在Severstal工业数据集上进行，对比朴素扩展、随机移除和仅用干净图像等基线。

Result: Severstal基准上，朴素扩展含9.46%异常patch，该"修剪"方法剔除78.1%异常，残余污染降至2.59%。在51,200-patch预算下，提议库达0.1084 AUPRC，优于朴素扩展(0.0950)、随机移除(0.0952)和八张干净图像(0.1030)。仅注入0.5%异常patch就把AUPRC从0.1030降至0.0759。五个held-out配对中提议库全部优于朴素扩展，平均增益0.0142 AUPRC。

Conclusion: 参考库纯度是异常检测中的一阶设计变量。未经验证的图像只有在显式过滤其贡献时才有使用价值。基于距离的修剪加核心集选择提供了一种无需掩码的安全扩库策略，可显著改善大规模patch内存的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Patch-memory anomaly detectors assume that their reference bank is normal, an assumption that is difficult to guarantee when additional industrial images are unverified. We study whether a few trusted normal images can safely recover useful normal patches from such references without defect masks. Starting from the DINOv2 patch-memory formulation used by AnomalyDINO, we score candidate patches by distance to a clean seed bank, discard the most suspicious 20%, merge the retained patches with the seed, and enforce a fixed budget by greedy coreset selection. On Severstal, naive additional references contain 9.46% anomalous patches; the proposed trim rejects 78.1\% of them and reduces residual contamination to 2.59%. At an equal 51,200-patch development budget, the proposed bank reaches 0.1084 AUPRC versus 0.0950 for naive expansion, 0.0952 for random removal, and 0.1030 for eight clean images. Injecting only 0.5\% anomalous patches into a clean bank reduces AUPRC from 0.1030 to 0.0759. On all five completed held-out pairs, the proposed bank improves over naive expansion, with a mean gain of 0.0142 AUPRC. Reference purity is therefore a first-order design variable, and unverified images are useful only when their contribution is filtered explicitly.

</details>


### [68] [Three-Phase Scribble-Adaptive Curriculum Learning for autoPETV Grand Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22096)
*Libo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Libo Zhang在autoPETV Grand Challenge中提出的交互式全身PET/CT病灶分割算法方案：将用户交互（前景/背景涂鸦）编码为额外输入通道，采用约1.4亿参数的残差编码器U-Net，通过三阶段课程学习（先学全自动分割、再观察真实标注涂鸦、最后通过在线模拟自我纠错）在4000个epoch内训练。基于1811例autoPET和DeepPSMA数据，集成五折的logit平均结果。在六步交互的五折交叉验证中取得平均AUC-Dice 3.836和AUC-DMM 3.869，呈单调提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决医学影像中交互式病灶分割这一实际临床问题：医生通过点击或涂鸦提供少量交互信号，期望模型快速准确地分割PET/CT中的病灶。现有方法在交互利用效率和纠错能力上仍有不足，作者希望通过课程学习范式让网络从全自动分割逐步过渡到交互式分割，并模拟真实交互中的错误纠正过程。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 将用户前后景涂鸦栅格化为两个额外输入通道；(2) 约1.4亿参数的残差编码器U-Net；(3) 三阶段课程训练：第一阶段静默交互通道学习全自动分割，第二阶段使用标注派生的涂鸦在不同可见模式下训练，第三阶段通过在线模拟最多五步错误驱动修正来适应自身错误；(4) 五折集成，通过logit平均融合最佳和最终检查点。

Result: 在六步交互的五折交叉验证中，最终检查点达到平均AUC-Dice 3.836和AUC-DMM 3.869，每一折均单调提升，约一半的总增益由第一次纠正涂鸦带来。代码和模型权重已开源。

Conclusion: 该课程学习方法有效结合全自动与交互式分割，使网络逐步适应人类交互模式，并通过自我纠错模拟显著提升交互效率。这是一个针对医学影像分割的竞赛方案，方法具有可借鉴性但属于医学领域应用。

Abstract: This report describes Libo Zhang's algorithmic solution to autoPETV Grand Challenge on interactive lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT.
