We propose a simple, motion-free cross-modal calibration framework that uses a temporally modulated, blended ChArUco target presented on standard consumer displays. By alternating between the original pattern and a partially blended version, the target reliably triggers events while remaining continuously observable to a frame-based camera, avoiding blank frames and reducing synchronization constraints to a coarse, trigger-based alignment. We discretize events into frames coarsely aligned with the RGB images, apply lightweight denoising, and perform ChArUco-based intrinsic and stereo extrinsic calibration. Extensive experiments assess robustness to blending opacity, display brightness, external illumination, viewing angle, and handheld acquisition. Compared to the strongest motion-based reference (E2Calib + Kalibr) and a non-motion-based reference (Plasberg et al.), our approach reduces the mean reprojection error by $44\%$ and $6\%$, respectively, while substantially simplifying the calibration procedure. Finally, we demonstrate practical utility in a robotic eye-to-hand calibration case study, showing consistent transformations and stable downstream geometric measurements even under partial occlusions. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/nhessenthaler/simple-evrgb-cal.

</details>


### [138] [Optimize Surgical Triplet Recognition: A Knowledge-Driven Mixture-of-Experts Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22972)
*Yiyi Zhang,Yuchen Yuan,Ying Zheng,Jialun Pei,Jinpeng Li,Zheng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoE-Coa（MoeCo）框架，通过组件定制适配器（解耦时空特征）、协调梯度学习策略（解决类别不平衡）、以及知识驱动的混合专家机制（集成多模态大语言模型领域知识）来解决手术动作三元组识别中的组件级和类别级优化冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术动作三元组识别（instrument-verb-target）对上下文感知机器人手术至关重要，但现有方法面临三大挑战：特征空间纠缠导致组件级优化冲突、类别数据严重不平衡导致类别级优化冲突、缺乏领域知识引导限制模型可解释性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出MoeCo框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 组件定制适配器（component-tailored adapter），在时空维度上解耦任务特定特征；(2) 协调梯度学习策略（coordinated gradient learning），自适应重平衡正负梯度以增强稀有类别感知；(3) 知识驱动的混合专家机制（knowledge-driven MoE），利用多模态大语言模型引导的动态先验知识通过激活的专家集成到优化流水线中。

Result: 在公开的CholecT45和CholecT50数据集上的大量实验证实了所提出共优化流水线的有效性，以及通过知识驱动混合专家机制集成动态先验的优越性。

Conclusion: MoeCo通过解耦特征空间、平衡梯度更新并引入LLM引导的领域知识，有效解决了手术场景中组件级和类别级优化冲突，提升了手术动作识别的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Surgical action triplet recognition constitutes a critical task in context-aware robot-assisted surgery, facilitating automatic surgical action perception by identifying instrument, verb, target, and their association. However, existing works struggle to analyze such complex surgical scenes due to three main issues: (1) component-level optimization conflicts caused by entangled feature spaces, (2) category-level optimization conflicts arising from severe data imbalance, and (3) lack of domain knowledge guidance that limits model interpretability and robustness. To address these challenges, we propose a Mixture-of-Experts-guided Co-Optimization (\textit{MoeCo}) framework powered by knowledge-driven learning. Within the co-optimization pipeline, to first mitigate component-level conflicts, we introduce a component-tailored adapter that disentangles task-specific features across spatial-temporal regimes, facilitating effective component specialization. Next, we develop a coordinated gradient learning strategy to handle category-level conflicts, which adaptively rebalances positive-negative gradients to enhance the perception of rare categories. Notably, inspired by surgical domain expertise, we introduce a knowledge-driven mixture-of-experts mechanism that dynamically integrates multimodal large language model-guided knowledge via activated experts, thereby enriching the co-optimization pipeline with more expressive and robust representations. Extensive experiments on the public CholecT45 and CholecT50 datasets confirm the effectiveness of the proposed co-optimization pipeline and the superiority of dynamic priors integration via the knowledge-driven mixture-of-experts mechanism.

</details>


### [139] [ENCORE: Entropy-Guided Cropping and Attention Regularization for Robust Vision--Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22996)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ENCORE提出了一个基于熵引导的框架，通过推理时的熵基裁剪策略（ECS）和训练时的熵正则化（ERT）来动态保留视觉编码器中与提示相关的完整区域，在仅微调0.14%参数的情况下，在十个VQA基准上平均提升1.43%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级VLM的Transformer视觉编码器将图像分割为固定分辨率的子图像，破坏了对象完整性。已有的方法仅关注视觉模态，无法动态保留与提示相关区域的完整性，限制了VLM性能。

Method: 提出了ENCORE框架，包含两个组件：(1) 推理时的熵基裁剪策略（ECS），评估一小批候选裁剪区域，选择交叉模态注意力熵最小者，保留与提示相关的连续区域；(2) 训练时的熵正则化（ERT），在下一token预测中加入熵项，强化对关键视觉token的关注并降低不相关token权重。

Result: 在十个VQA基准上，ENCORE仅微调0.14%的参数，实现了平均1.43%的准确率提升，在近期2B参数VLM中达到最先进性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作发现跨模态注意力的早期层图像-文本熵与答案接地质量和任务准确率强相关，并利用这一发现通过熵引导的裁剪和正则化来提升轻量级VLM的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on diverse vision-language tasks, but transformer-based visual encoders split images into fixed-resolution sub-images, compromising object integrity in lightweight VLMs. Existing methods only focus on the visual modality and fail to dynamically preserve the integrity of prompt-relevant regions, limiting performance. In this work, we observe that the early-layer image-text entropy of cross-modal attention strongly correlates with answer grounding quality and task accuracy. Building on this finding, we propose \textbf{ENCORE}, an entropy-guided framework with two components: At inference, an \textbf{Entropy-based Cropping Strategy} (ECS) evaluates a small set of candidate crops and selects the one with minimal entropy, preserving contiguous regions relevant to the prompt. At training, \textbf{Entropy Regularization Training} (ERT) augments next-token prediction with an entropy term that sharpens attention on key visual tokens while down-weighting irrelevant ones. Experiments on ten VQA benchmarks show that ENCORE, fine-tuning only 0.14\% of parameters, achieves an average 1.43\% accuracy gain and state-of-the-art performance among recent 2B-parameter VLMs. Our code is released in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/ENCORE.

</details>


### [140] [Coarse Indexing, Fine Evidence: Decoupling Temporal Granularity in Long-Video RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23011)
*Zhe Jin,Zhimin Lin,Bin Zheng,Junhua Fang,Huihua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAGC（Density-Aware Graph Construction），一个无需训练的图构建方法，通过解耦查询无关的粗粒度检索索引与细粒度证据空间，在长视频RAG中仅保留40-50%的图节点，实现1.3-1.7倍端到端加速，同时保持约99%的QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的RAG系统在构建检索索引时继承了视频分割的固定时间粒度，这使索引粒度与证据粒度不必要地耦合。粗粒度表示通常足以定位相关时间区域，但下游推理仍需要细粒度证据，因此需要解耦这两者以提升效率。

Method: DAGC通过合并视觉冗余的相邻块构建紧凑的自适应密度图索引，保留到原始时间单元的映射。检索到的粗粒度区域再展开回原始块粒度用于细粒度证据精炼和答案生成。该方法无需训练，将查询无关的粗检索索引与原始细粒度证据空间解耦。

Result: 在MLVU、VideoMME和LongVideoBench上，DAGC仅保留约40-50%的原始图节点，实现1.3-1.7倍端到端墙钟加速，同时保持约99%的原始QA性能。增益可迁移到不同的LVLM主干和视频RAG流程。

Conclusion: 长视频RAG无需在索引和证据推理中保持相同的时间粒度，解耦索引粒度与证据粒度可以在几乎不损失性能的情况下显著提升效率。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides a scalable paradigm for long-video understanding, but existing systems typically inherit a fixed temporal granularity from video segmentation when constructing their retrieval index. We argue that this design unnecessarily couples indexing granularity with evidence granularity: coarse representations can often suffice for locating relevant temporal regions, while fine-grained evidence remains important for downstream reasoning. We propose \textbf{Density-Aware Graph Construction (DAGC)}, a training-free approach that decouples a query-independent coarse retrieval index from the original fine-grained evidence space. DAGC constructs a compact, density-adaptive graph index by merging visually redundant neighboring chunks, while preserving mappings to the original temporal units. Retrieved coarse regions are subsequently expanded back to the original chunk granularity for fine-grained evidence refinement and answer generation. Experiments on MLVU, VideoMME, and LongVideoBench show that DAGC retains only about 40--50\% of the original graph nodes and achieves $1.3$--$1.7\times$ end-to-end wall-clock acceleration while preserving approximately 99\% of the original QA performance. The gains transfer across different LVLM backbones and video RAG pipelines, suggesting that long-video RAG need not maintain the same temporal granularity for indexing and evidence reasoning.

</details>


### [141] [Misanthrope: A Privacy-Preserving Keypoint Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23012)
*Francesco Vultaggio,Predrag Djindjic,Markus Gerke,Sebastian Tschiatschek,Phillipp Fanta-Jende*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Misanthrope，一种通过自蒸馏训练的隐私保护关键点检测器，避免在人体上检测关键点，从而从源头缓解针对局部图像特征的倒置攻击（inversion attacks），保护场景中的隐私敏感内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像特征容易受到倒置攻击，攻击者可借此重建场景中的隐私敏感内容（尤其是人物）。在分布式计算场景中，特征离开边缘设备交由远程服务器处理，加剧了隐私风险。现有方法多为事后混淆（post-hoc obfuscation），作者希望在源头规避。

Method: 通过自蒸馏（self-distillation）训练一个隐私保护的关键点检测器，使其学会不在人物区域检测关键点，从而在特征提取阶段就避免捕获人物相关隐私信息，而非事后对特征进行后处理。

Result: Misanthrope 能有效缓解针对人物的重识别攻击，同时保持与最先进方法相当的图像匹配性能，在人物作为干扰物的困难场景（如 phototourism 和 in-the-wild odometry）甚至超越现有方法。在 IMC 2021 Phototourism 测试集 9 个场景中的 7 个上取得最佳稀疏特征提取器表现。

Conclusion: Misanthrope 证明了通过在源头避免检测人体关键点，可以在不牺牲匹配性能的情况下有效缓解隐私泄露风险，为分布式场景中的隐私保护视觉定位提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Image matching is a core component of applications such as Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), Visual Localization, and Structure from Motion (SfM). However, the local image features central to this task are vulnerable to inversion attacks, which enable adversaries to reconstruct privacy-sensitive scene content from local features. These attacks pose a particular threat in distributed computing scenarios where the pre-computed features leave edge devices to be processed by remote servers. In this work, we introduce Misanthrope, a novel privacy-preserving keypoint detector trained through self-distillation to avoid detecting keypoints on people---a predominant source of privacy-sensitive content in most localization scenarios---thus mitigating inversion attacks at the source rather than through post-hoc obfuscation. We demonstrate how inverted images from traditional feature detection pipelines can be used to detect and re-identify people in the scene, while Misanthrope is able to mitigate these attacks. Furthermore, Misanthrope maintains image matching performance on par with the state of the art and even surpasses it in challenging settings where people act as distractors, such as phototourism and in-the-wild odometry. On the Image Matching Challenge 2021 Phototourism test set, Misanthrope is the top-performing sparse feature extractor in 7 out of 9 scenes. We make our model and its evaluation script available here: https://github.com/fratopa/misanthrope

</details>


### [142] [AnaDiffusion: Anatomically CompositionalLatent Diffusion for Controllable 3D Brain MRI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23014)
*Huiwen Han,Lulin Liu,Bangya Liu,Yuanhao Cai,Nuo Chen,Xiaoqing Wang,Ziqian Xie,Chenyu You,Shuiwang Ji,Degui Zhi,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnaDiffusion是一个解剖学组合式潜扩散框架，将3D脑MRI生成分解为不同解剖区域分别建模，再通过部分到整体的组装和全局去噪精化，生成既包含显式部位资产又保持全局一致性的完整脑部体积，支持无需推理时密集分割图的局部可控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D脑部MRI生成模型通常整体合成3D体积，忽略了局部解剖结构，缺乏区域级可控性。作者希望突破这一局限，实现解剖学上有意义、可局部编辑的脑部生成。

Method: 采用解剖组合式潜扩散框架：首先训练多个部位扩散模型捕获局部结构先验；然后将组装好的解剖组合注入全脑潜表示中继续去噪，使模型在解决全局上下文的同时保留注入的解剖结构，实现部分到整体的组装和全局精化。

Result: 在ADNI测试集上，AnaDiffusion在整脑、左/右半球、小脑-脑干复合体和接缝区域均取得最低FID；在小脑、脑室和脑干上取得最佳或次佳的绝对Cohen's d值。局部编辑实验中的配对MS-SSIM显示高目标迁移率和低非目标改变。

Conclusion: AnaDiffusion通过解剖组合式生成实现了更好的全局一致性、区域质量和局部可控编辑能力，无需推理时稠密分割图即可进行部位替换，同时最小化非目标区域改动。

Abstract: 3D brain MRI generation has made significant advances in medical imaging, simulation, and controllable anatomical analysis. However, existing generative models typically synthesize 3D volumes monolithically, often overlooking regional anatomical structures and limiting local controllability. To address these limitations, we introduce AnaDiffusion, an anatomically compositional latent diffusion framework that factorizes the generation process into distinct, anatomically meaningful regions, followed by part-to-whole assembly and global refinement. Our approach first trains part diffusion models to capture local structural priors. We then inject an assembled anatomical composite of the parts into the whole-brain latent representation and continue denoising. This mechanism enables the model to resolve global context while preserving the injected anatomy. As a result, AnaDiffusion produces both explicit part assets and a globally coherent volume, thereby enabling controllable part editing without requiring subject-specific dense segmentation maps at inference time while maintaining consistent part-to-whole brain structure. On the subject-disjoint ADNI test split, AnaDiffusion achieves the lowest FID across the whole brain, left and right hemispheres, cerebellar-brainstem complex, and seam regions. It also achieves the best cerebellar and second-best ventricular and brainstem absolute Cohen's d values among the evaluated methods. In localized editing experiments, paired MS-SSIM demonstrates high target transfer and off-target preservation, supporting controllable part replacement with minimal unintended anatomical alterations.

</details>


### [143] [When the Edit Changes the Patient: Measuring Identity Preservation in Counterfactual Retinal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23024)
*Andrea Posada,Wenke Karbole,Bach Ngoc Doan,Alexander Weers,Solmaz Abdolrahimzadeh,Maria Patsiamanidi,Kahkashan Haider,Vaishali Khare,Daniel Rueckert,Andrew Lotery,Sobha Sivaprasad,Martin J. Menten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了医学图像反事实生成任务中身份保持（identity preservation）这一关键属性。作者针对三类文本条件编辑方法（源锚定、结构化提示、配对训练）在视网膜OCT图像上使用裁判分类器、嵌入对齐分数和盲法读者研究进行了显式测量，发现所有方法都能生成高质量图像且编辑成功率相当，但身份保持差异显著：源锚定方法频繁改变被摄主体，配对训练方法保存最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学反事实图像生成方法大多复用通用图像编辑技术，默认假设将生成锚定到源图像即可隐式保持身份信息，但这一假设几乎没有被检验过。在视网膜OCT等领域中生物特征线索极为微妙，身份保持可能失败，因此需要显式测量和验证。

Method: 对三类文本条件编辑方法（source-anchored、structured-prompt、paired-training）在视网膜OCT图像上进行系统评估。使用三种评估手段：裁判分类器（referee classifiers）判断被摄主体是否改变、嵌入对齐分数（embedding alignment scores）定量衡量身份一致性、盲法读者研究（blind reader study）进行临床视角的验证。同时评估图像质量和编辑成功率作为对比基线。

Result: 所有方法在视网膜OCT图像生成上都产生了高质量结果且编辑成功率相当，但身份保持表现差异显著：source-anchored编辑方法频繁改变被摄主体，paired-training方法在身份保持上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 医学反事实生成领域未来的工作必须将身份保持作为与图像真实感和编辑成功率同等重要的指标进行显式测量和报告，不能再依赖隐式假设。

Abstract: Counterfactual medical image generation aims to modify an existing image to reflect a hypothetical scenario in which certain characteristics of the imaged subject are altered, while keeping their identity fixed. Most existing works repurpose established image editing methods, which do not directly supervise identity preservation. Instead, they assume that identity is implicitly preserved by anchoring generation to the source image. This assumption is rarely tested and may fail in domains where biometric cues are subtle, such as retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT). In this work, we explicitly measure identity preservation for three groups of text-conditioned editing methods - source-anchored, structured-prompt, and paired-training - using referee classifiers, embedding alignment scores, and a blind reader study. We find that all methods produce high-quality OCT images with comparable editing success, yet their identity preservation differs markedly. Source-anchored editing frequently alters the depicted subject, while paired-training preserves it best. We argue that future work on medical counterfactual generation must explicitly measure and report identity preservation alongside image realism and editing success.

</details>


### [144] [Cultural Moment Benchmark: Evaluating Video Cultural Reasoning and Grounding in Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23065)
*Burak Satar,Zhixin Ma,Cheng Yu-Tong,Huy Hoang Tran,Phuong Anh Nguyen,Chong-Wah Ngo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出文化时刻基准（CMB），将视频中的文化理解分解为三个能力：命名文化概念、在视频中视觉识别概念、定位子事件的时间位置。该基准包含来自东南亚七国的306个专家策展概念、五大类别，通过三个阶段分别测试每种能力，并采用语义相似干扰项、未标注视频时刻、和不同示例视频的自由定位等设计来隔离各能力。对六个视觉-语言模型的评估显示，三阶段全对的得分普遍低于30%，三种能力并非完全级联，音频的作用因概念而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频文化基准将"what is seen"的能力坍缩为一个单一分数，无法揭示模型在命名、识别、时间定位三种不同能力中的具体瓶颈。作者希望通过分解这三种能力并分别评估，构建一个能归因失败原因的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建CMB基准：306个专家策展的文化概念来自东南亚七国、五类。每个概念通过三阶段评估：S1从四个候选名中选择概念名；S2从四个候选视频时刻中选择正确时刻；S3在另一个示例视频上预测时刻的起止时间。采用三种设计选择确保每个阶段聚焦单一能力：语义相似干扰项（S1/S2）、未标注视频时刻（S2）、不同示例视频上的自由定位（S3）。论文还设计了人工研究（14名评分者）对比专家与普通人的表现。

Result: 最强闭源模型在三阶段全对时得分低于30%；三种能力不完全级联——正确命名概念能帮助一半模型在视频上识别，但识别后对时间定位帮助甚微；音频具有概念依赖性——互补、冗余或干扰作用，且在非拉丁文国家更多造成干扰；移除音频和字幕对"游戏"和"音乐"类影响最大；专家评分者在邻国文化概念上的得分低于随机水平，表明CMB需要特定的国家文化知识。

Conclusion: CMB作为诊断性测试台，可将失败归因到特定能力或模态。传统单一分数会掩盖瓶颈，分解三阶段评估能更精确地揭示多模态模型在文化视频理解上的不足，并指出音频、字幕等模态在文化理解中的复杂作用。

Abstract: Cultural understanding in video means more than recognizing what is visible; it requires grasping the symbolic and temporal significance of cultural concepts. We decompose this into three abilities: naming what a concept symbolizes, visually recognizing it on video, and locating its sub-events in time. Existing video-cultural benchmarks tend to test what is seen, collapsing these three abilities into a single score that hides the bottleneck. We introduce the Cultural Moment Benchmark (CMB): 306 expert-curated concepts from seven countries in Southeast Asia across five categories. We evaluate each concept through three stages, one per ability. Given a description, Stage 1 (S1) selects from four candidate concept names, Stage 2 (S2) selects from four candidate video moments, and Stage 3 (S3) predicts the start and end times of the moment in a video. To keep each stage focused on a distinct ability, we use three design choices: semantic-similarity distractors (S1, S2), unlabeled video moments (S2), and free-form localization on a different example video (S3). Across six vision-language models, failure modes vary by ability and modality. i) Even the strongest closed-source models score below 30% when all three stages must be correct; ii) The three abilities do not fully cascade: naming a concept correctly helps half the models recognize it on video, but recognizing it has little effect on locating the sub-event in time; iii) Audio is complementary, redundant, or distracting depending on the concept, more often distracting in non-Latin-script countries; removing both audio and subtitles hurts Games and Music the most. Our 14-rater human study shows that even Expert raters score below chance on concepts from a neighboring country, indicating that CMB requires country-specific cultural knowledge. CMB acts as a diagnostic harness, attributing failures to a specific ability or modality.

</details>


### [145] [Grounding Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Need Object Localization for Spatial Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23074)
*Xiwei Liu,Yulong Li,Xinlin Zhuang,Xuhui Li,Zhixiang Lu,Haolin Yang,Imran Razzak,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用多种机制可解释性工具（token消融、逐层探测、注意力头剔除、因果中介分析）系统研究了视觉语言模型（LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL）如何将物体定位转化为空间关系推理。发现空间关系预测遵循分阶段的"定位→推理"过程：物体对齐token建立粗略的锚点参考，不需要精确的边界框；位置信息在关系决策之前就可解码；少量注意力头中介了定位与空间推理的因果效应；两个任务共享早期定位相关处理但依赖部分不同的专门通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型能回答空间问题，但物体定位与空间推理之间的内部机制连接机制尚不清楚。作者希望探究空间推理是否内部需要精确的物体定位，还是可以通过全局布局线索绕过显式定位，从而理解VLMs如何将"物体在哪里"转化为"物体如何关联"。

Method: 对两个代表性VLM家族（LLaVA-1.5和Qwen2.5-VL）使用一套机制可解释性工具套件，包括：(1) token消融（token ablation）测试哪些token对空间推理至关重要；(2) 逐层探测（layer-wise probing）分析位置信息在各层何时变得可解码；(3) 注意力头剔除（attention knockout）识别关键注意力头；(4) 因果中介分析（causal mediation analysis）确定定位与空间推理之间的因果通路。实验在token、层、注意力头三个粒度上分析处理过程。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 空间关系预测遵循分阶段的定位→推理过程，物体对齐token建立粗略的目标-参考锚点，精确的边界框边界并非必需；(2) 位置信息在关系决策出现之前就已可解码；(3) 少量注意力头中介了定位与空间推理两者的因果效应；(4) 定位与空间推理共享早期处理但最终依赖部分不同的专门通路。核心结论："知道物体在哪里"不等于"知道它们如何关联"。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了token、层、注意力头级别的VLMs空间推理机制细粒度分析，揭示了空间推理可以绕开精确物体定位，通过粗略锚点和全局布局线索实现。这为理解VLM内部计算结构以及改进空间推理能力提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer spatial questions, yet the mechanisms connecting object grounding to spatial reasoning remain poorly understood. It is underexplored whether spatial reasoning internally requires precise objects localization, or can bypass explicit localization through global layout cues. In this work, we investigate two representative model families, LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL, using a suite of mechanistic interpretability tools, including token ablation, layer-wise probing, attention knockout, and causal mediation analysis. We find that spatial relation prediction follows a staged grounding-to-reasoning process in which object-aligned tokens establish coarse target-reference anchors, while precise bounding-box boundaries are not required. Positional information becomes decodable before relation decisions emerge, and a small set of attention heads mediates the causal effects of both localization and spatial reasoning. The two tasks share early grounding-related processing but ultimately rely on partially distinct specialized pathways. Through rigorous experiments, we provide a token-, layer-, and head-level account of how VLMs transform object grounding into spatial relations, showing that knowing where objects are is not equivalent to knowing how they relate.

</details>


### [146] [Loopy: Seamless Video Loop Generation via Anchored Looping Shift of Positional Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23090)
*Haotian Dong,Wenjing Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Xin Wang,Di Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次揭示DiT中不同注意力层的位置嵌入对时间顺序的控制程度不同，其中最显著的一层作为"锚点层"。基于此提出锚点位置嵌入移位策略（将各层时间感知从直线变为圆形），开发了通用框架Loopy用于高质量循环视频生成，支持RGB和RGBA视频，并支持identity控制与风格迁移等AIGC功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环视频在网页图形、游戏开发、社交媒体等实际应用中至关重要，但现有方法因忽视了视频生成模型如何感知时间顺序及其与循环行为的关系，难以生成高质量的循环视频。

Method: 1) 首次揭示DiT中不同注意力层的位置嵌入具有不同级别的时序控制力，最显著层作为锚点层，为其余层提供强上下文先验。2) 提出锚点位置嵌入移位策略，根据不同层的时间控制效果应用层特定的移位长度，将DiT的时间感知从直线转变为圆形。3) 构建通用框架Loopy，支持RGB和RGBA视频，并集成identity控制和风格转移等高级AIGC功能。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升了生成循环视频的时间一致性和视觉保真度。模型已开源发布。

Conclusion: 通过理解DiT各层位置嵌入的时间控制特性并利用锚点层进行策略性移位，可以有效地将扩散模型的时序感知从线性变为循环，实现更高质量的循环视频生成，同时框架具备良好的通用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Looping videos are essential for practical applications such as web graphics, game development, and social media. However, existing approaches typically fail to generate high-quality looping videos due to the neglect of how video generation models perceive temporal order and how this relates to the looping behavior. In this work, we are the first to reveal that position embedding at different attention layers within DiT exhibits varying levels of positional control, with the most pronounced layer acting as an anchor. We formulate this anchored layer as the reference point of the looping video, offering strong contextual priors for the remaining layers to facilitate the generation of seamless and coherent video content. Based on this insight, we propose an anchored position embedding shifting strategy that applies layer-specific shift lengths according to each layer's temporal control effect, effectively transforming DiT's temporal perception from a straight line to a circle. Leveraging this strategy, we develop a general framework, Loopy, for high-quality looping video generation, supporting both RGB and RGBA videos, while also enabling advanced AIGC features such as identity control and style transfer. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves temporal consistency and visual fidelity in generated looping videos. The released model is available on our website: https://donghaotian123.github.io/Loopy.

</details>


### [147] [Training-Free Pseudo-Fusion for Composed Image Retrieval with Diffusion Models and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23102)
*Fan Xu,Luis A. Leiva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PeFuse，一个无需训练的零样本组合图像检索（CIR）框架，利用预训练扩散模型和多模态大语言模型，通过单向/双向生成式转换将CIR转化为四个单模态检索问题，绕开任务特定训练，在标准基准上取得具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CIR方法依赖多模态融合训练来对齐组合查询与检索目标，需要任务特定的训练模块。本文旨在探索无需训练、即插即用的伪融合方案，利用现有生成式模型的能力来桥接视觉与文本模态。

Method: 提出PeFuse训练无关框架，核心是uni-directional和bi-directional两种生成式转换策略，将CIR拆解为四个单模态检索子问题（文本到图像、图像到图像、图像到文本等），利用预训练扩散模型和MLLM完成模态间转换，检索模块可替换。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，将CIR转化为文本到图像检索任务比其他转换方式更有效，达到与现有方法相当或更优的性能，同时因管线组件可替换而具有高度灵活性。

Conclusion: 伪融合范式（Pseudo-fusion）对零样本CIR是有效的，证明了无需任务特定训练、仅依赖预训练生成模型的CIR可行性。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is an emerging paradigm in content-based image retrieval that enables users to formulate compositional queries by combining a reference image with an auxiliary modality, usually text-based. This approach supports fine-grained search where the target image shares structural elements with the user-provided image while incorporating the modifications specified by the auxiliary text. Conventional CIR methods rely on multimodal fusion to combine visual and textual features into a joint query embedding, which requires training modules that align composed queries with the targets. In this work, we propose PeFuse (for pseudo-fusion), a training-free framework that leverages pretrained Diffusion Models and Multimodal Large Language Models to bridge modalities via generative conversion. We introduce two novel strategies: uni-directional and bi-directional conversion, which convert CIR into four single-modality retrieval problems. These methods reformulate CIR as either intra-modal or cross-modal single-query retrieval tasks, bypassing the need for dedicated task-specific training. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that converting CIR into text-to-image retrieval tasks is more effective than alternative conversion strategies, achieving competitive or superior performance compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining high flexibility thanks to replaceable components of the conversion pipeline. These results highlight the effectiveness of the pseudo-fusion paradigm for zero-shot CIR. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/StevenXuf/PeFuse4CIR.

</details>


### [148] [Bridge Damage Detection from Low-Light UAV Imagery via Degradation-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23136)
*Hu Wang,Hongxu Pu,Zhiqi Hu,Fangzhou Lin,Wang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DaL-MoE，一个检测器无关的低光图像恢复前端模块，结合ISP感知的低光合成流水线和退化感知的专家混合（噪声抑制、色彩调整、结构细节恢复），用于提升无人机桥梁图像中的缺陷检测性能，并验证了从合成退化到真实检测场景的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机桥梁巡检在低光照条件下，小型低对比度缺陷会被遮挡，降低自动检测的可靠性和操作灵活性。现有方法缺乏针对低光条件下缺陷检测的恢复-检测联合方案，且真实低光数据缺乏配对的正常光照参考，难以训练。

Method: 提出DaL-MoE：1) ISP感知的低光合成流水线生成配对训练数据；2) 退化感知引导估计模块预测退化类型；3) 互补专家网络分别处理噪声抑制、色彩调整和结构细节恢复；4) 作为检测器无关的前端模块，可与任意检测器（如YOLOv11m）组合。

Result: 在配对合成数据上PSNR达23.12 dB、SSIM达0.8482；将YOLOv11m的box mAP50从0.3097提升至0.4923，mask mAP50从0.2281提升至0.3529。在无配对参考的真实低光无人机图像上，sim-to-real评估显示缺陷可见性显著提升，检测更完整。

Conclusion: 退化感知的图像恢复前端能有效提升低光条件下无人机桥梁缺陷检测性能，并具备从合成退化到真实场景的迁移能力。未来工作将开发低光感知的专用桥梁缺陷检测器以增强跨场景泛化。

Abstract: Poor illumination obscures small, low-contrast defects in UAV bridge imagery, reducing the reliability and operational flexibility of automated inspection. This paper investigates whether degradation-aware image restoration can improve bridge damage detection under low-light conditions and transfer from synthetic degradations to real inspection scenes. We propose DaL- MoE, a detector-agnostic restoration front end trained with an ISP-aware low-light synthesis pipeline and equipped with degradation-aware guidance estimation and complementary experts for noise suppression, color adjustment, and structural-detail recovery. On paired synthetic data, DaL-MoE achieves 23.12 dB PSNR and 0.8482 SSIM, increasing YOLOv11m box mAP50 from 0.3097 to 0.4923 and mask mAP50 from 0.2281 to 0.3529. On real low-light UAV imagery without paired normal-light references, sim-to-real evaluation shows improved defect visibility and more complete detections than direct inference on raw low-light inputs. Future work will develop low-light-aware bridge damage detectors with stronger cross-scene generalization across bridge sites, imaging conditions, and illumination levels.

</details>


### [149] [A Simulator-Grounded Framework For Constructing Verifiable Muscle-Grounded QA From 3D Tongue Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23137)
*Seungho Eum,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于仿真器构建数据集的框架3DTongueQA，将ArtiSynth Badin有限元模型映射的11维肌肉激活转化为结构化生物力学记录，并生成确定性的QA数据（每语言891,156条）。通过语言自然化验证跨语言可移植性（英语/韩语），并提供SpiralNet++--Qwen3-8B基线实验，展示结构化预测与自然语言QA均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发音运动学语料库（实时MRI、电磁发音描记术）能捕捉舌形和运动，但无法提供生成观测构型的肌肉驱动过程的可追溯标签。需要一种仿真器驱动的数据构建方法来建立肌肉状态与几何结构之间的可标注映射。

Method: 将11维受控肌肉激活映射到固定拓扑舌网格（ArtiSynth Badin有限元模型），转换为结构化生物力学记录，渲染为关于肌肉状态、几何形态和目标导向变化的确定性QA。筛选295,157个构型，保留295,115个有效网格，每语言构建891,156条QA记录。语言自然化仅改变表面形式并对照源记录验证，采用SpiralNet++与Qwen3-8B作为可替换基线。

Result: 基线达到62.9±9.2 Muscle EM、74.0±0.2 Value Accuracy、65.9±4.7 Direction EM；网格错配使Muscle EM降至2.2；锚点留出模型保留全库存分数80.4-98.6%；任务特定结构读出达到88.7±0.7 Muscle EM和93.3±1.0 Direction EM。

Conclusion: 构建的监督信号同时支持高效结构化预测和异构自然语言QA，不依赖于特定解码器架构，验证了框架的通用性和跨语言可移植性。

Abstract: Existing articulatory corpora based on real-time MRI and electromagnetic articulography capture tongue shape and motion but do not provide traceable labels for the muscle-driven process that generated an observed configuration. We introduce a simulator-grounded data-construction framework and instantiate it as 3DTongueQA. Controlled 11-dimensional muscle activations are mapped to fixed-topology tongue meshes with the ArtiSynth Badin finite-element model, converted into structured biomechanical records, and rendered as deterministic QA on muscle state, geometry, and target-directed change. We screen 295,157 configurations, retain 295,115 valid meshes, and construct 891,156 QA records per language. Language naturalization changes only surface form and is verified against the source records; English and Korean instantiations demonstrate construction-level portability. A swappable SpiralNet++--Qwen3-8B baseline reaches 62.9 $\pm$ 9.2 Muscle EM, 74.0 $\pm$ 0.2 Value Accuracy, and 65.9 $\pm$ 4.7 Direction EM, while mismatching the paired mesh reduces Muscle EM to 2.2; a dataset-leakage-controlled anchor-held-out model retains 80.4--98.6\% of the full-inventory scores on unseen anchors. Task-specific structured readouts further reach 88.7 $\pm$ 0.7 Muscle EM and 93.3 $\pm$ 1.0 Direction EM. These complementary results show that the constructed supervision supports both efficient structured prediction and heterogeneous natural-language QA rather than being tied to a particular decoder architecture.

</details>


### [150] [How Merge-Tolerant Are Vision Transformers for Wheat Phenotyping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23142)
*Simon Ravé,Pejman Rasti,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种免训练token合并技术（ToMe和Mutual Pair Merging）在基于视觉的小麦表型分析任务（生长阶段分类、麦穗检测、麦穗器官分割）中的表现，测量了任务质量、吞吐量、token数量和峰值GPU内存，并补充了树莓派5上的边缘推理测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通ViT虽然为小麦表型分析的多项任务提供了统一架构，但二次复杂度的注意力机制限制了高通量和边缘推理性能。免训练token合并方法可以无需重训练地插入已有模型，因此有必要系统评估其在农业视觉任务中的实际价值。

Method: 对ToMe和Mutual Pair Merging进行了系统基准测试，涵盖生长阶段分类、麦穗检测和麦穗器官分割三类任务；测量任务质量、吞吐量、token数与峰值GPU内存，并额外在Raspberry Pi 5上做边缘部署测量。

Result: 分类任务对token合并高度容忍，而检测和分割任务受重复实例、细薄器官、密集边界、重建需求及运行时开销限制。优化的注意力后端可能抵消表面上的加速效果，最终部署价值必须依据目标运行时的实测而非token数推断。

Conclusion: token合并的部署收益高度依赖具体任务和运行时环境，需针对目标硬件进行实际性能剖析，而不能仅凭token削减量判断加速效果。

Abstract: Vision-based wheat phenotyping requires repeated measurements under deployment constraints, from growth-stage recognition to wheat-head counting and organ segmentation. Plain Vision Transformers (ViTs) provide a common architecture for these tasks, but quadratic attention limits high-throughput and edge inference. Training-free token merging is attractive because it can be inserted into trained models without retraining. We provide a systematic benchmark of ToMe and Mutual Pair Merging across growth-stage classification, wheat-head detection, and wheat-organ segmentation, measuring task quality, throughput, token count, and peak GPU memory, with additional Raspberry Pi 5 measurements. The benchmark reveals a clear hierarchy: classification is highly merge-tolerant, while detection and segmentation are constrained by repeated instances, thin organs, dense boundaries, reconstruction, and runtime overhead. Optimized attention backends can erase apparent speedups, so deployment value must be profiled on the target runtime rather than inferred from token count.

</details>


### [151] [An end-to-end-trained vision-language model for native-language prostate pathology report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23143)
*Christian Grashei,Fabian Gülhan,Maximilian Legnar,Fabian Stögbauer,Cleo-Aron Weis,Carolin Mogler,Peter Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语言无关的前列腺活检报告生成框架，从零训练tokenizer和模型（以德语演示），并利用本地部署的LLM自动将复合报告拆分为核心特异的图像-文本对，仅从2,402例历史病例获得17,344对训练数据。模型在恶性检测上F1达96.2%，Gleason分级达65.2%，并使用潜在空间增强在三个外部队列上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌是全球常见恶性肿瘤，结构化报告活检核心对病理医生负担沉重。现有工具将问题框架化为分类任务，仍需要医生自行组装报告；且许多切片级视觉-语言模型依赖英语为中心的编码器，跨语言迁移效果差。需要一种语言无关的端到端报告生成框架。

Method: 从零训练tokenizer和视觉-语言模型（演示语言为德语），实现语言无关性。使用本地部署的大语言模型将历史复合病理报告自动拆分为各活检核心对应的图像-文本对，无需人工标注，从2,402例获得17,344对。模型评估聚焦临床属性而非语言相似度，并通过潜在空间增强在外部队列上验证分级性能。

Result: 恶性检测F1达96.2%，Gleason分级F1达65.2%，与FDA批准的临床分类器相当。在三个外部队列上通过潜在空间增强进一步验证了分级性能。

Conclusion: 机构可以利用自身档案训练母语报告生成模型，无需依赖英语为中心的基础模型或大规模人工标注，实现语言无关的多语言病理报告自动化。

Abstract: Prostate cancer is among the most frequently diagnosed malignancies worldwide, and structured reporting of each biopsy core burdens pathologists. Existing tools frame this as classification, leaving pathologists to assemble coherent reports, while many slide-level vision-language models rely on English-centric encoders that transfer poorly to other clinical languages. We present a slide-level framework generating prostate biopsy reports that is language-independent by construction: tokenizer and model are trained from scratch, demonstrated here in German. To address paired-data scarcity, an automated pipeline uses a locally deployed large language model to split composite reports into core-specific image-text pairs, yielding 17,344 pairs from 2,402 historical cases without manual annotation. Evaluated for clinical attributes rather than linguistic similarity, the model achieves 96.2% F1 for malignancy detection and 65.2% for Gleason grading, competitive with an FDA-cleared classifier. Grading is further validated on three external cohorts with latent-space augmentation. Institutions can thus train native-language reporting models on their own archives.

</details>


### [152] [BenthicFlow: Generating Extensible Underwater Environments via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23173)
*Joaquín Figueira,Camile Lendering,Manfred Gonzalez-Hernandez,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Erkut Akdag,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BenthicFlow是一个基于单一条件流匹配模型的统一框架，可联合生成对齐的水下海底纹理和深度图，通过MultiDiffusion启发的采样过程在生成轨迹中协调重叠窗口，实现空间可扩展的RGBD马赛克而无需单独的拼接模型，最终通过表面对齐的高斯surfel将生成的马赛克提升为显式3D海底环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下3D场景理解面临高质量3D数据匮乏和表面训练模型泛化能力不足的挑战。现有方法通过后验拼接独立生成的瓦片来组装大场景，需要单独训练的模型，且仅能展示单个勘察场地内的异质景观，缺乏统一、端到端可扩展的生成方案。

Method: 提出统一框架BenthicFlow，基于单个条件流匹配模型联合生成对齐的纹理和深度图。采用MultiDiffusion启发的采样程序在生成轨迹中协调重叠窗口，实现无需拼接模型的空间可扩展RGBD马赛克。生成的马赛克进一步通过表面对齐的高斯surfel（surface-aligned Gaussian surfels）转化为显式3D海底环境。

Result: 在地理上不同的勘察场地上的实验表明，BenthicFlow能保持特定场地的外观特征，同时生成连贯、大规模且紧密匹配目标分布的3D场景。代码和训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: BenthicFlow证明了一个统一的生成模型能够在无需额外拼接的情况下生成大规模连贯的水下3D场景，为水下环境3D场景理解提供了可行路径，并在跨场地泛化方面展现了良好效果。

Abstract: Computer vision applications for 3D scene understanding in underwater environments remain challenging due to the lack of high-quality 3D data and the inability of surface-trained models to generalize to underwater scenes. To address this challenge, an emerging trend is to employ generative models to close the data domain gap. However, existing methods assemble large scenes by stitching independently generated tiles post hoc with separately trained models, while demonstrating heterogeneous landscapes only within individual survey sites. We introduce BenthicFlow, a unified framework based on a single conditional flow-matching model that jointly generates aligned textures and depth maps. A MultiDiffusion-inspired sampling procedure reconciles overlapping windows throughout the generative trajectory, enabling spatially extensible RGBD mosaics without a separate stitching model. The generated mosaics are subsequently lifted into explicit 3D benthic environments using surface-aligned Gaussian surfels. Experiments across geographically distinct survey sites demonstrate that BenthicFlow preserves site-specific appearance while generating coherent, large-scale 3D scenes that closely match the target distributions. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/jacomof/BenthicFlow.

</details>


### [153] [Neighbor-Aware View Synthesis for Restoring Missing Views in Light-Field Camera Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23175)
*Sakshi Goel,Ayush Goyal,K S Venkatesh,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对光场相机阵列中相机故障导致子孔径图像缺失的问题，提出基于条件GAN的生成式框架，利用精心挑选的相邻子集相机图像和位置编码图来合成缺失视角，实现几何一致的重建。在合成和真实光场数据集上验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场成像系统中硬件故障（相机阵列中单个或多个相机失效）会导致子孔径图像缺失，严重降低后续重建质量。需要一个容错机制来从剩余相机中恢复缺失视角，保证系统鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）框架：从故障相机阵列中仔细选择一组相邻相机图像作为输入，配合包含相机位置和目标视角信息的位置编码图，训练cGAN以几何一致的方式合成缺失的目标视角。

Result: 在合成和真实世界光场数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能产生视觉上合理且光度精确的重建，在定量和定性指标上均优于基线视角插值方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为容错的光场图像采集提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案，有效恢复故障相机造成的缺失视角。

Abstract: In light-field (LF) imaging systems, dense spatial sampling from a camera array enables powerful post-capture capabilities such as refocusing and depth estimation. However, real-world LF capture is often affected by hardware malfunctions, where one or more cameras in the array fail, leading to missing sub-aperture images and degraded reconstruction quality. This paper addresses the problem of defective or missing view restoration in light-field camera arrays. We propose a novel generative framework that synthesizes the absent views by exploiting information from a carefully selected subset of neighboring cameras. These selected images, along with a positional encoding map indicating both their locations and the desired target view, are fed into a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) trained to generate the missing viewpoint in a geometrically consistent manner. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world LF datasets demonstrate that our method produces visually plausible and photometrically accurate reconstructions, outperforming baselines for view interpolation both quantitatively and qualitatively. The proposed framework thus offers a robust and efficient solution for fault-tolerant light-field image acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [EchoWM: Open and Enterable Omnimodal World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23189)
*Songchun Zhang,Yaowei Li,Junhao Zhuang,Weiyang Jin,Haoyu Wang,Xin Lu,Yilang Sun,Shiyi Zhang,Haoran Li,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yuming Li,Yijun Liu,Yaofeng Su,Yanwen Ma,Haoyu Wu,Zihan Su,Yue Ma,Lvmin Zhang,Haoyang Huang,Zeyue Xue,Anyi Rao,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoWM是一个统一的多模态世界模型，能够响应用户连续导航指令，同时联合生成720p视频、环境音、音乐和语音。它将离散命令和连续姿态映射到统一的公制尺度6-DoF相对轨迹上，通过互补数据引擎和渐进训练+自回归后训练实现长时程生成，在公开世界模型基准上表现出强大的轨迹跟随能力和高质量的音频-视频联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式媒体和世界模型通常只处理单一模态（如仅视频），并且缺乏可进入的交互式导航能力。作者希望构建一个"可进入的"生成媒体系统，让用户能够连续导航并同时获得视觉、环境音、音乐和语音的多模态沉浸式体验。

Method: 1) 以相机意图为核心组织交互：第一人称场景指定观察者运动，第三人称场景从数据中学习相机-角色动力学而不需要视角特定控制器。2) 将离散命令和连续姿态映射到统一公制尺度相对6-DoF轨迹，通过数据集级校准保留异构数据间的运动幅度。3) 构建互补数据引擎用于联合学习音视频生成与轨迹控制。4) 采用渐进训练（progressive training）配合自回归后训练（autoregressive post-training）支持长时程生成。

Result: 在公开世界模型基准上取得了强轨迹跟随能力与高视觉质量，支持第一/第三人称交互及多种主体，在长时程生成中保持环境音和语音的同步性。评价显示EchoWM在轨迹跟随和视觉质量上均表现优异。

Conclusion: EchoWM展示了构建可进入的交互式生成媒体的可行性，统一了视频、音频、语音和轨迹控制四大模块，为omnimodal世界模型的研究提供了一个完整框架。未来可进一步扩展交互复杂度和长时程一致性。

Abstract: We present EchoWM, an omnimodal world model for enterable generative media that responds to continuous navigation while jointly generating 720p video, environmental sound, music and speech. We organize interaction around camera intent: in first-person scenes, it specifies observer motion, while in third-person scenes, camera--character dynamics are learned from data without view-specific controllers. Discrete commands and continuous poses are mapped to a shared metric-scale relative 6-DoF trajectory, with dataset-level calibration preserving motion magnitude across heterogeneous data. To jointly learn audio-visual generation and trajectory control, we construct a complementary data engine and adopt progressive training followed by autoregressive post-training for long-horizon generation. Extensive evaluations show that \model achieves strong trajectory following and high visual quality on public world-model benchmarks, supporting both first- and third-person interaction across varied subjects, and maintaining synchronized environmental sound and speech over long-horizon generation.

</details>


### [155] [Toward a Foundation Plug-and-Play Prior for Computed Tomography Reconstruction via a Multimodal Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23190)
*Haley Duba-Sullivan,Patxi Fernandez-Zelaia,Obaidullah Rahman,Amirkoushyar Ziabari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究能否用单个跨多个成像域训练的扩散模型作为多种CT重建问题的通用先验。使用同一冻结模型在三个不同数据集（增材制造金属零件的锥束X射线CT、混凝土微观结构的平行束中子CT等）上评估，该方法在所有三种场景下均优于解析重建，迈向了可复用的异构CT重建基础先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT吞吐量受扫描时间限制（采集投影数量与探测器积分时间），稀疏视角或低剂量测量需要高质量的图像先验。现有方法通常为特定扫描设置训练一个神经网络，当模态、几何或材料变化时需要重新训练。作者希望探索一个单一的跨域扩散模型能否作为多种CT问题的通用先验，避免针对每个设置重复训练。

Method: 提出使用一个在多个成像域上训练的单一扩散模型作为先验，用于多种CT重建问题。该模型在不同模态（X射线、中子）、不同束几何（锥束、平行束）、不同材料和不同退化类型的数据集上共享同一套冻结权重。

Result: 在三个不同数据集上，使用同一冻结模型进行重建，所有情况下均优于解析重建方法。数据集覆盖增材制造金属零件锥束X射线CT中的缺陷分析，以及混凝土微观结构的平行束中子CT等异构成像场景。

Conclusion: 跨域训练的单一扩散模型可以作为多种异构CT重建问题的有效通用先验，性能优于解析重建，为构建可复用的CT重建基础先验提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) throughput is limited by scan time, which grows with both the number of projections acquired and the detector integration time for each. Reconstructing high-quality volumes from sparse-view or low-dose measurements therefore depends on an informative prior, typically a neural network trained for one specific scan setting and retrained whenever the modality, geometry, or material changes. We investigate whether a single diffusion model trained across several imaging domains can instead serve as a prior for many CT problems simultaneously. We evaluate the proposed method using the same frozen model on three datasets that differ in modality, beam geometry, material, and degradation type, spanning flaw analysis in additively manufactured metal parts imaged with cone-beam X-ray CT and concrete microstructure imaged with parallel-beam neutron CT. Our proposed method out-performs analytic reconstructions in all three cases, providing a step toward a reusable foundation prior for heterogeneous CT reconstruction problems.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Spherical Occupancy Profiles for Multi-View 3D Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23206)
*YiHsuan Tsai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用球面逐射线占据概率轮廓（spherical occupancy profiles）作为多视图3D重建的统一定义表示，同时支持判别式与生成式重建。在999个Google Scanned Objects物体上训练了FiLM条件感知的判别式解码器和基于profile VAE+潜在扩散模型的生成式管线，取得归一化中位深度误差0.035，并在两个DTU真实场景上验证了跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图3D重建中，判别式重建和生成式重建通常使用不同的表示与流程，缺乏统一的中间表示来衔接二者。作者希望找到一个紧凑、可学习、且能表达不确定性的中间表示，既能支持判别式重建也能支撑生成式重建，从而打通多视图重建与生成先验之间的接口。

Method: 从多视图3D高斯重建中蒸馏出球面占据概率轮廓P(r)=T(r)o(r)（透明度×占据度的逐射线概率）。训练两大模块：(i) 判别式逐射线解码器——将全局视角平均与射线特定图像证据注入FiLM条件化的轮廓头；(ii) 生成式管线——基于轮廓VAE加潜在扩散模型，支持无条件采样与图像条件多解重建，并通过classifier-free guidance调节解的散布。此外分析了轮廓形态：后验功率锐化与学习式锐化目标均可恢复真实轮廓宽度而不损失深度精度，揭示了L1逐射线损失族中的单调宽度-峰值前沿。

Result: 在独立90物体测试集上，判别式解码器归一化中位深度误差为0.035；生成式管线支持无条件采样匹配重建流形以及图像条件下可量化、可调的多解重建；两个DTU真实场景验证表明管线可迁移至非合成输入。

Conclusion: 逐射线占据概率轮廓提供了一种紧凑、可学习、且具有不确定性感知的统一接口，连接多视图重建与生成先验，前景广阔。

Abstract: We study spherical occupancy profiles-the ray-wise occupancy probability profiles P(r) = T(r) o(r) distilled from multi-view 3D Gaussian reconstructions-as a unified intermediate representation for both discriminative and generative 3D reconstruction from images. On a 999-object subset of Google Scanned Objects with 48 turntable views each, we train (i) a discriminative per-ray decoder that injects global view-averaged and ray-specific image evidence into a FiLM-conditioned profile head, reaching median soft depth error 0.035 (normalized) on an independent 90-object test split, and (ii) a generative pipeline built on a profile VAE and a latent diffusion model, which supports unconditional sampling that matches the reconstruction manifold and image-conditioned multi-solution reconstruction whose per-object solution spread is quantifiable and tunable via classifier-free guidance. We further analyze the morphology of predicted profiles: post-hoc power sharpening and a learned sharpening target both recover ground-truth profile width without degrading depth, exposing a monotonic width-peak frontier in the L1-per-ray loss family and motivating a principled redefinition of morphology gates. Real-photo validation on two DTU scenes confirms the pipeline transfers to non-synthetic input. Our results suggest that ray-wise occupancy profiles offer a compact, learned, and uncertainty-aware interface between multi-view reconstruction and generative priors.

</details>


### [157] [Bee Detection and Tracking at Hive Entrance using YOLO11 and ByteTrack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23213)
*Thi Thu Thao Nguyen,Johannes Reschke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于YOLO11迁移学习和ByteTrack跟踪算法的蜜蜂蜂巢入口自动监测系统，研究了数据增强、骨干网络冻结和跟踪器参数优化对小型快速移动蜜蜂检测计数的影响。渐进式骨干解冻策略达到约97.0%精度和98.7% mAP50，参数优化后的系统在独立25 FPS侧视视频上正确计数91.5%进入蜜蜂，但仅23.3%外出蜜蜂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂蜂巢入口的自动监测对生态研究和养蜂业管理具有重要意义，但小型快速移动的蜜蜂在实际录制条件下（运动模糊、低置信度检测）难以准确检测和计数，现有方法缺乏针对该场景的系统性优化。

Method: 采用YOLO11进行迁移学习检测，对比不同数据增强策略和骨干网络冻结策略（包括渐进式解冻vs完全微调），结合ByteTrack多目标跟踪算法进行蜜蜂计数，并优化跟踪器参数以改善低置信度检测下的轨迹连续性。在独立25 FPS侧视视频上评估系统性能。

Result: 渐进式骨干解冻策略达到97.0%精度和98.7% mAP50，且收敛更稳定；轻量数据增强优于重度增强。优化后的YOLO11-ByteTrack系统在独立视频上正确计数43/47只进入蜜蜂（91.5%）和7/30只外出蜜蜂（23.3%）。主要误差来源为快速运动和运动模糊导致的漏检，跟踪失败经参数优化后显著减少。

Conclusion: 适度数据增强、渐进式骨干解冻和ByteTrack参数调优能有效提升自动蜜蜂入口监测在真实录制条件下的可靠性，为精准农业/生态监测中的小目标检测跟踪提供了实用方法指导。

Abstract: This work presents an automatic bee entrance monitoring system based on YOLO11 transfer learning and the ByteTrack tracking algorithm. The study investigates the influence of data augmentation, backbone freezing, and tracker parameter optimization on the detection and counting of small, fast-moving bees. The detector with progressive backbone unfreezing strategy achieved about 97.0% precision and 98.7% mAP50, while providing more stable convergence than full fine-tuning. Experiments also showed that light augmentation outperformed heavy augmentation. For tracking, ByteTrack parameters were optimized to improve trajectory continuity under low-confidence detections. On an independent 25 FPS side-view video, the optimized YOLO11-ByteTrack system correctly counted 43 of 47 incoming bees (91.5%) and 7 of 30 outgoing bees (23.3%). Error analysis showed that most counting errors were caused by missed detections due to rapid bee motion and motion blur, while tracking failures became less frequent after parameter optimization. Overall, the results indicate that moderate augmentation, progressive backbone unfreezing, and ByteTrack tuning improve the reliability of automatic bee entrance monitoring under realistic recording conditions.

</details>


### [158] [BenthicDINO: Physics-Informed Self-Distillation for View-Invariant Side-Scan Sonar Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23215)
*Taqi Hamoda,Hayat Rajani,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息自蒸馏的侧扫声呐(SSS)影像感知框架，基于DINOv3架构和ConvNeXt-v2-Tiny骨干网络，通过物理驱动的数据增强（模拟散斑噪声、距离衰减、辐射校正误差）和HSIC惩罚项显式解耦学习特征与物理观测参数，从而实现视图不变性。结合四阶段稠密层级特征融合保留细节，在S3Seg数据集上仅用10%标注数据即达96%峰值性能，最终mIoU 71.4%、整体精度86.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧扫声呐影像受物理声学伪影影响，使得固有海底反射率与瞬时观测几何混合，阻碍自动化感知。现有自监督学习框架使用为自然图像设计的增强策略，无法应对声学退化，也未显式强制视图不变性，导致表征质量受限。

Method: 构建基于DINOv3架构（ConvNeXt-v2-Tiny骨干）的物理信息自蒸馏框架，通过两条机制实现视图不变性：(1) 物理启发的增强——模拟散斑噪声、距离相关衰减和辐射失校准；(2) HSIC惩罚项——显式解耦稠密patch特征与物理观测参数。另提出跨四阶段的稠密层级特征融合策略，兼顾细粒度沉积物细节与深层语义抽象。

Result: 在S3Seg数据集上，自监督框架无需人工标注即可将复杂海底地形聚类为稳定无噪声的语义簇。监督下游任务中，仅使用10%标注数据即达到绝对峰值性能的96%，最终mIoU达71.4%，整体精度86.5%。

Conclusion: 物理感知的增强与显式解耦约束能显著提升声呐影像自监督表征质量，且稠密层级特征融合带来优异的数据效率和分割性能，展示了领域定制化SSL对于水下声学感知的重要价值。

Abstract: Automated perception in side-scan sonar (SSS) imagery is severely hindered by physical acoustic artifacts, resulting in representations that inextricably mix intrinsic seabed reflectivity with transient viewing geometries. Existing self-supervised learning (SSL) frameworks rely on augmentations designed for natural images, failing to account for acoustic degradation and explicitly enforce view-invariance. To address this gap, we introduce a physics-informed self-distillation framework built upon the DINOv3 architecture utilizing a ConvNeXt-v2-Tiny backbone to maximize data efficiency. The proposed methodology enforces view-invariance through two primary mechanisms: physically motivated augmentations that simulate speckle noise, range-dependent attenuation, and radiometric miscalibration; and a Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) penalty that explicitly decouples learned dense patch features from physical viewing parameters. Furthermore, we propose a dense, hierarchical feature fusion strategy across all four network stages to preserve fine-grained sediment details alongside deep semantic abstractions. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that the framework natively groups complex benthic topographies into stable, noise-free semantic clusters without relying on manual annotations. During supervised downstream tasks on the S3Seg dataset, the fused representations exhibited exceptional data efficiency, achieving 96% of its absolute peak performance using only 10% of the available annotated data, ultimately reaching a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 71.4% and an overall accuracy of 86.5%.

</details>


### [159] [MLLM-Assisted Audio VOS: A 3rd Place Report for the MeViS-Audio Track, 8th LSVOS Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23234)
*Liangtao Shi,Jinxia Xie,Xiantao Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的音频引导视频目标分割框架，通过将多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）与SAM类分割模型相结合，在无需额外模型训练或任务微调的情况下，利用MLLM的多模态推理能力建模文本-视觉对应关系，并采用SAM模型生成精确的目标掩码，在MeViS-Audio赛道取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频引导的视频目标分割是一个具有挑战性的多模态任务，传统方法通常需要任务特定的训练或微调。作者希望通过组合现成的基座模型，构建一个无需额外训练即可完成该任务的框架，验证基座模型在此类多模态分割任务中的有效性和潜力。

Method: 该框架将音频引导视频目标分割任务分解为多个阶段，每个阶段选取合适的现成基座模型：利用MLLM的强大多模态推理能力来建模文本与视觉之间的对应关系（处理音频到文本的转换及文本-视觉对齐），然后使用SAM系列模型进行精确的目标掩码生成。整个过程不引入额外的模型训练或任务特定的微调。

Result: 该框架在第8届LSVOS挑战赛的MeViS-Audio赛道中取得了具有竞争力的性能，证明了利用基座模型组合进行音频引导分割的有效性，且整个过程无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 通过将任务分解并组合现成的MLLM和SAM系列基座模型，可以无需训练即可实现有效的音频引导视频目标分割，验证了基座模型组合范式在多模态分割任务中的可行性。

Abstract: In this technical report, we present a training-free framework for audio-guided video object segmentation, which integrates Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with SAM-based segmentation models. We decompose the task into several stages and identify suitable foundation models for each stage. Without introducing additional model training or task-specific fine-tuning, our approach leverages the strong multimodal reasoning capabilities of MLLMs to model text-visual correspondence and employs SAM-based models for accurate object mask generation. The proposed framework demonstrates the effectiveness of leveraging foundation models for audio-guided video segmentation and achieves competitive performance in the MeViS-Audio Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge.

</details>


### [160] [Mover360: Controllable Object Manipulation in 360° Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23238)
*Haoyi Zhong,Fang-Lue Zhang,Andrew Chalmers,Taehyun Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mover360是一个针对360°全景图像的可控物体操作框架，核心任务为物体平移（点、框、掩码三种引导模式），辅助支持参考引导的插入和移除。它通过轻量适配预训练扩散Transformer，并利用UE5构建大规模配对数据和双域基准，在保真度、语义一致性和分布质量上超越现有透视编辑、插入和修复基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视编辑器主要针对透视图像设计，无法直接处理ERP投影的360°图像——其具有水平环绕、纬度相关畸变和全局场景连续性等特性，使得物体编辑既难以被现有方法生成，也难以被用户指定。因此需要专门面向全景的物体操控框架。

Method: Mover360以物体平移为核心任务，将点、框、掩码三种引导统一编码为固定提示词+紧凑的ERP对齐指令图；默认单点击即可平移物体，模型利用全景上下文和辅助深度条件推断合理尺寸、支撑关系和光照。结构上是对预训练扩散Transformer的轻量适配；为生成配对监督数据，构建了UE5数据管线（含表面感知物体放置和随机化光照），产出大规模配对数据和合成+真实双域基准。

Result: 在两个测试域和两种评估协议下，Mover360在重建保真度、语义一致性和分布质量上均优于透视编辑、插入和修复的强基线。代码和基准数据集已开源。

Conclusion: Mover360证明了针对ERP全景的轻量扩散适配在可控物体操作上的有效性，并提供了大规模合成+真实基准以推动该方向的研究。

Abstract: We present Mover360, a controllable object manipulation framework for 360° images. Unlike perspective images, 360° images in equirectangular projection (ERP) exhibit horizontal wrap-around, latitude-dependent distortion, and global scene continuity, which makes object-level edits difficult for existing perspective editors to produce and for users to specify. To address this, Mover360 centers on object Translation (relocating a specified object within an existing panorama) while supporting reference-guided Insert and Remove as auxiliary tasks. Its interface unifies point-, bbox-, and mask-guided control by encoding each task into a fixed prompt and a compact, ERP-aligned instruction map. In the default point mode, a single click relocates an object, allowing the model to infer a plausible size, support, and illumination using panoramic context and an auxiliary depth condition. Structurally, Mover360 is a lightweight adaptation of a pretrained diffusion transformer. To generate paired supervision, we construct a UE5 data-generation pipeline with surface-aware object placement and randomized illumination, yielding large-scale paired data and a dual-domain benchmark of synthetic and real panoramas with ground truth for all three tasks. Across both test domains and two evaluation protocols, Mover360 outperforms strong baselines for perspective editing, insertion, and inpainting in reconstruction fidelity, semantic consistency, and distributional quality. Code and our benchmark dataset are available at https://zhonghaoyi.github.io/Mover360/.

</details>


### [161] [Semantic Reconstruction and 3-D Detection via Learned Multi-Pair Fusion in RF Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23249)
*Amir Rezaei,Wen-Xin Pan,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究多基地射频成像的各向异性问题，利用BP、LASSO等图像形成方法生成每对Tx-Rx的重建，再通过3D U-Net融合多对重建并进行体素级语义分类，最终经几何后处理得到目标实例及其方向包围盒，并引入未知类以处理训练集外的新物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多基地射频成像仅产生强度重建，在低信噪比下严重退化；实际场景需要语义级理解（分类+实例分割），且场景中存在训练未见过的物体类别，需要显式处理未知类。

Method: 对每个Tx-Rx对使用标准逆问题求解器（BP、LASSO、非相干BP、group-LASSO）生成图像，将每个成像方法的多对重建作为多通道输入送入3D U-Net，U-Net隐式融合并逐体素输出类别概率；取最大概率得到语义重建；通过聚类和PCA进行几何后处理生成实例及方向包围盒；对未知类使用outlier exposure训练。

Result: 在广泛的信噪比范围内，语义重建（以分割IoU评估）和3D检测相比经典强度重建退化更加平缓；尤其在检测方面，在强度重建已完全失效的噪声水平下仍保持可靠。unknown类能正确标记未见类别物体而非误分类为已知类。

Conclusion: 语义重建对噪声的鲁棒性显著优于经典强度重建，且显式未知类的加入使框架能处理开放世界场景，提升了系统的实用性与可靠性。

Abstract: We consider a multistatic radio-frequency imaging problem with anisotropy, in which the reflection from a point depends on the positions of the transmit (Tx) and receive (Rx) arrays. The goal is to label the voxels of a field of view by a finite set of semantic classes and to group them into object instances. For the image formation of each Tx--Rx pair we apply a standard inverse-problem solver, and we feed the resulting per-pair reconstructions into a trained three-dimensional (3-D) U-Net that performs the fusion implicitly and the per-voxel classification explicitly. On a controlled, under-determined multistatic setup, we consider the following image formation methods: back-projection (BP) and the least absolute shrinkage and selection operator (LASSO) from a single deterministic snapshot, and incoherent BP and group-LASSO from multiple fading snapshots. For each imaging method we train a separate U-Net that fuses the six Tx--Rx pairs (its input channels) and assigns each voxel a probability vector over the classes. Taking the most probable class gives a labeled volume---the semantic reconstruction. Object instances and their oriented bounding boxes then follow by geometric post-processing (clustering and principal-component analysis). Across a wide range of signal-to-noise ratio, the semantic reconstruction (scored against ground truth by segmentation intersection-over-union) and the resulting 3-D detection degrade far more gracefully than the classical intensity reconstruction: the detection in particular stays reliable well into noise levels at which that reconstruction has dissolved. Because real scenes contain objects of classes the network was not trained on, we add an explicit unknown class trained by outlier exposure, which labels held-out novel objects as unknown instead of mislabeling them as a known class by reconstructed shape.

</details>


### [162] [E2S-Pruner: Progressive Two-Stage Evidence Fusion for Visual Token Pruning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23253)
*Taoyu Qian,Qi Wang,Daqian Shi,Yuanhao Jiang,Shang Gao,Hualong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出E2S-Pruner，一种无需额外模型、可学习参数或微调的两阶段证据融合框架，用于视觉token剪枝以加速视觉-语言模型推理。第一阶段将每个注意力头视为独立证据源，估计可靠性并用三态（重要/不重要/不确定）表征视觉token；第二阶段使用Dempster-Shafer证据理论量化层间冲突并融合多层互补证据，同时引入空间新颖性约束避免token过度集中。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，保留192/128/64个token时分别保持98.0%/96.8%/90.6%的聚合性能，吞吐量提升1.96x和2.09x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型通常将图像编码为数百个视觉token，导致显著的推理延迟和GPU显存开销。现有剪枝方法大多依赖注意力分数并直接跨注意力头和网络层聚合输出，难以刻画证据不确定性和冲突，因此需要更鲁棒的token剪枝框架。

Method: 提出两阶段证据融合框架E2S-Pruner：(1)第一阶段将每个注意力头视为独立证据源，根据证据清晰度和头间一致性估计可靠性，并将每个视觉token表示为三态（重要/不重要/不确定）；(2)第二阶段利用Dempster-Shafer证据理论量化跨层冲突并融合多层互补证据；(3)引入空间新颖性约束，促进不同图像区域的覆盖，防止保留token集中在局部显著区域。无需辅助模型、可训练参数或微调。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，保留192/128/64个token时分别维持98.0%/96.8%/90.6%的聚合性能，在128-token和64-token设置下吞吐量提升1.96x和2.09x。在Qwen2-VL-7B上验证了跨模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: E2S-Pruner通过证据理论与不确定性建模实现了无需训练的有效视觉token剪枝，在保持高比例性能的同时显著提升推理吞吐量，并在多个模型上展现良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models typically encode an image into hundreds of visual tokens, incurring substantial inference latency and GPU memory overhead. Existing pruning methods largely rely on attention scores and directly aggregate outputs across attention heads and network layers, making it difficult to characterize evidential uncertainty and conflict. We propose E2S-Pruner, a progressive two-stage evidence-fusion framework for visual token pruning that requires no auxiliary model, trainable parameters, or fine-tuning. In the first stage, E2S-Pruner treats each attention head as an independent evidence source, estimates its reliability from evidence clarity and inter-head consistency, and represents each visual token using three states: important, unimportant, and uncertain. In the second stage, Dempster--Shafer evidence theory is used to quantify inter-layer conflict and fuse complementary evidence from multiple network layers. We further introduce a spatial novelty constraint that promotes coverage of distinct image regions and prevents the retained tokens from concentrating in a few locally salient areas. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, E2S-Pruner retains 98.0%, 96.8%, and 90.6% of the aggregate performance when the average numbers of retained visual tokens are 192, 128, and 64, respectively, while improving throughput by 1.96x and 2.09x under the 128-token and 64-token settings. Experiments on Qwen2-VL-7B further demonstrate cross-model generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/taoyu-qian/E2S-Pruner.git.

</details>


### [163] [Progressively Learning Heterogeneous Skills in a Unified Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23258)
*Yue-Yi Zhang,Ming Gong,Linpu He,Wei-Shi Zheng,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HetSkills框架，在统一潜空间中渐进式学习异构技能，用于基于物理的角色控制。核心是将潜空间作为共享可执行接口，整合来自不同数据源、监督形式和任务的技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的角色控制需要掌握多种异构技能（运动跟踪、文本驱动动作生成、动作补全等），但这些技能来自不同数据源和任务形式，难以在统一框架下整合与复用。

Method: 先学习一个跟踪技能建立运动控制基础并创建可复用的共享运动解码器；引入运动直觉蒸馏将文本到运动生成锚定在语言语义上，防止走捷径；设计任务引导模块根据高层语言指令动态调整动作。

Result: 在运动跟踪、文本到运动生成、运动补全和下游任务适配等实验中均展示了有效性，在挑战性条件下取得令人印象深刻的成功率。

Conclusion: HetSkills能够在保持自然运动的同时持续扩展技能库，对长时程任务具有高度适应性，无需为不同任务重训或使用独立控制器。

Abstract: We propose HetSkills, a novel framework designed to progressively learn heterogeneous skills within a unified latent space for physics-based character control. The core idea is to treat this latent space as a shared executable interface, enabling seamless integration of skills learned from diverse data sources, supervision forms, and tasks. HetSkills begins by learning a tracking skill that establishes a strong foundation in motion control and creates a shared motion decoder, which can be reused across tasks without the need for retraining or separate controllers. To prevent the text-to-motion skill from exploiting shortcut pathways instead of learning language semantics, we introduce motion intuition distillation to ground text-to-motion generation in language semantics and a task-guidance module that dynamically adjusts actions based on high-level language instructions. This enables HetSkills to preserve natural motion while continuously expanding its skill repertoire, making it highly adaptable for long-horizon tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness in motion tracking, text-to-motion generation, motion completion, and downstream task adaptation, achieving impressive success rates even under challenging conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Dual-Grained Agent Memory and Shapley Context Attribution for Multimodal Agentic Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23268)
*Jieke Wang,Tiancheng Shen,Yibo Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DG-Mem 是一个双粒度智能体记忆框架，通过非参数化、外部存储的记忆增强冻结的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），无需梯度更新即可在科学和数学推理上提升性能。它将记忆分为实例级范例记忆和类别级 IF-THEN 规则模式记忆，并采用在线概念分类器和 Shapley 上下文归因机制。在 MathVista、MMMU、MMMU-Pro 上对四个开放权重/专有骨干模型（Qwen3.5-27B、Qwen3.5-122B-A10B、GPT-5-Nano、Gemini-3-Flash）上均优于无记忆和竞争性记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态大语言模型在感知上表现出色，但在科学和数学推理上仍显不足。对于闭源权重或端侧骨干模型无法进行参数级适配，而无状态提示无法利用已解决问题的累积收益。作者希望在不访问参数的情况下，通过外部记忆增强来提升推理能力。

Method: DG-Mem 提出双粒度智能体记忆框架：1）实例句范例记忆 + 类别级 schema 记忆（IF-THEN规则），由瞬态反思存储中介构建，schema 仅从抽象反思合成而非范例文本；2）在线概念分类器在训练中增量增长类别空间，不依赖预定义分类体系；3）Shapley 上下文归因分解整个检索规则集的正确性贡献，得到每条规则的效用并在测试时重新加权检索。全程无梯度更新，可部署在闭源权重或端侧骨干上。

Result: 在 MathVista、MMMU、MMMU-Pro 三个基准上，覆盖四个开放权重和专有骨干模型（Qwen3.5-27B、Qwen3.5-122B-A10B、GPT-5-Nano、Gemini-3-Flash），DG-Mem 一致性地优于无记忆基线和竞争性记忆基线。

Conclusion: DG-Mem 证明了一种无需参数访问的记忆增强方法可以在多模态科学/数学推理任务上稳定提升冻结 MLLM 的性能，且适用于闭源权重和端侧部署场景，为该方向的非参数化推理增强提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) deliver impressive perception yet still falter on scientific and mathematical reasoning. Parameter-level adaptation is unavailable for closed-weight or on-device backbones, and stateless prompting forfeits any compounding benefit from problems already solved. We propose \textbf{DG-Mem}, a dual-grained agentic memory framework that augments a frozen MLLM with a non-parametric, externally stored memory built once from training-time rollouts and consulted read-only at test time. Motivated by the Complementary Learning Systems (CLS) account of human memory, DG-Mem factors its store into an instance-grounded exemplar memory and a category-level schema memory of IF-THEN rules, with a transient reflection store mediating their construction so that schemas are synthesized only from abstract reflections, never from exemplar text. Two design choices distinguish DG-Mem: an online concept categorizer that grows the category space incrementally during training rather than committing to a predefined taxonomy, and a Shapley context attribution procedure that decomposes correctness across the entire retrieved rule set and yields a per-rule utility that re-weights retrieval at test time. The pipeline introduces no gradient updates and is deployable on closed-weight or on-device backbones. Across MathVista, MMMU, and MMMU-Pro on four open-weight and proprietary backbones (Qwen3.5-27B, Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, GPT-5-Nano, Gemini-3-Flash), DG-Mem improves consistently over no-memory and competitive memory baselines.

</details>


### [165] [Spatiotemporally Decoupled Autoregressive Diffusion Model for Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23279)
*Chengqun Yang,Liang Xu,Yanping Li,Fulong Liu,Jingnan Gao,Weili Zeng,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeMoDiff，一种时空解耦的统一框架，通过空间-时间VAE对每个身体关节点进行编码替代整体运动压缩，并结合时空掩码与注意力机制的自回归扩散生成器，在HumanML3D和KIT-ML上取得SOTA重建性能和有竞争力的生成结果，同时具备时空编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动动作合成方法中，VQ方法存在固有信息损失（损害质量/多样性/泛化），而连续表示在整体身体运动上缺乏部件级灵活性；同时扩散和自回归扩散模型对单个身体部位的细粒度控制能力有限。需要一种同时重新设计表示与架构的统一步骤来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出DeMoDiff：1) 空间-时间VAE（spatial-temporal VAE），对每个身体关节点分别编码，而非将全身运动压缩到单一潜在空间，增强表示提取能力和部件级可控性；2) 自回归扩散生成器，融入时空掩码和注意力机制，同时实现生成能力和可控编辑性。

Result: 在HumanML3D和KIT-ML数据集上实现SOTA重建性能和具有竞争力的动作生成效果，并展示出强的时间与空间编辑能力。

Conclusion: 时空解耦的表示与生成架构结合，能同时兼顾生成质量、多样性和部件级可控性，为文本驱动动作合成提供了新的统一范式。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion synthesis has made substantial development with two core modules of motion representation and generative architecture. For representation, Vector Quantization (VQ)-based methods compress motion data into discrete tokens while latent-based models operate directly in continuous space. However, both of these representations exhibit significant limitations. VQ-based methods suffer from inherent information loss, which compromises the quality, diversity, and generalization of generated motions, while continuous representation on holistic whole-body motion hinders part-level flexibility. For architecture, diffusion and autoregressive diffusion models have demonstrated their superiority, yet the fine-grained controllability over individual body parts is also limited. Thus, we propose a unified spatiotemporally decoupled framework named DeMoDiff, which jointly redesigns representation and architecture. To enhance representation extraction capabilities and offer greater part-level controllability, we present a spatial-temporal VAE that encodes each body joint rather than compressing the whole-body motion into a single latent space. Then, we incorporate spatial-temporal masking and attention mechanisms into an autoregressive diffusion generator, achieving both generative capability and controllable editability. Extensive experiments on the HumanML3D and KIT-ML datasets demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction performance and compelling motion generation results. Moreover, our framework demonstrates strong temporal and spatial editing capabilities, further validating its effectiveness. Our project page: https://rex0191.github.io/DeMoDiff/

</details>


### [166] [Spotter: Efficient Urban Visual Localization via Geo-Referenced Facade Landmarks in GPS-Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23290)
*Antoni Valls,Jordi Sanchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spotter是一个实时视觉定位框架，利用建筑立面作为全局地理参考，离线阶段通过语义分割Google Street View全景图并结合多视图立体深度与制图数据构建紧凑度量数据库，运行时通过级联检索和几何验证实现全局相机定位，在巴塞罗那多区域可穿戴智能眼镜数据集上超越里程计基线，达到与地图匹配方法相当的精度且帧率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集城市场景中，GPS信号因多径传播经常退化，视觉里程计存在累积漂移，而地图匹配方法依赖可靠的GPS先验且计算量过大无法实时边缘执行。需要一种鲁棒且实时的视觉定位方案，不依赖GPS也能提供全局地理参考。

Method: 离线阶段：对Google Street View全景图进行语义分割提取建筑立面，结合多视图立体深度和制图数据构建紧凑的度量数据库。运行时：通过级联检索（cascaded retrieval）和几何验证（geometric verification）流水线匹配查询图像，恢复细粒度全局相机定位。框架保留在有GPS信号时整合GPS的能力。

Result: 在巴塞罗那多个区域使用可穿戴智能眼镜采集的行人序列数据集上，Spotter优于基于里程计的基线方法，定位精度与最先进的地图匹配方法相当，同时运行帧率显著更高。

Conclusion: 利用建筑立面作为全球地理参考源是一种规避GPS退化问题的有效途径，Spotter证明了将离线地图构建与运行时级联检索结合可以在精度和实时性之间取得良好平衡，适用于机器人和可穿戴平台。

Abstract: Accurate visual localization on robotic and wearable platforms remains challenging in dense urban environments. Existing methodologies typically rely on GPS for absolute positioning, yet GPS signals frequently degrade in urban canyons due to multipath propagation. Consequently, standard solutions like visual odometry suffer from unmitigated drift over time, while map-matching techniques struggle to acquire the reliable GPS priors they need, on top of being too computationally heavy for real-time edge execution. To address these limitations, we propose Spotter, a robuts and real-time visual localization framework that uses building facades as a reliable source of global geo-reference, while retaining the capability to integrate GPS signals when available. In an offline stage, Spotter processes Google Street View panoramas by semantically segmenting facades and pairing multi-view stereo depth with cartographic data to build a compact metric database. At runtime, query images are matched via a cascaded retrieval and geometric verification pipeline to recover fine-grained global camera localization. We benchmark Spotter on a newly collected dataset of pedestrian sequences acquired with wearable smart glasses across several districts of Barcelona. Experimental results show that Spotter outperforms odometry-based baselines and achieves localization accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art map-based methods while operating at significantly higher frame rates.

</details>


### [167] [What Memory Composition Does Not Tell Us About Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23295)
*Joongwon Chae,Runming Wang,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于记忆的异常检测器中，覆盖选集（patch选择策略）在稀疏训练污染下的行为。发现全局覆盖选择会严重过度代表稀疏污染，而提出的CLEANCON跨图像支持门控机制可将最终记忆污染降至约零，并在全部12组匹配比较中提升P-AP。但沿保留率扫描时，最低污染的记忆并不获得最高P-AP，性能在污染上升时继续改善，表明记忆污染程度并不决定最终记忆的P-AP排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于记忆的异常检测器将训练补丁存入记忆库作为正常参考，但所选补丁在语义上是否真实可靠缺乏独立校验。作者希望探究稀疏训练污染下补丁选择策略与检测性能之间的耦合关系，并设计一种在不改变表示、记忆大小、构建器和推理规则的前提下，仅通过改变候选图像资格来降低污染的门控机制。

Method: 在固定表示和记忆预算下，比较随机、medoid、局部和全局四种覆盖选择策略。提出CLEANCON，一种袋外（out-of-bag）跨图像支持门控机制，它在保持表征、绝对记忆大小、构建器与推理规则不变的前提下，改变候选图像的资格（eligibility），从而筛选出污染较少的记忆。

Result: 全局覆盖选择严重过度代表稀疏污染。CLEANCON将最终记忆污染降至近似零，并在全部12组匹配对比中提升类别宏平均P-AP。然而，沿保留率（retention sweep）扫描时，最低污染的记忆并未达到最高P-AP——性能在污染上升时仍继续改善，说明记忆污染与P-AP之间并非单调对应关系。

Conclusion: CLEANCON能有效消除记忆中的稀疏污染并提升检测性能，但记忆污染水平本身并不能预测或排序记忆的P-AP表现，说明除污染外还有其他因素决定最终记忆质量。这挑战了"污染越低性能越好"的直观假设，为记忆构建策略提供了更细致的理解。

Abstract: Memory-based anomaly detectors store nominal training patches and score test patches against this memory. A patch selected for coverage therefore becomes a nor- mal reference without a separate check that geometric rarity makes it safe to trust. We probe this coupling with sparse training contamination. Under fixed representa- tions and memory budgets, we compare random, medoid, local, and global coverage selectors. We then use CLEANCON, an out-of-bag cross-image support gate that changes candidate-image eligibility while fixing the representation, absolute mem- ory size, builder, and inference rule. Global coverage strongly over-represents sparse contamination. CLEANCON reduces final-memory contamination to approx- imately zero and increases category-macro P-AP in all 12 matched comparisons. Yet along a retention sweep, the lowest-contamination memory does not attain the highest P-AP; performance continues to improve while contamination rises. Mem- ory contamination therefore does not order the resulting memories by P-AP

</details>


### [168] [What Remains Normal? Clean Images Miss Useful Near-Defect Normal Patches for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23299)
*Joongwon Chae,Runming Wang,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于记忆的异常检测器中，补丁选择策略与训练污染之间的耦合问题。作者发现全局覆盖选择会过度代表稀疏污染，并提出CLEANCON——一种基于袋外交叉图像支持门控的方法，在固定表征、内存大小、构建器和推理规则的前提下改变候选图像的合格性。CLEANCON将最终内存污染降至接近零，并在所有12组匹配比较中提升类别宏P-AP。但通过保留曲线扫描发现，最低污染内存并非最高P-AP，性能随污染上升继续改善，表明内存污染程度不能按P-AP对最终内存排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于内存的异常检测器在选择补丁作为正常参考时，缺乏对几何稀疏性（即补丁是否安全可信）的独立检查。在训练数据存在稀疏污染的情况下，这一耦合可能导致污染补丁被选为正常参考，影响检测性能。作者希望解耦选择与信任，以量化污染的影响并提出缓解方案。

Method: 在固定表征和内存预算下，比较随机、medoid、局部和全局四种覆盖选择器。提出CLEANCON：一种基于袋外（out-of-bag）跨图像支持门控的方法，通过在保持表征、绝对内存大小、构建器和推理规则不变的前提下，改变候选图像的合格性来过滤污染补丁。

Result: 全局覆盖选择会严重过度代表稀疏污染。CLEANCON将最终内存污染降至接近零，并在所有12组匹配比较中提高类别宏P-AP。然而，沿着保留（retention）曲线扫描发现，最低污染内存并未达到最高P-AP；性能在污染上升时仍继续改善，说明内存污染程度不能作为按P-AP排序内存的充分指标。

Conclusion: 内存污染与下游检测性能（P-AP）之间并非单调对应关系。降低污染并不总是等同于提高性能，这提示基于内存的异常检测器的设计需要更细致地权衡污染控制与信息保持。CLEANCON虽能有效清除污染并有性能提升，但最优化污染与最优性能之间存在差异，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Memory-based anomaly detectors store nominal training patches and score test patches against this memory. A patch selected for coverage therefore becomes a nor- mal reference without a separate check that geometric rarity makes it safe to trust. We probe this coupling with sparse training contamination. Under fixed representa- tions and memory budgets, we compare random, medoid, local, and global coverage selectors. We then use CLEANCON, an out-of-bag cross-image support gate that changes candidate-image eligibility while fixing the representation, absolute mem- ory size, builder, and inference rule. Global coverage strongly over-represents sparse contamination. CLEANCON reduces final-memory contamination to approx- imately zero and increases category-macro P-AP in all 12 matched comparisons. Yet along a retention sweep, the lowest-contamination memory does not attain the highest P-AP; performance continues to improve while contamination rises. Mem- ory contamination therefore does not order the resulting memories by P-AP.Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jw-chae/cleancon.

</details>


### [169] [Grounding Free-Form Instructions for Fashion Complementary Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23302)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Claudio Pomo,Alessandro De Bellis,Danilo Danese,Dietmar Jannach,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自由形式指令下的时尚互补图像生成任务（CIG with free-form instructions），通过VLM生成低、中、高三种特异性级别的自然语言指令来丰富三个CIG基准，并提出StyleFlow——一种基于Rectified Flow Matching的多模态Transformer模型，联合条件化种子图像和指令生成风格协调的服装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIG基准依赖僵硬的模板提示（如"a photo of a skirt"），无法反映用户真实查询的自然表达，也掩盖了模型在不同语言特异性水平下的行为差异。作者希望建立更接近真实用户意图的多模态语言接地设置。

Method: 1) 丰富三个CIG基准，使用VLM生成低/中/高特异性指令并经人工验证；2) 提出StyleFlow，采用Rectified Flow Matching，在单一多模态Transformer中联合条件化种子图像和指令。

Result: 在图像质量指标、目录对齐分析、消融实验和人工评估中，StyleFlow一致地生成与指令对齐且风格协调的服装，同时相比辅助模块方法降低了架构复杂性和推理成本。

Conclusion: 自由形式指令能够更真实地反映用户查询，StyleFlow通过简化的单Transformer架构在保持生成质量的同时降低计算开销，为该任务提供了有效基线。

Abstract: Fashion complementary image generation (CIG) aims to create garments that stylistically match a seed item based on user intent, making it a natural multimodal grounding problem where models must interpret language in visual context. Existing CIG benchmarks rely on rigid template prompts (e.g., "a photo of a skirt"), failing to reflect natural user queries and obscuring model behavior across levels of linguistic specificity. We introduce fashion complementary image generation with free-form instructions, a multimodal language-grounding setting where a model generates a compatible garment from a seed image and a natural-language instruction. To this end, we enrich three CIG benchmarks with low-, medium-, and high-specificity instructions generated by a vision-language model and validated by human annotators. We instantiate the task with StyleFlow, a Rectified Flow Matching model that jointly conditions on the seed image and instruction within a single multimodal transformer. Across image quality metrics, catalog-alignment analysis, ablations, and human evaluation, StyleFlow consistently produces instruction-aligned and stylistically coherent garments while reducing architectural complexity and inference cost relative to auxiliary-module approaches.

</details>


### [170] [Thinking Beyond Videos: Unifying Video Reasoning and Deep Research for Open-World Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23329)
*Wenqi Liu,Shijie Ma,Yunxiao Wang,Meng Liu,Qile Su,Han Liu,Bohan Hou,Xuanyu Zheng,Changyi Liu,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Kaiyu Jiang,Yingxin Li,Jiankang Chen,Xu Wang,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Jianhua Yin,Yinwei Wei,Xuemeng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRover是一个统一的视频深度研究框架，迭代协调视频裁剪、多模态搜索和网页浏览三种工具，让视频定位引导外部检索、检索证据反过来触发进一步视频检查，并通过26K SFT轨迹和3K RL实例的自动化数据管道训练，在VideoDR和自建基准上达到与专有模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界视频理解需要同时定位稀疏视觉证据和获取视频/参数记忆之外的外部知识。现有的Thinking-with-Videos侧重主动时间感知，Deep Research侧重多步信息检索，但两者通常被孤立开发，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出VideoRover统一框架，迭代协调视频裁剪、多模态搜索和网页浏览三种工具，每个工具结果用于选择下一个动作。构建自动化数据 curation 管道，产出26K SFT轨迹和3K RL实例。引入VideoRover-Bench基准，按视频时长和研究难度分层。采用8B模型进行SFT后RL训练。

Result: VideoRover-8B-RL在VideoDR和VideoRover-Bench上达到与专有模型相当的直接回答性能，超越使用相同工具套件的更大开源模型。消融实验验证了视频接地、外部检索和长视野RL的互补作用。

Conclusion: 主动视频定位、外部检索和长视野强化学习三者互补，构成了视频深度研究的有效范式。

Abstract: Open-world video understanding often requires a model to locate sparse visual evidence and acquire external knowledge that is absent from the video and its parametric memory. While Thinking-with-Videos enables active temporal perception and Deep Research supports multi-step information seeking, the two capabilities are typically developed in isolation. We introduce VideoRover, a unified Video Deep Research framework that iteratively coordinates video cropping, multimodal search, and webpage browsing. Given a video-question pair, VideoRover uses each tool result to select the next action, so localized video clips guide external retrieval and retrieved evidence triggers further video inspection and verification. To develop this capability, we construct an automated data curation pipeline, producing 26K verified SFT trajectories and 3K challenging RL instances. We also introduce VideoRover-Bench, a benchmark stratified by video duration and research difficulty. Experiments on VideoDR and VideoRover-Bench show that our VideoRover-8B-RL achieves performance comparable to proprietary models in the direct-answer setting without tool use while outperforming larger open-source models equipped with the same tool suite. Ablation studies and training dynamics further validate the complementary roles of active video grounding, external retrieval, and long-horizon reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [171] [IntentQA: Intent Question Answering in Videos by Cognitive Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23330)
*Jiapeng Li,Ping Wei,Wenjuan Han,Song-Chun Zhu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IntentQA视频问答任务与数据集，用于从视觉观察推理人类行为背后的意图（"社会智能暗物质"）。通过LLM生成五个对比集并引入"对比性能下降"指标来评估模型鲁棒性。提出X-CaVIR框架，利用三种认知上下文（情境、对比、常识）增强视频分析，并通过透明管道整合LLM实现可解释推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解模型仅能识别视觉事实，无法理解人类行为背后的意图。标准评估指标因数据集偏差而高估模型能力，缺乏针对意图推理的专用数据集和鲁棒性评估方法，且传统黑盒模型推理过程不透明不可解释。

Method: 1) 构建IntentQA大规模VideoQA数据集；2) 用LLM生成五个对比集并设计"Contrast Performance Decline"指标评估鲁棒性；3) 提出X-CaVIR框架，包含三个模块：跨模态Video Query Language模块（情境上下文）、对比学习模块（对比上下文）、常识推理模块（常识上下文）；4) 采用透明管道整合LLM，将视频字幕与VQA模型输出协同，实现可解释推理。

Result: 大量实验表明各组件有效，X-CaVIR优于SOTA基线方法，且在对比集上对扰动具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过结合多类型认知上下文与透明LLM集成管道，X-CaVIR在意图推理任务上取得SOTA性能，同时使推理过程可解释并具备鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video understanding requires intelligent agents to transcend mere recognition of visual facts and comprehend the underlying intents behind human actions (often termed the "dark matter" of social intelligence). To bridge the gap between visual observation and intent reasoning, we introduce a novel task, IntentQA, and contribute a large-scale VideoQA dataset specifically tailored for this purpose. However, recognizing that standard metrics may overestimate capabilities due to dataset biases, we go beyond simple accuracy to rigorously evaluate model robustness. We augment the benchmark by generating five distinct contrast sets via Large Language Models (LLMs) and introducing a "Contrast Performance Decline" metric.
