We propose the X-CaVIR (eXplainable Context-aware Video Intent Reasoning) framework, which leverages three types of "Cognitive Context" to enhance video analysis: i) Situational Context via a cross-modal Video Query Language (VQL) module, ii) Contrastive Context via a Contrastive Learning module, and iii) Commonsense Context via a Commonsense Reasoning module. Crucially, to overcome the opacity of traditional black-box models, we refine the integration of LLMs within X-CaVIR by employing a transparent pipeline that synergizes video captions with VQA model outputs. This approach not only improves performance by effectively utilizing rich commonsense knowledge but also renders the reasoning process explicitly interpretable. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our components, the superiority of X-CaVIR over state-of-the-art baselines, and its stability against perturbations on the contrast sets.

</details>


### [172] [Can Coding Agents Build Robust Baselines? A Skill-Based Approach for Automating the Medical Imaging Model-Development Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23336)
*Eugenia Moris,José Ignacio Orlando*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个agentic AI Scientist工作流，结合文献引导推理、自动代码生成和假设驱动实验，端到端生成医学影像基准的竞争性基线模型。在四个公开基准（分割、分类、检测）上验证，包括PUMA两个赛道第6名和MILK10k第31名，无需任务特定重新设计即可跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像领域开发竞争性深度学习基线仍高度依赖人工迭代（文献综述、实现、实验、专家调优），现有自动化方法只优化孤立组件（如架构搜索或超参数调优），缺乏对整个基线开发流程的端到端自动化。

Method: 提出一个agentic AI Scientist工作流，核心包含：(1) 文献引导推理（literature-guided reasoning）辅助方法选择；(2) 自动化代码生成；(3) 假设驱动实验（hypothesis-driven experimentation）进行迭代优化。该框架作为完整的实验流水线，无需针对特定任务重新设计即可应用于不同医学影像挑战。

Result: 在四个公开基准上验证，覆盖分割、分类和检测任务。Experiment Pipeline持续提升验证性能：PUMA两个赛道均获第6名（15支队伍），MILK10k获得第31名（125支队伍）。在MIDOG25上展现出跨扫描仪、肿瘤类型和物种的强领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 技能化、文献引导的agentic工作流能够大幅减少开发竞争性医学影像基线所需的工程投入，且同一工作流无需任务特定重新设计即可跨多个挑战复用，证明了自动化智能体的潜力。

Abstract: Developing competitive deep learning baselines for medical imaging remains a highly iterative process requiring literature review, implementation, experimentation, and expert refinement. Existing automation approaches typically optimize isolated components, such as architecture search or hyperparameter tuning, rather than the complete baseline development process. We present an agentic AI Scientist workflow that combines literature-guided reasoning, automated code generation, and hypothesis-driven experimentation to generate competitive baseline models for medical imaging challenges. The framework is evaluated on four public benchmarks spanning segmentation, classification, and detection. Across all tasks, the Experimentation Pipeline consistently improves validation performance, achieving competitive leaderboard results, including 6th place on both PUMA tracks (15 teams) and 31st place on MILK10k (125 teams). On MIDOG25, the resulting model also demonstrates strong domain generalization across scanners, tumor types, and species. Using the same workflow across all challenges without task-specific redesign, we demonstrate that skill-based, literature-guided agentic workflows can substantially reduce the engineering effort required to develop competitive medical imaging baselines.

</details>


### [173] [Controllable blind deblurring with diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23343)
*Imane Si Salah,Emile Cribelier,Thomas Veit,Wolf Hauser,Arthur Leclaire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperSharpen，一种基于扩散模型的盲去模糊方法，针对摄影图像中的未知各向同性模糊。方法通过模糊度量显式控制复原强度，比较了ControlNet式适配器与对扩散先验的完全微调两种条件化策略，实验表明微调能获得更好的保真度并减少幻觉细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摄影图像获取过程中会因光学系统、传感器和低层处理产生模糊退化。针对未知模糊核的盲去模糊问题，现有生成模型难以在产生照片级真实细节的同时保持与输入图像的保真度，需要一种可控且忠实的方法。

Method: 提出SuperSharpen扩散去模糊框架，通过模糊度量提供对复原强度的显式控制。比较两种条件化策略：(1) 在冻结主干上使用ControlNet风格适配器；(2) 对扩散先验进行完全微调。

Result: 在合成与真实模糊数据上的实验表明，完全微调策略相比ControlNet适配器能实现更高的保真度并产生更少的幻觉细节，整体上改善了感知质量并实现了可控的复原强度。

Conclusion: SuperSharpen实现了摄影盲去模糊中感知质量与保真度之间更好的平衡，并提供了用户可控的复原强度调节能力，完全微调策略优于适配器方案。

Abstract: Image acquisition with a camera involves several degradations due to the optical system, sensor, or low-level processing steps. We address blind deblurring in professional photography: we aim to invert unknown isotropic blur without knowledge of the degradation kernel.For such inverse problems,where some high-frequency information is lost, it is challenging to use generative models to produce details that are both photo-realistic and faithful to the input. We propose SuperSharpen, a diffusion-based blind deblurring method offering explicit control over restoration strength through a blur measure. We compare two conditioning strategies: a ControlNet-style adapter on a frozen backbone, and full finetuning of the diffusion prior. Our experiments show that finetuning achieves better fidelity with fewer hallucinated details. We validate our approach on synthetic and real-world blur, demonstrating improved perceptual quality and controllable restoration strength.

</details>


### [174] [DF-MoE: Generalizable Deepfake Detection via Multimodal Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23363)
*Vlad Hondru,Florinel Alin Croitoru,Iuliana Georgescu,A. Sophia Koepke,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-MoE框架，通过集成多种预训练模型提取音频和视觉模态的高层线索（口部运动、人脸解析、面部表情、头部姿态、视线跟踪、心率、音频情感、语音活动），并使用Mixture-of-Experts骨干网络整合单模态和多模态线索进行音视频深度伪造检测。在五个基准数据集（MAVOS-DD, AVLips, PolyGlotFake, BioDeepAV, FakeAVCeleb）的域内和跨域实验中优于所有对比方法，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频深度伪造检测的核心挑战是跨伪造生成方法的泛化能力，现有检测器容易过拟合。作者推测通过预训练模型提取多种高层线索可以有效缓解过拟合问题。

Method: 构建多预训练模型的特征提取器集（口部运动、人脸解析、表情、头部姿态、视线、心率、音频情感、语音活动），通过Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)骨干网络统一整合单模态与多模态线索进行深度伪造判别。

Result: 在五个基准数据集的域内和跨域实验中，DF-MoE在所有对比方法中取得最优深度伪造检测结果。

Conclusion: 多源预训练特征结合MoE融合策略能有效提升音视频深度伪造检测的泛化能力，优于现有SOTA方法，代码已公开。

Abstract: Audio-visual deepfake detection is an actively studied topic, where one of the main challenges is to develop detectors able to generalize across deepfake generation methods. We conjecture that overfitting can be mitigated by extracting multiple high-level cues from the available audio and visual modalities via pre-trained models. We therefore assemble a wide variety of pre-trained models to extract features that encode mouth movements, face parsing, facial expressions, head pose, gaze tracking, heart rate, audio emotion and speech activity. We further integrate both unimodal and multimodal cues via a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone to detect deepfakes. We perform in-domain and cross-domain experiments on five benchmarks for deepfake detection (MAVOS-DD, AVLips, PolyGlotFake, BioDeepAV, FakeAVCeleb) to compare our framework (DF-MoE) with state-of-the-art methods. Our results indicate that DF-MoE obtains superior deepfake detection results, surpassing all competing methods. We release our code at https://github.com/vladhondru25/DF-MoE.

</details>


### [175] [Long-Horizon Audio-Visual Generation for Persistent Stories and Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23383)
*Nan Duan,Haoyang Huang,Weiyang Jin,Haoran Li,Yaowei Li,Yuming Li,Yijun Liu,Xin Lu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yanwen Ma,Yaofeng Su,Yilang Sun,Haoyu Wang,Zeyue Xue,Songchun Zhang,Junhao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Echo-1.5是一个统一的音视频生成系统，包含两个变体：长视频变体通过可组合的跨镜头记忆和语音线索实现角色外观与声音身份的持久保持；世界模型变体将异构导航输入转换为度量6-DoF相机轨迹并通过几何感知条件注入实现控制器无关的交互。系统还提出了因果少步生成器训练方案（渐进式教师强制和短/长时域Self-Gradient Forcing），在长视频和世界模型基准上均取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成正从孤立片段向长时叙事和交互式世界发展，现有模型在保持身份一致性、遵循用户控制以及在长时间rollout中的稳定性方面存在不足，需要一个统一的音视频生成系统来同时解决长视频持久性和世界模型交互性问题。

Method: 提出JoyAI-Echo-1.5系统，包含：1）长视频变体采用可组合跨镜头记忆（聚合多镜头视觉证据）+语音过滤的全镜头音频语音线索；2）世界模型变体将异构导航输入转换为校准的度量6-DoF相机轨迹，通过几何感知条件路径注入；3）将双向音视频骨干转换为因果少步生成器，使用渐进式教师强制和短/长时域Self-Gradient Forcing在自生成rollout上训练以支持高效长时生成。

Result: 长视频变体在跨镜头一致性、视觉质量、文本对齐和语音保真度上超越现有长视频基线。世界模型变体在WBench排名第一（平均分81.7），在SANA-WM-Bench上取得领先的视觉质量和长时程持久性。

Conclusion: 记忆、几何控制和rollout感知训练为生成连贯故事和持续演化的交互式世界提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Video generation is progressing beyond isolated clips toward long-form narratives and interactive worlds, requiring models to preserve identities, follow user controls, and remain stable over extended rollouts. We present JoyAI-Echo-1.5, a unified audio-visual generation system with two purpose-built variants. The long-video variant introduces composable cross-shot memory that aggregates visual evidence across multiple prior shots and speaker cues derived from speech-filtered full-shot audio, enabling persistent character appearance and voice identity across flexible combinations of text, image, and memory conditioning. The world-model variant converts heterogeneous navigation inputs into calibrated metric 6-DoF camera trajectories and injects them through a geometry-aware conditioning pathway, enabling controller-agnostic interaction across flexible viewpoints. To support efficient long-horizon generation, we transform a bidirectional audio-visual backbone into a causal few-step generator using progressive teacher forcing and short- and long-horizon Self-Gradient Forcing on self-generated rollouts. Experiments demonstrate strong performance in both settings. JoyAI-Echo-1.5 achieves improvements over existing long-video baselines in cross-shot consistency, visual quality, text alignment, and speech fidelity. Its world-model variant ranks first on WBench, with an average score of 81.7, and achieves leading visual quality and long-horizon persistence on SANA-WM-Bench. Together, these results indicate that memory, geometric control, and rollout-aware training provide a practical foundation for generating coherent stories and continuously evolving interactive worlds. Project page: https://echo-team-joy-future-academy-jd.github.io/Echo-1.5-Page/.

</details>


### [176] [Photorealistic Novel View Synthesis of Human Faces using Next-Scale Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23410)
*Federico Stella,Fei Jiang,Zhongshi Jiang,Zohar Barzelay,Emanuel Garbin,Amin Jourabloo,Liuhao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于next-scale自回归范式实现人体（人脸）多视角新视角合成，支持高分辨率、多相机输出和跨视角一致性，在单次前向传播中完成。在合成人脸数据集上训练，利用低分辨率通用预训练减少目标数据需求，并可配合3D高斯提升生成像素对齐的3D人脸模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体/人脸的高分辨率多视角新视角合成在保持身份、细节和几何一致性上面临挑战，现有方法（如扩散模型）依赖大量2D预训练和目的数据，效率低。

Method: 基于next-scale自回归范式，扩展至更高分辨率、多视角输出与更强的跨视角一致性；利用低分辨率通用预训练权重，仅在最后阶段使用全分辨率目标数据训练；可与Transformer-based像素对齐3D高斯提升模型耦合。

Result: 在合成人脸数据集上训练后，模型能生成清晰逼真的视角图像，支持同时合成多个新视角以获得更好的一致视图；在感知保真度和跨视角一致性上取得增益，并能配合3D高斯提升生成精确的3D人脸模型。

Conclusion: Next-scale自回归是人体多视角合成中可扩展、多输出的有效骨干，相较扩散模型具有更少的数据依赖和更强的跨视角一致性。

Abstract: Photorealistic novel view synthesis of people remains challenging at high spatial resolutions and across multiple target cameras, where preserving identity, fine appearance details, and geometric coherence is critical. We build on the next-scale autoregressive paradigm and adapt it for human-centric view synthesis by enabling higher image resolutions, multi-view outputs and stronger cross-view consistency in a single forward pass. We train on a synthetic dataset of human faces spanning diverse identities and apparel. Contrary to diffusion models, this paradigm does not need 2D pre-training and, thanks to its next-scale architecture, it benefits from lower-resolution, general-purpose pre-trainings, with the full-sized purpose-specific images being used only in the last training stages. This enables our architecture to converge with a smaller amount of purpose-specific training data, allowing us to use a smaller but more realistic training dataset. The resulting model produces sharp and realistic views, with the option to synthesize multiple novel viewpoints simultaneously for improved agreement across views. Empirically, we observe gains in perceptual fidelity and cross-view coherence on human subjects, demonstrating that next-scale autoregression is an effective backbone for scalable, multi-output human view synthesis. We also couple our pipeline with an existing transformer-based model for pixel-aligned 3D gaussian lifting from multi-view facial inputs, resulting in accurate and photorealistic 3D models of human faces.

</details>


### [177] [Image-Conditioned Diffusion Models for Quality Assurance of Organ-at-Risk Segmentations in Radiotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23432)
*Clea Dronne,Catharine H Clark,Xavier Loizeau,Elizabeth Miles,Peter Hoskin,Jamie R McClelland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了VAE框架和图像条件分割扩散模型在头颈部CT放疗中器官危机器官分割错误检测上的表现，评估了脑干和脊髓分割的边界及宽度扰动检测能力，发现扩散模型在局部化细微边界错误方面更一致，支持其作为解剖感知的分割QA框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗计划中器官危机器官（OAR）分割的审查耗时且主观，需要自动化的分割错误检测方法来提高QA效率和一致性。

Method: 比较VAE框架与图像条件分割扩散模型，在RADCURE数据集的脑干和脊髓分割上模拟边界和宽度扰动，使用Dice相似系数和DTA（Distance to Agreement）评估输入与重建分割之间的误差检测能力。

Result: 两种模型都能检测部分模拟错误，但区域DTA分析显示扩散模型在局部化细微边界错误方面表现更一致。

Conclusion: 图像条件扩散重建是局部化、解剖感知分割QA的有前景框架。

Abstract: Accurate organ-at-risk segmentation is essential for radiotherapy planning, but reviewing segmentations is time-consuming and subjective. We investigate normative modelling for segmentation error detection in head-and-neck CT, comparing a VAE framework with an image-conditioned segmentation diffusion model. Models were evaluated on RADCURE brainstem and spinal cord segmentations using simulated boundary and width perturbations. Error detection was assessed using the Dice similarity coefficient and the Distance to Agreement (DTA) between the input and reconstructed segmentations. While both models detected some simulated errors, regional DTA showed that the diffusion model localised subtle boundary errors more consistently. These results support image-conditioned diffusion reconstruction as a promising framework for localised, anatomy-aware segmentation QA.

</details>


### [178] [Towards Comprehensive Basketball Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23435)
*Yirong Hu,Jiayuan Rao,Yu Zhang,Shangzhe Di,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BasketballBench，一个多模态基准测试，包含7,980个问题、十个任务，覆盖文本、图像和视频，基于2025-2026 NBA赛季数据构建。同时提出BasketballSkills智能体，通过组合八种篮球专用感知和检索工具（封装为四个可复用技能）来提升对篮球比赛的全面理解能力，实验表明其优于现有MLLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试一次只评估单一能力（事件识别、动作定位、球员识别、结构化游戏知识），尚未充分探索这些能力之间的交互。作者希望构建一个能综合评估并提升多模态篮球理解能力的基准和智能体。

Method: 构建包含2,501个篮球片段clip、530名球员数据及官方play-by-play的BasketballBench基准；提出BasketballSkills智能体，将八种篮球专用感知与检索工具组合为四个可复用技能，每个技能明确指定工具顺序、证据绑定和停止条件。

Result: 实验表明当前MLLM在处理需要多能力集成的问答时表现不佳，而BasketballSkills在综合篮球理解任务上显著优于现有方法，验证了显式组合领域专用能力的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式地组合领域特定的感知与检索能力能够有效提升对复杂体育场景的综合理解，为多模态智能体设计提供了参考。

Abstract: Understanding a basketball game requires recognizing events, localizing actions, identifying players, and relating these to structured game knowledge. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate these abilities one at a time, leaving the interactions among these abilities under-explored. We introduce BasketballBench, a multimodal benchmark comprising 7,980 questions across ten tasks in text, image, and video. It is built from the 2025-2026 NBA season and includes official playby-play, rosters and profiles for 530 active players, and 2,501 possession-level broadcast clips. We further propose BasketballSkills, an agent that composes eight basketball-specific perception and retrieval tools under four reusable skills that specify tool order, evidence bindings, and stopping conditions. Experiments show that current MLLMs struggle particularly on questions requiring the integration of multiple capabilities, whereas BasketballSkills outperforms them, highlighting the effectiveness of explicitly composing domain-specific capabilities for comprehensive basketball understanding.

</details>


### [179] [Geometry-Driven Opti-Acoustic Co-Registration and View-Invariant Reflectivity Mapping for Side-Scan Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23479)
*Taqi Hamoda,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何驱动的框架，利用SfM重建海床3D网格作为几何锚点，实现光学-声学(Side-Scan Sonar)像素级配准与视角不变的反射率映射，并通过FBR提取算法、逆Lambertian模型和双高斯加权函数消除声学畸变，自动化生成严格配准的多模态海底数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧扫声纳(SSS)是水下大尺度测绘的主要手段，但声学图像存在斑点噪声、阴影和极端视角依赖，传统手工特征与现代深度学习方法无法在没有3D几何约束下跨越光学与声学之间的物理域差异，严重阻碍了自动化感知与跨模态对齐。

Method: 1) 使用SfM重建稠密3D海床网格作为视觉与声学域之间的几何锚点；2) 提出First Bottom Return (FBR)提取算法动态校正未校准SfM引起的非线性高度漂移；3) 应用逆Lambertian模型和双高斯加权函数分离固有海底反射率，中和斜距传播损失与几何视角依赖性；4) 将分离后的声学属性与光学像素确定性关联，生成严格共配准的多模态数据集。

Result: 该自动化的物理引导框架生成高度精确、严格共配准的光学-声学多模态数据集，无需人工标注，为海底栖息地测绘中的自监督学习奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该工作通过物理引导的自动化流程消除了手工标注需求，打通了声学与光学域之间的几何对齐瓶颈，为实现先进的自监督学习驱动的海底栖息地测绘开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Side-Scan Sonar (SSS) is a primary modality for large-scale underwater mapping, yet automated perception and cross-modal alignment are severely bottlenecked by acoustic complexities such as speckle noise, shadows, and extreme viewpoint dependencies. Traditional handcrafted descriptors and modern deep learning matchers fail to bridge the physical domain gap between optical and acoustic imagery without 3D geometric constraints. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel geometry-driven framework for pixel-level opti-acoustic co-registration and view-invariant reflectivity mapping. Our method utilizes Structure-from-Motion (SfM) to reconstruct a dense 3D seafloor mesh, acting as a geometric anchor between the visual and acoustic domains. We introduce a First Bottom Return (FBR) extraction algorithm to dynamically correct non-linear altitude drift caused by uncalibrated SfM reconstruction. Furthermore, we apply an inverse Lambertian model and a dual-Gaussian weighting function to isolate the intrinsic seabed reflectivity, effectively neutralizing slant-range propagation loss and geometric view-dependence. By deterministically associating these isolated acoustic properties with optical pixels, our pipeline generates highly accurate, strictly co-registered multi-modal datasets. This automated, physics-guided approach eliminates the need for manual annotation and paves the way for advanced self-supervised learning in benthic habitat mapping.

</details>


### [180] [SVD-Based Typicality Maps for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23499)
*Aldo Sean Sartor,Leandro de Souza Rosa,Andriy Enttsel,Mauro Mangia,Riccardo Rovatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于SVD分解ViT内部表示的方法，将激活投影到主右奇异向量上得到紧凑表征，并拟合类条件密度模型生成"典型性图"。由此推导两个后验OOD检测分数：原型对齐分数（PAS）和多层软投票分数（MLSV），无需重训练或OOD暴露即可在ViT-B/16（CIFAR-100微调）上获得具有竞争力的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解和利用ViT内部参数几何结构，构建无需重训练、无需OOD样本暴露的分布外检测方法，提升深层视觉模型在开放场景下的可靠性。

Method: 对每个仿射层的权重矩阵进行SVD分解，将激活投影到前导右奇异向量上得到层内质表征；在每层拟合并类条件密度模型，计算逐类典型性得分并沿深度堆叠为典型性图；从图中推导PAS（与类参考原型模式的对齐度）和MLSV（跨层共识软投票）两个OOD检测分数。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上微调的ViT-B/16上，所提分数在无需重训练和OOD暴露的条件下实现了具有竞争力的OOD检测性能。

Conclusion: 利用ViT内部参数的几何结构可以有效提取类内证据演化信息，并构建轻量、无需训练的后验OOD检测机制，展现出较强的实用潜力。

Abstract: We present a method for analyzing the internal representations of Vision Transformers (ViTs) exploiting the geometry of their learned parameters. Each affine layer's weight matrix is factored via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), and activations are projected onto the leading right singular vectors to obtain compact, layer-intrinsic representations. A class-conditional density model is then fitted at each layer, producing per-class \emph{typicality scores} that are stacked across depth into \emph{typicality maps}: two-dimensional summaries of how class-specific evidence evolves through the network. From these maps, we derive two post-hoc scores for Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) detection: a \emph{Prototype Alignment Score} (PAS), measuring agreement with class reference prototype patterns, and a \emph{Multi-Layer Soft Voting} (MLSV) score, capturing cross-layer consensus without stored prototypes. On ViT-B/16 fine-tuned on CIFAR-100, the proposed scores achieve competitive detection performance without retraining or OOD exposure.

</details>


### [181] [Action-Aligned Retrieval with Pairwise Multimodal Reranking for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23503)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thanh-Nhan Vo,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ActPair，一个三阶段粗到细的统一框架，用于文-人异常行为检索。通过动作对齐的VLM微调、并行晚期融合检索（原文+LLM重写）和基于pivot-promote算法的成对视觉比较重排序模块，桥接姿态-语义鸿沟。在PAB数据集达到最优并在非异常数据集上有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-人物异常行为检索方法忽视上下文相关的细粒度行为语义：有的依赖孤立的骨架几何、有的在查询重写时丢失原始细节、有的用绝对逐点打分做多模态校验，难以捕捉上下文条件下的动作。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 用动作对齐多任务目标微调视觉语言模型，编码动作判别语义；(2) 并行晚期融合检索，同时用原始查询和LLM生成的上下文接地重写，保留互补语义；(3) 提出即插即用重排序模块，用pivot-promote算法做直接成对视觉比较，缓解残余空间和组合歧义，避免穷举评估的高推理成本。

Result: 在PAB公开测试集上取得相比方法最佳结果，并能有效迁移到未见过的非异常特定数据集。

Conclusion: ActPair通过动作对齐检索与成对多模态重排序的统一框架，有效解决了文本人物异常检索中姿态-语义鸿沟问题，兼顾了性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires distinguishing individuals based on fine-grained, context-dependent behaviors rather than mere appearance. Existing methods struggle to capture these context-conditioned actions, frequently relying on isolated skeletal geometry, discarding raw query details during reformulation, or utilizing absolute pointwise scoring for multimodal verification. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{ActPair}, a unified three-stage coarse-to-fine framework that combines action-aligned retrieval with pairwise multimodal reranking to bridge the pose-semantic gap. First, we fine-tune a vision-language model (VLM) with an action-aligned multi-task objective that encourages the representations to encode action-discriminative semantics. Second, we perform parallel late-fusion retrieval using the original query and a large language model (LLM)-generated context-grounded rewrite, retaining complementary details from both semantic views. Finally, we propose an efficient off-the-shelf reranking module that leverages a pivot-promote algorithm to perform direct pairwise visual comparisons, mitigating residual spatial and compositional ambiguities without the prohibitive inference costs of exhaustive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves the best results among the compared methods on the Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior (PAB) public test and transfers effectively to an unseen, non-anomaly-specific dataset.

</details>


### [182] [Investigating Relational Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23518)
*Adhithya Laxman Ravi Shankar Geetha,Aulia Kharis Rakhmasari,Haleema Ramzan,Xander Yap*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过设计合成几何图形数据集和针对性语言线索查询，系统探究Qwen3-VL-4B是否真正理解视觉关系，还是依赖语言捷径。结果发现现代VLM混合了真实的视觉推理与主要源于语言线索的快捷策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在视觉推理任务上表现强大，但其是否真正理解视觉关系（而非利用语言先验或线索）尚不明确。作者希望揭示VLM内部各层如何编码视觉信息，判断其推理能力是"真理解"还是"走捷径"。

Method: 1) 构建简单几何形状的合成数据集以实现受控分析；2) 设计精确针对语言线索的查询；3) 修改数据集以测试模型对视觉证据的因果依赖；4) 使用Qwen3-VL-4B，跨深度解码视觉信息编码方式。

Result: 当前VLM在视觉关系推理中既包含真实的视觉推理，也依赖以语言线索为主的快捷策略（shortcut strategies）。

Conclusion: VLMs的视觉推理能力是混合的——并非纯粹的视觉理解，也非纯粹的捷径依赖，而是两者并存；语言线索是主要捷径来源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong performance in visual reasoning tasks, but it remains unclear whether they understand visual relations, or simply employ shortcuts such as language cues or priors. To investigate this, we use the Qwen3-VL-4B (Bai et al., 2025), a modern VLM, to decode how visual information is encoded across depths. For this, we propose a synthetic dataset of simple geometric shapes for controlled analysis, along with queries crafted to precisely test language cues. Furthermore, the dataset is modified to test causal reliance on visual evidence. Our results show that current VLMs combine genuine visual reasoning with shortcut strategies primarily rooted in language cues.

</details>


### [183] [Predicting Multiple Clinical Outcomes Related to Functional Recovery and Social Isolation Among Older Adults After Lower-Limb Fracture or Hip Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23531)
*Santosh Ray,Pratik K. Mishra,Ali Abedi,Charlene H. Chu,Amir Ahmad,Shehroz S. Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用MAISON-LLF多模态传感器数据集（18名下肢骨折或髋关节置换康复中的老年人，最长8周监测），从室内运动、加速度、步数、心率、户外活动、睡眠中提取46个日特征，联合预测5个临床结局（社会隔离量表、牛津髋/膝评分、起立行走测试、30秒椅子站立测试）。将问题建模为多输出回归，发现联合预测优于单独预测，其中tabular深度学习多输出回归器NODE表现最佳（MSE=3.96，MAE=1.02），SHAP分析证实多模态传感器的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下肢骨折或髋关节置换术后的老年人康复轨迹复杂，但以往研究往往孤立研究单一临床方面，掩盖了功能恢复与社会参与等多维度的联合影响。需要利用多模态传感器数据同时评估多个临床结局，以支持更全面的康复监测。

Method: 从MAISON-LLF数据集中46个多模态传感器日特征出发，将5个临床结局预测建模为多输出回归问题。测试了多种机器学习和深度学习的单输出与多输出回归算法，比较联合预测与独立预测的效果，并使用SHAP进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 联合预测临床评分优于单独预测。多输出回归器NODE（tabluar深度学习）表现最佳，达到MSE=3.96，MAE=1.02。SHAP分析表明多模态传感器特征（室内运动、户外活动、睡眠等）对康复轨迹估计均具有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 多输出回归联合建模能够同时评估老年人功能恢复与社会参与，多模态传感数据可有效支持社区老年人的康复轨迹预测，有望改善其护理和生活质量。

Abstract: Older adults recovering after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement may experience complex recovery trajectories. Most of the time, these clinical aspects are studied in isolation, masking their joint impact on recovery. This study used the MAISON-LLF dataset, which contains multimodal sensor and clinical assessment data from 18 older adults recovering in the community after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement. Participants were monitored for up to eight weeks, corresponding to a maximum of 1,008 participant-days of sensor monitoring. Forty-six daily features were extracted from indoor motion, acceleration, step count, heart rate, out-of-home mobility, and sleep data. Five clinical outcomes were assessed every two weeks: the Social Isolation Scale, Oxford Hip Score, Oxford Knee Score, Timed Up and Go test, and 30-second Chair Stand test. We utilize an inherent relationship between multi-modal sensor data and different clinical scores and formulate it as a multi-output regression problem. We tested various machine learning and deep learning single- and multi-output regression algorithms to predict these scores simultaneously. The results showed that predicting clinical scores jointly was better than separately. The tabular DL multi-output regressor, NODE, gave a remarkable performance of MSE=3.96 and MAE=1.02 in comparison to other multi- and single-output regressors. The SHAP feature analysis further showed the importance of including multimodal sensors to provide a good estimate of patients' recovery trajectory. This work may support the simultaneous assessment of functional recovery and social engagement among community-dwelling older adults and ultimately help improve their care and quality of life.

</details>


### [184] [FixAnything: 3D-Consistent Rendering Refinement via Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23549)
*Khiem Vuong,Deva Ramanan,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FixAnything通过复用预训练视频生成模型，将其隐式多视图先验用于修复多种3D表示（3DGS、NeRF、网格、点云）产生的渲染伪影，只需轻量微调和最小化架构修改，即可统一替代多个专用精化管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角或远距离目标视角下，现有3D表示方法（3DGS/NeRF/网格/点云）渲染会产生伪影。已有工作使用扩散先验缓解，但每种表示都需要定制架构或大量重训练，缺乏统一的通用解决方案。

Method: 将噪声渲染序列视为视频，重新利用预训练视频生成模型做视频到视频翻译（修复）。引入二值掩码标记干净像素以锚定高质量输入结构；使用structure-from-motion恢复的相机位姿精度作为奖励信号，通过直接偏好优化（DPO）鼓励3D一致的渲染结果以支持下游重建。

Result: 在四种不同的3D表示上，FixAnything仅需轻量微调即可一致地提升渲染质量，表明单一通用视频先验可替代多个专用精化管线。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型的隐式多视图先验可被重用为通用的渲染伪影修复器，框架简洁，未来可直接适配更强的视频模型而无需架构重新设计。

Abstract: Rendering views using 3D scene representations such as Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), meshes, or even point clouds produces artifacts when input views are sparse or target views lie far from the input. Recent work mitigates these artifacts using diffusion-based generative priors, but is specialized to individual representations and require custom architectures or extensive retraining. We present FixAnything, a single model for fixing a wide range of rendering artifacts. It does so by repurposing a pretrained video generative model, leveraging its implicit multi-view priors with only minimal modification and lightweight finetuning. Our key insight is that even noisily-rendered sequences preserve camera motion and coarse scene structure, allowing cleanup to be formulated as video-to-video translation. To control what scene structure should be preserved, we introduce a binary mask denoting the clean pixels, enabling the model to anchor its output to high-quality inputs (e.g. training views) while refining the rest. To encourage FixAnything to produce 3D-consistent renderings that support downstream reconstruction, we use camera pose accuracy (recovered via structure-from-motion) as a reward signal for direct preference optimization (DPO). Across four distinct 3D representations, FixAnything consistently improves rendering quality with lightweight finetuning, demonstrating that a single generalist video prior can replace multiple specialist refinement pipelines. The simplicity of the framework enables immediate adoption of stronger future video models without architectural redesign.

</details>


### [185] [EG-ARSA: An Expert-Grounded Open Model for Visual Road Safety Auditing in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23563)
*Md Thamed Bin Zaman Chowdhury,Moazzem Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出专家接地蒸馏（EGD）框架，将机构道路安全审计专业知识蒸馏到80亿参数的紧凑视觉-语言模型中，实现可扩展的视觉道路安全审计。核心创新在于量化专家接地阶段：教师模型首先与权威现场审计校准（Cohen's kappa=0.74），然后生成结构化监督信号，通过LoRA蒸馏到学生模型。同时发布了BD-ARSA（孟加拉道路安全审计数据集，21,947条图像审计记录）和EG-ARSA（首个专用视觉-语言模型）。实验表明接地微调显著提升序数风险评估性能，且紧凑学生在盲审专家评估中超越310亿参数的教师和Gemini-2.5-Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家的道路交通事故伤亡仍是重大挑战，但主动式道路安全审计受限于不完整的碰撞记录、缺乏合格审计人员以及大规模现场检查的高成本。需要一种可扩展、低成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出EGD框架：(1) 教师VLM与权威现场审计定量校准（kappa=0.74）后允许大规模标注；(2) 校准教师生成结构化监督，通过LoRA和单一无泄漏提示蒸馏到80亿参数学生模型；(3) 构建BD-ARSA数据集（21,947条记录，近全国覆盖）和EG-ARSA模型。

Result: 接地微调显著提升序数风险评估性能（优于零样本基线）；盲审专家评估显示80亿参数学生模型优于310亿参数教师和Gemini-2.5-Flash。

Conclusion: EGD为资源受限环境下的大规模道路安全审计提供了有效且可扩展的工程解决方案，证明了专家知识蒸馏到紧凑模型的可行性。

Abstract: Road traffic injuries remain a major challenge in low- and middle-income countries, where proactive road safety auditing is limited by incomplete crash records, shortages of qualified auditors, and the high cost of large-scale field inspections. To address this problem, we propose Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD), a novel artificial intelligence framework that transfers institutional road safety expertise into a compact vision-language model for scalable visual road safety auditing. The key innovation is a quantified expert-grounding stage in which the teacher vision-language model is calibrated against authoritative field audits. Large-scale annotation is permitted only after the teacher reaches substantial agreement with expert risk assessments (Cohen's kappa = 0.74). The calibrated teacher then generates structured supervision that is distilled into an 8-billion-parameter student vision-language model using Low-Rank Adaptation and a single leakage-free prompt. We also introduce Bangladesh Road Safety Audit (BD-ARSA), the first open, expert-grounded Bangladeshi visual road safety audit dataset containing 21,947 image-audit records with near-national coverage, and Expert-Grounded Road Safety Auditor (EG-ARSA), the first vision-language model developed specifically for this task. Experimental results show that grounded fine-tuning substantially improves ordinal risk assessment over the zero-shot baseline, while blind expert evaluation demonstrates that the compact student outperforms both its 31 billion-parameter teacher and Gemini-2.5-Flash. These findings demonstrate that EGD provides an effective and scalable engineering solution for proactive road safety auditing in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [RoboShape: Information-Theoretic Point Cloud Representations for Privacy-Aware Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21380)
*Oguzhan Baser,Mirac Sozen,Kaan Kale,Sandeep Chinchali,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboShape提出了一种信息论引导的压缩头，在冻结的Sonata编码器之后通过Donsker-Varadhan互信息公式投影体素级嵌入，最大化与物体理解相关的互信息同时最小化与隐私属性的互信息，实现87.5%更小的嵌入、保留98.7%物体分类效用、敏感属性预测降低39.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类环境中通过扫描和共享3D表示（如舰队学习、云端规划、协同建图）采集的点云不仅揭示了场景中的物体，还暴露了敏感空间上下文（如房间功能或未获同意披露的信息）。传统点云编码器缺乏原则性控制——要么全部保留，要么全部丢弃，因此需要一种能选择性保留任务相关信息而过滤隐私信息的压缩方法。

Method: RoboShape采用信息论引导的压缩头，紧随冻结的Sonata编码器之后。通过Donsker-Varadhan公式化互信息(MI)，将体素级嵌入投影到低维空间：最大化嵌入与物体级理解之间的互信息，同时最小化与隐私属性的互信息，实现隐私感知的嵌入压缩。

Result: 在三个真实世界室内LiDAR数据集上，RoboShape实现87.5%更小的嵌入，保留98.7%的物体分类效用，同时将敏感属性预测降低39.3%。隐私保护嵌入在网络传输和下游任务训练上更经济高效。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RoboShape提供了一种编码器无关的实用工具，使机器人感知管线更加紧凑、隐私感知且部署就绪，为机器人社区在3D表示共享时提供了隐私保护的有效方案。

Abstract: With the increased adoption of robotic agents operating in human environments by scanning and sharing 3D representations (e.g., for fleet learning, cloud-based planning, or collaborative mapping), collected point clouds reveal not just the objects in a scene but also sensitive spatial context, such as room function or information that occupants never consented to disclose. Traditional point cloud encoders offer no principled control over this: either all is preserved, or none. Hence, we introduce RoboShape, an information theory guided compression head following the frozen {\tt Sonata} encoder. We project voxel-level embeddings using the Donsker-Varadhan formulation of mutual information (MI). Specifically, we maximize the MI between embeddings and object-level understanding while minimizing it for private attributes. RoboShape leads to 87.5\% smaller embeddings that retain 98.7\% of object classification utility while collapsing sensitive attribute predictions by 39.3\% across the three real-world indoor LiDAR datasets. Its privacy-preserving embeddings are cheaper to transmit over the network or to train a model for any downstream tasks. We release the RoboShape codebase to give the robotics community a practical, encoder-agnostic tool for building perception pipelines that are compact, privacy-aware, and deployment-ready.

</details>


### [187] [Multimodal-Language-Model-Driven Interaction and Companionship for Service Robots in Elderly-Care Facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21387)
*Ching-Chieh Liu,Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向养老院的智能陪伴机器人系统，集成主动视觉人体跟随、LLM驱动的语音交互（意图理解与任务执行）以及VLM驱动的安全监控（跌倒检测与异常姿态评估），实现连续陪伴、自然交互和安全管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务机器人研究多聚焦孤立的单一服务功能，缺乏将连续陪伴、自然交互和安全监控集成的能力，难以满足养老院日常护理需求。

Method: 系统分为感知层与交互层。感知层采用主动云台保持用户在遮挡或剧烈运动时始终在视野内，实现鲁棒的人体跟踪；交互层使用LLM理解口语请求并映射为机器人动作（如护送、语义导航）；同时VLM安全代理持续分析视觉观测，检测跌倒或异常姿态并触发紧急响应。

Result: 实验结果表明系统能够可靠地跟随和与人类交互，同时有效检测潜在跌倒以保证用户安全。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了将多模态感知、LLM交互与VLM安全监控整合到单一陪伴机器人平台中的可行性，为养老场景提供了一种集成解决方案。

Abstract: Service robots are increasingly deployed in elderly-care facilities to alleviate caregiver workload and enhance the quality of daily care. However, most existing studies focus on isolated service functions and lack integrated capabilities for continuous companionship, natural interaction, and safety monitoring. In this paper, we present an intelligent companion robot system that unifies active visual human-following, real-time LLM-driven speech interaction for intent understanding and task execution, and VLM-based safety monitoring for fall detection and abnormal posture assessment. The perception layer ensures robust human tracking and uses an active gimbal to maintain the user in view during occlusions or abrupt movements. At the interaction layer, a Large Language Model interprets spoken requests and maps them to robot actions, enabling escorting and semantic navigation. Simultaneously, a VLM-based safety agent continuously analyzes visual observations to detect fall-related or abnormal postures and triggers emergency responses when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to reliably follow and interact with humans, while effectively detecting potential falls to ensure user safety.

</details>


### [188] [Gimbal-Based Human Tracking for Companion Robots Using Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21388)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Ching-Chieh Liu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于云台相机与移动机器人的持续人体跟踪方法，通过主动控制云台保持目标在视野内，并采用持续学习策略进行行人重识别以适应外观变化，实验表明显著提升了跟踪稳定性与连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体跟踪方法依赖可穿戴标签或固定视角相机，系统灵活性差，且目标移出感知范围时跟踪容易失败，难以满足伴随机器人场景下可靠、连续的人体跟踪需求。

Method: 在移动机器人上集成云台相机，通过主动控制云台动态调整相机视角以维持目标在视野内；对行人重识别（ReID）任务应用持续学习策略，以适应长期跟踪中目标外观和环境条件的变化。

Result: 实验表明该系统显著提升了人体跟踪的稳定性和连续性，支持实时重识别，并能从步行到跑步全程提供响应式反馈；用户研究进一步表明该方法无需穿戴标签，提升了用户舒适度。

Conclusion: 基于云台相机的主动视觉跟踪结合持续学习的ReID策略，是实现无穿戴设备、可靠且连续的伴随机器人人体跟踪的有效方案。

Abstract: Reliable and continuous human tracking is essential for natural human-robot interaction, particularly for companion robots. However, many existing approaches rely on wearable tags or fixed cameras with limited fields of view, which reduces system flexibility and often causes tracking failures when the target moves outside the sensing range. In this paper, we present a human tracking approach based on a gimbal-mounted camera integrated into a mobile robot. By actively controlling the gimbal mechanism, the camera can dynamically adjust its viewing direction to maintain the target within the field of view, even under substantial relative motion between the robot and the human. Furthermore, a continual learning strategy is applied to the person re-identification (ReID) task to adapt to changes in appearance and environmental conditions during long-term tracking. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed system significantly improves the stability and continuity of human tracking, enables real-time re-identification, and provides responsive feedback for reliable tracking of human motion from walking to running. User studies further indicate that the proposed approach enhances user comfort by eliminating the need for wearable tags.

</details>


### [189] [On the Optimized Use of Non-Orthonormality Constraints for the Quasi-Static INS Alignment of Autonomous Underwater and Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21390)
*Carlos Renato C. Durao,Felipe O. Silva,Itzik Klein,Vinıcius M. G. B. Cavalcanti,Adriano Frutuoso,Ettore A. de Barros,Jay A. Farrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对惯性导航系统（INS）的初始对准问题，提出优化的TRIAD-CBE粗对准方法和引入非正交性（NON）误差约束的细对准EKF观测模型，将失准角和偏置估计的收敛时间从分钟级缩短到秒级，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真和两类不同等级IMU的真实实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统INS初始对准方法（TRIAD粗对准+ZVU-EKF细对准）存在收敛速度慢、偏置可估计性有限的问题，需要更快的对准方案以满足自主水下/水面车辆的应用需求。

Method: (1) 优化最近提出的TRIAD-CBE（TRIAD with Coarse Bias Estimation）方法，实现粗对准阶段的偏置估计；(2) 提出新的细对准EKF观测模型，将TRIAD推导出的非正交性（NON）误差约束直接映射到惯性传感器偏置上，实现更快的失准角与偏置联合收敛。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真和两类不同精度等级IMU的真实实验表明，该方法将失准角和偏置的收敛时间从分钟级加速到秒级，同时保持与传统方法相当的精度/准度。

Conclusion: 优化的TRIAD-CBE与NON约束EKF相结合，能显著加速INS初始对准过程而不牺牲精度，对实时性要求高的自主导航系统具有实用价值。

Abstract: Inertial navigation systems are specialized navigation apparatuses that equip almost all autonomous underwater and surface vehicles. They require precise initial alignment, i.e., determination of their initial attitude, which is typically achieved: (a) in quasi-static conditions (whenever possible); and (b) in two stages: Coarse Alignment (CA), using methods like TRI-axis Attitude Determination (TRIAD), and Fine Alignment (FA), via Zero Velocity Update (ZVU)-based Extended Kalman Filtering (EKF). However, conventional methods suffer from slow convergence and limited bias estimability. In response, this paper introduces: (a) an optimized version of a recently proposed CA method, namely, TRIAD with Coarse Bias Estimation (TRIAD-CBE); and (b) a novel FA EKF observation model that incorporates Non-Orthonormality (NON) error constraints derived from TRIAD, directly linking these errors to the inertial sensor biases. As validated through extensive Monte Carlo (MC) simulations, as well as real-world experiments using two Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) of different grades, our approaches substantially accelerate the convergence of misalignment and bias estimates (from minutes to seconds), while maintaining accuracy/precision comparable to traditional techniques.

</details>


### [190] [ODG-NoMaD: Overhead-Camera Direction-Guided NoMaD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21395)
*Blossom Treesa Bastian,Keerthi S. Shetty,Manish Kolachalam,Rani Malhotra,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ODG-NoMaD为NoMaD自监督视觉导航策略的探索模式增加了全局方向感，无需重训策略。部署时用顶部深度相机构建占据栅格地图并规划全局路径，分段得到期望航向，再结合机器人本体深度相机的逐帧可穿越性地图细化为无碰撞方向，最后将余弦方向代价的梯度注入去噪步骤，使采样轨迹朝向该方向旋转并保留多模态探索特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NoMaD虽然在单一目标掩码扩散策略中统一了目标导航和探索，但在未见环境中（无目标图像或拓扑地图）只能无方向地随机游走，缺乏全局感知。作者希望在不为探索模式重训策略的前提下，赋予其全局方向引导能力。

Method: 1) 部署时用一次顶部深度相机构建占据地图并规划全局路径；2) 将路径分段得到期望航向；3) 用机器人本体深度的逐帧可穿越性地图细化航向为无碰撞方向；4) 将余弦方向代价的梯度注入扩散模型最终去噪步骤，旋转采样轨迹朝向目标方向，同时保持探索多模态性。

Result: 在带/不带随机障碍的模拟办公室环境中，ODG-NoMaD将剩余到目标距离比无引导探索降低最多一个数量级，优于NaviDiffusor的点目标代价引导，并且是唯一在所有试验中保持无碰撞的配置。

Conclusion: 通过在推理时注入全局方向引导，无需重训即可显著提升NoMaD探索模式的全局导航能力与安全性，验证了将传统全局规划与学习型扩散策略结合的有效性。

Abstract: NoMaD [31] is a learned vision-navigation policy that unifies goal-conditioned navigation and exploration in a single goal-masked diffusion policy. In an unseen environment, however - where neither a goal image nor a topological map is available - it can only explore undirectedly, wandering without global awareness. We present ODG-NoMaD, which gives NoMaD's exploration mode a global sense of where to proceed, without retraining the policy. An overhead depth camera is used once on deployment to build an occupancy map and plan a global path, which is segmented to yield a desired heading; a per-frame traversability map from the robot's onboard depth then refines this into a collision-free direction. The gradient of a cosine direction cost is injected into the final denoising steps, rotating sampled trajectories toward this direction while preserving the multimodality of exploration. In simulated office environments with and without random obstacles, ODG-NoMaD reduces the residual distance to the target by up to an order of magnitude over unguided exploration, outperforms the point-goal cost guidance of NaviDiffusor [37], and is the only configuration that remains collision-free on every trial.

</details>


### [191] [Active Interaction-Aware Model Predictive Path Integral via Ego-Conditioned Generative Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21400)
*Khaled A. Mustafa,Mohamed-Khalil Bouzidi,Christian Schlauch,Ahmad Gazar,Nadja Klein,Joerg Reichardt,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个将ego条件式生成自回归预测模型集成到MPPI控制中的规划框架，通过嵌套采样实现交互感知的随机多模态预测与代价评估，使自车能够主动探测不同候选动作对交互结果的影响并减少不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集交通本质上是交互式的，自车与周围智能体互相影响反应，传统的predict-then-plan方法忽略了自车行为对周围环境的影响，无法实现主动的what-if推理，而被动交互感知方法也未能充分利用自车动作对预测分布的反向调节作用，因此需要一种主动的交互感知规划框架。

Method: 将ego-conditioned生成式自回归预测模型集成到MPPI(Model Predictive Path Integral)控制框架中。生成预测模型以自车每个候选动作为条件，输出周围智能体的随机多模态预测分布；通过嵌套采样方案在诱导分布下对期望代价和碰撞风险进行可处理的评估，使自车能够主动探测不同候选动作如何塑造交互结果，并识别能减少不确定交互中模糊性的动作。

Result: 闭环仿真实验表明，与传统的predict-then-plan方法和被动的交互感知方法相比，该框架在安全性和效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将自车条件生成预测模型嵌入MPPI控制的框架，实现主动交互感知规划，能够改善密集交通场景下驾驶的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Dense traffic is inherently interactive. The ego vehicle and surrounding agents continuously influence each other's reactions, making "what-if" reasoning essential for safe and efficient driving. To enable such an active interaction-aware behavior, we propose a planning framework that integrates an ego-conditioned generative autoregressive prediction model within Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control. The generative prediction model outputs stochastic, multi-modal predictions of surrounding agents conditioned on each of the ego's considered future actions. A nested sampling scheme enables tractable evaluation of expected cost and collision risk under the induced distribution. This formulation allows the ego to actively probe how different candidate actions shape the interaction outcomes and to identify actions that reduce ambiguity in uncertain interactions. Closed-loop simulations demonstrate improved safety and efficiency compared to conventional predict-then-plan and passive interaction-aware approaches.

</details>


### [192] [Selective Cross-View Consistency for World Action Models: Held-Out Viewpoint Robustness Without Test-Time Camera Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21402)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究世界动作模型（WAM）在跨视角一致性训练中的输出坐标选择问题。证明对视图协变块（预测的未来场景）施加一致性损失在数学上有害，会将合法的视角特定内容缩小为真实值的1/(1+4λ)；提出选择性跨视角一致性（SCVC），仅约束视图不变块（动作、未来本体感觉、价值），无需相机标签、外参、深度或视图合成。在LIBERO-Plus相机轨道上引入carve-and-hold-out评估协议，在训练包络外的保持视角上超过匹配控制12.2点（闭合循环成功率），并通过交叉骨干审计揭示已发表的相机鲁棒性结果受腕部相机位姿稳定性混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机视角变化是WAM最难应对的扰动轴之一。现有方法尝试用跨视角图像对进行一致性训练，但WAM的降噪目标混合了视图协变坐标（未来场景）和视图不变坐标（动作等），在哪些输出坐标上施加一致性损失是尚未被系统研究的关键问题。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明对视图协变块施加一致性损失会按1/(1+4λ)的比例收缩合法的视角特定内容，并通过受控实验验证该收缩定律。2) 提出SCVC：仅对视图不变块施加一致性约束，无需任何相机标签或视图合成。3) 引入carve-and-hold-out评估协议，区分分布匹配上限与真实的插值/外推能力，并用匹配的成对训练控制隔离一致性项的影响。4) 进行交叉骨干审计揭示腕部相机位姿稳定性对相机鲁棒性指标的混淆。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus相机轨道上，对超出训练包络的轨道视角，SCVC相比匹配控制提升12.2点（95% CI [7.4, 17.0]），第二个独立种子下提升15.5点（CI [11.7, 19.4]）；另外两个相机轴也复现了该效果。包络内插值无增益（-1.2和-4.3点），分布内能力保持（-0.6, -0.2）。交叉骨干审计表明已发表的相机鲁棒性结果被腕部相机位姿稳定性混淆。

Conclusion: 对WAM而言，跨视角一致性应仅施加于视图不变坐标块（动作、本体感觉、价值），而避免施加于视图协变块（未来场景预测），后者在数学上会损害视角特定内容。SCVC实现了无需相机标签/外参/深度/视图合成的保持视角泛化，同时保留了分布内能力和包络内插值性能。同时指出需要重新审视已发表的相机鲁棒性评估方法。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) jointly denoise future video frames and robot actions, and the video prior is expected to generalize their control. Camera viewpoint change remains one of their hardest perturbation axes. We study a question specific to this model class: when training with same-state cross-view image pairs, on which output coordinates should a consistency loss be imposed? The WAM denoising target mixes view-covariant coordinates, namely the predicted future scene, with view-invariant coordinates, namely the action chunk, future proprioception, and value. We show that consistency applied to the covariant block is provably harmful, shrinking legitimate view-specific content to a fraction $1/(1+4λ)$ of its true value, and we verify this shrinkage law in controlled experiments. Selective cross-view consistency (SCVC) therefore constrains only the invariant block, requires no camera labels, extrinsics, depth, or view synthesis at training or test time, and leaves the deployment interface unchanged. We introduce a carve-and-hold-out evaluation protocol on the LIBERO-Plus camera track that separates a distribution-matched ceiling from genuine interpolation and extrapolation to held-out viewpoints, with a matched pair-trained control isolating the effect of the consistency term from pair exposure. On held-out orbital viewpoints beyond the training envelope, SCVC improves closed-loop success over the matched control by 12.2 points (95% CI [7.4, 17.0]; +15.5, CI [11.7, 19.4], under an independent second seed) -- an effect two further camera axes replicate -- while interpolation within the envelope shows no gain in either seed (-1.2 and -4.3 points) and in-distribution competence is preserved (-0.6, -0.2). We also report a cross-backbone audit showing that published camera-robustness numbers are confounded by wrist-camera pose stability.

</details>


### [193] [Tolerance-Dependent Inspection Disagreement Between a Fixed CMM and a Portable Articulated-Arm CMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21404)
*Md Manjurul Ahsan,Hamidreza Samadi,Shivakumar Raman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定坐标测量机(CMM)与便携式关节臂CMM在同一检测任务中的互换性问题。通过对圆柱、立方体和球形特征在20°C和30°C条件下的大量测量记录分析，提出了一个"公差映射图"(tolerance map)，用于识别哪些特征-公差组合下更换测量仪器会改变零件的合格判定标签。研究发现固定CMM系统比关节臂系统平均高出约11.2μm，且所有四个形位公差轮廓均落在10μm上限的对侧，证明了平均差异不能作为替换风险的衡量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定式和便携关节臂式坐标测量机常被分配到同一检测任务，但标称精度规格无法说明更换仪器是否会改变零件合格判定。这不仅是两个结果差异有多大的问题，而是该差异是否跨越公差边界的问题，直接影响制造质量控制和仪器替换决策。

Method: 记录圆柱、立方体和球形特征在标称20°C和30°C条件下的测量数据，剔除缺乏足够采集信息的重复记录和粗糙度轮廓后，保留6个尺寸特征和4个形位公差轮廓。对于每个尺寸特征，计算两个系统均值到标称值的距离，定义两个系统获得相反直接标签的精确公差区间，构建公差映射图。

Result: 固定CMM系统在两个温度条件下均比关节臂系统高约11.2μm。所有4个形位公差轮廓均落在记录的10μm上限的对侧。尺寸不一致区间重叠强烈，其平均宽度在20°C为6.573μm，在30°C为4.995μm。这表明平均差异本身不足以评估替换风险。

Conclusion: 提出的公差映射图能识别仪器选择会改变检测标签的特征-公差组合，从而确定在替换仪器前需要进行受控等效性研究和任务特定的不确定度预算的具体场景。平均仪器差异不能单独作为替换风险的衡量指标。

Abstract: Fixed coordinate measuring machines (CMMs) and portable articulated-arm CMMs are often assigned to the same inspection task, but their nominal accuracy specifications do not show whether a change of instrument will preserve the disposition of a part. The question is not simply how far the two results differ, but whether that difference crosses the tolerance boundary. We examined this issue with recorded measurements of cylindrical, cubic, and spherical features under nominal 20 °C and 30 °C conditions. Repeated records and two roughness profiles without sufficient acquisition information were removed, leaving six dimensional and four form profiles. For each dimensional feature, the distances of the two system means from nominal define the exact tolerance interval in which the systems receive opposite direct labels. The fixed-CMM stream was approximately 11.2 μm higher than the articulated-arm stream at both conditions. All four form profiles fell on opposite sides of the recorded 10 μm upper limit. The dimensional disagreement intervals also overlapped strongly; their mean widths were 6.573 μm at 20 °C and 4.995 μm at 30 °C. The results clarify why an average difference between instruments is not, by itself, a measure of substitution risk. The proposed tolerance map identifies the feature-tolerance combinations for which instrument choice can change the recorded inspection label and, therefore, where a controlled equivalence study and a task-specific uncertainty budget are needed before substitution.

</details>


### [194] [Mamba-based Selective State Space Modeling Improves the Accuracy-Complexity Tradeoff of SmolVLA Vision-Language-Action Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21407)
*Farida Mohsen,Thowayba Elkaffash,Mohammad Reza Chalak Qazani,Mohamed Mabrok,Nader Meskin,Ali Safa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在SmolVLA模型的action expert中用Mamba选择性状态空间模型替代因果自注意力，以改善视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在任务成功率与策略调用频率之间的权衡。在LIBERO基准上，Mamba专家在长执行步长（N=50和N=25）下分别超越Transformer基线7.8%和3.7%，在N=1时成功率达到持平的同时将参数复杂度降低24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在单步推理（N=1）时精度高但计算开销大，无法实时部署；而长执行步长（N≫1）虽降低计算复杂度但会损害任务成功率。现有架构无法有效平衡这一精度-复杂度权衡，亟需探索更高效的序列建模方案。

Method: 将SmolVLA模型中的action expert从因果自注意力替换为Mamba的选择性状态空间模型（SSM），在保持视觉骨干和语言对齐不变的前提下，评估两种架构在LIBERO基准上N∈{1,25,50}三种执行步长下的成功率与计算复杂度表现。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上：N=50时Mamba专家超越Transformer基线7.8%；N=25时超越3.7%；N=1时两者成功率持平，但Mamba专家参数减少24%，显著降低了模型复杂度。

Conclusion: Mamba选择性状态空间模型作为VLA action expert的替代架构，能够在长执行步长下保持更高的任务成功率，并显著降低参数复杂度，为VLA模型的实时部署提供了更优的精度-复杂度权衡方案，尤其适用于实际机器人部署场景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models face a crucial tradeoff between their task success rate and the policy-call frequency. Executing a single action per inference ($N=1$) enables accurate robot control but comes at the cost of huge compute time overheads, making real-time implementation infeasible. On the other hand, executing longer action horizons before replanning ($N\gg1$) reduces compute complexity, but inevitably degrades the system's success rate. In order to improve the VLA accuracy-complexity tradeoff, this paper investigates Mamba's selective state-space modeling as an alternative to causal self-attention within the action expert of the popular SmolVLA model, widely used as a reference model for its highly accurate yet low complexity nature. We evaluate both the Mamba- and Transformer-based experts on the widely-adopted LIBERO benchmark suites across three execution horizons $N\!\in\!\{1,25,50\}$, respectively corresponding to high, moderate and low compute complexities. Our results remarkably show that the advantage of the Mamba expert increases with the execution horizon, indicating significant success retention under long execution horizons $N = 50$ and $N = 25$. When $N = 50$ actions are executed before replanning (i.e., corresponding to feasible real-time deployment), the Mamba expert outperforms the Transformer baseline by $7.8\%$. In addition, when $N = 25$ actions are executed before replanning, our Mamba expert outperforms the Transformer baseline by $3.7\%$. Finally, under per-action replanning ($N=1$), our Mamba variant matches the Transformer-based mean success rate while significantly reducing the overall model parameter complexity by $24\%$ thanks to Mamba's compute-efficient nature.

</details>


### [195] [Position: Robot Privacy as Embodied Boundary Work. Connecting Capabilities, Contexts, and Design Responses in Everyday Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21410)
*Liwen He,Shuning Zhang,Chengwen Zhang,Xin Yi,Chun Yu,Jihong Jeung,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身边界隐私"（embodied boundary privacy）框架，以"能力×情境"的视角审视物理在场的机器人如何通过移动、朝向、接近、物体访问、远程在场和社会表达等七种具身能力，在五种部署情境中重塑隐私边界。论文据此提出设计和研究启示，包括边界检查点、视点感知传感控制、远程在场披露、物体和身体层级的访问规则、对社会说服性隐私影响的约束以及本地中断权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人隐私研究多聚焦于数据流、界面设置或一次性同意，而忽视了机器人具身能力通过情境化行动重新塑造空间、身体、社会和关系边界的能力。作者认为需要一种新的隐私框架来捕捉物理在场机器人对隐私边界的动态重塑。

Method: 提出"embodied boundary privacy"概念框架，将隐私风险组织为七种机器人能力（移动、朝向、接近、物体访问、远程在场、社会表达等）与五种部署情境的交叉矩阵，分析具身能力如何跨越隐私边界，以及情境如何影响受影响者、解读方式和争议时机，并据此推导设计启示。

Result: 论文产出一个系统的分析框架及一系列设计/研究启示，呼吁HRI研究、设计和治理将机器人具身行为（移动、朝向、接近等）视为隐私相关行动，其意义取决于具体情境。

Conclusion: 机器人的具身能力通过情境化行动持续重塑隐私边界，隐私治理需要从数据为中心的视角转向能力与情境相结合的分析框架，并开发相应的具身隐私机制。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly entering everyday environments where privacy is shaped not only by data practices, but also by spatial, bodily, social, and relational boundaries. Their embodied capabilities allow them to reshape these boundaries through situated action, challenging privacy framings centered on data flows, interface settings, or one-time consent. Prior work has examined robot privacy through sensing, data collection, telepresence, transparency, consent, bystander awareness, and multi-stakeholder governance. Building on this work, we propose embodied boundary privacy as a capability-by-context framing for examining how physically present robots may reshape privacy boundaries in situated interaction. Specifically, this framing organizes privacy risks across seven robot capabilities and five deployment contexts, asking how embodied capabilities enable boundary crossings and how situated contexts shape who is affected, how these crossings are interpreted, and when they become contested. We use this perspective to outline design and research implications for embodied privacy mechanisms, including boundary checkpoints, viewpoint-aware sensing control, remote-presence disclosure, object- and body-level access rules, constraints on socially persuasive privacy influence, and local interruption rights. We encourage HRI research, design, and governance to treat robot movement, orientation, proximity, object access, remote presence, and social expression as privacy-relevant actions whose meaning depends on context.

</details>


### [196] [Social Graph Mamba: Forecasting Pedestrian Movements Based on Social Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21411)
*Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Social Graph Mamba（SGM），用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）替代注意力机制进行行人轨迹预测，通过动态交互图和社会三元组分解实现线性复杂度，并结合社区感知模块（可微MinCut）发现群体结构，在ETH/UCY和SDD基准上取得与二次复杂度注意力方法相当的性能，且已通过物理机器人实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力机制的轨迹预测方法虽然性能强，但社交交互建模的计算复杂度是二次的，限制可扩展性；同时现有方法在个体层面精度高，却未能充分捕捉真实场景中人群的自然运动行为，尤其是群体结构。

Method: SGM架构包含三部分：1）动态交互图构建，使用社会三元组分解将群体交互顺序解耦；2）用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）替代注意力进行图上社交推理，实现线性序列复杂度；3）社区感知模块通过可微MinCut优化发现群体结构，并将群体归属同时条件化嵌入空间和多模态解码器。

Result: 在ETH/UCY和SDD标准基准上取得与二次复杂度注意力方法相当的竞争力性能，同时保持线性序列复杂度；进一步通过物理机器人实验，将预测轨迹集成到社会力模型（SFM）中实现真实世界部署验证。

Conclusion: SGM证明SSM可以有效替代注意力进行社交交互建模，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度，且群体结构感知能改善真实场景中的预测自然性，并通过机器人实验验证了实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Forecasting pedestrian motion has always been fundamental for autonomous navigation in crowded environments. While attention-based methods achieve strong performance, they suffer from quadratic computational complexity in modeling social interactions, limiting scalability. Additionally, the existing methods often achieve high accuracy on prediction benchmarks at the individual level, but fail to fully capture the natural movement behaviors of crowds in real-world scenarios, particularly group structures. In this study, we propose Social Graph Mamba (SGM), a novel architecture that replaces attention-based social reasoning with Selective State Space Models (SSMs) operating on dynamically constructed interaction graphs. SGM introduces a dynamic interaction graph with social triplet factorization to decompose crowd interactions sequentially, and a community-aware module to effectively discover group structures via differentiable MinCut optimization and conditions both the embedding space and multi-modal decoder on group membership. Our experiments on standard benchmarks (ETH/UCY, SDD) demonstrate competitive performance with linear sequence complexity compared to quadratic attention-based methods. We further validate SGM in physical robot experiments by integrating predicted trajectories into a Social Force Model (SFM) for real-world implementation.

</details>


### [197] [RiskWorld: Object-Centric Latent World Modeling for Autonomous Driving Risk Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21414)
*Jingzheng Li,Yufei Ge,Qianren Mao,Zhijun Chen,Bing Li,Xingyu Peng,Baochang Zhang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskWorld提出一个以对象为中心的潜在世界模型，通过对每个候选对象与自车关系的想象演化来直接定位风险源，而非从场景级事故间接推断。在RiskBench上取得最佳总体F1=63.0%和最低误报率2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶风险识别方法要么预测场景级事故、要么从自车行为间接推断风险对象、要么在轨迹预测后进行几何检查，都没有直接利用预测的自我-对象关系来进行风险源定位。这导致风险识别不够直接和准确。

Method: RiskWorld结合预训练预测性视频表征与结构化的自我-对象历史，语境化观测交互，并使用RSSM风格的潜在动力学将关系感知的对象状态滚动到未来。它解码这个展开以得到对象级风险分数，并由辅助的未来关系和时间风险预测支持。推理时仅用当前时刻之前的观测，而记录的未来用于训练监督。

Result: 在RiskBench上，RiskWorld达到最佳总体F1分数63.0%和最低误报率2.1%。分析显示其学习的滚动展开能在关键事件发生前捕捉对象级风险的演变，且其筛选在过滤观测下保留了规划关键信息。

Conclusion: RiskWorld验证了以对象为中心的潜在世界模型能够通过直接想象自我-对象关系演化来准确定位风险源，优于间接推断和纯几何检查的方法，为自动驾驶风险识别提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous driving risk identification aims to determine which observed object is likely to become safety-critical to the ego vehicle. Existing approaches typically predict scene-level accidents, infer risk objects indirectly from ego behavior, or apply geometric checks after trajectory forecasting, without directly using predicted ego--object relations for risk-source localization. We propose RiskWorld, an object-centric latent world model that identifies risk from the imagined evolution of each candidate relative to the ego vehicle. RiskWorld combines pretrained predictive video representations with structured ego--object histories, contextualizes observed interactions, and rolls relation-aware object states into the future using RSSM-style latent dynamics. It decodes the rollout into object-level risk scores, supported by auxiliary future-relation and temporal-risk predictions. Inference uses only observations up to the current time, while logged futures provide training supervision. On RiskBench, RiskWorld achieves the best overall F1 of 63.0\% and the lowest false-alarm rate of 2.1\%. Further analyses show that the learned rollout captures the evolution of object-level risk before critical events, while RiskWorld's selections preserve planning-critical information under filtered observation.

</details>


### [198] [Operational digital twin clinics enable task-based evaluation of embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21416)
*Xinyuan Wu,Jingrao Zhang,Mengdi Xu,Henry K. Chu,Mingguang He,Danli Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用常规临床照片构建可操作的"数字孪生"环境，用于具身AI在临床环境的任务化评估。在39个眼科诊所场景中，将单张照片转换为可编辑、可用于模拟器的环境，并验证了重建质量、房间级几何、网格接地、多机器人可行性、扰动敏感性和闭环策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI必须在真实临床环境中测试，但构建真实且可被机器人测试的环境成本高昂且难以扩展。需要一种低成本、可扩展的方法来生成临床环境的可测试数字孪生。

Method: 使用39个眼科诊所场景，将单张照片转换为可编辑、模拟器就绪的环境。评估维度包括重建质量、房间级几何、网格接地（mesh grounding）、多机器人可行性、扰动敏感性和闭环策略性能。通过设备网格、碰撞代理和语义锚点将视觉重建转化为接触感知的模拟场景。

Result: 重建场景保留了工作空间结构，局部编辑支持受控设备重配置。三种机器人形态下，共享任务目标表现出不同的可达性和接触可行性模式。小幅设备平移旋转产生任务特定的接触余量变化，这些变化无法仅通过视觉相似性捕获。数字孪生轨迹支持局部策略学习和闭环评估。

Conclusion: 建立了临床数字孪生的操作有效性（operational validity）原则，为医疗具身AI的离线开发与物理部署之间提供了中间层。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence (AI) must be tested in the clinical environments where it will operate, but building realistic, robot-testable settings is costly and difficult to scale. Here we show that routine clinic images can be transformed into operational digital twins for task-based evaluation of embodied AI. Using 39 ophthalmic clinic scenes, we converted single photographs into editable, simulator-ready environments and assessed reconstruction quality, room-scale geometry, mesh grounding, multi-robot feasibility, perturbation sensitivity and closed-loop policy performance. The reconstructed scenes preserved workspace structure, while local editing enabled controlled device reconfiguration. Device meshes, collision proxies and semantic anchors converted visual reconstructions into contact-aware simulation scenes. Across three robot embodiments, shared task targets showed different patterns of reachability and contact feasibility. Small device translations and rotations produced task-specific changes in contact margins that were not captured by visual similarity alone. Digital-twin trajectories also supported local policy learning and closed-loop evaluation. These findings establish operational validity as a key principle for clinical digital twins and provide an intermediate layer between offline development and physical deployment of embodied AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [199] [Evaluating Human and LLM-Generated Thematic Analysis in HRI for Vulnerable Populations: A Comparative and Ethical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21420)
*Alva Markelius,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Julie Bailey,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对人机交互（HRI）研究中的脆弱人群，进行了人类生成与LLM生成的主题分析（TA）的对比研究，评估两者在客观与语义一致性上的差异，并探讨LLM生成的TA是否可能边缘化或歪曲脆弱参与者的经历，提出伦理与有效性方面的关切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题分析传统上被认为是人类固有的反思性与解释性过程，但LLM辅助或生成的TA在HRI涉及脆弱人群研究中的适用性尚未被系统检验，其有效性与伦理问题需要批判性审视。

Method: 采用比较研究方法，在HRI背景下对脆弱人群的质性数据分别进行人类生成与LLM生成的TA，评估主题的客观一致性与语义一致性，并分析观察到的分歧是否反映具有伦理意义的系统性解释模式。

Result: 研究揭示了人类与LLM生成主题之间存在分歧，并考察了这些分歧是否可能对脆弱参与者的经验造成边缘化或误表征，为LLM辅助TA在HRI中的使用提供证据基础。

Conclusion: LLM生成的TA在HRI脆弱人群研究中存在伦理与有效性风险，研究者若采用LLM辅助TA需对其解释性局限保持警惕，并考虑人类监督的必要性。

Abstract: Thematic analysis (TA) has long been regarded as an inherently human, reflexive, and interpretive process. However, the extent to which LLM-generated TA is appropriate for Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) research involving vulnerable populations remains largely unexamined and raises critical questions about validity and ethics, particularly in sensitive research contexts. This paper presents a comparative study of human- and LLM-generated TA in an HRI context with a focus on vulnerable populations. We evaluate both objective and semantic agreement between human- and LLMgenerated themes, and examine whether observed divergences reflect systematic interpretive patterns with ethical significance. Our analysis investigates whether LLM-generated TA risks marginalising or misrepresenting the experiences of vulnerable participants, with implications for researchers employing LLM-assisted TA in HRI.

</details>


### [200] [The Setting of IMU Parameters in Kalman Filtering-based Information Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21433)
*Qiang Hu,Yanhua Zou,Shuaiyi Huo,Haibo Ge,Wei Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在卡尔曼滤波框架下，基于Allan方差标定来设置IMU参数的方法。通过利用功率谱密度与Allan方差之间的关系来构建连续时间滤波中的过程不确定性，并在两个典型的IMU传感器融合系统中验证了该参数设置方法的可行性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMU参数（噪声和偏置不稳定性）的设置在传感器融合中非常棘手，因为IMU的实际工作条件比静止标定场景复杂得多。静止条件下标定的噪声和偏置不稳定性无法适应其他工况，导致IMU参数的有效调整很大程度上依赖于经验或对系统的深入理解，缺乏系统化的方法。

Method: 基于Allan方差标定来设置IMU参数，利用功率谱密度（PSD）与Allan方差之间的关系，在连续时间滤波框架中构建过程不确定性模型，将Allan方差标定结果系统性地映射到卡尔曼滤波器的过程噪声协方差中。

Result: 通过两个典型的IMU传感器融合系统（具体细节在摘要中未展开）验证了该参数设置方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Allan方差标定的IMU参数设置方法为传感器融合提供了更加系统和可靠的参数调优途径，减少了对经验调参的依赖。

Abstract: The setting or tuning of specifications for the inertial measurement unit (IMU) is tricky in sensor fusion. The underneath conundrum is caused by the fact that the working condition of IMU is more complex than the stationary calibration scenario. Since the noises and biases instabilities calibrated under static condition cannot accommodate other cases, the effective tuning of IMU parameters largely hinges on the experience or profound understanding of the system. In the current work, the setting method of IMU parameters based on Allan variance calibration is delved into within the Kalman filtering framework. Specifically, the relationship between the power sepctral density and Allan variance is leveraged in formulating the process uncertainty in continuous-time filtering. Two typical IMU-based sensor fusion systems are considered to show the feasibility and effectiveness of this parameter setting process.

</details>


### [201] [Geo-VLA: Geometry-Aware Vision-Language-Action Planning via Internalization of Map Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21440)
*Ran Chen,Jiaxing Ren,Zhikun Zhang,Yunhao Hou,Junbao Zhuo,Bochao Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Geo-VLA是一个即插即用的框架，通过几何感知的视觉表征学习来增强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自动驾驶模型。它在训练时注入地图几何语义（无需HD地图或额外车道信息推理），引入Geo-QA几何问答数据集，通过对比学习和指令微调将道路几何信息内化到视觉-语言表征中。在NAVSIM v1上达到92.1 PDMS，刷新了单相机VLA规划器的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在复杂驾驶环境中规划性能有限，因为纯图像表征无法充分捕捉与规划相关的道路几何和拓扑结构。

Method: Geo-VLA框架：(1) 训练阶段学习几何感知的视觉表征，内化几何地图语义；(2) 引入Geo-QA数据集，通过对比学习和指令微调将道路几何注入视觉-语言表征；(3) 推理时无需HD地图或额外车道信息，即插即用增强现有VLA模型。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1基准上，Geo-VLA一致性地提升了具有不同动作生成架构的VLA规划器，达到92.1 PDMS，成为单相机VLA规划器中的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的视觉表征学习，可以显著提升VLA模型在自动驾驶规划中的表现，且无需在推理时依赖外部地图信息，是一种高效且通用的增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by leveraging foundation models for semantic reasoning and long-tail generalization. However, their planning performance remains limited in complex driving environments because image-only representations inadequately capture planning-relevant road geometry and topology. In this paper, we propose Geo-VLA, a plug-and-play framework that enhances VLA models by learning geometry-aware visual representations. During training, Geo-VLA internalizes geometric map semantics to strengthen road-structure representations, while requiring no HD maps or additional lane information during inference. To support this approach, we introduce Geo-QA, a geometry-focused question-answering dataset that injects road geometry into vision-language representations through contrastive learning and instruction tuning. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 demonstrate that Geo-VLA consistently improves VLA planners with distinct action-generation architectures, achieving 92.1 PDMS and establishing a new state-of-the-art among single-camera VLA planners.

</details>


### [202] [Constructing Predictive Surgical Path for AI-based Capsulorhexis Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21441)
*Mohammad Javad Ahmadi,Hamid D. Taghirad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于外科手术训练的人工智能平台，结合ARAS-Farabi注释数据集和预训练的深度卷积神经网络，可从手术器械运动数据中提取技能特征，为受训外科医生生成改进的运动路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化外科医生培训可显著降低手术训练风险和成本。AI作为"第三方代理人"在训练早期介入，首先通过建议改进手术路径来辅助训练者，最终目标是从辅助代理人演进为AI训练师。

Method: 使用JIGSAWS和ARAS-Farabi数据集预训练深度CNN，从手术器械尖端运动数据中提取技能特征；构建专家运动轨迹的特征空间参考模型，通过包含两个损失函数的优化过程生成改进路径，在提升受训者技能水平的同时预测并保持其手术意图。

Result: 在AI代理辅助下，受训外科医生的运动路径可在保持手术意图的同时至少提升20%的技能水平；此外还开发了多种有形指标以验证受训者改进程度。

Conclusion: 该平台证明了AI作为第三方辅助代理可以有效增强外科手术训练过程，为未来AI从辅助者演进为独立训练者提供了可行性验证。

Abstract: Automated training of surgeons is one of the most crucial factors that significantly minimize surgical training risks and expenses. With recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) knowledge and available data from various surgeries, AI's involvement in surgical training is becoming very promising. It is recommended that at the early stages of AI development, it interferes in the surgery as a third agent alongside the trainer. As trust in AI increases, this process will lead to an AI agent acting as a trainer in the future. The first phase in which AI can intervene in the training process is to suggest an improved surgical path to the trainer. A platform must be constructed in the first step, to accomplish this task and to enhance the movement path of trainee surgeons. This paper introduces this platform along with an annotated capsulorhexis surgery dataset called the ARAS-Farabi dataset. In this research, a deep convolutional neural network is pre-trained with JIGSAWS and ARAS-Farabi surgical datasets that can extract surgical skill characteristics from surgery tool tip motion data. The proposed platform develops a reference model from the feature space of an expert surgeon's movement trajectory and proposes an improved path to enhance the skill of a novice surgeon. An optimization with two loss functions is utilized to create a path that raises the skill level of the novice surgeon's path while simultaneously predicting and preserving his/her intent. The results of this study reveal that, with the assistance of an AI agent, the trainee surgeon's movement path can be enhanced by at least 20 percent while maintaining his intentional objective. In addition to the recommended deep network, various tangible indicators have also been developed in this research to verify the level of trainee improvement.

</details>


### [203] [Model-Free Adaptive Parameter Tuning for Efficient Multi-Robot Warehouse Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21533)
*Pratap Tokekar,Mouhacine Benosman,Rahul Chandan,Alexandre Ormiga Galvao Barbosa,Michael Caldara,Joseph W. Durham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于极值搜索控制（ESC）的自适应参数调优框架，用于自动调节多机器人履约中心（FC）中检索任务（digout）规划器的参数。通过正弦扰动信号和相关性梯度估计，ESC能在无需模型的情况下实时调整规划器参数以最大化吞吐量，替代人工离线调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履约中心的digout规划器使用参数化代价函数控制遮挡pod的处理方式，产生从"移至其他块"到"块内重排"的策略谱，不同工作点对地面拥塞和吞吐量影响不同。最优工作点随设施配置和运营条件（如站需求变化、拥塞模式）而变化，离线调参不切实际。

Method: 基于极值搜索控制（ESC）的自适应参数调优框架：通过正弦抖动信号扰动规划器参数，并将扰动与吞吐量变化相关联以估计梯度，实现无模型优化。该方法能应对大型FC运营中多分钟延迟效应和信用分配（credit assignment）挑战。

Result: 仿真实验表明，自适应策略相比固定策略在多种条件下均有提升：跨地图和机器人车队规模变化时吞吐量平均提升5.0%，动态运营条件下提升8.4%。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了人工参数配置需求，实现实时自适应，为FC存储运营提供了自调优范式。

Abstract: Robotic Fulfillment Centers (FCs) store inventory on shelves (pods) arranged in dense blocks. Retrieving a target pod that is buried deep in a block requires moving obstructing pods out of the way (i.e., digout). Multi-robot planners use parameterized cost functions to control digout behavior, producing a spectrum of strategies: at one extreme, obstructing pods are sent to other blocks (using more robots in travel lanes); at the other, pods are shuffled within the block (avoiding lane congestion but increasing extraction time). Each point on this spectrum has different downstream consequences for floor congestion and throughput. The optimal operating point depends on the specific facility configuration and shifts with operational conditions such as varying station demand and congestion patterns, making offline tuning impractical. We present an adaptive parameter tuning framework based on Extremum Seeking Control (ESC) that continuously adjusts planner parameters in response to measured throughput. ESC performs model-free optimization by perturbing parameters with sinusoidal dither signals and correlating perturbations with performance changes to estimate gradients, making it robust to the multi-minute delayed effects and credit assignment challenges inherent in large FC operations. Simulation studies demonstrate that the adaptive policy improves upon fixed policies across several conditions. We observe an improvement in throughput by an average of 5.0% across map and robot fleet size variations, and by 8.4% under dynamic operating conditions. This work eliminates manual parameter provisioning and enables real-time adaptation, providing a self-tuning paradigm for FC storage operations.

</details>


### [204] [GOLEM: Modular Humanoid Autonomy Towards Electric Vehicle Battery Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21550)
*Max Conway,William Xie,Allen Devaraj,Yutong Zhang,Niraj Pudasaini,Mateo Feit,Adam Abid,Zachary Allen,Chen Liu,Xuan Tan,Jensen Lavering,Jason Chen,Lyle Antieau,Anthony Von Pischke,Alessandro Roncone,Zachary Sunberg,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOLEM是一个开源的人形机器人端到端系统架构，用于电动汽车电池包拆解。它基于Unitree H1-2机器人，采用Docker化的ROS 2抽象，将行走、操作、动态稳定、导航和空间记忆作为独立模块，并通过MuJoCo和IsaacLab数字孪生实现仿真到现实的开发。系统在真实Hyundai Ioniq 5电池包上验证了拆解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车电池包拆解对人类来说单调且危险，目前几乎全部由人工完成。需要一个可扩展、可组合的系统架构，使不同的感知、导航、操作和稳定控制方法能够被独立开发、替换和公平比较，从而推动人形机器人在这一危险工业场景中的应用。

Method: 采用模块化架构设计，将行走、操作、动态稳定性、导航和空间记忆作为具有抽象接口的独立模块。基于Docker的ROS 2框架，MuJoCo和IsaacLab数字孪生提供与物理机器人匹配的接口。通过"能力阶梯"评估方法逐个模块表征性能：LiDAR-惯性导航、学习型站立控制器、以及抓取松动的紧固件操作。

Result: LiDAR惯性导航使机器人在6米目标处定位误差为13.0cm；学习型站立控制器在外部扰动下优于基于采样的下半身MPC；在真实Hyundai Ioniq 5电池包上抓取松动紧固件的成功率从有线97%降至自由站立87%，加入导航诱导位姿方差后降至37%。

Conclusion: GOLEM展示了一种可组合、可定制的人形机器人在电池包拆解任务中的端到端部署路径，提供了从仿真到现实的完整验证，并为不同机器人模块提供了公平比较的方法论。

Abstract: Disassembling end-of-life electric vehicle (EV) battery packs is dull and dangerous work, performed almost entirely by humans. We present GOLEM (Generalized Open Library of Embodied Modules), an end-to-end, open-source system architecture for EV battery disassembly with the Unitree H1-2 humanoid robot in which walking, manipulation, dynamic stability, navigation, and spatial memory are independent modules with abstract interfaces, so that methods are easily developed, interchanged, and compared. GOLEM is deployed as a Docker-based ROS 2 abstraction in which MuJoCo and IsaacLab digital twins expose interfaces matching the physical robot. GOLEM's composability and per-module customization enable development and demonstration of humanoid EV battery disassembly, from simulation to reality. GOLEM provides fair comparison between humanoid modules, enabling evaluation as a capability ladder, in which one module is characterized at a time and added as a rung: LiDAR-inertial navigation places the robot within 13.0cm of a 6m goal; a learned standing controller recovers from external disturbances that sampling-based lower-body MPC does not; and grasping loosened fasteners from a real Hyundai Ioniq 5 pack degrades from 97% tethered to 87% free-standing to 37% under navigation-induced pose variance. Source code is available at the project page https://golem-humanoid.github.io

</details>


### [205] [Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Task Assignment Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21554)
*Daniel Garces,Sara Castro,Adrian Haimovich,Byron Crowe,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构多机器人服务的预测感知自适应rollout框架，处理带预约和实时请求的任务分配问题。该问题被建模为有限时域随机动态规划，策略通过采样未来请求场景评估当前分配，并结合自适应重加权、选择性重优化以及基于历史数据的车队组成选择来提升鲁棒性。在真实医院护理任务数据上实现了接近完整的服务率，并显著降低了服务请求的等待时间，特别是在尾延迟指标上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人服务系统需要在动态到达的新任务下持续进行任务分配与调度，而历史数据可用于预测未来需求，但过度依赖不准的预测在分布漂移下会降低性能。需要一种既能利用预测又对预测误差鲁棒的方法。

Method: 将问题建模为有限时域随机动态规划，包含机器人-任务兼容性、有序服务需求、路径约束、服务窗口和时段末返回要求。策略使用采样未来请求场景评估当前分配，限制立即承诺仅针对已观测请求。结合剪枝候选控制、等待动作和交互感知基策略进行未来成本估计；通过基于近期预测不匹配的自适应重加权和选择性重优化来提供对预测误差的鲁棒性；并引入基于历史数据的异构车队组成选择流程。

Result: 在真实医院住院楼层护理任务数据的案例研究中，该方法相比反应式、令牌传递、预测定位和贪婪基线实现了接近完整的服务率，并降低了服务请求的等待时间，在尾延迟指标上改进最大。

Conclusion: 预测感知的自适应rollout框架能够有效利用历史预测信息同时保持对预测误差的鲁棒性，在真实场景中显著优于传统基线方法，验证了方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot service systems must assign requests to compatible robots, construct feasible schedules, and adapt as new tasks arrive online. Historical data can help anticipate future demand, but relying too heavily on inaccurate predictions can degrade performance under distribution shifts. We develop a prediction-aware adaptive rollout framework for heterogeneous multi-robot task assignment with scheduled and real-time requests. The problem is formulated as a finite-horizon stochastic dynamic program incorporating robot-task compatibility, ordered service requirements, routing constraints, service windows, and end-of-horizon return requirements. The proposed policy evaluates current assignments using sampled future request scenarios while restricting immediate commitments to requests already observed. To enable online use, the framework combines pruned candidate controls, wait actions, and an interaction-aware base policy for efficient future-cost estimation. Robustness to forecast error is provided by adaptively reweighting predicted requests based on recent prediction mismatch and selectively re-optimizing assigned but unstarted requests. We also introduce a historical-data-driven procedure for selecting the heterogeneous fleet composition before deployment. In a case study using real nursing-task requests from hospital inpatient floors, the proposed approach achieves near-complete service and reduces serviced-request wait times relative to reactive, token-passing, prediction-positioning, and myopic greedy baselines, with the largest improvements in tail-delay metrics.

</details>


### [206] [Betting for Sim-to-Real Performance Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21572)
*Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"sim-to-real betting certificate"框架，利用大量仿真结果对真实实验结果进行下注，通过累积的下注财富收缩性能证书（置信区间）。在样本有限（≤30）的情况下，证书宽度相比经典和SOTA基线平均缩小32.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人测试成本高昂，样本量小导致性能证书（置信区间）通常很宽松。作者观察到"真实试验前先窥探仿真结果"这一直觉，希望利用丰富的仿真数据来压缩小样本下的置信区间。

Method: (i) 设计算法将大规模仿真器库与有效下注关联起来，通过累积下注财富生成证书；(ii) 证明返回的证书满足任意时刻有效性（anytime validity），无论使用什么仿真器库都能以规定概率覆盖真实均值；(iii) 证明财富遗憾界（wealth-regret bounds），为算法参数和仿真器库设计提供配置原则以产出紧致证书。

Result: 在合成分布和真实机器人测试（包括回放标准化测试和在线运行时评估）中，相比经典与SOTA基线，证书宽度平均收窄51.6%±16%；在极有限样本（≤30）场景下收窄32.26%±8%。

Conclusion: 利用仿真数据作为先验信息，通过下注机制可以在小样本真实测试下显著收紧性能证书，具备理论保证（anytime validity）和实际部署能力。

Abstract: Consider a typical test of a robot system: one observes a sequence of outcomes concerning some aspect of interest (crash or no crash, tracking error, time to completion), and reports a mean (crash risk, average error, mean time to completion) and, more importantly, an interval guaranteed to contain that mean at a prescribed confidence, referred to as a performance certificate. Given expensive real-world trials, the sample size is therefore small, and the certificate is often loose. Now consider the same procedure, except that before each real outcome is revealed, the operator ``peeks'' at a large bank of simulated results, and places a bet on where the real outcome will land. As the real outcomes settle the bets, the operator gains or loses wealth. One's ``trust'' over simulators also shifts within the portfolio. This paper develops that idea into a sim-to-real betting certificate framework with three contributions: (i) An algorithm that links a scalable bank of simulators to effective bets, and the accumulated betting wealth to the certificate. (ii) A proof that the returned certificate is anytime valid, covering the true mean with the prescribed probability, using any simulator bank. (iii) The guaranteed wealth-regret bounds yield configuration principles for the proposed algorithm and simulator bank design to deliver tight certificates. Experiments across synthetic distributions and real-world robot tests, covering both replayed standardized testing outcomes and online runtime evaluation, show the proposed method narrows the certificate by $51.6\%\pm16\%$ against classic and state-of-the-art baselines, and by $32.26\%\pm8\%$ in the extremely limited-sample regime ($\leq30$ samples).

</details>


### [207] [Force/Torque-Based Kinematic Adaptation for Robotic Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21592)
*Carl Glen Henshaw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应运动学估计方案，利用关节角传感和腕部力/力矩传感器，在线辨识机器人夹持的未知工具的运动学参数，无需外部感知工具尖端。推导了可证明稳定的运动学更新律，并通过QP统一形式化了估计与控制的耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
