In this work, we propose a Real2Sim2Real pipeline that improves Sim2Real transfer for torque-controlled robotic arms by combining trajectory matching, parameter optimization via genetic algorithms, and domain randomization. Using the 7-DOF Franka Emika Panda robot, we first identify friction, inertia, and gravity compensation parameters by minimizing the error between real and simulated joint trajectories. These calibrated dynamics are then used to train a TQC-based reinforcement learning agent in simulation. The trained policy is evaluated in both Gazebo and MuJoCo environments, and finally deployed on the real robot. Our results demonstrate a significant improvement in tracking accuracy and policy robustness after parameter tuning, with smooth policy transfer from simulation to the Real-World across multiple target-reaching tasks. This work highlights the effectiveness of accurate physical modelling in enabling stable and generalizable torque-based reinforcement learning policies.

</details>


### [241] [Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22657)
*Xinyuan Liu,Eren Sadikoglu,Riana Chatterjee,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Physical Agentic AI 框架，在语义规划与物理执行之间插入显式架构接口：每个机器人暴露类型化可执行技能库，非执行型 Mission Planner 基于 LLM 分解任务，确定性 Robot Orchestrator 逐技能验证和授权。在无人机-UGV 搜索-派遣任务（Gazebo 实况仿真）及人形-四足运输任务（包含 Unitree G1/Go2 实物试运行）上评估，证明检索将技能接地从 51% 提升至 96%，但即便信息充分的规划器仍派遣 23-29% 的错误步骤；逐派遣强制检查将错误派遣降至 0%，且无错误阻塞。消融实验确认是门控而非计划变化起作用，全部 8 个注入故障在无强制时跨越编排边界并产生机器人运动，有强制时全部在运动前被拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人物理团队（drone-UGV、人形-四足等异构机队）在执行开放式任务时，LLM 规划器即使拥有更丰富的具身能力、物理前置条件和跨机器人协调信息，仍会产出不可行、时机错误或不安全的物理动作。现有框架缺少语义规划与物理执行之间的显式接口层来验证每个计划动作。

Method: 提出 Physical Agentic AI：机器人暴露类型化可执行技能库；非执行型 Mission Planner 利用基础模型将任务分解为阶段并分配机器人-技能对；Robot Orchestration 层向规划器暴露技能库、机器人状态、命名位置和工作流契约；确定性 Robot Orchestrator 在每次执行前逐技能验证和授权（gate）。独立改变规划器知识与运行时强制两个变量进行对比实验。

Result: 检索将技能接地准确率从 51% 提升至 96%，但信息充分的规划器仍派遣 23-29% 的错误步骤；逐派遣强制（per-dispatch enforcement）将错误派遣降至 0% 且无误阻塞；held-plan 消融确认是门控而非计划变化起决定作用。实物运行中，无强制时 8 个注入故障全部跨越编排边界且 6 个产生机器人运动，有强制时全部在运动前被拒绝。

Conclusion: 语义规划与物理执行之间需要显式的确定性编排/门控接口，仅靠增强规划器知识与检索不足以保证物理安全；架构层面的逐技能验证与授权是实现可靠多机器人物理编排的关键。

Abstract: Agentic AI frameworks interpret open-ended task goals and decompose them into multi-step plans. Richer information about embodiment-specific capabilities, physical preconditions, and cross-robot coordination improves grounding, but does not eliminate infeasible, mistimed, or unsafe physical actions. Physical robot crews therefore require an explicit architectural interface between semantic planning and execution, where every planned action is verified against robot capabilities, system state, and workflow constraints before actuation. This paper introduces Physical Agentic AI, a framework for skill-grounded robot agent orchestration, in which each robot exposes a typed library of executable skills while a foundation model planner decomposes a task into phases and assigns each phase to a robot-skill pair. A Robot Orchestration layer exposes the skill library, robot state, named locations, and workflow contracts to a non-actuating Mission Planner, while a deterministic Robot Orchestrator validates and authorizes one skill at a time. We evaluate on a drone-UGV search-and-dispatch mission, where every mission in every condition is executed live in Gazebo, and on a humanoid-quadruped transportation task using hardware-equivalent skill interfaces plus two physical trials on a Unitree G1 and Go2. Varying planner knowledge and runtime enforcement independently, we find that retrieval raises skill grounding from 51% to 96% yet leaves informed planners dispatching 23-29% of faulted steps. Per-dispatch enforcement reduces false dispatch to 0% with no false blocks, and a held-plan ablation confirms that the gate, not plan variation, is responsible. Live execution makes the difference physical: without enforcement all eight injected faults crossed the orchestration boundary and six produced robot motion; with enforcement all eight were refused before motion.

</details>


### [242] [Exact Finite-Length Theory of Uniform Car Parking: Spatial Laws, Absorption, and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22671)
*Ganesh P Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发展了均匀停车场过程（一维随机顺序吸附）的精确有限长度严格理论，将停放位置的联合密度分解为堵塞单元，通过子集递归在O(2^n n)内求值；边际与间隙次序统计量以超对数表示；吸收计数与聚合量通过Rényi积分方程处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀停车场过程（Rényi's car-parking）是随机顺序吸附和统计物理中的经典模型，但其有限长度的精确理论此前尚不完整，特别是在联合密度结构和高效计算方面。

Method: 将停放位置的联合密度解析为堵塞单元（jamming cells），逐单元为有理函数；采用O(2^n n)的子集递归计算；通过超对数（hyperlogarithms）刻画边际与间隙阶统计量；基于Rényi积分方程处理吸收计数和聚合量。

Result: 给出了均匀停车场过程有限长度情形的完整体系统计理论：联合密度精确表达式、高效子集递归算法、阶统计量的超对数表示，以及与经典Rényi结果一致的积分方程处理。

Conclusion: 本文为均匀停车场过程建立了完整的有限长度精确理论框架，兼具数学严密性与计算可行性，为该经典随机模型的研究提供了系统性的工具。

Abstract: The uniform car-parking process is the one-dimensional random sequential adsorption of unit cars on a segment of finite length $s$: cars arrive at uniformly random positions and park wherever they fit, until no gap admits another. This paper develops the exact finite-$s$ theory. The joint density of the parked positions is resolved into jamming cells, on each of which it is a rational function, and evaluated by a subset recursion in $O(2^n n)$ operations; the marginal and gap order statistics are obtained as hyperlogarithms whose weight is fixed by the number of coordinates integrated out; and the absorption count and the aggregate quantities are treated through the integral equation descending from Rényi.

</details>


### [243] [VikPath: A Vision Kansformer Framework for Effective Obstacle Avoidance in Self-Supervised Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22675)
*Junyao Wang,Yulin Xu,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Design，一个自监督路径规划框架，通过新颖的Vision Kansformer模块在无标签轨迹的情况下学习障碍分布表征，并结合急转弯惩罚来同时优化障碍物距离和轨迹平滑度，在保证平滑路径生成的同时相比SOTA方法提升3.28%的障碍物间隙并降低87.07%的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式搜索算法（如A*）在复杂障碍环境中效率会大幅下降；现有学习式路径规划方法依赖监督学习，受标注质量限制，且主要优化路径长度而忽视障碍物间隙和轨迹平滑度，导致生成的路径在实际环境中难以安全执行。

Method: 提出自监督路径规划框架Design，核心是Vision Kansformer模块，无需标注轨迹即可学习障碍分布表征；引入sharp-turn penalty以鼓励生成更平滑、更实用的路径，联合优化障碍物距离和路径平滑度。

Result: 与SOTA方法相比，Design在保持平滑路径生成的同时，实现了平均3.28%的更大障碍物间隙和87.07%的更低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过自监督学习和Kansformer架构，Design在无需标注轨迹的情况下实现了更安全、更平滑且更高效的路径规划，解决了现有方法依赖监督和忽视安全性的局限。

Abstract: Pathfinding is a fundamental problem in artificial intelligence and autonomous systems. Traditional heuristic-based algorithms, such as A*, rely on predefined heuristic functions to guide the search process. Although effective in structured environments, their search efficiency can degrade substantially in complex, obstacle-rich scenarios, where handcrafted heuristics may provide limited guidance. Recent studies have explored learning-based approaches to improve pathfinding efficiency; however, most existing methods rely on supervised learning and require labels generated by conventional planners or obtained through manual annotation. As a result, their performance is inherently influenced by the quality of the underlying supervision and may degrade when the labeling heuristics fail to capture complex environmental structures. Moreover, existing methods primarily optimize for path length while paying limited attention to obstacle clearance and trajectory smoothness, which can lead to paths that are difficult or unsafe to execute in real-world environments. To address these limitations, we propose $\Design$, a self-supervised pathfinding framework that jointly considers obstacle proximity and path smoothness. At its core, our novel \textit{Vision Kansformer} module learns representations of obstacle distributions without relying on labeled trajectories, enabling the model to better adapt to complex environments. We further introduce a sharp-turn penalty to encourage smoother and more practically executable paths. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, $\Design$ achieves an average of 3.28\% greater obstacle clearance and 87.07\% lower inference latency while maintaining smooth path generation.

</details>


### [244] [RACO: Reliability-Aware Coarse-Goal Optimization for Inspection-Oriented UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22678)
*Sen Wang,Yiming Sun,Jiaxuan He,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LG-UVI物体中心巡检评估基准（基于CityNav/CityRefer构建），并针对UAV-VLN巡检中粗粒度目标不可靠导致局部细化失效的问题，提出RACO（可靠性感知自适应粗到细导航框架）。RACO将预测的粗目标视为运行时假设，通过物体级候选锚点在校准点进行检测修正，并采用尺度自适应末端细化处理接近漏检情况。实验显示RACO在验证和测试unseen集上较HETT基线SR提升9.53和7.98个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN以目标到达（goal reaching）作为评估标准，但实际巡检部署要求智能体在有效巡检区域内停下并避免误确认视觉/语义相似的干扰物。现有coarse-to-fine策略的粗阶段预测目标常被视为可靠，但可能漂移至看似合理却错误的目标区域，限制局部细化阶段的恢复能力。这一关键弱点被现有评估体系忽视。

Method: 1) 提出LG-UVI评估设置：扩展CityNav/CityRefer，加入目标物体、困难干扰物、类型感知巡检区域，以及巡检区域到达和物体级确认的诊断指标。2) 提出RACO框架：将粗目标视为运行时假设而非固定路点，在Stage 1前及Stage 1到Stage 2边界使用物体级候选锚点检查和修正粗定位；并采用尺度自适应末端细化处理终端near-miss情况，利用运行时可观测的几何和锚点证据。

Result: 在统一在线评估协议下，RACO在validation-unseen和test-unseen上分别比复现的HETT基线SR提升9.53和7.98个百分点；同时改善巡检区域到达率并降低误确认风险，证明粗目标可靠性优化是对现有coarse-to-fine UAV-VLN策略的有效补充。

Conclusion: 粗目标可靠性的自适应校验与修正能显著提升UAV-VLN在巡检场景中的表现，是现有coarse-to-fine策略的重要互补方向。LG-UVI为该问题提供了系统化的评测平台。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) is commonly evaluated as goal reaching, but inspection-oriented deployment requires the agent to stop within a valid inspection region and avoid falsely confirming visually or semantically similar distractors. This requirement exposes a key weakness in existing coarse-to-fine UAV-VLN policies: the coarse goal predicted before local refinement is often treated as reliable, although it may drift toward plausible but incorrect object regions and limit the ability of the local stage to recover. To systematically evaluate this problem, we introduce LG-UVI, an object-centric inspection evaluation setting derived from CityNav/CityRefer. LG-UVI extends standard UAV-VLN episodes with target objects, hard distractors, type-aware inspection regions, and diagnostics for inspection-region arrival and object-level confirmation. To address this inspection-oriented setting, we further propose RACO, a reliability-aware adaptive coarse-to-fine navigation framework. Instead of treating the predicted coarse goal as a fixed waypoint, RACO views it as a runtime hypothesis and uses object-level candidate anchors to check and correct coarse localization before Stage 1 and at the Stage 1-to-Stage 2 boundary. RACO also applies scale-adaptive terminal refinement to handle terminal near-miss cases using runtime-observable geometric and anchor-based evidence. Under a unified online evaluation protocol, RACO improves SR over the reproduced HETT baseline by 9.53 and 7.98 percentage points on validation-unseen and test-unseen, respectively. It also improves inspection-region arrival and reduces false verification risk, showing that coarse-goal reliability optimization is an effective complement to existing coarse-to-fine UAV-VLN policies.

</details>


### [245] [Physics Filtering Favors the Generalization of Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22701)
*Jindou Jia,Shixuan Han,Meng Wang,Gen Li,Zihan Yang,Sicheng Zhou,Kexin Guo,Jianfei Yang,Xiang Yu,Wei Wang,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyFilter，一种基于物理滤波的反馈机制模块，通过物理约束校正学习残差，使机器人即使在小规模训练数据下也能在动力学不确定性环境中实现泛化，并在四足机器人、无人机、空中机械臂和加速度微分器四个系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人泛化方法依赖大规模数据收集，但机器人领域收集与大规模语言模型相当规模的演示数据成本过高、速度过慢。需要一种不依赖海量数据即可实现泛化的替代方案。

Method: PhyFilter是一个轻量级、模型无关的反馈模块，利用物理滤波器校正学习的输出残差。其参数通过自动学习算法优化，无需人工调参，可无缝集成到各种机器人策略中。

Result: 在四个代表性机器人系统上验证：四足机器人泛化到未见地形、负载变化和速度范围；无人机在未见风力扰动下飞行；空中机械臂在风和质料不确定下实现厘米级空中捕获；加速度微分器在分布偏移下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于物理滤波的反馈机制可以成为大规模数据扩展的有力替代方案，使机器人能够在小数据条件下应对动力学不确定性实现泛化。

Abstract: Living organisms exhibit extraordinary adaptability to unseen environments through their intrinsic physical structures and lifelong feedback-driven learning. Endowing robots with comparable generalization is critical for reliable operation in the real world. While recent approaches attempt to improve generalization by scaling training data, such strategies remain impractical for robotics, where collecting real-world demonstrations at the scale of large language models is prohibitively costly and slow. Contrary to this reliance on massive datasets, we show that robots can generalize effectively under dynamics uncertainties even with limited training data by leveraging a feedback mechanism, namely PhyFilter, that corrects learning outputs with physics-filtered learning residuals. PhyFilter operates as a lightweight, model-agnostic module whose parameters can be automatically optimized through an auto-learning algorithm, eliminating manual tuning and enabling seamless integration with diverse robot policies. We validate PhyFilter across four representative robotic systems, demonstrating that it enables quadruped robots to generalize to unseen terrains, payload variations, and speed ranges; drones to flight under unseen wind disturbances; aerial manipulators to achieve centimeter-level in-air capture despite wind and mass uncertainties; and acceleration differentiators to remain robust with distribution shift. These results show that physics-filtered feedback can serve as a powerful alternative to massive data scaling.

</details>


### [246] [Reproducible Vision-Guided 6-DoF Robotic Manipulator with a Mixed Stepper-Driver Architecture and Browser-Native Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22799)
*Lasan Perera,Deneth Priyadarshana,Dulana Pitiwaduge,Isitha Dinujaya,Mokshan Colambage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍NeuralNexus Arm，一个由本科生团队构建的开源低成本6自由度机械臂，采用STM32H743微控制器和定制PCB，混合使用TMC2209、CL57T和DM542两套步进驱动策略，支持2kHz协调控制、浏览器端控制界面和基于视觉的自主抓取。文章重点记录了从开发板过渡到定制PCB过程中遇到的非显性硬件和固件故障模式，作为可复现性指南，所有设计文件和固件开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为降低机器人研究的教育门槛和硬件成本，提供一个完全开源、可复现、低成本且功能完整的6自由度机械臂平台，并记录开发过程中的工程经验与失败模式，帮助其他团队（特别是教育机构）复现和迭代。

Method: 机械设计上构建6自由度机械臂；电子设计上采用STM32H743单片机和定制PCB，混合使用推挽3.3V步进/方向信号驱动板载TMC2209（腕部三关节）和开漏输出驱动外部CL57T/DM542驱动器（近端三高扭矩关节）；固件采用中断驱动架构以2kHz速率进行六轴协调控制；软件层面开发了MATLAB/Simscape逆运动学管线、基于Web Serial API的浏览器原生控制界面，以及用于目标定位和自主抓取的轻量级视觉管线。

Result: 该平台在2kHz更新率下实现六轴协调运动控制，支持浏览器界面的手动操作和预录制动作执行，并完成自主拾取-放置任务。硬件和固件设计文件全部开源，可作为可复现的机器人教育平台。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个经济实用的开源机械臂平台，其价值在于完整的工程文档和故障排除经验分享，可作为机器人教育和原型验证的基础平台。但其本质是硬件平台搭建与系统集成工作，而非提出新的算法或方法。

Abstract: We present the NeuralNexus Arm, an open, low-cost 6-DOF robotic manipulator built by an undergraduate engineering team, together with the design decisions and debugging experience needed to reproduce it. The arm is driven by a single STM32H743 microcontroller on a custom printed circuit board (PCB) and combines two stepper-driver strategies on one controller: push-pull 3.3 V step/direction outputs for onboard TMC2209 drivers on the three wrist joints, and open-drain outputs for external CL57T and DM542 drivers on the three high-torque proximal joints. We describe the mechanical design, mixed-driver electronics, interrupt-driven firmware, a MATLAB/Simscape-based inverse-kinematics pipeline, a browser-native control interface using the Web Serial API, and a lightweight vision pipeline for object localisation and autonomous pick-and-place tasks. We also document non-obvious hardware and firmware failure modes encountered during the transition from a development board to the custom PCB as reproducibility guidance. All design files and firmware are released openly. The platform actuates all six axes under coordinated control at a 2 kHz update rate and executes both manual and pre-recorded motions from the browser interface.

</details>


### [247] [Triplet2Track: A Hierarchical System with Object-Centric Representations for Reliable Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22800)
*Jianxiang Liu,Gaojing Zhang,Chuan Wen,Qipeng Liu,Yuxuan Zhao,Ning Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍Triplet-to-Track System (TTS)，一种闭环长时程机器人模仿学习系统，利用人类视频减少机器人数据依赖。TTS将高层子目标表示为实例级三元组，转化为连续的轨迹先验用于执行，并从观测中监控任务进度进行在线重规划。在多种真实世界长时程任务中实现74.8%的平均成功率，支持物体级和组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作在不确定环境中难以保证可靠性。端到端VLA模型数据需求大且不透明，难以诊断和验证；层次化流水线虽可解释，但计划往往与观测弱关联、与底层动作弱对齐，且缺乏在线反馈，导致开环行为和幻觉。

Method: TTS系统：1) 将高层子目标表示为实例级三元组（instance-grounded triplets）；2) 将三元组翻译为连续的轨迹先验（track priors）用于执行；3) 从观测中监控任务进度，实现在线重规划。系统为闭环结构，并利用人类视频数据减少对机器人采集数据的依赖。

Result: 在多种真实世界长时程机器人操作任务中实现74.8%的平均成功率，并支持物体级泛化和组合泛化。

Conclusion: 通过将可解释的三元组子目标表示与连续轨迹先验结合，并加入在线监控与重规划机制，TTS在提升长时程操作可靠性的同时降低了机器人数据依赖，为层次化机器人操作提供了新的闭环方案。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in uncertain environments remains difficult for long-horizon robotic manipulation. End-to-end VLA models are data-heavy and opaque, making diagnosis and verification difficult. Hierarchical pipelines are more interpretable, but their plans are often weakly grounded in observations, weakly aligned with low-level actions, and computed without online feedback, leading to open-loop behavior and hallucinations. To address these issues, we introduce the Triplet-to-Track System (TTS), a closed-loop long-horizon imitation learning system that uses human videos to reduce reliance on robot-collected data. TTS represents high-level subgoals as instance-grounded triplets, translates them into continuous track priors for execution, and monitors task progress from observations for online replanning. Across diverse real-world long-horizon tasks, TTS achieves a 74.8\% average success rate and supports object-level and compositional generalization.

</details>


### [248] [UniMem: Unifying Multimodal Memory and Control for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22869)
*Lars Osterberg,Maggie Wang,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniMem 提出一个统一框架，将高层多模态记忆与低层控制整合在单一 VLA 骨干网络中。通过事件分类器管理记忆更新、关键帧编码器提供稠密空间记忆、以及关键帧缓存降低推理开销，解决了 VLA 在需要记忆的非马尔可夫任务中性能退化的问题。在 5 个仿真和 4 个硬件任务上验证，显著优于固定间隔采帧基线与分层基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽在长时程任务上表现优异，但在非马尔可夫（需记忆）任务上表现不佳。现有记忆方案依赖额外的 VLM 进行长时记忆管理，导致记忆瓶颈与训练流程割裂；而简单的多历史帧固定间隔采样会因帧选择不当而退化性能。

Method: UniMem 采用单一骨干统一高维记忆与管理与低层控制。包含三大组件：(1) 事件分类器负责记忆更新决策；(2) 关键帧编码器提供稠密空间记忆；(3) 关键帧缓存技术最小化策略 rollout 期间的计算开销。模型在训练阶段统一优化记忆生成与控制策略。

Result: 在 5 个仿真任务中，UniMem 达到 93.4% 成功率，优于固定间隔采帧基线 68.2%；在 4 个硬件任务中达到 80.0%，优于分层基线 43.5%。同时推理更快、训练流程更简单。

Conclusion: 通过将记忆管理与控制统一在单一骨干中，UniMem 消除了外部 VLM 带来的记忆瓶颈与训练割裂问题，在顺序记忆与空间记忆任务上均取得显著收益，并提供更快的推理速度和更简洁的部署流程。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have leveraged internet-scale pretraining and task-focused finetuning to achieve strong performance on long-horizon tasks, they often struggle with non-Markovian tasks that require memory. Existing approaches to memory typically involve additional Vision-Language-Models (VLMs) for long-term memory management, introducing a memory bottleneck and a fractured training pipeline. Conditioning on multiple historical frames can provide the VLA with access to more descriptive features of past scenes, but can degrade performance if frames are chosen at arbitrary, fixed intervals. To address these limitations, we present UniMem, a framework that unifies high-level, multimodal memory and low-level control under one backbone. UniMem employs an event classifier for memory updates, a keyframe encoder for dense spatial memory, and a keyframe caching technique to minimize overhead during policy rollouts. We evaluate UniMem across five simulation and four hardware tasks targeting sequential and spatial memory, demonstrating that our unified, single-model system outperforms fixed-interval image sampling baselines (93.4% vs. 68.2%) in simulation and hierarchical baselines (80.0% vs. 43.5%) in hardware, while offering faster inference and a simple training pipeline for easy adoption. Project website: https://losterberg3.github.io/unimem-vla/

</details>


### [249] [SuperMap: A Spatio-Temporal SLAM System for Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22896)
*Shibo Zhao,Guofei Chen,Honghao Zhu,Zhiheng Li,Changwei Yao,Nader Zantout,Seungchan Kim,Wenshan Wang,Ji Zhang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperMap 是一个面向语言引导导航的4D时空语义建图框架，整合高频几何SLAM与异步开放词表感知，通过一致性驱动的建图引擎实现3D感知实例关联/再激活和存在-标签置信度更新，维护稳定物体身份并清理过时场景内容，最终生成可查询的4D场景图表示以支持视觉-语言模型的组合式查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航需要能够调和开放词表感知与长期环境变化的时空语义表示。基础模型虽然提供强大的零样本识别，但其预测是间歇性和视角相关的，直接集成到建图管线会导致身份漂移和语义过时问题。

Method: 提出SuperMap框架：1) 结合高频几何SLAM与异步开放词表感知；2) 核心是一致性驱动的建图引擎，包含3D感知实例关联/再激活机制；3) 基于存在与标签置信度更新规则来维护稳定物体身份并裁剪过时地图内容；4) 生成可查询的4D场景图表示，支持组合式语义/关系查询。

Result: 在基准测试和真实机器人上进行了验证，包括物体出现/消失/重新定位的动态场景，并提供了消融研究和运行时分析。系统已开源发布。

Conclusion: SuperMap为开放词表时空建图提供了可部署的基线和完整开源实现，解决了动态环境中开放词表感知的身份漂移和语义过时问题。

Abstract: Robotic navigation in human environments requires a spatio-temporal semantic representation that can rec- oncile open-vocabulary perception with long-term environmental changes. While foundation models provide strong zero-shot recognition, their predictions are intermittent and view-dependent, and naively integrating them into mapping pipelines leads to identity drift and stale semantics over time. We present SuperMap, a 4D spatio-temporal mapping framework for language-guided navigation that integrates high-frequency geometric SLAM with asynchronous open-vocabulary perception. Our core contribution is a consistency-driven mapping engine that combines 3D-aware instance association/re-activation with a principled existence-and-label confidence update to maintain stable object identities and prune outdated map content under occlusions and scene changes. SuperMap produces a queryable 4D scene-graph representation that interfaces naturally with Vision-Language Models by supporting compositional queries over object semantics, relations,
  We demonstrate SuperMap on benchmarks and real robots, including dynamic scenes with appearance/disappearance and relocation, and provide ablations and runtime analysis. We release the full system as open-source to provide the community with a deployable baseline for open-vocabulary spatio-temporal mapping. Project website: superodometry.com/supermap.

</details>


### [250] [Privileged Critic Training Enables Sensor-Free Thruster Fault Adaptation in End-to-End RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22976)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Miguel A. Olivares-Méndez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFT（Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant），一种用于推力驱动机器人容错导航的强化学习策略。核心思想是在训练期间让PPO价值函数（critic）访问真实的推进器退化状态，而actor仅接收标准任务观测，从而在部署时无需专用故障传感器即可实现故障自适应。在8推力器+1反作用轮的浮动机器人平台上，面对最多4个同时故障，RAFT达到70.2%成功率，闭合了从无故障感知基线（4.8%）到全知oracle（82.4%）之间84%的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推力驱动机器人（如水下/空间机器人）的推进器故障具有连续退化、完全失效或卡死三种非二元且不完全可观测的状态。传统故障检测管线需要部署时不可用的专用传感器，而直接观测真实故障状态的oracle控制器同样不现实。因此需要一个无需专用故障传感即可在线适应故障的导航策略。

Method: 采用特权评判（privileged asymmetric critic）训练范式：在训练时PPO的value function获得真实退化状态d_gt，但actor仅接收标准任务观测，并配备循环记忆（recurrent memory）来隐式推断和适应故障。该方法命名为RAFT（Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant），即使用特权不对称critic训练的带循环记忆策略。

Result: 在8推力器+1反作用轮的浮动机器人平台上，面对最多4个同时发生的推力器故障，RAFT实现70.2%的四并发故障成功率，相比故障naive基线（4.8%）显著提升，并闭合了到全知oracle（82.4%）之间84%的性能差距。代码、检查点和数据全部开源。

Conclusion: 特权critic训练足以实现无传感器的故障自适应——训练时只让value function看到真实退化状态，就能塑造出部署时无需任何专用故障感知的容错策略。这一发现表明，将传感器成本转移到训练阶段是构建实际可部署容错导航的有效范式。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant navigation for thruster-actuated robots requires online adaptation to failures that are neither binary nor fully observable: thrusters may degrade continuously, fail dead, or jam stuck-open. Classical fault detection pipelines require dedicated sensors unavailable at deployment; oracle controllers that observe the true failure state are equally impractical. We show that privileged critic training is sufficient for sensor-free fault adaptation: giving the PPO value function access to the true degradation state dgt during training, while the actor receives only standard task observations, shapes a policy that compensates for failures at deployment without any dedicated fault sensing. We propose RAFT (Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant), a policy with recurrent memory trained with a privileged asymmetric critic. Evaluated on a floating-platform robot (8 thrusters, 1 reaction wheel) under up to four simultaneous thruster failures, RAFT achieves 70.2% success at four concurrent failures, closing 84% of the gap from a failure-naive baseline (4.8%) to an oracle policy that sees the full degradation state at deployment (82.4%). All code, checkpoints, and data are open-source.

</details>


### [251] [CSymPlan: Certified Symbolic Planning and Control for High-DOF Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22983)
*Aditya Narendra,Ashok Kumar Saini,Mahathi Anand,Mahmoud Khaled,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Abdalla Swikir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSymPlan 是一个面向高自由度机械臂的认证符号规划与控制框架，包含离线（预计算认证可达-避障策略）和在线（运行时并行合成符号策略）两种实现。通过反馈线性化将机械臂动力学约简为带扰动的双积分模型，将执行器误差、建模不精确和测量不确定性视为有界扰动，并通过量化-查找-力矩实现流程将策略精化到 Franka FR3 平台上。在随机仿真基准和感知驱动的真实实验中均实现零安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械臂运动生成堆栈将路径规划与底层控制分离，虽然计算上方便，但在执行器限制、跟踪误差、模型失配和小障碍间隙下难以执行。需要一种能够提供安全认证的规划与控制一体化框架。

Method: 将机械臂动力学通过反馈线性化约简为操作空间的采样扰动双积分模型，将所有不确定性建模为有界扰动；离线实现预计算认证的可达-避障符号策略表；在线实现通过运行时 pFaces 请求-合成-执行循环进行策略更新；两者共用相同的抽象与精化接口，通过量化-查找-力矩管线精化到 Franka FR3。

Result: 在随机仿真基准和感知驱动的 Franka FR3 真实实验中，两种实现均完成可达-避障任务且零安全违规；当没有认证动作时，机器人安全地保持、重规划或停止。

Conclusion: CSymPlan 证明了在真实高自由度机械臂上实现具有形式化安全保证的符号规划与控制框架是可行的，且同时适用于离线预计算和在线动态环境更新两种场景。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are commonly engineered around a decoupled motion-generation stack: a planner computes a collision-free path and a lower-level controller tracks the resulting reference. This separation is computationally convenient, but it can produce references that are difficult to execute under actuator limits, tracking error, model mismatch, and small obstacle clearances. We present CSymPlan, a certified symbolic planning and control framework for high-DOF manipulators with two complementary implementations: an offline implementation that precomputes certified reach-avoid feedback policies for known workspaces; and an online implementation that synthesizes or updates symbolic policies at runtime from changing task and perception information using parallelization. The offline implementation reduces the manipulator dynamics to a sampled perturbed double-integrator model in operational space through feedback linearization, treats torque-realization errors, modeling inaccuracies, and measurement uncertainty as bounded disturbances, and refines the synthesized symbolic policy to the Franka FR3 through a quantization--lookup--torque realization pipeline. The online implementation uses the same abstraction and refinement interface, but replaces the precomputed policy table with a runtime pFaces request--synthesis--execution loop. In randomized simulated benchmarks and perception-driven Franka FR3 experiments, both implementations complete reach-avoid tasks with zero safety violations; whenever no certified action exists, the robot holds, replans, or stops safely instead of executing an uncertified command.

</details>


### [252] [InstructMove: A Text-Indispensable Benchmark for Instruction-Following Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22990)
*Mengao Zhao,Ziang Li,Chaodong Huang,Mengchen Ma,Haoyi Jiang,Yiwei Jin,Xinjie Wang,Yun Du,Xuewu Lin,Taojun Ding,Hongyu Xie,Jackson Jiang,Chunlei Yu,Kaihua Zhang,Lichao Huang,Liu Liu,Tianwei Lin,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InstructMove基准，用于评估VLA策略是否真正遵循语言指令进行机器人抓取放置操作。其核心原则是"文本不可或缺"（text-indispensable）：场景中包含多种视觉和物理上均可行的动作，但只有一种与语言指令一致，从而防止策略通过视觉捷径而非语言理解取胜。基准涵盖类别识别、属性判别、空间推理和组合式抓放任务，并提供训练-评估协议和语言依赖性诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准对指令跟随能力的评估不够充分：目标物体或目的地往往在视觉上显著或唯一可行，策略无需真正理解语言指令即可成功。作者认为指令跟随评估应当"文本不可或缺"，即多种动作须在视觉和物理上均可行，仅有一种与语言指令一致，才能可靠诊断策略是否真正ground了语言。

Method: 构建InstructMove基准，采用带语义干扰物的抓放场景，将指令跟随分解为四个子任务：类别识别、属性判别、空间推理和组合式抓放。提供InstructMove训练数据与留出评估任务的训练-评估协议，并附带语言依赖性诊断工具。用代表性VLA策略进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明InstructMove提供了一个可控测试平台来诊断视觉捷径（visual shortcuts），且InstructMove仿真数据能够提升真实世界指令跟随操作性能。

Conclusion: InstructMove填补了指令跟随操作评估的空白，通过文本不可或缺原则暴露视觉捷径问题，并验证了仿真数据对真实世界指令跟随操作性能的正面迁移作用。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made general-purpose robot manipulation increasingly plausible by conditioning robot actions on natural-language instructions. A key test of such generality is whether policies actually follow language instructions. Yet many manipulation benchmarks leave this ability underdetermined: the intended object or destination is often visually salient or uniquely feasible, allowing policies to succeed without grounding the instruction. We argue that instruction-following evaluation should be text-indispensable: multiple actions should be visually and physically plausible, while only one should be consistent with the language instruction. We introduce InstructMove, a text-indispensable benchmark for instruction-following manipulation. InstructMove instantiates this principle in pick-and-place scenes with semantic distractors, decomposing instruction following into category identification, attribute discrimination, spatial reasoning, and compositional pick-and-place. InstructMove supports a train-eval protocol with InstructMove training data and held-out evaluation tasks, with additional diagnostics for language dependence. Experiments with representative VLA policies show that InstructMove provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing visual shortcuts and that InstructMove simulation data can improve real-world instruction-following manipulation performance. Code: https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/RoboOrchardSim

</details>


### [253] [Free-Energy-Gated Plasticity for Real-Time Online Motor Learning in Physical Human--Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23000)
*Hiroki Sawada,Jun Tani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Free-Energy-Gated Plasticity (FEGP)，扩展PV-RNN实现持续在线突触自适应，通过变分自由能门控有效学习率，使随机初始化的网络在无预训练、无回放、无任务边界信号的真实时空物理人机交互中在线习得三种循环运动模式，并显著提升模式覆盖率和已习得模式的保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全在线的具身学习需要突触自适应在持续交互中既习得新行为又保留已学到的动力学。传统方法依赖离线预训练、回放记忆或任务边界信号，不适用于真实的持续在线学习场景。

Method: 扩展PV-RNN使突触权重连续自适应，提出FEGP：根据变分自由能动态调节有效学习率。通过对照实验（10条随机教学流×5种网络初始化）比较FEGP与恒定学习率、以及打乱时序组织的同增益回放方案。

Result: FEGP显著提升模式覆盖率和先前习得模式的保持能力（模式离开近期观测窗口后仍未遗忘）。恒定学习率（匹配FEGP时间平均有效速率）和打乱时序组织的增益回放均无法复现这些改进，表明塑性在时间上的分配方式（相对于模型-环境失配的时刻）比平均幅度或分布更关键。

Conclusion: 在线持续学习中，塑性相对于模型-环境失配的时间分配是保持已有行为的核心机制，而不仅仅是平均学习率大小或增益分布。该发现为完全在线具身学习提供了新的原则性设计思路。

Abstract: Fully online embodied learning requires synaptic adaptation to acquire new behaviors while preserving previously learned dynamics during ongoing interaction. We extend the Predictive-Coding-inspired Variational Recurrent Neural Network (PV-RNN) to continuously adapt its synaptic weights and propose Free-Energy-Gated Plasticity (FEGP), which regulates the effective learning rate according to variational free energy. In real-time physical human--robot interaction, a randomly initialized network acquired three cyclic motor patterns without offline pretraining, replay, or task-boundary signals, with all three patterns emerging in autonomous rollouts. Controlled experiments over ten randomized teaching streams and five network initializations per stream showed that FEGP substantially improved repertoire coverage and retention of previously acquired patterns after they left the recent observation window. Neither a constant learning rate matched to the gate's time-averaged effective rate nor replay of the same gain values with disrupted temporal organization reproduced these improvements. These results indicate that the temporal allocation of plasticity relative to model--environment mismatch, rather than simply its average magnitude or distribution, is critical for maintaining previously acquired behaviors during continued online learning.

</details>


### [254] [RoboRacer Arena: Scaling High-Fidelity Autonomous Racing in Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23040)
*Mihaela-Larisa Clement,Agnes Poks,Ezio Bartocci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRacer Arena是一个将占用栅格地图自动转换为3D赛车模拟环境的系统，支持来自SLAM、F1赛道缩放或自然语言描述的输入，可在Isaac Sim中快速生成可复现的赛车环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboRacer平台提供了标准化1:10自动驾驶车辆研究平台，但现有赛道种类繁多导致策略获取效率低。占用栅格模拟器缺乏物理接触，3D模拟器需要每个赛道单独制作资产，扩展性差，缺乏一个可从占用图直接生成3D赛车环境的自动化系统。

Method: 使用洪水填充算法提取可行驶走廊并识别赛道边界以建立障碍物；计算距离场定义碰撞边界；将赛道表面、碰撞属性和材质组装为USD stage在Isaac Sim中自动生成环境。输入可从SLAM会话、缩放的F1赛道或自然语言描述获取，其中自然语言输入使用Gemma 4 31B生成赛道规格。采用几何筛选、程序化生成和栅格级验证确保一致性和可复现性。

Result: 环境初始化时间范围1.18-2.48秒，随栅格尺寸线性增加；30个匹配试验（10条赛道、3个种子）中生成21张地图全部通过验证；系统包含130条赛道并支持自然语言生成；基准测试在256个并行刚体车辆上达到8707车辆步/秒（不含渲染和策略执行时间）。

Conclusion: RoboRacer Arena实现了从占用栅格地图到3D模拟环境的自动化高效转换，显著提升赛道生成的扩展性和可复现性，为RoboRacer平台的策略研究提供了可扩展的仿真基础设施。

Abstract: RoboRacer offers a standardized platform for research using 1:10-scale autonomous vehicles, but the variety of available tracks hinders the process of acquiring policies. Although existing occupancy-grid simulators allow for the quick addition of new maps, they fail to include physical contact, while 3D simulators require each circuit to be implemented as a separate asset, thus limiting their scalability. In order to overcome this issue, we have developed RoboRacer Arena, a system that creates 3D racing environments directly from occupancy maps. Our method starts by using a flood fill algorithm to extract the drivable corridors and to identify the track boundaries, which are then used to establish the barriers. A distance field is calculated to define the collision boundaries. The track surfaces, collision properties, and materials are assembled into a USD stage, which allows for the automated and reproducible generation of the environment in Isaac Sim. The input maps can be obtained from SLAM sessions, from rescaled Formula 1 circuits, or from natural-language descriptions. When the input is based on natural language, we use Gemma 4 31B to generate a track specification without specifying any coordinates or geometry. To guarantee consistency and reproducibility, we apply geometric screening, procedural generation, and raster-level validation. The simulation environments are initialized in a time range of 1.18 to 2.48 seconds, with the initialization time increasing linearly as the raster size increases. In 30 matched trials involving 10 tracks and 3 seeds, 21 maps were generated and all passed validation. RoboRacer Arena currently contains 130 tracks and supports the generation of tracks from natural language. In benchmark tests, the system attains 8,707 vehicle-steps per second when using 256 parallel rigid-body vehicles, excluding the time taken for rendering and policy execution.

</details>


### [255] [Switched Turn-based Adaptive Source Seeking Strategy using Estimation and Information-driven Direction of Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23068)
*Shubhra Banerjee,Satadal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回路的源搜索框架，结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计和基于Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）的方向选择。源估计在运动过程中更新，航向在回路边界处依据估计不确定性和预测信息增益进行调整，并采用基于测量的停止条件在无需先验源位置信息的情况下检测收敛。仿真结果（静态和移动源场景）表明相比纯信息驱动或纯估计驱动策略，追踪性能和估计误差均有改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人源搜索中，估计与运动紧密耦合：测量改善源估计，而轨迹选择影响未来测量质量。现有基于回路的几何策略能生成可行运动，但未显式利用估计不确定性来调节方向更新，导致估计精度和追踪性能受限。

Method: 提出回路式源搜索框架，核心包括三个方面：(1) 使用EKF在运动过程中持续更新源位置估计；(2) 在回路边界处，结合估计不确定性和基于FIM的预测信息增益来选择航向方向；(3) 采用基于测量的停止条件检测收敛，无需源位置先验知识。

Result: 在静态源和移动源两类仿真场景下，所提出的方法相比纯信息驱动或纯估计驱动策略，展现出更优的追踪性能和更低的估计误差。

Conclusion: 结合估计不确定性和预测信息增益的回路式策略能有效平衡探索与利用，在源搜索任务中取得更好的性能。该方法具有实际应用潜力，但仍需在真实环境中进一步验证。

Abstract: Source seeking arises in applications such as gas leak localization, radiation monitoring, and environmental surveillance, where the origin of an unknown signal field must be estimated from spatial measurements. In practice, the source location is not directly observable and must be inferred from noisy scalar measurements collected during motion.In robotic source seeking, estimation and motion are closely linked: measurements improve the source estimate, while the chosen trajectory affects the quality of future measurements.Existing loop-based geometric strategies generate feasible motion but do not explicitly use estimation uncertainty to regulate direction updates.This paper presents a loop-based source-seeking framework that combines Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) estimation with Fisher Information Matrix (FIM)-based direction selection. The source estimate is updated during motion, and the heading is changed at loop boundaries using both estimation uncertainty and predicted information gain. A measurement-based stopping condition is used to detect convergence without requiring prior knowledge of the source location.Simulation results under stationary and moving source scenarios demonstrate improved tracking performance and reduced estimation error compared to purely information-driven or estimate-driven strategies.

</details>


### [256] [Shaping the Evolutionary Dynamics of Robot Morphology via Adaptive Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23100)
*Junru Song,Yang Yang,Yaqing Xu,Ying Wen,Wei Peng,Guozhen Li,Wei'en Zhou,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人协同设计（大脑-身体联合进化）中控制学习与形态进化之间的双向关系，提出"真实潜力"（true potential）与"形态智能"（morphological intelligence）正交解耦的框架，指出过早的适应度评估会系统性低估真实潜力并偏向快速学习器，导致设计空间探索受限和多样性下降。作者提出AdaControl方法，监控进化过程中对形态智能的选择偏差并自适应分配最小的控制学习量以实现无偏评估，使简单遗传算法在发现多样高性能设计上媲美最先进的生成模型方法，同时计算开销降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究确立了形态适应性促进控制学习速度（即形态智能），但控制学习如何反过来塑造形态进化方向尚未被探索。为了对大脑-身体协同交互建立整体性理解，需要同时考察两个方向的影响，并解决早期适应度评估不准确导致进化偏差的问题。

Method: 首先将形态对控制学习的贡献解耦为两个正交维度：收敛速度（形态智能）和性能天花板（真实潜力）。建立功能关系从个体学习曲线中联合表征这两个量，并在种群层面聚合以捕捉进化特征。基于此框架提出AdaControl方法，在进化过程中监测对形态智能的过度选择倾向，动态分配满足无偏适应度评估所需的最少控制学习量。

Result: 在体素软体机器人仿真实验中发现：过早适应度评估系统性低估真实潜力并偏向快速学习器，限制设计空间探索，损害优化效率和形态多样性。广泛观察到的形态Baldwin效应实际上是这种评估偏差的产物而非一般进化趋势。采用AdaControl后，简单遗传算法在发现多样高性能设计方面媲美最先进的基于生成模型的协同设计方法，同时相比穷举控制方案降低高达80%的计算开销。

Conclusion: 大脑-身体协同进化中控制学习与形态进化的双向关系应被统一建模。研究揭示了进化评估偏差这一关键问题，并证明通过自适应的最少充分控制学习分配，可以在显著降低计算成本的同时实现更优的设计发现和形态多样性，对机器人协同设计领域具有重要的方法论意义。

Abstract: Robot co-design via bi-level optimization couples within-lifetime controller learning for fitness evaluation with cross-generational morphological evolution. Prior work has established that well-adapted morphology facilitates faster control learning, a property termed morphological intelligence. Yet how control learning reciprocally shapes morphological evolution remains unexplored. This paper examines both directions for a holistic account of brain-body interplay. We first show that morphological contributions to control learning decouple into two orthogonal dimensions. We formalize the convergence speed as morphological intelligence and identify the performance ceiling as a complementary quantity termed true potential. A concise functional relation is then established to jointly characterize both quantities from individual learning curves, which, when aggregated at the population level, capture evolutionary profiles. Through extensive experiments on simulated voxel-based soft robots, we reveal that premature fitness evaluation systematically underestimates true potential and biases selection towards fast learners. This restricts design space exploration, compromising both optimization efficiency and morphological diversity. Notably, the widely recognized morphological Baldwin effect emerges as an artifact of this bias rather than a general evolutionary tendency. We therefore propose AdaControl, which monitors disproportionate selection for morphological intelligence during evolution and allocates minimally sufficient control learning for unbiased fitness evaluation. With AdaControl, a simple genetic algorithm rivals state-of-the-art generative-model-based co-design methods in discovering diverse high-performing designs while cutting computation by up to 80% versus exhaustive control.

</details>


### [257] [Pointing-VLA: Typed Spatial Grounding Interfaces for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23138)
*Xiwen Chen,Zelin Li,Zhiruo Zhou,Huiming Chen,Chenwei Wang,Xiaojun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pointing-VLA 在 Embodied-R1 之上引入类型化隐藏状态空间读出接口，用几何专用头预测归一化点、物体功能接地（OFG）热图和视觉轨迹，替代将几何序列化为文本的方式，实现高效且可检查的具身推理到机器人执行的桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常通过自回归文本坐标或模糊动作 token 暴露空间接地，在多模态推理与机器人执行之间形成脆弱接口。需要更直接、可检查且高效的空间读出方式。

Method: 基于 Embodied-R1 构建 Pointing-VLA，使用几何专用 head 预测归一化点、OFG 热图和视觉轨迹，不将几何序列化为文本。显式执行契约将 PICK 分配给源条件 OFG，PLACE 分配给 Pointing，提供阶段对齐的空间目标。

Result: Bridge/WidowX 平均 72.9% SOTA（四任务集，无 Bridge 微调，碰撞启用的 CuRobo 执行）；OFG/接触读出迁移到 NORA-1.5 保持或提升成功率并减少控制器时间 20x+；类型化 head 比 Embodied-R1 文本解码快 6.68-6.90x；作为 π0.5 动作策略的空间引导，真实机器人成功率从 52.7% 提升到 80.7%。

Conclusion: 类型化空间读出可作为具身推理与机器人执行之间高效、可检查的接口，Pointing 与 OFG 展现互补优势。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often expose spatial grounding through autoregressive text coordinates or opaque action tokens, creating brittle interfaces between multimodal reasoning and robot execution. We present Pointing-VLA, a typed hidden-state spatial readout built on Embodied-R1. Geometry-specific heads predict normalized points, object-functional grounding (OFG) heatmaps, and visual trajectories without serializing geometry as text. For the evaluated Bridge/WidowX and physical pick-place deployments, an explicit execution contract assigns PICK to source-conditioned OFG and PLACE to Pointing, providing direct stage-aligned spatial targets. Pointing-VLA achieves SOTA performance on Bridge/WidowX, averaging 72.9\% across the evaluated four-task set without Bridge-specific finetuning under collision-enabled CuRobo execution. Pointing and OFG show complementary strengths across native and cross-dataset evaluations. The OFG/contact readout transfers to NORA-1.5, preserving or improving success while reducing recorded controller time by more than 20$\times$; typed heads are also 6.68--6.90$\times$ faster than Embodied-R1 text decoding on a shared external suite. When integrated as spatial guidance for a $π_{0.5}$ action policy, Pointing-VLA raises autonomous real-robot success from 52.7\% to 80.7\% across three visual contexts. These results establish typed spatial readouts as an efficient, inspectable interface between embodied reasoning and robot execution.

</details>


### [258] [Spinning Quadrotor: Hover Thrust Augmentation with Passive Lifting Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23163)
*Aniketh Parkala,Harikumar Kandath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型自旋四旋翼架构，通过有意的持续偏航旋转取代传统的偏航抑制，将原本用于偏航调节的功率转化为有用的气动效应。初步硬件测试显示所需推力降低22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼在悬停时主动抑制偏航旋转，消耗额外功率维持固定航向，但偏航调节并非力平衡或高度控制所必需。本文旨在挑战这一范式，提出将偏航旋转作为设计机制而非待抑制的扰动。

Method: 开发旋转四旋翼的动力学模型；在低雷诺数范围内进行分析以选择升力面的翼型；搭建硬件原型进行实验验证，测量所需推力变化。

Result: 初步硬件测试显示旋转四旋翼所需的推力降低了22%。

Conclusion: 有意的偏航旋转可以被利用为提高多旋翼飞行效率和鲁棒性的设计机制，而非需要被抑制的干扰。

Abstract: Conventional multirotor aerial vehicles actively suppress yaw rotation during hover, expending power to maintain a fixed heading despite the fact that yaw regulation is not required for force balance or altitude control. This paper challenges that paradigm by proposing a spinning quadrotor architecture that intentionally operates at a sustained yaw rate, converting power traditionally spent on yaw regulation into useful aerodynamic effects. A dynamic model of the spinning quadrotor is developed, analysis for low Re range is conducted to choose an airfoil for lifting surfaces. Preliminary hardware tests show a 22% reduction in thrust required. These findings suggest that intentional yaw rotation, rather than being suppressed, can be exploited as a design mechanism for efficient and robust multirotor flight.

</details>


### [259] [Guided Riemannian Optimization (GuRO): Bridging Model Predictive Control and Decision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23204)
*Hossein Abdi,Satya Prakash Dash,Mingfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将MPC与RL统一到序列决策框架中的新方法，利用MPC提供局部最优轨迹引导决策Transformer，并通过黎曼参数空间中的曲率感知优化方法加速训练，在四足机器人高维控制任务上取得优于TRPO、SAC和在线决策Transformer的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型预测控制（MPC）在模型不准确或长时域预测时性能退化，而模型无关强化学习样本效率低且优化不稳定。Transformer序列决策框架虽能统一两者，但面临高度非凸损失景观导致的训练困难。需要一个既保留MPC样本效率与可解释性、又具备RL灵活性的高效统一框架。

Method: 构建MPC与RL融合的序列决策框架：MPC提供局部最优轨迹预测来引导决策Transformer，减少对大量离线预训练的依赖；训练时在黎曼参数空间中使用曲率感知（Riemannian）优化方法来应对非凸损失景观，实现更快更稳定的收敛。

Result: 在高维四足机器人控制任务上，该框架相比TRPO、SAC和Online Decision Transformer等强基线，取得更高的累积回报和更快的收敛速度，且在多个任务上表现一致稳定。

Conclusion: 通过将MPC与RL在序列决策框架中结合，并采用曲率感知的黎曼优化，能够有效解决Transformer策略训练中的非凸优化难题，在保留样本效率的同时实现高性能的决策，为高维非线性系统的机器人控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Decision-making in high-dimensional, nonlinear systems remains a central challenge in robotics. While model-based methods like Model Predictive Control (MPC) offer sample efficiency and interpretability, their performance degrades when the dynamics model is inaccurate or long-horizon predictions are required. Conversely, model-free reinforcement learning (RL) learns policies directly from interaction but suffers from high sample complexity and unstable optimization. Recent advances in sequence modeling have inspired transformer-based decision-making frameworks that can unify MPC and RL, but their training typically faces significant optimization challenges due to highly non-convex loss landscapes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that integrates MPC with RL in a sequence decision-making framework and leverages a curvature-aware optimization to efficiently tackle non-convex loss landscapes. MPC provides predictions of locally optimal trajectories that guide the decision transformer, removing the need for extensive offline pretraining. To address the slow and unstable convergence of traditional optimizers, we train the policy in a Riemannian parameter space using an efficient Riemannian (curvature-aware) method, leading to faster and more robust optimization. We evaluate our framework on high-dimensional quadruped control tasks and demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, including TRPO, SAC, and Online Decision Transformer, achieving higher returns and faster convergence.

</details>


### [260] [Think Only When Needed: Prompt-Authority Control for Selective Slow-Path Intervention in Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23224)
*Zhiruo Zhou,Zelin Li,Xiwen Chen,Jiazhuo Li,Chenwei Wang,Huiming Chen,Xiaojun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文发现VLA策略中，向冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型追加文本提示会引发"提示形式坍缩"（prompt-form collapse）：改变指令形式而非增加语义即可主导执行（原始文本追加使成功率从92.47%跌至3.00%）。作者提出TOWN-VLA（Think Only When Needed），一个提示权限接口，将候选生成与修改策略输入权限分离，通过固定兼容性规则授权规范的紧凑指令，否则精确恢复原始Base提示。在900条审计路线中合约完全遵守；LIBERO-Plus评估中成功率从69.5%提升至73.1%（+362 episodes），物理PiPER机械臂上成功率从52.7%提升至78.7%（p=3.16e-6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强可高效地为冻结的VLA策略提供外部知识而无需重训练，但检索文本一旦进入执行提示就变成了控制干预。研究发现随意的文本追加会严重破坏策略执行（即使是有意义的内容也会导致失败），揭示了"提示形式坍缩"这一关键问题——即提示的形式改变（而非语义内容）会主导执行。因此需要一种机制来确保提示检索只在确实需要时才改变策略输入，即"权限感知"的提示管理。

Method: 提出TOWN-VLA提示权限接口：1) 将候选生成（candidate generation）与修改策略输入的权限（permission）解耦；2) 采用固定兼容性规则（fixed compatibility rule），只有符合规则的紧凑规范指令才被授权进入策略提示；3) 不满足授权条件时，接口精确恢复原始Base提示（逐字节匹配），确保策略输入不被未经授权的改变破坏。核心思想是"仅在需要时思考"——用严格的形式约束来防御prompt-form collapse。

Result: ①匹配审计中，原始文本追加使平均成功率从92.47%降至3.00%，有意义与等长无意义追加均在500个状态上全部失败；②900条审计路线全部遵守合约：525条恢复Base（哈希匹配），375条授权提示保持任务签名；③LIBERO-Plus 4x7匹配评估（每方法10,030 episodes）成功率从69.5%升至73.1%（+362 episodes，95% CI 1.89-5.45点），优于六个扰动轴与全部四个套件；④物理PiPER臂（冻结π-zero-five检查点）150次试验成功率从52.7%升至78.7%（p=3.16e-6）。

Conclusion: 提示权限（prompt authority）对冻结控制器是可强制执行的，能有效防御提示形式坍缩带来的灾难性失败，同时保持检索增强的收益。下一步部署目标是无需oracle的准入校准（oracle-free admission calibration），使该系统可实际部署。

Abstract: Retrieval can efficiently and effectively augment a frozen vision--language--action (VLA) policy without retraining, yet retrieved text becomes a control intervention once it enters the executed prompt. In a matched audit, raw appended text reduces mean success from 92.47\% to 3.00\%, while meaningful and length-matched meaningless appends both fail on all 500 states. This result identifies \emph{prompt-form collapse}: changing the instruction form, rather than adding useful semantics, can dominate execution. We introduce TOWN-VLA (Think Only When Needed), a prompt-authority interface that separates candidate generation from permission to alter the policy input. A fixed compatibility rule authorizes a canonical compact instruction; otherwise, the interface restores the original Base prompt exactly. Across 900 audited routes, every route follows this contract: 525 routes recover Base with matching hashes, and all 375 authorized prompts preserve the task signature. On a matched $4\times7$ LIBERO-Plus evaluation with 10{,}030 episodes per method, success rises from 69.5\% to 73.1\% ($+362$ episodes; 95\% CI 1.89--5.45 points), improving on six perturbation axes and all four suites. On a physical PiPER arm with a frozen \pizerofive{} checkpoint, success rises from 52.7\% to 78.7\% over 150 trials per method ($p=3.16\times10^{-6}$). Prompt authority is enforceable for a frozen controller; oracle-free admission calibration is the next deployment target.

</details>


### [261] [Design of a Biomimetic Joint-Covering Skin with Tissue-Like Structure to Enhance Proprioception in a Musculoskeletal Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23304)
*Akihiro Miki,Shun Hasegawa,Yoshimoto Ribayashi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了用于肌肉骨骼人形机器人的仿生关节覆盖皮肤，该皮肤具有组织样分层结构，集成了压力与拉伸敏感元件，并在Musashi-W上验证了其独立本体感觉能力（关节角度估计平均误差约3度）及与肌肉传感的融合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼人形机器人的本体感觉主要依赖肌肉传感，但生物系统中分布在软组织内的机械感受器能补充肌肉反馈并支持可靠的关节状态估计。关节周围皮肤形变对本体感觉的贡献尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了仿生关节覆盖皮肤，具有组织样分层结构，集成压力敏感与拉伸敏感元件；在肌肉骨骼人形机器人Musashi-W上实现，并分别评估独立本体感觉能力以及与肌肉传感的融合性能。

Result: 该皮肤单独即可实现关节角度估计，平均误差约3度；与肌肉传感集成后估计精度进一步提升。关节覆盖结构可能机械性地减轻外部扰动对肌肉的影响，多模态融合有助于识别肌肉传感难以解释的外部刺激。

Conclusion: 提出了一种仿生关节覆盖皮肤的设计方法论，证明组织结构化皮肤可作为扩展肌肉骨骼人形机器人本体感觉系统的有效途径。

Abstract: Proprioception in musculoskeletal humanoids is typically estimated primarily from muscle sensing, while the role of cutaneous deformation around joints remains insufficiently explored. In biological systems, mechanoreceptors distributed within soft tissue complement muscle feedback and support reliable joint state estimation. This study presents the design of a biomimetic joint-covering skin with a tissue-like layered structure that integrates pressure- and stretch-sensitive elements within the joint-covering tissue. The proposed skin is implemented on the musculoskeletal humanoid Musashi-W, and its independent proprioceptive capability as well as its integration with muscle sensing are evaluated. Experimental results show that the proposed skin alone achieves joint angle estimation with an average error of approximately 3 degrees. Furthermore, integration with muscle sensing improves estimation accuracy. Owing to its joint-covering structure, the skin may mechanically mitigate the influence of external disturbances on the muscles, and the integration of multiple modalities suggests the possibility of contributing to the identification of external stimuli that are difficult to interpret using muscle sensing alone. This work presents a design methodology for biomimetic joint-covering skin and demonstrates that such tissue-structured skin can serve as an effective approach for extending proprioceptive systems in musculoskeletal humanoids.

</details>


### [262] [ROS2SmolVLA: Enabling Small Vision-Language-Action Models for Integration into Industrial-Grade Lightweight Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23320)
*Nils Mandischer,Noah Böckmann,Ludwig Holl,Lars Mikelsons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将Hugging Face的SmolVLA模型适配到Universal Robots轻量级机器人上，并开源ROS2SmolVLA接口库，实现ROS 2与SmolVLA之间的连接，使工业级硬件能够应用VLA模型进行本地计算。作者在UR10e上通过拾放任务验证了功能，并提供了实现指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业制造面临小批量、多品种的生产挑战，传统机器人自动化系统静态化、难以适应变化。现有的VLA模型要么过于庞大无法在现场计算（带来合规和安全问题），要么依赖实验室级硬件而无法在真实工业场景中部署。因此需要一种轻量级、可现场计算且兼容工业硬件的VLA方案。

Method: 将Hugging Face的SmolVLA适配到Universal Robots轻量级机器人平台，开发并开源ROS2SmolVLA——一个连接ROS 2与SmolVLA的接口，使SmolVLA可在工业级硬件上运行。在UR10e机器人上以拾放任务验证其功能，并给出具体实现指南。

Result: SmolVLA在UR10e上成功完成拾放任务，验证了其功能有效性。结果表明SmolVLA适合在本地计算环境下处理小规模任务，同时开源的ROS2SmolVLA提供了工业环境的适配接口。

Conclusion: SmolVLA是适合需要在本地计算的小型工业任务的良好选择，通过ROS2SmolVLA接口可以较为平滑地在实验室和工业环境中部署VLA模型。

Abstract: Industrial demand changes the paradigms of production. Due to smaller batch sizes and more variations in products, companies face a growing challenge to adopt more adaptive production systems. In particular, robot-based automation is usually static and fails to respond to constantly changing processes. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models are a promising opportunity to mitigate this challenge by generating robot actions based on the observed system state. However, current research either focuses on large models that cannot be computed on premise, creating compliance and security challenges, or use lab-grade robot hardware that obscures exploitation in real industrial settings. In this work, we adapt Hugging Face's SmolVLA for Universal Robots lightweight robots. Further, we release the open-source repository ROS2SmolVLA that implements an interface for ROS 2 to SmolVLA, and makes it applicable for industrial-grade hardware. By this, we allow a lenient adoption into lab and industrial environments. We validate the functionality of SmolVLA for a Universal Robots UR10e using a pick-and-place task and give implementation guidelines. Our findings support that SmolVLA is a well-suited option for small-sized tasks that need to be computed on premise.

</details>


### [263] [OptiSight: Bridging Semantic Reasoning and Geometric Control for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23354)
*Alperen Avan,Jordi Sanchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptiSight是一个混合式自主室内导航框架，结合视觉语言模型推理与确定性视觉伺服控制，通过有限状态链式思维架构实现零样本导航，在AI Habitat中验证，运行于8GB显存预算内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主室内导航需要同时具备语义理解和精确几何控制，现有方法要么依赖密集建图导致计算开销大，要么纯VLM推理缺乏几何精度和实时性。作者旨在平衡语义推理能力与几何控制的效率。

Method: 采用混合架构：Grounded-SAM用于开放词汇目标定位；相机投影几何将视觉观测转换为导航指令（免密集建图）；VLM仅在关键决策点被查询以降低计算开销；有限状态链式思维（CoT）架构协调语义推理与几何控制的切换。

Result: 在AI Habitat仿真环境中实现多种室内场景的可靠零样本导航，包括避障和语义歧义处理，并能在8GB VRAM预算内运行，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了VLM语义推理与几何视觉伺服混合架构在零样本室内导航中的可行性，兼顾了语义能力和计算效率，但验证仅停留在仿真层面。

Abstract: Autonomous indoor navigation requires both semantic understanding and precise geometric control. We propose OptiSight, a hybrid framework that combines Vision-Language Model reasoning with deterministic visual servoing through a finite-state Chain-of-Thought architecture. Grounded-SAM localizes open-vocabulary targets, while camera projection geometry converts visual observations into navigation commands without requiring dense mapping. The VLM is queried only at key decision points, reducing computational overhead while geometric control handles continuous navigation. Experiments in AI Habitat demonstrate reliable zero-shot navigation across diverse indoor scenarios, including obstacle avoidance and semantic ambiguity, while operating within an 8~GB VRAM budget. The source code is available at https://github.com/avanalperen/OptiSight-Python-Multimodal-CoT-for-Visual-Reasoning.

</details>


### [264] [Reward-Free Continual Adaptation for Resilient Space Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23452)
*Andrej Orsula,Miguel Olivares-Mendez,Carol Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免奖励（reward-free）的持续学习框架，利用潜在状态世界模型应对空间机器人硬件退化。通过冻结观测编码器和奖励预测器，仅更新世界模型的转移动力学，并在想象轨迹上训练策略，使智能体无需收到新奖励即可适应动力学变化。在行星穿越、轨道导航和精密装配任务上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间机器人在极端环境下硬件退化会破坏传统控制策略。持续强化学习需要部署时的奖励信号，但空间环境中缺乏外部跟踪系统且环境复杂，无法精确计算奖励，因此需要一种无需奖励即可在线自适应的机制。

Method: 1) 在多样化仿真中预训练基于模型（model-based）的智能体，使世界模型在潜在空间学习到鲁棒的奖励结构预测器；2) 部署到硬件严重退化的环境后，冻结观测编码器和奖励预测器，仅通过无监督rollout更新世界模型的转移动力学；3) 完全在更新后的世界模型生成的想象轨迹上训练策略，无需接收新奖励即可适应改变后的动力学。

Result: 在模拟行星穿越、轨道导航和精密装配任务中，在遭遇严重形态故障（severe morphological failures）的条件下验证了该框架的有效性，表明智能体能够仅通过无监督想象轨迹适应动力学变化。

Conclusion: 通过冻结观测编码器和奖励预测器、仅更新转移动力学的方式，世界模型可以在无奖励信号条件下实现持续适应，为空间机器人等奖励不可观测环境提供了可行的在线自适应方案。

Abstract: Space robots operate in extreme environments where hardware degradation can critically compromise traditional control strategies. While continual reinforcement learning offers a promising mechanism for online adaptation, it inherently requires access to a reward signal during deployment. However, precise reward computation in space is often infeasible due to the lack of external tracking systems and the overall complexity of the environment. To address the challenge of unobservable rewards, we introduce a reward-free continual learning framework that leverages latent-state world models. By pre-training a model-based agent across diverse simulations, the world model learns a robust predictor of the reward structure within its latent space. Upon deployment to an environment with severe hardware degradation, we freeze the observation encoder and reward predictor to update only the transition dynamics of the world model through unsupervised rollouts. By training the policy entirely on imagined trajectories generated by this updated world model, the agent adapts to altered dynamics without receiving new rewards. We demonstrate our approach across simulated planetary traversal, orbital navigation, and precision assembly tasks subjected to severe morphological failures.

</details>


### [265] [Act with Intent: Distilling Behavior Intent for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23478)
*Sangoh Lee,Sangwoo Mo,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出意图蒸馏（INDI）方法，通过冻结的教师VLM将行为级意图蒸馏到VLA模型的动作解码器中。训练时教师模型从当前观测、指令、动作摘要和视频中解释意图；部署时VLA在中间解码层恢复多模态意图表征，用于组织动作预测。在SimplerEnv-Bridge上使GR00T-N1.7从64.3%提升至84.7%，在RoboCasa Kitchen上从64.1%提升至70.3%，真实世界任务平均成功率从62.0%提升至68.7%，长时程任务增益最高达12个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作解码器主要依赖行为克隆训练，仅监督具体执行的电机指令，忽略了行为所服务的局部语义目标。虽然未来信息监督（帧、隐状态、轨迹、运动表征）可以增强动作学习，但这些信号只捕获可能发生的具体实现，而非后续行为的共享语义目标，导致模型缺乏对行为意图的显式建模。

Method: INDI方法包含两个阶段：训练时，一个冻结的教师VLM将当前观测、指令、粗略动作摘要和对应执行视频作为输入，以解释该演示片段的行为级意图；部署时，VLA模型从标准输入中在中间解码器层恢复该多模态意图表征，并将其与行为如何展开及实现什么的表征一起用于组织动作预测。

Result: 在SimplerEnv-Bridge上，INDI将GR00T-N1.7从64.3%提升至84.7%；在RoboCasa Kitchen上从64.1%提升至70.3%，在两个基准上对π_0.5也有一致提升。真实世界任务平均成功率从62.0%提升至68.7%，长时程任务增益最高达12.0个百分点。分析表明恢复的隐表征确实被解码器使用，捕获了行为目标与执行进度，并以目标相关的方式组织下游预测。

Conclusion: 动作解码器从显式建模其所生成行为的语义目标中获益显著，意图蒸馏方法通过教师-学生框架有效提升VLA模型的动作预测质量与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can turn multimodal context into robot actions, but their action decoders are still trained largely by behavior cloning. This supervises which motor command was demonstrated while leaving implicit the local objective served by the behavior under the instruction. Future-based supervision enriches action learning with frames, latent observations, trajectories, or motion representations, but these signals capture particular realizations of what may happen rather than the shared semantic objective of the forthcoming behavior. We propose Intention Distillation (INDI), which distills behavior-level intent into the action decoder. During training, a frozen teacher VLM interprets a demonstrated segment from the current observation, instruction, coarse action summary, and corresponding execution video. From its standard inputs, the deployed VLA recovers the resulting multimodal intent representation at an intermediate decoder layer and uses it to organize action prediction together with representations of how the behavior unfolds and what it achieves. On SimplerEnv-Bridge, INDI improves GR00T-N1.7 from 64.3% to 84.7%, and on RoboCasa Kitchen it improves the controlled GR00T-N1.7 baseline from 64.1% to 70.3%, with consistent gains on $π_{0.5}$ across both benchmarks. In real-world tasks, INDI improves average success from 62.0% to 68.7%, with gains of up to 12.0 pp on longer-horizon tasks. Further analyses show that the recovered latent is used by the decoder, captures behavior objective and execution progress, and organizes downstream predictions in an objective-dependent manner. These results show that action decoders benefit from explicitly modeling the semantic objective of the behavior they generate.

</details>
